### [1] [Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Task Learning for Joint Modulation Recognition and SINR Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28865)
*Kosar Nourolahi,Vahid Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种不确定性感知的多任务学习模型，实现了调制识别与SINR估计的联合优化。通过任务特定适配器和联合不确定性评分，在匹配信道上提升14.86%准确率，在未见信道上提升8.61%准确率，同时降低SINR估计误差约1.6 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能接收机中联合调制识别与SINR估计可减少重复处理，但这两个任务具有不同的不确定性特征，传统多任务学习难以有效处理这种差异性。

Method: 提出不确定性感知多任务模型，将短归一化I/Q窗口转换为36个确定性统计量，学习共享表示，并使用任务特定适配器进行调制分类和异方差SINR回归，结合分类熵与预测回归方差构建联合不确定性评分以支持选择性推理。

Result: 在QPSK、8PSK、16QAM和64QAM调制信号上，与常规多任务学习相比，匹配信道准确率提升14.86%，未见信道准确率提升8.61%，SINR平均绝对误差分别降低1.60 dB和1.61 dB。基于置信度的拒绝机制进一步降低了信道失配下的调制错误率。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定性感知多任务学习方法有效解决了调制识别与SINR估计任务的不确定性差异问题，显著提升了模型在匹配和未见信道条件下的性能，为智能接收机的联合信号处理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Joint modulation recognition and signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) estimation can reduce duplicated processing in intelligent receivers, but the two tasks have different uncertainty characteristics. This letter proposes an uncertainty-aware multi-task model that transforms each short normalized in-phase/quadrature window into 36 deterministic, label-free statistics, learns a shared representation, and uses task-specific adapters for modulation classification and heteroscedastic SINR regression. A joint uncertainty score combines classification entropy and predicted regression variance to support selective inference. Simulations cover QPSK, 8PSK, 16QAM, and 64QAM under matched additive white Gaussian noise/Rayleigh channels and an unseen frequency-selective Rician channel. Over five independent seeds, the proposed model improves matched and unseen-channel accuracy over conventional multi-task learning by 14.86 and 8.61 percentage points, respectively, while reducing SINR mean absolute error by 1.60 and 1.61 dB. Confidence-based rejection further lowers modulation error under channel mismatch.

</details>


### [2] [SmartBAN on Silicon by Structured Behavioral Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29036)
*Masato Yoshimi,Takahiro Ito,Kento Tanaka,Hirokazu Tanaka*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次在商业硬件上实现了ETSI SmartBAN标准，通过有限状态机建模解决了标准中设备行为规范缺失的问题，并在nRF54L15平台上验证了系统的可靠性和确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETSI SmartBAN标准虽然定义了基于TDMA的轻量级WBAN协议框架，但缺乏对设备内部行为的规范：相位控制和连接生命周期缺乏状态转换逻辑，时隙级时序和调度策略缺乏参数指导，且尚无商业硬件实现报道。

Method: 采用结构化行为建模和模型驱动实现方法：设计了两个Mealy型有限自动机（Hub：3状态5转换，Node：5状态8转换）捕捉相位控制和连接生命周期，其转换表直接映射为固件调度逻辑；在基于Arm Cortex-M33的nRF54L15无线SoC上运行Zephyr RTOS实现时隙级时序和调度策略。

Result: 在16个并发传感器节点、25小时的实验中验证了初始连接和上行数据路径：所有13个建模转换均被触发，时隙时序抖动低于1毫秒（P99 < 754μs，16个时隙间无差异），数据包投递率99.99%，支持自主断线恢复；同SoC的BLE对比显示SmartBAN以更高能耗换取了显著更低的时序抖动。

Conclusion: 有限状态机建模可有效填补标准规范空白并指导商业硬件实现；SmartBAN相比BLE能提供更好的时序确定性，适用于对时序要求严格的医疗物联网应用，但其能耗开销主要来自软件无线电处理而非协议级占空比。

Abstract: Wireless body area networks (WBANs) are a key enabling technology for the Internet of Medical Things (IoMT). SmartBAN, standardized by ETSI and later adopted as an IEC international standard, defines a lightweight WBAN protocol with time-division multiple access (TDMA)-based physical (PHY) and media access control (MAC) layers, yet no implementation on commercial hardware has been reported. The standard specifies frame formats and channel structure but leaves internal device behaviors unspecified: phase control and connection lifecycle lack transition logic, while slot-level timing and scheduling policy lack parametric guidance. This paper addresses these omissions through structured behavioral modeling and model-driven implementation. Two Mealy-type finite automata -- one for the Hub (3 states, 5 transitions), one for each Node (5 states, 8 transitions) -- capture phase control and connection lifecycle as a hardware-independent design blueprint whose transition tables map directly to firmware dispatch logic; slot-level timing and scheduling policy are resolved through realization on the nRF54L15, a commercial Arm Cortex-M33 wireless system-on-chip (SoC) running Zephyr real-time operating system (RTOS). Experiments with sixteen concurrently scheduled sensor nodes over 25 hours validate the design for the initial connection and uplink data paths: all 13 modeled transitions were exercised with sub-millisecond per-slot timing jitter ($P_{99} <$ 754 $μ$s, slot-independent across all 16 slots), 99.99% packet delivery, and autonomous disconnection recovery. A same-SoC Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) comparison quantifies the determinism-efficiency tradeoff: SmartBAN achieves substantially lower timing jitter at higher energy cost, the majority of which is attributable to software radio processing rather than the protocol-level duty cycle.

</details>


### [3] [A-MADiff: Attention-Guided Multi-Agent DRL with Diffusion Policies for Memory-Aware Task Orchestration in Mobile AIGC Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29255)
*Chongzhi Wu,Zhengtao Li,Jiawen Kang,Jinbo Wen,Xiaohuan Li,Maomao Zhang,Ekram Hossain*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了A-MADiff框架，通过多智能体深度强化学习解决移动AIGC网络中的GPU内存约束任务编排问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC任务编排研究忽略了GPU内存可行性约束。AIGC推理任务在完成前占用GPU内存，会导致边缘ASP出现GPU内存耗尽和内存不足故障，而不仅仅是增加服务延迟。

Method: 提出协作多智能体编排框架，将编排过程建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP）。设计A-MADiff算法，采用集中训练-去中心化执行范式，包含基于扩散的去中心化执行器生成多模态动作偏好，以及注意力引导的集中式评论器在GPU内存异构性下估计各智能体价值。

Result: 数值结果表明，A-MADiff在累积奖励方面显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的注意力引导多智能体深度强化学习方法有效解决了移动AIGC网络中GPU内存约束下的任务编排问题，为边缘计算环境下的AIGC服务部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence-Generated Content (AIGC) services employ Generative AI (GenAI) models to automatically generate diverse content. Mobile AIGC networks host GenAI models on edge-located AIGC Service Providers (ASPs) to deliver low-latency and personalized AIGC services for mobile users. However, AIGC inference tasks typically occupy GPU memory until task completion, causing GPU memory exhaustion at serving ASPs and triggering out-of-memory failures rather than merely increasing service latency. Existing studies on AIGC task orchestration have largely overlooked GPU memory feasibility constraints. To address this issue, we develop a cooperative multi-agent orchestration framework, in which each edge node is equipped with a scheduling agent to route tasks to local ASPs or neighboring edge nodes. Since scheduling agents make decisions based only on local observations, while peer offloading couples their resource states and long-term utilities, we formulate the orchestration process as a cooperative Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP). To solve the Dec-POMDP, we propose an \underline{A}ttention-guided \underline{M}ulti-\underline{A}gent deep reinforcement learning algorithm with \underline{Diff}usion policies (A-MADiff) under the centralized training with a decentralized execution paradigm. A-MADiff employs diffusion-based decentralized actors to generate multi-modal preferences over feasible orchestration actions, and an attention-guided centralized critic to estimate per-agent values from cross-agent states under GPU memory heterogeneity. Numerical results demonstrate that A-MADiff significantly improves the cumulative reward over the state-of-the-art baseline.

</details>


### [4] [Sense Once, Serve Many: Common-Trace Factorized Constrained PPO for Online Sensing-Session Consolidation in Multi-Tenant ISAC Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29256)
*Dang-Dung Vu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对ISAC网络中的资源管理问题，提出了CT-PPO算法，通过共轨迹因子化和奖励信用机制，显著提升了系统性能和资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC网络能够通过共享感知会话来服务兼容请求，但资源整合使接入控制、资源复用、配置选择、感知服务等级协议（SLA）、通信服务质量（QoS）以及未来承诺等多个因素相互耦合，形成复杂的联合优化问题。

Method: 将该问题建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，并提出共轨迹因子化约束近端策略优化算法（CT-PPO）。在训练过程中，随机策略副本共享相同的基础工作负载轨迹；使用留一折现蒙特卡洛回报对比为适用的行动者因子提供奖励信用，而约束信用保持因子/前缀特定。

Result: 在五个训练种子和匹配工作负载下，CT-PPO达到最高的平均宏回报，相比匹配的联合信用PPO（JC-PPO）提高0.934（95%置信区间[0.702, 1.164]），相比SLA感知贪心算法提高1.847；与JC-PPO相比，感知资源成本降低6.277，每创建会话的接受请求数提高0.0806。消融实验显示因子化代理单独无显著收益，而添加共轨迹奖励信用产生主要改进。

Conclusion: CT-PPO在不同到达负载下保持回报优势，在集群到达时增益最强；部署时使用公共观测和硬掩码，额外参数仅在训练端，行动者占用量与JC-PPO匹配，CPU延迟基本不变。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) networks can serve compatible requests through shared sensing sessions, but consolidation couples admission, reuse, profile selection, sensing service-level agreements (SLAs), communication quality of service (QoS), and future commitments. We formulate this problem as a constrained Markov decision process and propose Common-Trace Factorized Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization (CT-PPO). During training, stochastic policy replicas share the same primitive workload trace; leave-one-out discounted Monte Carlo return contrasts provide reward credit to applicable actor factors, while constraint credit remains factor/prefix-specific. Across five training seeds and matched workloads, CT-PPO achieves the highest mean macro return, exceeding matched Joint-Credit PPO (JC-PPO) by 0.934 (95% confidence interval [0.702, 1.164]) and SLA-Aware Greedy by 1.847; versus JC-PPO, it reduces sensing-resource cost by 6.277 and raises accepted requests per created session by 0.0806. A four-way ablation shows that the factorized surrogate alone yields no detectable macro-return gain, whereas adding common-trace reward credit produces the dominant improvement. Without retraining, CT-PPO retains a return advantage at low, nominal, and high arrival loads, with the strongest gain under clustered arrivals. Deployment uses public observations and hard masks; CT-PPO's extra parameters are training-side, its actor footprint matches JC-PPO, and actor-only CPU latency is effectively unchanged.

</details>


### [5] [Who Resolves Your DNS? Measuring Resolver Opacity and Closing the Visibility Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29371)
*Kedar Thiagarajan,Fabian E. Bustamante*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: DNS解析缺乏可验证的解析器路径，导致用户查询在不知情下跨越组织和国家边界。本文通过测量发现39.8%的解析链跨AS，提出了Resolver-Path方案，通过协作式带内披露和认证机制提供可验证的解析器路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNS解析协议缺乏可验证的解析器路径机制，当ISP将解析外包给第三方时，用户查询可能跨越组织和国家边界而不被察觉，客户端无法获知哪些解析器处理了查询及其位置和运营者，这种不透明性是架构性缺陷而非部署事故。

Method: 首先使用RIPE Atlas在190个国家进行测量，量化解析路径跨越组织和国家边界的现象；然后提出Resolver-Path方案，包含两个核心组件：(1)协作式带内披露，参与解析器在转发查询时报告其身份；(2)认证机制，验证合作断言的完整性、顺序性和新鲜度。

Result: 测量结果显示：在保守处理下，39.8%的解析链(6,622/16,636)使用与客户端不同AS的前端；四分之一的可地理定位任播前端对在客户端国家之外解析；Google Public DNS占跨国家情况的三分之二。Resolver-Path方案在吞吐量、延迟和CPU成本上接近中性开销，能够提供有界证据证明所选响应的报告解析器链。

Conclusion: 可报告、可验证的解析器路径应该是DNS解析协议的一等公民目标。Resolver-Path通过披露和认证机制为DNS提供了当前缺失的管辖问责性基础设施，填补了DNS架构中的重要空白。

Abstract: DNS resolution has no notion of a verifiable resolver path. When an ISP outsources resolution to a third-party provider, a user's queries can cross organizational and national boundaries without their awareness---and the client that issued them has no protocol mechanism to learn which resolvers handled the query, where they sat, or who operated them. We argue that this opacity is an architectural gap rather than a deployment accident, and that a reported, verifiable resolver path should be a first-class goal of the resolution protocol.
  We motivate this with measurement and then show the gap is cheap to close. Using RIPE Atlas across 190 countries, we find that resolution routinely leaves the client's organization and country: under a conservative in-AS treatment of unattributable observations, 39.8% of observed resolver chains (6,622 of 16,636) use a frontend in a different AS than the client, one in four geolocatable anycast frontend pairs resolves outside the client's country, and a single operator---Google Public DNS---accounts for roughly two-thirds of those out-of-country cases. We then present Resolver-Path, an approach in which participating resolvers report their identity as they forward the query. Its base layer is cooperative in-band disclosure, it carries resolver-path metadata at near-neutral throughput, latency, and CPU cost. Because a non-participating resolver can ignore or strip the option, disclosure alone establishes the verifiable presence of reported hops, not the absence of hidden ones. Attestation authenticates the integrity, ordering, and freshness of the cooperative assertions carried by the selected response. Together, disclosure and attestation provide bounded evidence about the selected response's reported resolver chain---the substrate DNS currently lacks for jurisdictional accountability.

</details>


### [6] [RL-based Network Slice Embedding over Space Division Multiplexed Elastic Optical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29444)
*Divya Khanure,Riti Gour†,Congzhou Li,Jason P. Jue*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对SDM-EON中网络切片的计算与频谱资源耦合问题，提出PPO-Full强化学习框架，通过联合优化计算节点选择和路由调制，在高负载热点流量下显著提升请求接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在SDM-EON网络切片中独立处理计算放置与路由频谱决策，导致资源耦合问题：频谱分配后可能因计算资源不足失败，或计算资源分配未考虑路径频谱可用性，造成资源浪费和效率低下。

Method: 提出路径约束强化学习框架PPO-Full，采用多维度动作空间联合选择计算节点和路由路径。通过将RL智能体动作空间限制在k最短路径节点上，并引入奖励塑形技术增强高负载下的鲁棒性，实现频谱、空间芯和计算资源的协同优化。

Result: 在24节点USNET拓扑和热点流量条件下，对比距离启发式、贪心基线和VONE-DRL基线方法，PPO-Full在高负载下持续提升请求接受率，且随着流量强度增加性能优势更加显著。

Conclusion: PPO-Full框架成功解决了SDM-EON网络切片中计算与频谱资源的联合优化问题，证明了约束动作空间强化学习方法在多资源耦合场景下的有效性，为未来网络资源管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Network slicing over space-division-multiplexed elastic optical networks (SDM-EONs) requires jointly managing spectrum, spatial cores, and compute resources, a coupling that many existing studies ignore by treating compute placement independently from routing and spectrum decisions. This disconnect can cause the spectrum to be allocated along a path, only for the request to fail due to insufficient compute resources along the path, or may result in compute resources being allocated without consideration for spectrum resource availability on the path between compute nodes. We propose a path-constrained reinforcement learning framework that addresses compute node selection and RMCSA, being aware of both resources, restricting the RL agent's action space to nodes along $k$-shortest paths between request endpoints. Training incorporates reward shaping to improve robustness under high load. We propose PPO-Full (Proximal Policy Optimization-Full), which jointly selects compute nodes and routing paths via a multi-dimensional action space, against distance-based heuristics, a greedy baseline, and a decoupled VONE-DRL baseline on a 24-node USNET topology under hotspot traffic conditions. Results demonstrate consistent improvements in acceptance rate over all baselines at high load, with gains becoming more pronounced as traffic intensity increases.

</details>


### [7] [RadioSight: Predictive mmWave XR Network Optimization from Dynamic Neural Radio Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29504)
*Lihao Zhang,Paul Kudyba,Zhenlin An,Haijian Sun*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出RadioSight系统，通过实时多模态无线场预测实现毫米波波束的主动优化，将波束搜索误差降低约50%，吞吐量提升2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代扩展现实网络依赖毫米波通信提供多Gbps吞吐量，但高方向性链路易受用户移动和障碍物影响，导致频繁中断；现有神经无线场方法仅限于离线信道重建，无法满足实时优化需求。

Method: 结合后向波束追踪与实时语义对象同步技术，预测RF几何变化而无需完整模型重训练；作为边缘可执行管线部署于商用28GHz阵列，在前一调度窗口内确定下一窗口的波束配置，避免穷举波束扫描。

Result: 实验表明：波束搜索误差降低高达约50%，中位吞吐量提升2倍，链路稳定性显著增强。

Conclusion: RadioSight实现了毫米波通信的实时预测性波束管理，为下一代XR网络提供了高效、稳定的无线连接解决方案。

Abstract: Next-generation extended reality (XR) networks rely on mmWave communication for multi-gigabit throughput, yet highly directional links are vulnerable to user mobility and blockages, causing frequent outages under reactive beam management. Emerging neural radio fields can predict radio propagation, but prior work remains limited to offline channel reconstruction. We introduce RadioSight, a real-time multi-modal radio field system for predictive mmWave optimization and proactive Multi-User MIMO beamforming. RadioSight combines backward beam-tracing with real-time semantic object synchronization to anticipate RF geometry changes without full model retraining. Implemented as an edge-executable pipeline for commercial 28 GHz arrays, RadioSight determines each scheduling window's beams during the preceding window without exhaustive beam sweeps. Experiments show that RadioSight reduces beam-search error by up to ~50%, improves median throughput by 2x, and enhances link stability.

</details>


### [8] [Matched-View Cross-Domain Evaluation of WireGuard VPN Traffic Classification Using Early-Flow Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30000)
*Yasameen Sajid Razooqi,Adrian Pekar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的VPN加密流量分类跨域评估方法，利用匹配捕获的WireGuard数据集消除会话级混淆因素，发现多尺度CNN1D模型在序列化SPLT特征上实现了最佳的跨域迁移性能（平衡准确率0.98），且训练过程无需VPN数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VPN流量分类研究使用分别采集的非VPN和VPN数据集，将会话级别的用户行为、时间差异与VPN封装效应混淆，导致模型性能评估不准确。

Method: 采用具有99.9%以上包级匹配率的WireGuard隧道数据集，在同一底层流量的非VPN视图上训练、VPN视图上测试；比较流特征统计聚合与SPLT早期流指纹，以及随机森林、XGBoost和多尺度CNN1D三种模型。

Result: 跨域迁移性能依赖于特征表示与模型选择：树集成模型在FlowFeatures上达到0.84-0.93平衡准确率，在扁平化SPLT上仅为0.60-0.75；CNN1D将SPLT作为序列处理，无需VPN训练数据即达到最佳迁移性能（平衡准确率0.98，宏F1值0.89）。

Conclusion: 通过消除会话级混淆因素的匹配捕获设计，证明了序列化特征表示与CNN架构相结合能够实现强健的跨域VPN流量分类，为无监督跨域流量分类提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Classifying VPN-encrypted traffic by application category typically relies on datasets that collect non-VPN and VPN traffic in separate sessions, conflating encapsulation effects with session-level differences in user behavior, timing, and application mix. We use a recently published WireGuard tunnel dataset in which pre- and post-tunnel traffic is captured simultaneously, with a packet-level match ratio above 99.9%. This matched-capture design eliminates session-level confounds and enables a cross-domain benchmark: models are trained on non-VPN flows and tested on the VPN view of the same underlying flows. We compare whole-flow statistical aggregates (FlowFeatures) and Sequence of Packet Length and Time (SPLT) early-flow fingerprints across Random Forest, XGBoost, and a multi-scale CNN1D. Cross-domain transfer depends jointly on representation and model: tree ensembles achieve balanced accuracy of 0.84-0.93 with FlowFeatures but only 0.60-0.75 with flattened SPLT, whereas CNN1D processes the same SPLT fingerprint as a sequence and achieves the strongest transfer overall (balanced accuracy 0.98, macro F1 0.89) without any VPN data during training.

</details>


### [9] [Predictive Traffic Shaping as a UE Network Control Loop in Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30019)
*Shriram Vasudevan,Subramanian Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究预测性流量整形技术，通过用户设备（UE）提前数秒预测服务条件并调整需求到达时间，利用预测置信度触发、剩余资源约束和债务账户公平性机制，在共享小区仿真中显著扩大了稳定可行域，并实现了原型系统PRISM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线系统通常仅对当前信道条件、队列状态和策略做出反应，而服务条件往往可以提前数秒预测。这种被动响应方式未能充分利用未来可预知的信息来优化网络资源使用和服务质量。

Method: 提出预测性流量整形方法：UE估计有用的事件前需求并在前瞻窗口内释放；采用预测置信度触发机制；将预测性服务限制在可用剩余资源内；通过债务账户保持长期公平性；使用带宽-时间模型分析效率增益、负载平滑、预测浪费和共享资源成本；设计安全服务上限和准入控制机制。

Result: 在风格化共享小区仿真中，节奏性需求释放显著扩大了稳定可行域；安全服务上限和准入控制在较长窗口下提供额外增益；PRISM原型系统成功实现了本地控制策略。

Conclusion: 预测性流量整形通过提前释放灵活需求、利用预测信息和剩余资源，能够有效提升无线系统性能，扩大稳定服务区域，同时通过债务账户机制维持长期公平性，为无线网络资源管理提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Wireless systems usually react to current channel conditions, queue state, and policy. Yet service conditions can often be anticipated seconds ahead. This paper studies predictive traffic shaping, a slower user-equipment (UE) control loop that changes when flexible demand reaches the radio access network (RAN). The UE estimates useful pre-event demand and releases it across a lookahead window. Cooperative deployments may also send a compact future-risk descriptor to the network. The trigger uses prediction confidence. Predictive service is confined to available surplus, while a debt account preserves long-term fairness after temporary pre-event preference. A bandwidth-time model captures efficiency gains and load smoothing. It also accounts for prediction waste and shared-resource cost. In a stylized shared- cell simulation, paced demand release substantially expands the stable-feasible region. A safe service cap and admission control provide further gains at longer windows. PRISM, an application-owned middleware prototype, implements the local control policy using ordinary mobile transfer mechanisms.

</details>


### [10] [Ray Tracing-Based LoRaWAN Gateway Placement for Reliable Connectivity in Amazonian Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30890)
*Cláudio Modesto,Lucas Mozart,Cleverson Nahum,Bruno Castro,Aldebaro Klautau*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究森林环境中LoRaWAN网络规划中信道模型选择对网关部署的影响，发现基于特定站点的信道模型比通用信道模型能更准确地部署更少的网关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在亚马逊雨林等森林环境中部署物联网时，确保终端设备与网关之间的可靠通信是关键挑战。信道条件严重影响通信可靠性，因此网络规划阶段选择合适的信道模型对于准确模拟至关重要。现有研究缺乏对不同信道模型如何影响网络规划决策（特别是网关部署）的系统评估。

Method: 提出优化模型评估不同类型信道对森林场景覆盖率和数据包交付率的影响。使用射线追踪、经验性和随机性三类信道方法，评估信道选择对网关部署、终端设备覆盖率和通信系统可靠性的影响。

Result: 基于站点无关信道模型的网关部署会高估满足网络要求所需的网关数量；而使用站点特定信道模型可以在满足相同网络要求的前提下，用更少的网关实现目标，从而降低网络部署成本。

Conclusion: 信道模型选择对物联网网络规划具有重要影响。在网络规划阶段采用准确的、特定站点的信道模型，能够实现更优化的网关部署方案，避免因信道模型不准确导致的资源浪费，为森林等复杂环境中的网络规划提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Network planning is an important task in wireless communications, as it helps network operators avoid unnecessary costs. In the context of the internet of things, using long-range wide-area network technologies in the Amazon rainforest, a key challenge is ensuring reliable communication between end-devices and gateways (GWs). In this sense, this reliability is strongly affected by channel conditions. Thus, during the planning phase, choosing the appropriate channel model is an important decision for accurate simulations. Given this motivation, in this work, we propose an optimization model to evaluate the impact of different types of channels on coverage and packet delivery ratio in a forest scenario. We used channels from ray tracing, empirical, and stochastic approaches to assess how decisions made during the network planning phase, in terms of the channel used, affect GW placement and, specifically, the percentage of end-devices covered and the reliability of the communication system. Our results show that GW placement based on site-independent channels can overestimate the number of GWs required to meet the network requirements, whereas using site-specific channels allows us to satisfy the same requirements with fewer GWs.

</details>

### [11] [Adaptive KV Retention for LLM Agents at Human-Approval Timescales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30830)
*Minseo Choi,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文针对需要人工审批的智能体LLM请求（可能暂停数分钟到数小时），提出了一种分层KV保留控制器。通过GPU机会成本模型，在主机内存中动态选择保留或驱逐暂停请求的KV状态，相比vLLM基线提升活跃请求吞吐量23-51%，相比MORI提升22-29%，相比Continuum提升41-52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM服务系统仅考虑秒级的工具调用暂停，而智能体LLM请求可能因等待人工审批而被暂停数分钟甚至数小时。这种长时间暂停对GPU服务性能提出了新的挑战：保留暂停请求的KV状态虽能实现快速恢复，但会占用GPU容量导致活跃服务吞吐量下降41%；而驱逐KV状态虽避免资源占用，但恢复时延迟增加近10倍。需要在两者之间寻求平衡。

Method: 提出基于GPU机会成本的分层保留控制器。核心创新是将保留或重建暂停请求KV状态所消耗的服务容量统一表示为GPU时间成本。控制器在主机内存层面运作，在无限期保留和基于负载索引的过期策略之间进行选择，通过校准等待样本进行决策，无需预测每个请求的具体等待时间。

Result: 在人工审批工作负载下，所提出的控制器显著提升了系统性能：相比vLLM基线提升活跃请求吞吐量23-51%；相比MORI提升22-29%；相比Continuum提升41-52%。控制器有效平衡了资源保留成本与恢复延迟之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 通过GPU机会成本建模，本文成功解决了长时间暂停请求的KV状态管理问题。所提出的分层控制器无需请求级等待时间预测即可做出有效决策，在人工审批场景下显著优于现有系统。这为智能体LLM服务中的长时暂停场景提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Unlike the seconds-scale tool-call pauses targeted by prior agent-serving systems, agentic LLM requests can be suspended for minutes or hours while waiting for human approval. We study how suspension and resumption affect GPU serving performance and develop a retention policy that balances active-serving capacity against future recomputation under uncertain approval waits. The central tension is severe because retaining suspended KV preserves fast resume but can consume enough GPU capacity to reduce active-serving goodput by 41%, while evicting it avoids that residency cost at the expense of nearly $10\times$ higher resume latency when the request returns. We develop a tiered retention controller around GPU opportunity cost, which expresses the serving capacity consumed by preserving or reconstructing a suspended request's KV state in a common GPU-time cost. Within host memory, the controller selects between indefinite retention and load-indexed expiration using calibration wait samples, without requiring per-request wait prediction. On human-scale approval workloads, our controller improves active-request goodput by 23-51% over the vLLM baselines, 22-29% over MORI, and 41-52% over Continuum.

</details>

### [12] [Performance Evaluation of RED-ONION: A High-Speed Disk-to-Disk Transfer System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29053)
*Keichi Takahashi,Hiroaki Kataoka,Takeo Hosomi,Ayahiro Takaki,Yasunori Kakizawa,Shuichi Ihara,Nobuaki Hashizume,Susumu Date*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RED-ONION系统实现了跨太平洋100 Gbps链路上单文件90 Gbps传输速率，95秒完成1TB数据传输，解决了实验仪器数据传输瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代实验仪器产生数据的速度超过了通用文件传输接口的传输能力，导致数据传输成为研究过程的瓶颈。此外，数据产生仪器与高性能计算系统在地理和组织上分离，需要无缝连接以支持数据驱动科学研究。

Method: 提出RED-ONION高速磁盘到磁盘传输系统，集成数据传输节点、专用高带宽网络、全闪存并行文件系统和多线程传输软件。系统并行化网络传输和存储访问，针对从发送端读取到接收端写入的整个路径进行端到端优化，目标是实现单文件单节点对的线速传输。

Result: 在Atlanta与Tokyo之间100 Gbps、150ms RTT的跨太平洋链路上部署原型系统，单文件1TB传输达到90 Gbps，约95秒完成。该性能使大规模数据集传输成为常规操作，计算中心可像本地仪器一样服务远程设备。

Conclusion: RED-ONION成功解决了实验数据传输瓶颈，实现了跨地理和组织边界的无缝连接，使得计算中心能够高效服务于远程研究仪器，为数据驱动科学提供了关键基础设施支持。

Abstract: Modern experimental instruments produce data faster than general-purpose file transfer interfaces can move it, so delivery to the computing infrastructure has become a bottleneck in the research process. At many universities and research institutes, moreover, the instruments that generate research data and the high-performance computing systems that analyze it are separated both geographically and organizationally, because each demands its own expertise and installation environment. Connecting the two seamlessly is a pressing challenge for data-driven science. This article presents RED-ONION, a high-speed disk-to-disk transfer system that connects research facilities, on campus and beyond, to a computing center. The system combines data transfer nodes, a dedicated high-bandwidth network, an all-flash parallel file system, and multi-threaded transfer software that parallelizes network transmission and storage access. The design targets the wire rate both along the entire path, from the read on the sender storage to the write on the receiver storage, and for a single file between one pair of nodes rather than only in aggregate over many files or nodes. We describe the end-to-end optimizations across the transfer software, the operating system, and the storage that this requires. We evaluate a prototype deployed over a 100 Gbps transpacific path between Atlanta and Tokyo with a 150 ms round-trip time, on which a single 1 TB file transfer reached 90 Gbps, delivering a terabyte in approximately 95 s. Moving a dataset of this size therefore becomes a routine step, and the computing center serves an instrument as if the two were co-located.

</details>


### [13] [CLASP: Chained-Request-Aware Scaling and Operator Placement for Serverless Stream Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29103)
*Tianyu Qi,Maria A. Rodriguez,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CLASP，一种面向有状态无服务器流处理的扩缩容与调度策略，通过建模链式请求开销来优化实例部署与状态迁移，显著提升吞吐量并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流处理系统在调整算子并行度与实例放置时未充分考虑链式请求开销，导致worker数量估算偏差：数量不足无法跟上输入速率，数量过多则增加跨worker的链式请求比例，从而提高端到端延迟。

Method: 提出CLASP策略，在运行时从观测指标估算执行开销与链式请求开销；基于覆盖两种开销的容量模型调整算子并行度，并将算子打包至能维持目标输入速率的最少worker上；做出扩缩容决策后，将算子状态与实例一起迁移以最小化执行暂停时间。

Result: 实验表明，与最先进的扩缩容策略相比，CLASP将吞吐量提升高达3.3倍，并将中位端到端延迟降低高达76%。

Conclusion: CLASP通过在容量模型中显式建模链式请求开销并协同优化扩缩容与状态迁移，有效解决了有状态无服务器流处理环境中的性能瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Stateful serverless (Function-as-a-Service) environments, whose workers host state servers, are increasingly used for stream processing. A stream application is a pipeline of operators, where each operator forwards intermediate data downstream through a chained request. As input rates fluctuate, the system should adjust operator parallelism and place instances across workers to sustain the incoming rate. Existing approaches do so without fully accounting for chained-request overhead, leading them to misestimate the required number of workers. Too few leave the cluster unable to keep up with the input rate, while too many route a larger fraction of chained requests across worker boundaries, increasing end-to-end latency.
  We propose CLASP, a scaling and scheduling strategy for stream processing in stateful serverless environments. At runtime, CLASP estimates execution cost and chained-request cost from observed metrics. Under a capacity model that covers the two costs, it adjusts operator parallelism and packs operators onto the fewest workers that can sustain the target input rate. Once a scaling decision is made, CLASP migrates each operator's state together with its instances, thereby minimizing execution pause time. Experiments show that CLASP improves throughput by up to 3.3x and reduces median end-to-end latency by up to 76% compared with state-of-the-art scaling strategies.

</details>


### [14] [Bridging Agent Semantics with Spot Capacity: An Elastic and Recoverable Service Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29581)
*Minchen Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SemSpot，一种语义感知的服务模型，使代理应用能够利用LLM推理平台的Spot容量，在保证任务语义完成的前提下显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理驱动的云推理工作负载日益增长，这些工作负载中的模型调用在紧急性、冗余性、完成语义和重放成本方面存在差异。现有的MaaS平台服务模型主要在请求、作业或端点级别运作，对将瞬时平台供应与代理任务的演进语义相结合的支持有限。

Method: SemSpot在请求级别让提供商发布短期报价（包括价格、完成概率和失败通知截止时间），代理运行时根据当前任务状态和完成规则选择报价。通过对六个代理基准的1,535个案例进行审计，识别出四种常见的工作流结构。在Token级别，SemSpot进一步保留了提供商推理状态和运行时验证的语义片段。

Result: 审计识别了四种重复出现的工作流结构，展示了该服务模型如何产生不同的成本、服务时间和回退行为。Token级别的SemSpot能够在长请求中保留提供商推理状态和语义片段。

Conclusion: SemSpot需要开发服务模型、经济边界和跨层研究议程来实现，为MaaS平台提供了专门的语义感知支持，使代理应用能够有效利用Spot容量。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly drive long-running cloud inference workloads in which model calls differ in urgency, redundancy, completion semantics, and replay cost. Model-as-a-Service (MaaS) platforms expose several service models for trading cost against latency, availability, and capacity commitment. These models operate primarily at request, job, or endpoint scopes and provide limited support for combining transient platform supply with the evolving semantics of an agent task.
  We present SemSpot, a semantics-aware service model that allows agent applications to leverage the spot capacity of LLM inference platforms. At the request level, SemSpot lets a provider publish short-lived offers over successful price, completion probability, and failure-notification deadline; the agent runtime selects among these offers using the current task state and completion rule. An audit of 1,535 cases from six agent benchmarks identifies four recurring workflow structures and shows how this service model may produce different cost, service-time, and fallback behavior. With specialized MaaS support, token-level SemSpot further preserves provider inference state and runtime-verified semantic segments inside a long request. We develop the service model, economic boundary, and the cross-layer research agenda required to realize SemSpot.

</details>


### [15] [A Smallest-Need-First Job Scheduling Framework with Adaptive Optimization of Idle Node Counts for Energy-Efficient HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29656)
*Reza Pulungan,Raka Satya Prasasta,Santana Yuda Pradata,Mursalim,Hiroyuki Takizawa,Muhammad Alfian Amrizal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了SNF-ICON事件驱动控制器，结合最小需求优先调度、预测唤醒和自适应热备控制，在减少空闲能耗的同时控制唤醒延迟。实验表明其能降低平均等待时间，但最优策略高度依赖平台特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算（HPC）集群的电源状态管理需要在减少空闲能耗和避免刚性并行作业的过度唤醒延迟之间取得平衡，现有方法难以兼顾两者。

Method: 提出SNF-ICON事件驱动控制器，包含三个核心组件：1）最小需求优先（SNF）gang调度；2）基于样本筛选（充分性、指数类变异性、低自相关、可接受KS距离）的预测唤醒时机；3）满足队列、事件和到达新近度条件时的自适应热备优化。根据数据条件在ICON模式和SNF+IPM回退模式间切换。

Result: 在DAS2跟踪片段和生成的工作负载上评估，相比FCFS+回填基线，SNF-ICON在所有6个案例中降低了平均等待时间，在5个案例中能耗接近至少一个启发式基线。生成的工作负载主要在ICON模式下运行，DAS2工作负载主要在回退模式下运行。跨平台结果显示对节点转换和功耗模型有强依赖性。

Conclusion: 不存在适用于所有情况的最优策略或参数集，策略选择需根据具体平台的节点转换特性和功耗模型进行适配。

Abstract: Power-state management in high-performance computing (HPC) clusters must reduce idle energy without excessive wake-up delays for rigid parallel jobs. This paper presents SNF-ICON, an event-driven controller combining smallest-need-first (SNF) gang scheduling, predictive wake timing, and adaptive warm-spare control. At each scheduler invocation, recent interarrival and completed-service samples are screened for sufficiency, exponential-like variability, low lag-one autocorrelation, and acceptable Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance. Rejected or data-sparse windows use SNF+IPM (Intelligent Power Manager), whereas accepted windows activate release prediction and an exponential next-event model. Warm-spare optimization is applied only when queue, event, and arrival-recency conditions permit, balancing estimated waiting and non-compute energy over a timeout-capped horizon. We evaluate four DAS2 trace segments and a generated Markovian workload on AOBA-derived 64-node models, plus SDSC Blue on an AOBA-derived 1152-node model. SNF-ICON is compared with SNF+IPM and First Come First Served (FCFS) + backfilling with IPM. It reduces average waiting time relative to the FCFS-based baseline in all six cases and remains close to at least one heuristic energy baseline in five. The generated workload spends substantial time in ICON mode, whereas DAS2 workloads operate mainly in fallback. Furthermore, cross-platform results show strong dependence on node-transition and power models. Thus, no single policy or parameter set works best in every case.

</details>


### [16] [Projection-Free Bandit Online Optimization for Multi-Agent Systems with Dynamic Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30159)
*Xia Jiang,Lu Liu,Gang Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对多智能体动态系统，提出了一种基于零阶梯度估计和无投影条件梯度更新的分布式bandit在线反馈优化算法，在输入约束和时变成本函数条件下仅利用实时输入输出数据实现了次线性动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线学习与优化算法通常需要精确的系统模型，限制了其在实际场景中的应用；因此亟需一种仅依赖实时输入输出数据、能够在输入约束与时变环境下工作的分布式在线优化方法。

Method: 采用平滑零阶单点估计器，仅基于代价评估构建局部梯度近似，从而避免对模型信息的依赖；引入无投影的条件梯度更新以有效处理输入约束，适合在线与大规模场景。

Result: 建立了依赖系统非平稳性时变度量的次线性动态遗憾界，并通过数值仿真验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式bandit在线反馈优化算法在无需系统模型信息的情况下，能够在输入约束与时变环境下实现有效决策，为多智能体动态系统的在线优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper investigates distributed online optimization for multi-agent dynamical systems with constrained inputs and time-varying cost functions. While online convex optimization offers a principal framework for sequential decision-making, existing online learning and optimization algorithms typically require accurate system models, limiting their applicability in practical settings. To overcome this challenge, we propose a distributed bandit online feedback optimization algorithm that relies solely on real-time input-output data. The algorithm employs a smoothing zeroth-order one-point estimator to construct local gradient approximations directly from cost evaluations. Additionally, to enforce input constraints effectively, we integrate a projection-free conditional gradient update, making the algorithm well-suited for online and large-scale settings. Furthermore, we establish a sublinear dynamic regret bound that depends on a temporal variation measure of system non-stationarity. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [17] [PowerSlider: Exploiting Phase Asymmetry for LLM Serving under Demand Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21719)
*Yueying Li,Jiayang Chen,Yuanfan Chen,Leo Han,Haoran Qiu,Esha Choukse,Rodrigo Fonseca,Udit Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PowerSlider是一个面向LLM推理集群的动态功耗管理系统，通过Prefill-Think-Answer解耦和频率调节，在30%功耗削减下保持78.3%有效吞吐量，比最佳基线提升1.64倍，在电网紧急场景下维持92%平均吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI推理集群受瞬时功率限制约束增强，电网运营商要求需求响应并施加时变功率上限。现有LLM服务系统要么优化静态能耗目标，要么在负载下丢弃固定优先级层级，导致功率包络变化时有效吞吐量急剧下降。LLM流水线各阶段（计算密集型prefill、内存密集型decode、思考阶段）对频率敏感度不同，需要将功率上限引导到每瓦性能损失最小的阶段。

Method: 提出Flex SLO合约将用户松弛度转化为优化约束；采用prefill-think-answer解耦以暴露各阶段频率和KV控制；使用KKT在线求解器在7.7毫秒内响应功率上限变化；设计故障安全机制在DVFS触及静态功耗底线时对耗尽实例进行功率门控。

Result: 在SGLang和生产追踪数据上测试：30%功耗削减下维持78.3%在线有效吞吐量，而最佳基线仅47.6%（提升1.64倍）；延迟关键尾延迟保持在标称值的1.3倍内（基线2.3-6倍，最高达12倍）；在CAISO电网紧急日重放中实现92%平均有效吞吐量（谷值54%，所有基线低于7%）。

Conclusion: PowerSlider成功实现了LLM推理集群在动态功率约束下的高效运行，通过阶段感知的功率调控显著提升了系统在功率受限场景下的性能保持能力，为AI基础设施应对电网需求响应提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: AI inference clusters are increasingly constrained by instantaneous power, not just energy: grid operators condition new capacity on demand response, imposing time-varying power caps. Existing LLM serving systems optimize a static energy objective or shed fixed priority tiers under load; either way, goodput collapses when the power envelope moves. An LLM pipeline is not a uniform load: compute-bound prefill loses throughput almost linearly with GPU frequency, memory-bound answer decode sustains it down to $0.57\times$ nominal, and reasoning's thinking phase couples KV-cache capacity to scheduling -- so a cap should be steered to where each watt costs the least performance. PowerSlider does so with a new Flex SLO contract that turns bounded user slack into an optimization constraint, prefill--think--answer disaggregation exposing per-stage frequency and KV control, and a Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) online solver re-solving within 7.7 ms of every cap change, backed by a consolidated fail-safe that power-gates drained instances when DVFS bottoms out on static power. On SGLang with production traces, \sys{} sustains 78.3\% online goodput at a 30\% cap reduction versus 47.6\% for the best of five baselines ($1.64\times$), holds latency-critical tails within $1.3\times$ of nominal (baselines: $2.3$--$6\times$, up to $12\times$), and delivers 92\% mean goodput through a replayed CAISO grid-emergency day bottoming at $0.41\times$ (54\% at the trough; every baseline below 7\%).

</details>

### [18] [High-Performance Low-Power Adiabatic Systolic Array Design in Advanced FinFET Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30058)
*Jun Yin,Liangtao Dai,Yimin Gao,Mircea R. Stan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在16nm FinFET工艺下实现的绝热逻辑MAC脉动阵列设计方法，在1GHz时钟频率下实现了核心级和系统级分别高达42%和36%的功耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着FinFET工艺节点的发展，由于功耗和热问题（暗硅现象）的限制，时钟频率已经趋于稳定，即使器件本征速度仍在持续提升。这种收敛为绝热逻辑在GHz时钟频率下保持绝热行为创造了机会，打破了传统绝热逻辑仅适用于低时钟频率的限制。

Method: 在商用16nm FinFET工艺中实现了绝热逻辑(Adiabatic Logic, AL)设计方法，具体通过MAC脉动阵列进行验证。设计包括谐振式4相电源时钟(PCK)发生器，以及数字到AL和AL到数字的接口电路。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在1GHz工作频率下，绝热逻辑MAC脉动阵列相比数字对应方案，在核心层级实现高达42%的功耗降低，在系统层级实现36%的功耗降低。研究还指出，扩展到更先进的工艺节点应该能提供更好的功耗/性能指标。

Conclusion: 绝热逻辑在先进FinFET工艺节点下能够在GHz级时钟频率下有效工作并实现显著的功耗降低，证明了绝热逻辑在高性能应用中的可行性。随着工艺节点的进一步推进，绝热逻辑的功耗/性能优势将更加明显。

Abstract: Adiabatic logic has traditionally been recognized as a low-power solution but constrained to low clock speeds to preserve adiabatic behavior. For advanced FinFET nodes, however, clock frequencies have plateaued due to power/thermal concerns (dark silicon) even as the intrinsic device speeds have continued to scale. This convergence opens an opportunity for adiabatic logic to maintain adiabatic behavior even at GHz clocks. We demonstrate an adiabatic logic (AL) design methodology through a MAC systolic array implemented in commercial 16 nm FinFET technology with a resonant 4-phase power clock (PCK) generator, including digital-to-AL and AL-to-digital interfaces. Simulations show that the AL MAC systolic array at 1 GHz achieves power reductions of up to 42% and 36% at the core and system levels, respectively, compared to digital counterparts. Scaling to more advanced nodes should provide even better power/performance metrics.

</details>


### [19] [FABO: Agent-Guided Discovery of Joint Breakpoint Optimization for Timing-Driven Routing Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30268)
*Shang Liu,Wenji Fang,Jing Wang,Hongxin Kong,Yao Lu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文提出了FABO算法，通过联合优化共享路径的断点位置改进了现有SALT算法的布线树结构，在保证路径延迟约束的同时有效降低了线长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布线树的拓扑结构直接影响多端线网的线长、拥塞、电容和延迟。SALT是当前解决线长最小化和路径延迟约束问题的最先进构造算法，但其存在结构性限制：逐个修复sink路径导致无法联合决策共享根端线段的分叉点位置，可能过早分叉造成线段重复。

Method: 开发了结合并行探索与独立验证的智能体框架来引导算法搜索；基于该框架发现的SALT结构性限制，提出了流感知断点优化算法FABO，对共享线段的根到sink路径进行联合断点优化，同时保持每个sink的延迟约束预算。

Result: 在ICCAD15的129万个线网和SALT的20点延迟容忍度调度上，FABO在每个设置下都降低了平均FLUTE归一化线长，整体峰值下降0.83%，对于至少30引脚的线网下降2.66%；FABO-FAST在SALT 1.3倍运行时间内识别并优化了FABO能显著降低线长的大部分线网。

Conclusion: 语言模型引导的搜索能够发现现有算法的结构性限制并构造改进算法；FABO成功突破了SALT的局部决策限制，在保持延迟约束的同时有效降低了线长，为早期布线阶段提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: The topology of a routing tree determines how a multi-pin net branches and shares physical wire, directly affecting wirelength, congestion, capacitance, and delay. We study a central early-stage routing problem: minimizing wirelength while bounding the root-to-sink path stretch for every sink. SALT is the state-of-the-art constructive algorithm for this problem. We ask whether language-model-guided search can discover a constructive algorithm that improves on SALT. To make this search reliable, we develop an agent framework that combines parallel exploration with independent checking. Applied to SALT, the framework discovers a structural limitation: SALT repairs one sink path at a time and therefore never jointly decides where paths sharing root-side wire should split. This sink-local choice can split the paths too early and duplicate wire. This discovery leads to Flow-Aware Breakpoint Optimization (FABO), which jointly optimizes breakpoints across root-to-sink paths that share wire while preserving every sink's stretch budget. Across 1.29 million ICCAD15 nets and SALT's 20-point stretch-tolerance schedule, FABO reduces average FLUTE-normalized wirelength at every setting, with peak same reductions of 0.83% overall and 2.66% for nets with at least 30 pins. With 1.3x SALT's runtime, FABO-FAST identifies and optimizes most nets for which FABO provides a substantial wirelength reduction. Code is available at https://github.com/DevinShang/routing-FABO.

</details>


### [20] [Scalable AXI4 Transaction Monitoring for Mixed-Criticality SoCs: From Phase-Level Precision to ID-Level Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30435)
*Chaoqun Liang,Thomas Benz,Alessandro Ottaviano,Michael Rogenmoser,Luca Benini,Angelo Garofalo,Davide Rossi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可配置的AXI4协议违规和时序故障检测硬件IP，通过cut-and-drain隔离机制恢复互连活性，在面积开销与监测粒度之间实现有效权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AXI4开放标准协议的片上互连混合关键性SoC缺乏协议级超时机制，当从设备或主设备因硬件故障、辐射诱发干扰或软件错误而发生故障或停顿时，系统面临死锁和错过实时截止期的风险。

Method: 提出三种递减监测粒度的设计：相位级跟踪(PLT)提供跨单个协议相位的周期精确故障定位；通道级跟踪(CLT)将各相位监控器合并为通道级监控；ID级跟踪(ILT)仅监控每ID事务边界以实现亚线性面积扩展。采用cut-and-drain隔离机制在运行时检测AXI4协议违规和时序故障并恢复互连活性。

Result: 在GlobalFoundries 12nm工艺下，CLT相比PLT面积减少36.7%，同时保持最坏情况检测界限且检测延迟开销最小；ILT面积减少89.2%，适用于严格约束部署，代价是中位检测延迟提高3.7倍且故障定位更粗糙。在RISC-V SoC上进行120万场景故障注入实验，所有表现为AXI4协议或活性违规的故障均被检测到，观测到的检测延迟始终在理论最坏情况预测范围内。

Conclusion: 该硬件IP能够有效检测AXI4协议违规和时序故障，在保证无漏检的前提下实现了面积与监测粒度的灵活权衡，为混合关键性SoC提供了可靠的互连活性保障方案。

Abstract: Mixed-criticality Systems-on-Chip (SoCs) with on-chip interconnects based on the AXI4 open standard protocol lack a protocol-level timeout mechanism, exposing systems to deadlocks and missed real-time deadlines when subordinate devices or managers fail or stall due to hardware faults, radiation-induced upsets, or software errors. This work presents a configurable hardware intellectual property (IP), non-intrusive in fault-free operation, that detects AXI4 protocol violations and timing faults at runtime and restores interconnect liveness through a cut-and- drain isolation mechanism. To address the fundamental trade-off between monitoring granularity and area cost, we introduce three designs at decreasing monitoring granularity: Phase-Level Track-ing (PLT), which provides cycle-accurate fault localization across individual protocol phases; Channel-Level Tracking (CLT), which coalesces per-phase monitors into channel-level supervision; and ID-Level Tracking (ILT), which achieves sub-linear area scaling by monitoring only per-ID transaction boundaries. Synthesized in GlobalFoundries 12 nm technology, CLT reduces area by 36.7% relative to PLT while preserving worst-case detection bounds at a minimal detection latency overhead, whereas ILT achieves an 89.2% area reduction suitable for tightly constrained deployments at the cost of a 3.7x higher median detection latency with coarser fault localization. Fault injection campaigns on a RISC-V SoC across 1.2 million scenarios confirm that no fault manifesting as an AXI4 protocol or liveness violation escaped detection, with observed detection latencies consistently bounded by theoretical worst-case predictions.

</details>


### [21] [Non-uniform Memory Partitioning For Low-Power Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30444)
*Simon Richter,Darío Fernández Khatiboun,Maryam Sadeghi,Milad Zamani,Farshad Moradi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经元发放率差异的非均匀片上存储架构，通过将高频神经元的突触权重分配到低功耗存储区，在降低61%存储访问功耗的同时，面积开销仅为传统方案的2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）在处理时序稀疏数据方面具有优势，但其时间步进式处理模式导致对存储在SRAM中的突触权重的频繁访问，这成为功耗的主要来源。传统均匀分区存储方案在降低功耗时面临较大的面积开销。

Method: 利用网络中神经元平均发放率的显著差异，提出一种由多个非均匀大小存储体组成的片上存储架构；将高频发放神经元的权重分配至浅层、低访问成本的存储区，将低频访问的权重分配至深层、高密度存储区；基于应用需求和硬件约束进行自动探索以寻找最优存储配置。

Result: 在28nm CMOS工艺下，与传统设计相比，突触权重存储访问功耗降低高达61%；与达到类似功耗降低效果的传统均匀分区存储相比，面积开销降低2.1倍。

Conclusion: 该非均匀存储架构有效解决了SNN功耗与面积开销之间的权衡问题，证明了利用神经元发放率异质性进行存储优化的有效性，为低功耗SNN硬件实现提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) naturally excel in processing temporally rich and sparse data. However, because of their time-stepped processing, memory access, specifically to synaptic weights stored in SRAM (static random-access memory), tends to dominate total power consumption. To address this issue, without incurring a large area overhead, we propose to leverage the greatly varying average firing rate of neurons in the network to efficiently allocate synaptic weights to an on-chip memory consisting of multiple non-uniformly sized memory banks. By assigning weights of frequently firing neurons to shallow, low-access cost memory and less actively accessed weights to deeper, high-density memories, the average power consumption of the synaptic weight memory is decreased without incurring a large area overhead. To benchmark our proposed architecture and find optimal configurations of memory arrangements, we perform an automatic exploration based on application requirements and hardware constraints. For memory designs synthesized in 28-nm CMOS technology, we show that our architecture can achieve a synaptic weight memory access power reduction of up to 61\% compared to a conventional design, with a 2.1$\times$ lower area overhead, as compared to a traditional uniformly partitioned memory bank that achieves a comparable reduction.

</details>


### [22] [CHIPSMORE: Compute-in-Interconnect and -Memory Chiplets for Multi-Mode Multi-Request LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30509)
*Yue Jiet Chong,Yimin Wang,Zhen Wu,Zixuan Wang,Wei Zhang,Xuanyao Fong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CHIPSMORE是一种多模式、多请求LLM推理加速器，通过整合计算互连和CIM技术，实现了高达2.38倍的吞吐量提升和27倍的能效提升（相比Nvidia H100）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推理在适应模式、上下文长度和请求并发方面表现出显著的可变性，这给在计算内存（CIM）加速器上维持高利用率、内存效率和可扩展性能带来了挑战。现有的解决方案难以同时满足多样化工作负载下的性能需求。

Method: CHIPSMORE采用异构处理单元，由阻性存储器模拟计算内存（RRAM-ACIM）和静态存储器数字计算内存（SRAM-DCIM）组成，通过可编程的PE间计算网络（IPCN）互连。提出可组合的分层键值（KV）内存方案，动态分配路由暂存器、SRAM-DCIM和嵌入式DRAM资源；设计非复制多请求执行流水线，利用请求级并行性而不复制预训练权重；实现状态感知资源重配置机制，选择性保留运行时状态并对非活动资源进行电源门控。

Result: 使用周期精确的硬件-软件协同仿真进行评估，CHIPSMORE在各种模型大小、上下文长度和批处理大小下有效维持高吞吐量，同时保持良好的功耗扩展性。与Nvidia H100相比，在Mistral-7B推理任务中，CHIPSMORE实现了高达2.38倍的吞吐量提升和27倍的能效提升，同时消除了多请求服务中的权重复制。

Conclusion: CHIPSMORE成功解决了LLM推理中的多样化工作负载挑战，通过创新的异构架构和资源管理机制，在保持高利用率、内存效率和能效的同时，实现了对长上下文和批量推理的可扩展支持，为LLM推理加速提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference exhibits substantial variability across adaptation modes, context lengths, and request concurrency, creating challenges for maintaining high utilization, memory efficiency, and scalable performance on compute-in-memory (CIM) accelerators. This paper presents CHIPSMORE, a multi-mode and multi-request LLM inference accelerator that integrates compute-in-interconnect and CIM to support both base-mode and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) inference under diverse workloads. CHIPSMORE employs heterogeneous processing elements consisting of resistive RAM analog compute-in-memory (RRAM-ACIM) and static RAM digital compute-in-memory (SRAM-DCIM) interconnected through a programmable Inter-PE computational network (IPCN). A composable hierarchical key-value (KV) memory scheme dynamically allocates router scratchpad, SRAM-DCIM, and embedded DRAM (eDRAM) resources according to workload requirements, enabling scalable support for long-context and batched inference. Furthermore, a non-replicated multi-request execution pipeline exploits request-level parallelism without duplicating pretrained weights, while a state-aware resource reconfiguration mechanism selectively retains runtime states and power-gates inactive resources to improve energy efficiency. Evaluation using cycle-accurate hardware-software co-simulation demonstrates that CHIPSMORE effectively sustains high throughput across varying model sizes, context lengths, and batch sizes while maintaining favorable power scaling. Compared with Nvidia H100, CHIPSMORE achieves up to $2.38\times$ higher throughput and $27\times$ higher energy efficiency on Mistral-7B inference while eliminating weight replication for multi-request serving.

</details>


### [23] [LLM-based Hardware Development with Hierarchical IRs and End-to-End Multi-Agent Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30659)
*Chenyang Yin,Agasthi Haputhanthri,Aditya Anirudh Jonnalagadda,Zhenyu Bai,Yuanming Song,Saranyu Chattopadhyay,Mohammad Fadiheh,Tom Zelazny,Subhasish Mitra,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于LLM的硬件开发框架，通过分层中间表示和多智能体工作流实现复杂硬件设计，在Verilog-Eval基准上达到95.5%的pass@5率，是唯一能够完成复杂端到端功能设计的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在软件开发中广泛应用，但在复杂硬件设计中的应用仍受限。主要瓶颈包括：公开硬件训练数据稀缺，硬件设计方法论与软件根本不同，硬件设计要求模型理解模块边界、模块间连接及验证需求，而非仅进行直接RTL生成。

Method: 提出分层中间表示和多智能体端到端工作流：1）引入两种结构化IR——架构草图（捕捉模块拓扑和互连）和操作规范（定义模块功能和接口）；2）利用IR将复杂设计分解为子模块，指定模块功能，推导测试验证方案；3）集成多智能体调试循环，支持错误反馈和仿真信号探针控制。

Result: 在Verilog-Eval基准上达到95.5%的pass@5率，超越当前最先进LLM生成框架；在涵盖通用处理器到数字信号处理系统的新案例研究中，现有方法无法完成此类复杂设计，而本框架是唯一能产生功能完整的端到端设计的方案；生成的RTL符合工业标准设计规则，通过lint检查，功能正确且完全可综合。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功解决了LLM在复杂硬件设计中的应用瓶颈，通过分层抽象和多智能体协作实现了从规范到验证的完整设计流程，为LLM在硬件设计领域的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software development, but their use in complex hardware design remains limited. This gap stems from both the scarcity of public hardware training data and the fundamentally different methodologies used in hardware design. In particular, applying LLMs to hardware requires more than direct RTL generation: the model must understand module boundaries, inter-module connections, and verification requirements. In this paper, we present an LLM-based hardware development framework with hierarchical intermediate representations (IRs) and an end-to-end multi-agent workflow. The core idea is to provide an abstraction of hardware design to LLMs through two structured IRs: Architectural Sketch, which captures module topology and interconnection, and Operational Specification, which defines per-module functionality and interfaces. Our framework uses these IRs to decompose a complex design into sub-modules, specify the per-block functionality, and derive how each module should be tested and verified. We incorporate a multi-agent debug loop in the framework, allowing agents to get the error feedback and control the debug details such as the signals to be probed for simulation. We evaluate our framework on Verilog-Eval benchmark, achieving a pass@5 rate of 95.5%, which surpasses current state-of-the-art LLM generation frameworks. To better assess performance on complex, realistic designs, we introduce a new case study spanning applications from general-purpose processors to digital signal processing systems. Experimental results indicate that such complex designs exceed the capabilities of existing approaches, whereas our framework is the only one capable of producing functional end-to-end design. Our generated RTL follows all industry-standard design rules, is lint-clean, functionally correct and fully synthesizable.

</details>


### [24] [Beacon: LLM Multi-Agent Driven Hardware Design Space Exploration for Heterogeneous Multi-Chiplet Deep Learning Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30932)
*Boyu Li,Zongwei Zhu,Qianyue Cao,Xi Li,Xuehai Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了Beacon框架，利用LLM多智能体分析仿真报告来指导异构多芯片加速器的硬件设计空间探索，在有限迭代预算下显著降低了延迟-能耗-成本目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多芯片加速器的仿真评估开销大，限制了硬件设计空间探索的迭代次数。主流数据驱动方法依赖最终指标和少量预定义状态，需要大量搜索迭代来隐式学习参数与目标的关系，在小迭代预算下效果不佳。

Method: 提出Beacon，一个报告驱动的LLM多智能体框架。采用分层智能体进行瓶颈定位、根因诊断和硬件候选生成，结合分析工具箱和RAG记忆实现闭环搜索，利用仿真报告中的执行时间线、资源利用、内存访问和通信行为等信息。

Result: 在相同的有限迭代预算下，Beacon相比随机搜索、贝叶斯优化和强化学习，将复合延迟-能耗-成本目标降低了25.1%至93.5%。

Conclusion: 通过利用LLM结合领域知识和详细仿真报告显式识别瓶颈、退化原因和参数调整方向，Beacon能够在有限迭代预算下有效指导异构多芯片硬件设计，显著优于传统数据驱动方法。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-chiplet accelerators allow chiplets to be configured independently to better match different operator characteristics and improve inference efficiency. However, heterogeneity makes simulator evaluation expensive, limiting the number of iterations affordable for hardware design space exploration (HW-DSE). Mainstream data-driven methods rely mainly on final metrics and a few predefined states, and require many search iterations to implicitly learn the relationships between input parameters and optimization objectives, making them less effective in this setting. In practice, evaluators also generate detailed reports on execution timelines, resource utilization, memory accesses, and communication behavior. Large language models (LLMs) can combine domain knowledge with these reports to explicitly identify bottleneck locations, degradation causes, and parameter adjustment directions, thereby improving each design decision under limited iteration budgets. Based on this observation, we propose Beacon, a report-driven LLM multi-agent framework for heterogeneous multi-chiplet HW-DSE. Beacon employs hierarchical agents for bottleneck localization, root-cause diagnosis, and hardware candidate generation, together with an Analysis Toolbox and RAG memory for closed-loop search. Under the same limited iteration budget, Beacon reduces the composite latency-energy-monetary-cost objective by 25.1\%--93.5\% compared with random search, Bayesian optimization, and reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [25] [Storage-Centric System Designs for Enabling Fast, Efficient, and Low-Cost Genomic and Metagenomic Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31004)
*Nika Mansouri Ghiasi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出存储中心化系统，通过在存储系统内部直接分析（宏）基因组数据以及实现大规模序列数据的高压缩存储与高性能访问，解决了数据移动和数据准备两大瓶颈问题，显著提升了系统性能、能效和成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组学和宏基因组学分析在精准医疗、临床诊断、传染病预警等领域至关重要。然而，海量序列数据的分析与存储面临两大瓶颈：(i) 大量低复用数据从存储移动到分析单元造成的数据移动瓶颈，给系统带来不必要的负担；(ii) 压缩序列数据需先解压和格式化的数据准备瓶颈。现有加速分析和压缩存储技术未能解决这些访问层面的根本问题。

Method: 提出定制化的存储中心化系统（storage-centric systems），采用两大核心策略：(i) 在存储系统内部直接对（宏）基因组数据进行分析，避免数据移动；(ii) 实现大规模序列数据的高压缩存储与高性能访问，从而消除数据移动、计算和数据准备的开销。

Result: 实验证明，所提出的系统显著提升了（宏）基因组分析的系统性能、能源效率和系统成本效益。通过存储内计算和高效数据访问机制，有效缓解了传统架构中的数据移动和数据准备瓶颈。

Conclusion: 存储中心化系统有望促进（宏）基因组分析的广泛应用，并为未来改善健康与生命科学领域其他数据密集型应用场景的性能、能效和成本效益研究提供启示。该研究为解决基因组学数据处理的存储-计算协同优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Genomic and metagenomic analyses play critical roles in many fields, such as precision medicine, urgent clinical settings, discovering early warnings of communicable diseases, ensuring food safety through pathogen monitoring, agriculture, and scientific discovery. Due to the challenges of analyzing and storing massive volumes of genomic and metagenomic sequence data, significant efforts have been made to accelerate (meta)genomic analyses and store sequence data compressed. Despite the benefits of these techniques, we identify two major outstanding problems in accessing stored sequence data and supplying it to the analysis units: (i) the data movement bottleneck due to moving large amounts of low-reuse data from storage and the unnecessary burden on the rest of the system, and (ii) the data preparation bottleneck, where compressed sequence data needs to be first decompressed and formatted before analysis.
  In this dissertation, we present customized storage-centric systems, which efficiently (i) analyze (meta)genomic data inside the storage system, and (ii) enable highly-compressed storage and high-performance access of large-scale sequence data, thereby alleviating the overheads of data movement, computation, and data preparation. We demonstrate that the proposed systems significantly improve system performance, energy efficiency, and system cost-efficiency of (meta)genomic analysis. We hope that the storage-centric systems proposed in this dissertation facilitate the broader adoption of (meta)genomic analyses and inspire future research to fundamentally improve the performance, energy efficiency, and cost-effectiveness of other data-intensive application domains related to health and life sciences.

</details>


### [26] [Clock-Gating Insertion Strategies on an Open-Source MSP430 Core: A Reproducible PPA Study and a Gate-Level Simulation Caveat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30954)
*Xingran Huang,Qiming Guo,Jinwen Tang,Wenqi Jia,Dongzheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明手工RTL时钟门控与综合工具插入的ICG单元不等效：前者在门级仿真中出现时序竞争导致功能错误，后者功能正确且能显著降低功耗，建议低功耗设计优先使用工具插入的ICG单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时钟门控是降低动态功耗的标准技术，通常以手工RTL行为级门控或综合工具自动插入ICG单元两种方式实现。业界普遍认为这两种方法功能等效且可互换，但缺乏对二者实际差异的验证。

Method: 基于开源16位微控制器核心(openMSP430)，使用32nm标准单元库进行综合。对比三种门控策略：(1)RTL行为级门控(Opt1)、(2)综合ICG插入(Opt2)、(3)两者结合(Opt3)。在四种工作负载和三种工艺角(ss/tt/ff)下，通过门级仿真和SDF反标注验证功能正确性，并量化PPA(功耗/面积/时序)指标。

Result: RTL行为级门控在理想RTL仿真中通过全部测试，但在门级仿真中因锁存器+AND门控引入保持时间竞争导致乘法器结果丢失；ICG单元在所有配置下功能正确。ICG(Opt2)在各工艺角降低动态功耗74-81%、总功耗25-30%，同时面积和漏电流减少24-30%。在32nm工艺下，总功耗降低主要来自面积/漏电流减少而非动态功耗节省。

Conclusion: 手工RTL行为级时钟门控与工具插入ICG单元在实践中并不等效，前者存在时序竞争风险。推荐低功耗设计优先采用工具插入的ICG单元，该方法功能可靠且具有工艺角鲁棒性。完整的验证流程已作为开源工具发布。

Abstract: Clock gating, the standard technique for cutting dynamic power, is introduced either as hand-written behavioral clock gates at the register-transfer level (RTL) or as integrated clock-gating (ICG) cells inserted automatically during synthesis; the two are widely treated as interchangeable. In this paper we show, on a real open-source 16-bit microcontroller core (openMSP430) synthesized with a 32 nm standard-cell library, that they are not equivalent in practice: behavioral latch-based RTL gating is functionally correct in ideal RTL simulation (10/10 self-checking testcases, identical to the ungated baseline) yet fails at gate level: the gated multiplier result is never captured and reads zero, while tool-inserted ICG cells pass gate-level simulation cleanly (10/10). We root-cause the failure to a hold race introduced by the late latch+AND gated clock, and show it persists across eight simulation configurations including full Standard Delay Format (SDF) back-annotation, not a simulator-setting artifact. We then quantify the power/area/timing (PPA) impact of three gating strengths: RTL behavioral (Opt1), synthesis ICG (Opt2), and both (Opt3), against the ungated baseline, across four workloads and three process corners (ss/tt/ff). The benefit is corner-robust: ICG (Opt2) cuts dynamic power by 74-81% and total power by 25-30% at every corner. We also show that in this leakage-dominated 32 nm regime the total-power win comes from the area/leakage reduction that gating brings (leakage -24 to -30%), not from the large dynamic saving, which instead dominates active-mode energy. Our recommendation for low-power design on open-source cores is to prefer tool-inserted ICG cells over hand-written behavioral clock gates. The full flow (Design Compiler synthesis, PrimeTime PX power, and self-checking verification) is released as an open artifact.

</details>

### [27] [DS-Lighting: Making Agent Harnesses Explicit for Data-Science Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28590)
*Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DS-Lighting统一框架工具包，通过显式设计数据科学自动化工具的四个层级（数据、工作流、执行、评估），提升了大语言模型智能体在数据科学任务中的可复现性、可比性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据科学LLM智能体的工具设计通常是隐式的，导致结果难以在不同任务间复现、比较和归因，且端到端性能严重依赖于工具的任务表示、执行状态管理和评估反馈机制。

Method: 提出了DS-Lighting统一框架工具包，将工具分解为数据、工作流、执行和评估四个可复用层级，将不同智能体表示为支持预定义管道和自适应搜索的可执行算子程序，并整合多个开源数据科学基准到统一的MLE-Bench风格任务格式中，提供共享任务接口、沙箱运行时和度量协议。

Result: 跨智能体、工具、模型和消融实验表明，显式的工具设计提高了可复现性、可比性和可靠性，同时减少了端到端数据科学工作流中可避免的系统级故障。

Conclusion: 显式的工具设计对于提升数据科学LLM智能体的性能至关重要，DS-Lighting为数据科学自动化提供了可复现、可比较的标准化框架，有助于推动该领域的系统性研究。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise for automating data-science workflows, yet their end-to-end performance depends critically on the agent harness that represents tasks, manages execution state, constrains output artifacts, and provides evaluation feedback. Existing data-science agents often leave this harness implicit, making results difficult to reproduce, compare, and attribute across heterogeneous tasks. We introduce DS-Lighting, a unified harness toolkit that makes harness design explicit for data-science automation. DS-Lighting decomposes the harness into four reusable layers: data, workflow, execution, and evaluation, and represents diverse agents as executable operator programs that support both predefined pipelines and adaptive search. We further integrate multiple open-source data-science benchmarks into an MLE-Bench-style task format, enabling controlled comparison under a shared task interface, sandboxed runtime, and metric protocol. Experiments across agents, harnesses, models, and ablations show that explicit harness design improves reproducibility, comparability, and reliability, while reducing avoidable system-level failures in end-to-end data-science workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/dslighting

</details>


### [28] [Expert-validated STEM QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28591)
*Kihwan Han,Saurabh Patil,Chinmayee Shukla,Abhinav Sharma,Marko Pavlovic,Anshuman Lall,Mahesh Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究构建了一个由领域专家验证的高质量STEM问答数据集（N=398），填补了现有数据集在饱和度、分布偏差和格式错位等方面的不足，前沿模型在该数据集上表现较差（<25%），且在私有版本上的后训练能提升开源模型性能，表明该数据集对模型训练和评估具有重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前STEM领域的高质量评估数据集存在明显缺陷：模型性能饱和导致评估空间不足、分类分布偏斜、选择题格式与实际科研场景不匹配、以及因竞赛式收集和限时评审导致答案不准确，需要构建更高质量的专家验证数据集来推动AI在STEM领域的发展。

Method: 研究团队邀请了241位领域专家，通过四个关键步骤构建数据集：(1)设计平衡的分类体系；(2)建立质量驱动的激励机制审核问题贡献者；(3)基于共识进行多轮专家评审和修订验证；(4)采用可验证的问答格式。数据集涵盖物理、化学、生物和数学四个学科。

Result: 前沿AI模型在该数据集上的性能低于25%，表明数据集具有挑战性；在私有版本数据集（N=2,000）上进行后训练后，开源模型在HLE-verified数据集的STEM子集上相对基线模型性能提升15%（p=0.045），证明了数据集在模型训练方面的实用价值。

Conclusion: 研究成功构建了一个高质量、专家验证的STEM问答数据集，有效解决了现有数据集的缺陷，既可作为具有挑战性的基准测试集，也展现出在模型训练中的实用性。研究已向AI研究社区开源部分数据集，为STEM领域的AI发展提供了有价值的资源。

Abstract: Recent advancements in AI are helping scientists achieve breakthroughs in fields such as mathematics, medicine, and materials sciences. New evaluation datasets for AI models contribute to such advancement in AI. In the STEM domain, frontier models have consumed most of the available online data, creating the need for human-created datasets that codify the knowledge of leading experts in the domain. There are several STEM datasets available for the research community in this field. However, there are some gaps in these datasets, leaving room for improvement. Examples of gaps include (1) saturation in model performance on these datasets, leaving no head-room for meaningful evaluations, (2) skewed taxonomy distributions, (3) multiple choice question format that is misaligned with how scientists use AI in the real world, and (4) inaccurate answers and rationales partially led by a contest-based data collection and a time-bound review process. In this study, we present 'Expert-validated STEM QA', a high-quality, expert-validated STEM dataset (N=398) in Physics, Chemistry, Biology, and Mathematics, created by 241 domain experts. We (1) carefully designed a taxonomy with balanced distribution, (2) vetted question contributors with quality-driven incentive, (3) conducted multiple rounds of reviews with revisions validated by domain experts based on consensus, and (4) created the dataset in verifiable question and answer format. Our study demonstrated low performance ($<25\%$) of frontier AI models on the dataset as a benchmark. Post-training on a separate, private version of the dataset (N=2,000) increased performance of the open source model by $15\%$ relative to the baseline model (p=0.045) on the STEM subset of HLE-verified dataset, indicating potential utility of the dataset for model training. We have open-sourced a portion of our dataset for the AI research community.

</details>


### [29] [A collective capability boundary in frontier large language models on guideline-conformant and case-specific oncology decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28592)
*Zhang Sheng,Jinming Li,Wangyang Chen,Zhiwei Bao,Yu YoSean Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究构建了肿瘤决策边界基准（ODBB），评估9个前沿大语言模型在肿瘤临床决策中的表现。发现42.1%的决策点所有模型均失败，失败集中在指南路径选择的"元判断"盲点，且部分模型过度果断会导致不安全承诺。结论指出模型质量已不再是临床部署的主要瓶颈，真正约束在于假设单一模型可作为临床决策的唯一基础，需要能检测能力边界的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要测量医学知识的事实记忆能力，但真实肿瘤学实践并非知识测试，而是指南路径选择、升级判断和不确定性下的决策承诺。尚不清楚前沿大语言模型在决策路径上是否存在组合模型也无法修复的盲点。研究旨在揭示模型在临床决策边界处的真实能力缺陷。

Method: 构建肿瘤决策边界基准（ODBB），包含2005个肿瘤决策点（涵盖NCCN指南和结直肠癌病例）。评估2025年6月至2026年4月发布的9个前沿大语言模型（4个闭源、5个开源权重模型族）。使用全确定性评分器（零LLM推理）将输出分类为14种失败类型，两位肿瘤科医生在225项分层样本上独立验证（Cohen加权κ=0.939和0.790）。

Result: 将9个模型作为池化超级模型，42.1%（95%CI 40.0-44.3%）的决策点所有模型均失败，其中NCCN项目占35.7%，结直肠癌病例占66.4%。失败集中在指南路径选择而非路径内推理，形成临床元判断的一致性盲点。两个针对果断性调优的模型（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview）做出不安全承诺的频率是7个谨慎模型的3-5倍，但得分并未更高。在3-9%的项目中，模型陈述了正确的下一步临床步骤却未做出承诺——这是决策失败而非知识失败。

Conclusion: 模型质量已不再是临床大语言模型部署的主要瓶颈；关键约束在于假设任何单一模型都可作为临床决策的唯一基础。进展需要能够检测模型何时达到其能力边界并将决策路由给临床医生的架构，而非单纯增加训练数据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve high scores on medical knowledge examinations, yet real-world oncology is not a knowledge test--it is a sequence of guideline-pathway choices, escalation judgments, and commitments under uncertainty. Existing benchmarks largely measure factual recall, leaving open whether frontier LLMs share decision-path blind spots that combining models cannot fix. We built the Oncology Decision Boundary Benchmark (ODBB)--2,005 oncology decision points across NCCN guidelines and colorectal cancer cases--and evaluated nine frontier LLMs (four closed-source, five open-weight families) released between June 2025 and April 2026. A fully deterministic scorer (zero LLM inference) classified outputs into 14 failure types, independently validated by two oncologists (Cohen's weighted $κ$ = 0.939 and 0.790) on a 225-item stratified sample. Treating the nine as a pooled super-model, 42.1% (Wilson 95% CI 40.0--44.3%) of all items--35.7% of the 1,586 NCCN items and 66.4% of the 419 colorectal-cancer cases--were answered correctly by none, with failures concentrated in choosing between guideline pathways before reasoning within any: a consistent blind spot in clinical meta-judgment that likely requires architectural intervention rather than more training data. Two models tuned for decisiveness (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview) made unsafe commitments three to five times more often than the seven cautious models without scoring higher. In 3--9% of items, models stated the correct next clinical step yet did not commit to it--failures of decision, not knowledge. Model quality is no longer the primary bottleneck for clinical LLM deployment; the binding constraint is the assumption that any single model can be the sole basis for a clinical decision. Progress requires architectures that detect when a model reaches its competence boundary and route the decision to a clinician.

</details>


### [30] [Statutory AI: Aligning Large Language Models With Legal Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28593)
*Cindy Delage,Stéphane Canu,Marc Décombas,Jonathan Foureur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Statutory AI方法，利用法律文本作为宪法框架指导AI行为，在5个惩罚主题下有害内容减少52-59个百分点，优于传统Constitutional AI且计算时间减少超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法存在局限：Constitutional AI依赖人类监督，而宽泛的规范性框架如"有益人类"原则过于笼统模糊，难以提供可操作的治理指导。随着AI监管框架的发展，确保生成式模型符合法律和伦理标准成为关键优先事项。

Method: 提出混合方法Statutory AI，采用法律语料库中特定主题的既有法律文本作为宪法框架。分两阶段使用Chain-of-Thought提示：第一阶段将用户提示分类到已识别主题之一；第二阶段结合从该主题法律语料库中选出的相关条款进行分析，使AI系统能够根据既定规范自主批判和修正输出。

Result: 在1,000个红队测试提示和五个惩罚主题（歧视、机密信息披露、暴力、欺诈、虐待弱势群体）的实验中，Statutory AI在测试模型中有害内容减少52-59个百分点，比标准Constitutional AI高约10个百分点，同时计算时间减少超50%。

Conclusion: Statutory AI通过利用具体法律文本作为行为准则框架，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，在减少有害内容和提升计算效率方面表现优异，为AI系统的法律合规治理提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: With the increasing development of AI regulatory frameworks, ensuring that artificial intelligence systems, particularly generative models, operate in accordance with legal and ethical standards has become a critical priority. Existing proposals for AI alignment and value-guided behavior, however, face some limitations. Approaches such as Constitutional AI depend on human supervision, while broad normative frameworks like the Good-for-Humanity (GfH) principle may be overly general and ambiguous to provide actionable governance guidance. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid approach called Statutory AI that employs pre-existing human-authored principles drawn from specific themes within a legal corpus. Specifically, Statutory AI uses legal texts as a constitutional framework, enabling AI systems to autonomously critique and revise their outputs according to established norms. It operates in two stages, both using Chain-of-Thought prompting. The first stage classifies the user prompt into one of the identified themes, while the second stage analyzes it in conjunction with relevant articles selected from the legal corpus of that theme. To illustrate the potential of our approach, we conducted an experiment involving 1,000 red-teaming prompts and five penal themes: discrimination, disclosure of confidential information, violence, fraud, and abuse of vulnerable persons. Statutory AI reduced harmful content by 52 to 59 percentage points across tested models, approximately 10 percentage points higher than standard Constitutional AI, while cutting computation time by over 50%.

</details>


### [31] [From Question-First to Analyst-First: Domain-Expert Skills and Verified Knowledge Compilation for Proactive Enterprise Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28594)
*Harmohit Singh,Rahul Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从"问题优先"到"分析师优先"的反转式对话分析系统架构，通过可插拔的领域专家技能抽象和离线知识编译循环，在用户输入查询之前即可提供验证过的洞察报告和建议问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话分析系统假设用户已具备明确的问题，但非专家用户面对陌生企业模式时往往无从下手。商业主动分析工具仅通过检测预定义指标层的统计异常来缩小差距，学术界的下一问题推荐器则依赖新数据集所缺乏的查询日志。这种"空白查询框"问题迫切需要一种新的交互范式。

Method: 提出两个耦合的架构创新：（1）可插拔领域专家"技能"抽象——一种基于文件夹、无数据库依赖的主题包（包含清单、分阶段提示切面、关键词路由引用、报告模板及可选计算组件），通过确定性模式匹配按（客户端、数据集）自动选择，作为横切关注点编织进智能体流水线、模式探索器和报告引擎的每个阶段；（2）离线知识编译循环——智能体通过DuckDB探查数据集Parquet文件（零生产负载），执行批评器门控的逐表收敛并带自愈重试，通过值重叠验证连接，产出持久化模式知识，驱动持续的专家报告（每个发布指标均通过重新执行其证据SQL来验证）和建议问题。

Result: 形成了一个主动循环：报告呈现数字，数字催生问题，点击即可启动经过验证的深度分析，所有这些都在使用查询框之前完成。论文提供了形式化模型和单租户示例证据，每个发布的指标都通过重新执行其证据SQL进行验证。

Conclusion: 核心贡献在于架构设计及其可辩护性，而非用户研究或基准测试。该架构将交互模型从"先有问题后有分析"反转为"先有分析师（技能）主动呈现"，通过可扩展的市场化领域专家技能目录和离线知识编译，弥合了非专家用户与陌生企业模式之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Conversational analytics systems assume the user already has a well-formed question, leaving a non-expert facing a blank query box on an unfamiliar enterprise schema. Commercial 'proactive' tools narrow this gap only by detecting statistical anomalies over analyst-curated metric layers, and academic next-question recommenders depend on query logs that a fresh dataset lacks. We describe a production analytics system that inverts the interaction model from question-first to analyst-first through two coupled architectural ideas. First, a pluggable domain-expert 'skill' abstraction: a folder-based, database-free subject-matter pack (a manifest, per-stage prompt facets, keyword-routed references, report templates, and optional compute) auto-selected per (client, dataset) by deterministic schema matching and spliced as a cross-cutting concern into every stage of an agentic pipeline, the schema explorer, and the report engines, degrading to a strict no-op when absent. Because a skill is a self-contained folder resolved deterministically, the catalogue is open-ended: an extensible marketplace of domain experts. Second, an offline knowledge-compilation loop: an agent probes the dataset's parquet via DuckDB (zero load on production), runs critic-gated per-table convergence with self-healing retries, and data-validates joins by value overlap, producing durable schema knowledge that drives standing expert reports whose every published metric is re-verified by re-executing its evidence SQL, plus suggested questions that mirror the report agenda. These close a proactive loop: reports surface numbers, the numbers seed questions, and a click launches a verified deep dive, all before the query box is used. We give a formal model and report illustrative single-tenant evidence. We make no user-study or benchmark claims; the contribution is the architecture and its defensibility.

</details>
