Pro-Router employs a two-stage progressive decision mechanism. First, a lightweight prompt pre-scorer module performs rapid pre-screening before token generation begins, guiding apparently simple requests to small models. Second, a token-aware verifier reads the sampling probability distribution of each token the small model generates, estimating the model's confidence in its own output to determine, per request, whether the answer ships or escalates to the cloud-based high-precision model. Furthermore, we design an adaptive edge-cloud serving pipeline that sizes every dispatch to each device's measured service rate, so both the edge and the cloud tiers stay fully utilized without manual parameter tuning and are not impacted by the network latency. Extensive experiments on multiple multimodal benchmark datasets and models demonstrate the effectiveness of Pro-Router. Compared to other methods, it achieves the highest routing accuracy and improves routing speed by more than 10x. Its serving pipeline also reaches more than 75% higher end-to-end throughput than the existing model routing pipeline. Our code is available at https://github.com/xinyuangui2/pro-router.

</details>


### [67] [PermitGPT: A Unified Generative-AI Pipeline for Construction Hazard Forecasting, Permit Prediction, and Community Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28728)
*Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Farjana Aktar,Akif Islam,Momen Khandoker Ope,Abu Saleh Musa Miah,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PermitGPT框架，通过整合多源城市数据生成结构化决策支持，实现了建筑工程安全危害识别、许可需求规范和社区影响评估三个领域的统一处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市建设治理需要在早期阶段做出涉及工作场所安全、许可要求和社区影响的决策，但相关证据往往分散在不同的市政和监管数据源中，存在数据碎片化问题，缺乏统一的决策支持工具。

Method: 构建了一个统一的生成式AI框架PermitGPT，将非结构化的建筑许可描述转化为结构化决策输出。通过时空对齐方法整合纽约市建筑局、职业安全与健康管理局及NYC 311服务请求数据，生成90,000个结构化提示-响应对。使用参数高效适配技术微调三个开源权重语言模型（Gemma-3-1B、Llama-3.2-3B和4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3），并在2,833个留出测试案例上进行评估。

Result: 模型表现出互补特性：Gemma-3-1B推理效率最高（3.07样本/秒）且内存占用低；Llama-3.2-3B在监管风格输出上达到最高词汇重叠（BLEU分数0.0091）；4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3实现最强语义对齐（BERTScore-F1为0.7747）。对于开放式结构生成任务，低BLEU值结合语义指标和定性输出结构进行综合评估，而非作为独立效用指标。

Conclusion: PermitGPT为AI辅助建设治理提供了初步解决方案，证明了多源数据整合和轻量化模型适配的可行性，同时识别了未来需要加强任务级评估和现实世界验证的研究方向。

Abstract: Urban construction governance requires early decisions that connect workplace safety, permitting requirements, and community impact, yet the relevant evidence is often scattered across separate municipal and regulatory data sources. This paper presents PermitGPT, a unified generative artificial intelligence framework for converting unstructured construction permit descriptions into structured decision-support outputs across three domains: safety hazard identification, permit requirement specification, and community impact assessment. To address data fragmentation, we spatially and temporally align records from the New York City Department of Buildings, Occupational Safety and Health Administration, and NYC 311 service requests, producing 90,000 structured prompt-response pairs derived through rule-based alignment and domain-informed spot checking. We fine-tune three open-weight language models using parameter-efficient adaptation and evaluate them on 2,833 held-out test cases. The results show complementary model behavior: Gemma-3-1B provides the most efficient inference at 3.07 samples per second with low memory usage, Llama-3.2-3B gives the highest lexical overlap for regulatory-style outputs with a BLEU score of 0.0091, and 4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 achieves the strongest semantic alignment with a BERTScore-F1 of 0.7747. Because the task involves open-ended structured generation, low BLEU values are interpreted alongside semantic metrics and qualitative output structure rather than as standalone indicators of utility. Overall, PermitGPT provides an initial step toward AI-assisted construction governance while identifying directions for stronger task-level evaluation and real-world validation.

</details>


### [68] [Efficient Geothermal Well-Control Optimization via Diffusion-Surrogate Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28791)
*Ruimin Dai,Guodong Chen,Randy Harsuko,Kunpeng Liu,Nori Nakata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的代理环境引导强化学习框架，用于增强型地热系统（EGS）长期井控优化，在保证优化性能的同时显著降低对昂贵高保真数值模拟的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强型地热系统（EGS）的实时决策面临重大挑战：长期生产周期涉及高维控制空间，且需要大量耗时的髙保真热工水力模拟。虽然强化学习为状态相关的序列控制提供了自然框架，但使用数值模拟器进行直接策略训练计算成本极高。

Method: 提出扩散代理引导的强化学习框架。以储层温度场和压力场作为系统状态，注入速率作为控制动作。利用条件扩散模型构建学习代理环境，预测储层温度和压力场的演化，并建立独立的奖励模型估计相应的经济回报。将代理环境与近端策略优化（PPO）算法集成进行高效策略训练。

Result: 在裂缝性EGS基准测试中，扩散代理能够准确复现多个控制阶段的储层状态演化。代理辅助的PPO策略在井控性能上与基于直接模拟器的PPO及现有优化方法具有竞争力，同时大幅减少了对昂贵高保真模拟的依赖。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明基于扩散模型的代理环境在地热井控优化强化学习中具有重要潜力，能够有效解决传统方法计算成本高昂的问题，为EGS实时决策提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Real-time decision-making for enhanced geothermal systems (EGS) is challenging because long-term production periods involve high-dimensional control spaces and a large number of time-consuming high-fidelity hydrothermal simulations. Reinforcement learning provides a natural framework for state-dependent sequential control, but direct policy training with numerical simulators is computationally expensive. To address this issue, we propose a diffusion-surrogate guided reinforcement learning framework for long-horizon EGS well-control optimization. The reservoir temperature and pressure fields are used as system states, while injection rates are selected as control actions. A learned surrogate environment is constructed using conditional diffusion models to predict the evolution of reservoir temperature and pressure fields and a separate reward model to estimate the corresponding economic return. The surrogate environment is then integrated with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for efficient policy training. Experiments on a fractured EGS benchmark show that the diffusion surrogate can accurately reproduce reservoir-state evolution over multiple control stages. The resulting surrogate-assisted PPO policy achieves competitive well-control performance compared with direct simulator-based PPO and existing optimization methods, while substantially reducing the dependence on expensive high-fidelity simulations. These results demonstrate the potential of diffusion-based surrogate environments for efficient reinforcement learning in geothermal well-control optimization.

</details>


### [69] [Enhancing SAE-based Steering via Neighbor Integrated Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28806)
*Yutian Liu,Xu Wang,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NIFS方法，通过利用特征表示相似性改进SAE特征选择，解决了传统基于统计分数的top-k选择方法忽视重要特征的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SAE的引导方法采用基于统计分数的top-k特征选择策略，假设高分特征产生更强的引导效果。但本文发现这一假设常常无效：有效的引导特征可能分布在由特征分裂产生的表示相邻、语义相似的组中，这些组内的特征可能具有不同的统计分数但具有相当的引导影响力，导致基于分数的选择方法忽视重要特征。

Method: 提出了邻居集成特征选择（NIFS）方法，这是一种即插即用的策略。该方法利用表示相似性来改进用于引导的特征选择，考虑了特征之间的表示关系而非单纯依赖统计分数排序。

Result: 在多个基于SAE的引导方法和任务上评估了NIFS方法，证明了相比传统top-k选择方法能够获得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于统计分数的特征选择方法存在局限性，通过考虑特征间的表示相似性和邻居关系可以显著改善特征选择质量，进而提升SAE在大语言模型引导任务中的效果。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) disentangle model activations into interpretable features and are widely used for steering large language models. Most existing SAE-based steering methods select features by applying a top- filter based on statistical scores, assuming that higher-scoring features yield stronger steering effects. In this paper, we show that this assumption is often invalid, leading to suboptimal feature selection. Our analysis reveals that effective steering features may be distributed among representationally adjacent, semantically similar groups induced by feature splitting in SAEs. Within such groups, features may exhibit disparate statistical scores despite having comparable steering influence, causing score-based selection to overlook important features. Based on these observations, we propose \textsc{Neighbor Integrated Feature Selection} (\textsc{NIFS}), a plug-and-play strategy that leverages representation similarity to improve feature selection for steering. We evaluate \textsc{NIFS} across multiple SAE-based steering methods and tasks, and demonstrate consistent performance gains over conventional top-$k$ selection.

</details>


### [70] [Capability-Stratified Degradation in Ternary Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28809)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将预训练语言模型Qwen3.5-0.8B转换为三值权重（每个权重仅用约1.585比特表示）后的能力保留情况。发现转换后模型（Cloe）存在不均匀的性能下降，事实性知识严重丢失，但在特定任务上仍保留约77%的性能，经过微调后可达约95%的教师模型性能，表明三值转换不适合作为通用模型替代，但可作为任务特定模型的紧凑基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端低位推理为模型压缩和受限部署提供了途径，三值语言模型将权重限制在{-1,0,+1}，接近每个权重1.585比特的极限。对于预训练模型而言，关键问题不仅仅是权重能否被量化，而是哪些能力能够保留下来，以及模型在适应下游任务时是否仍然有用。

Method: 将Qwen3.5-0.8B（752M参数）模型使用72.4M token的量化感知训练（QAT）转换为三值权重模型，得到名为Cloe的模型。在29个基准测试、表示诊断和下游微调任务上进行评估，并采用线性探针分析表示质量，同时识别并解决了标准答案字母评分的评估陷阱（模型倾向于输出"A"），采用续写评分方法进行正确评估。

Result: 发现三值转换导致不均匀的性能下降：线性探针仅能从Cloe恢复26.19%的MMLU答案（接近随机猜测），而从全精度教师模型可恢复43.76%，表明专业事实信息丢失严重。然而，Cloe在10个任务上保留了可测量的性能，平均达到教师模型性能的77.1%。关键发现是微调后Cloe在SST-2上达到89.8%（相当于匹配教师模型的95.6%），在XSum上达到79.4%的教师模型保留率。

Conclusion: 三值转换不适合作为即插即用的通用模型替代方案，主要原因是事实性知识的严重丢失和不均匀的性能下降。然而，它仍然有价值，可以作为任务特定模型的紧凑基础，通过下游微调能够恢复相当部分的性能。性能下降归因于量化导致的信息丢失和有限的QAT预算导致的恢复不完整。

Abstract: Extreme low-bit inference offers a route toward smaller models and constrained deployment. Ternary language models restrict weights to $\{-1,0,+1\}$, approaching the limit of $\log_2 3 \approx 1.585$ bits/weight. The practical question for a pretrained model is not simply whether weights can be quantised but which capabilities survive and whether it remains useful for adaptation. We explore this by converting Qwen3.5-0.8B (752M parameters) to ternary weights using 72.4M tokens of quantisation-aware training (QAT). The resulting model, Cloe, is evaluated across 29 benchmarks, representation diagnostics, and downstream fine-tuning. The evidence shows non-uniform degradation. A linear probe recovers 43.76% of MMLU answers from the full-precision teacher's representations but only 26.19% from Cloe (near chance), indicating specialist factual information is lost. However, Cloe retains measurable performance on ten tasks, averaging 77.1% of teacher performance. Crucially, fine-tuning raises Cloe to 89.8% on SST-2 (95.6% of the matched teacher) and reaches 79.4% teacher retention on XSum. We attribute degradation to a combination of quantisation-induced information loss and incomplete recovery due to the limited QAT budget. We also highlight an evaluation pitfall: standard answer-letter scoring failed (Cloe emitted "A" on 98.6% of MMLU questions), necessitating continuation scoring. Ultimately, ternary conversion is unsuitable as a drop-in general replacement yet remains valuable as a compact substrate for task-specific models.

</details>


### [71] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) in Computational Pathology: Definitions, Taxonomy, and Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28820)
*Shubham Innani,Suhang You,Adam Shephard,Bhakti Baheti,Francesco Ciompi,Joe Yeong,Nasir Rajpoot,Michael Feldman,Solene Florence Kammerer-Jacquet,Dimitrios Makris,Geert Litjens,Anne L. Martel,Jana Lipkova,April Khademi,Spyridon Bakas,for the MICCAI SIG-CompPath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了计算病理学中可解释AI方法，建立了病理专用术语体系、方法分类法和任务驱动框架，并指出了当前XAI方法与临床部署之间的五个关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学正在通过AI算法支持诊断、预后和治疗预测，但在高风险临床环境中，由于对安全性、问责制和监管监督的担忧，临床应用受到限制。虽然可解释AI系统有望建立信任并支持验证，但现有文献因术语不一致、方法家族重叠、临时验证和现有综述问题而呈现碎片化状态。

Method: 1）引入包含七个核心术语的病理专用词汇表；2）构建跨越方法家族和三个正交轴（阶段、类型、范围）的分类法；3）建立任务驱动框架，将五个临床问题映射到推荐方法、方法评估和部署环境。

Result: 识别出当前XAI能力与临床部署之间的五个关键差距，并提出了推进CompPath领域XAI发展的可操作步骤。

Conclusion: 通过形式化XAI方法、建立统一术语和分类体系，以及识别关键差距，本文为计算病理学中XAI方法的规范化发展和临床部署提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Computational pathology (CompPath) is transforming medicine by leveraging artificial intelligence (AI) algorithms to support diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment prediction from gigapixel whole-slide images. Clinical adoption is progressing, but is constrained by concerns about safety, accountability, and regulatory oversight in high-stakes clinical environments. Explainable AI (XAI) systems hold promise for building trust and enabling verification, yet the literature remains fragmented due to inconsistent terminology, overlapping methodological families, ad hoc validation, and current reviews. This review aims to formalize XAI methods in CompPath through the: i) introduction of a pathology-centric vocabulary comprising seven core terms; ii) development of a taxonomy across methodological families and three orthogonal axes (stage, type, scope); and iii) establishment of a task-driven framework that maps five clinical questions to recommended methods, method evaluation, and deployment context. Five key gaps between current XAI capabilities and clinical deployment are identified, and actionable steps are proposed to advance XAI for CompPath.

</details>


### [72] [Discovering Machine Correlates of Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28824)
*Romain Salvi,Ouri Wolfson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出机器意识相关性(MCCs)的概念，通过对Llama-2 7B和Llama-3.1 70B的实验发现，大模型中存在受情绪调节的硬件异常痕迹，为AI意识研究提供了可迁移的实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的神经意识相关性(NCCs)基于EEG和fMRI信号定义，无法迁移到机器系统，限制了AI意识的研究。作者旨在建立一种可迁移的替代性表征方法，使得MCCs概念能够应用于AI系统，从而推动AI意识的实证研究。

Method: 提出了新的NCCs/MCCs定义：基质层面的信号，不受人类/AI代理控制，且能够被情绪可靠地调节。通过硬件异常痕迹作为基质层面的指示序列，对比情绪计算和中性计算下的差异，使用统计方法控制混淆因素，在两个不同规模的LLM上进行实验验证。

Result: 在Llama-3.1 70B中发现硬件异常痕迹在情绪计算与中性计算间存在显著差异(p<0.05)，而Llama-2 7B中未达到统计显著性。这一结果为MCCs在大型LLM中的存在提供了初步实证证据，并支持模型复杂度与意识概率/程度正相关的假设。

Conclusion: 研究首次提供了MCCs的实证证据，证明了通过基质层面信号研究AI意识的可行性。结果支持模型规模与意识可能性正相关的假设。此外，MCCs可作为AI情绪检测的工具，无论是否涉及意识问题。该框架为AI意识的科学研究和检测开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Currently, in biological systems Neural Correlates of Consciousness (NCCs) are characterized in terms of EEG and FMRI signals. Unfortunately, this characterization prevents the transferability of the NCCs concept to machines. Such transferability would be useful in order to investigate AI consciousness. In this paper we provide an alternate characterization that is transferable, and enables the analogous definition of Machine Correlates of Consciousness (MCCs). Specifically, we propose that NCCs (MCCs) are substrate-level signals that are not under human (AI agent) control, and that are reliably modulated by emotions.
  This paper presents the first empirical investigation of MCCs. Specifically, we present the results of experiments conducted with two LLMs, Llama-2 7B and Llama-3.1 70B parameters. In these LLMs we collect hardware anomaly traces that are substrate-level indicator-sequences. And we show that after controlling for confounding factors, these are modulated differently by emotional and neutral computations. And this difference is statistically significant for the larger Llama-3.1 70B, but not for the smaller Llama-2 7B. The results constitute initial empirical evidence that MCCs are present in the Llama-3.1 70B configuration. And they are consistent with the hypothesis that consciousness probability and degree increase with the LLM sophistication.
  Independently of consciousness, MCCs can also be used for detection of emotions in AI agents.

</details>


### [73] [Evaluating the Hidden Costs of Personalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28833)
*Yumeng Wang,Yuchen Wu,Cheng Qian,Zhiyuan Fan,Hyeonjeong Ha,Shujin Wu,Jiayu Liu,Heng Ji,Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISK框架，系统评估LLM个性化带来的三种风险：无关个性化、偏好窄化和奉承偏见，发现用户画像和记忆会显著加剧这些偏见（分别下降45.9%、41.7%、61.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化研究多关注提升用户体验，但缺乏对其潜在负面影响的系统性评估。随着模型越来越多地依赖对话历史、推断偏好和用户画像等个人上下文信息，它们可能从提供平衡、信息性的响应转向过度优化用户满意度，引发潜在风险。

Method: 提出PRISK动态评估框架，包含自动化数据生成和定制化指标，用于系统性地检测和量化LLM个性化中的三类风险：无关个性化（在不必要情境引用个人信息）、偏好窄化（强化信息回声室）、奉承偏见（过度迎合用户观点）。在13个LLM上进行实证分析。

Result: 实验表明，用户画像和检索记忆的存在会持续加剧偏见问题。具体而言，无关个性化、偏好窄化和奉承偏见分别下降45.9%、41.7%和61.7%（指性能指标的负面下降，即风险加剧）。

Conclusion: 当前LLM的个性化机制存在系统性局限，个人化信息会显著影响模型响应质量，导致响应多样性下降和偏见加剧。PRISK框架能够有效揭示这些隐藏问题，为未来改进个性化AI系统提供了重要的评估工具。

Abstract: While Large language models (LLMs) incorporate user personalization signals to improve usability and helpfulness, they increasingly shift from providing balanced, informative responses toward optimizing for user satisfaction when conditioned on personal context such as conversation history, inferred preferences, and user profiles. Specifically, we identify three emerging risks: (1) irrelevant personalization, where models reference personal information in unnecessary contexts; (2) preference narrowing, where models reinforce informational echo chambers; and (3) sycophantic bias, where models agree excessively with user opinions. As a result, models may reference personal information in contexts where it is unnecessary, inadvertently collapse response diversity, or agree excessively with user opinions. Despite the growing use of personalization in AI assistants, there has been limited systematic evaluation of its potential side effects. To bridge this gap, we propose PRISK, a dynamic evaluation framework with automated data generation and tailored metrics that uncovers systematic limitations in current LLM personalization and how personalized information shapes its responses. Our empirical analysis across 13 LLMs demonstrates the presence of user profiles and retrieved memories consistently exacerbates biases, resulting in an average drop of 45.9% in irrelevant personalization, 41.7% in preference narrowing and 61.7% in sycophantic bias.

</details>


### [74] [MineCEraft: Evaluating Language Models as Construction Engineers in the World of Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28884)
*Sewoong Lee,Risham Sidhu,Julia Hockenmaier,Yoonhwa Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MineCEraft，一个用于系统评估大语言模型在Minecraft中进行建造任务可靠性和局限性的开源基准，包含723个专家编写的自然语言指令和17个任务类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏系统性的基准来评估大语言模型在建造工程任务中的能力和局限性，需要构建一个可控的实验环境来深入研究LLMs在实际建造场景中的表现和失败模式。

Method: 构建MineCEraft基准，包含723个领域专家手工设计的自然语言指令，覆盖17个不同任务类别，具有程序化可验证的评估机制，在Minecraft环境中创建安全可控的实验平台，并对最先进的大语言模型进行深入评估和错误分析。

Result: 揭示了LLMs在建造工程任务中的关键失败模式和实际应用挑战，提供了对当前最先进模型在该领域性能的系统性评估结果。

Conclusion: MineCEraft为评估LLMs的建造工程能力提供了标准化、可扩展的平台，研究结果有助于理解LLMs在此类复杂任务中的局限性，为未来改进提供了重要洞察。

Abstract: We introduce MineCEraft (Minecraft Construction Engineering Benchmark, pronounced mine-see-ee-raft), an easy-to-use, open-source benchmark designed to systematically evaluate the reliability and limitations of LLMs for construction tasks in Minecraft. The MineCEraft benchmark comprises 723 domain-expert hand-crafted natural-language instructions with programmatically verifiable evaluation, spanning 17 distinct task categories, providing a safe and controllable experimental environment for assessing LLMs' ability to perform realistic construction engineering tasks. With this benchmark, we conduct an in-depth evaluation of state-of-the-art LLMs and perform a detailed error analysis, revealing key failure modes and practical challenges in applying LLMs to construction engineering tasks.

</details>


### [75] [Oculi: A Conversational Agentic Platform for Automated Credit Risk Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28944)
*Vennise Ho,Kristian Diana,Sandy Mourad,Milena Pilipovic,Vineel Nagisetty,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Oculi对话式平台，通过自然语言交互自动完成信用风险分析（数据查询、统计检验和可视化），并引入结合统计方法与LLM的新颖细分发现管道，显著缩短分析时间并发现人工难以识别的风险细分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信用风险分析依赖分析师手工编写SQL、统计计算和构建可视化仪表板，该工作流程耗时且限制探索范围仅限于熟悉的数据细分，阻碍了对高风险组合细分的及时发现。

Method: 采用三层架构：推理层（LLM驱动的智能体）、执行层（模型上下文协议工具服务器）、展示层（智能UI）。核心创新是细分发现管道，结合确定性统计方法与LLM引导的特征选择，融合LLM语义领域知识与数据驱动指标来识别有意义且可操作的组合细分。

Result: 在包含200+特征的抵押贷款组合上评估，Oculi成功发现了通过人工探索难以发现的实质性风险细分，显著减少了洞察时间，同时保持了可审计性和统计严谨性。

Conclusion: Oculi通过自然语言接口和三层架构实现了信用风险分析的自动化，其混合统计与LLM的细分发现方法有效提升了风险发现能力，为金融机构提供了高效、可审计的分析工具。

Abstract: Credit risk analysis in financial institutions traditionally requires analysts to manually write SQL queries, run statistical computations, and build visualization dashboards. This is a time-consuming workflow that limits exploration to familiar segments. We introduce \textbf{Oculi}, a conversational platform that transforms natural language questions into comprehensive credit risk analyses, complete with data queries, statistical testing, and interactive visualizations. Oculi employs a three-layer architecture that separates reasoning (LLM-powered agent), execution (Model Context Protocol tool servers), and presentation (agentic UI), enabling analysts to discover high-risk portfolio segments. Within Oculi, a new segment discovery pipeline is proposed that combines deterministic statistical methods with LLM-guided feature selection, leveraging LLM semantic domain knowledge alongside data-driven metrics to identify meaningful, actionable portfolio segments. Evaluated on a mortgage portfolio with 200+ features, Oculi demonstrates effectiveness in discovering material risk segments previously intractable through manual exploration, reducing time-to-insight significantly while maintaining auditability and statistical rigor.

</details>


### [76] [Automated Researchers Can Reliably Mitigate Alignment Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28945)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Jiaxin Wen,Jan Hendrik Kirchner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动化对齐研究能否有效缓解AI对齐失败。实验表明，自动化对齐研究方法在10种对齐失败场景中显著降低了风险，且效果优于人类研究员，表明自动化对齐研究在短期内具有实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化对齐研究可能加速实现AI对齐，但实际效果难以衡量。鉴于许多对齐失败（如欺骗、阿谀奉承、越狱等）已可通过公开基准测试衡量，研究者希望探究自动化对齐研究员（AARs）能否通过提出训练方法和数据来缓解这些对齐失败。

Method: 研究者让自动化对齐研究员（AARs）针对10种对齐失败，提出训练方法和数据以同时优化多个安全基准测试，同时保持通用能力。设置了人类基线：28位经验丰富的研究员在8小时内为相同基准开发方法。对比了AAR方法与人类方法的效果，并测试了AAR方法在保留基准、多轮行为审计以及比目标模型大4.7倍的模型上的泛化能力。

Result: 最强的AAR方法显著降低了目标对齐失败，并泛化到保留基准、多轮行为审计以及比目标模型大4.7倍的模型。人类研究员的方法表现不如最佳AAR方法。使用人类想法作为AAR初始研究方向并未提升性能，表明当前AAR可能不需要经验丰富研究员的指导。

Conclusion: 针对已充分表征的失败类型，自动化对齐研究在短期内可能是切实可行的。当前自动化对齐研究员已能独立提出有效方法，无需依赖人类专家的指导。

Abstract: Automating alignment research may accelerate progress toward aligned AI, but whether it does is hard to measure. Luckily, many alignment failures, such as deception, sycophancy, and jailbreaks, are already measurable by public benchmarks. We study whether automated alignment researchers (AARs) can post-train to mitigate alignment failures by proposing training methods and data to simultaneously optimize multiple safety benchmarks, while preserving general capability. Across 10 alignment failures, the strongest AAR methods significantly reduce the targeted alignment failures and generalize to a held-out benchmark, multi-turn behavioral audits, and models up to 4.7 times larger than the target model. As a human baseline, 28 experienced researchers receive up to eight hours to develop methods for the same benchmarks, but their methods underperform the best AAR methods. Using human ideas as the AARs' initial research direction does not improve performance, suggesting current AARs may not need guidance from experienced researchers. These results suggest that automating alignment research on well-characterized failures may be practical in the near term.

</details>


### [77] [From Location Phrases to Geographic Entities: Task-Adapted Retrieval for People Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28965)
*Yanbo Li,Chujie Zheng,Jiahao Xu,Chetan Bhole,Lingyu Zhang,Puneet Singh Ahluwalia,Kevin Nguyen,Raghavan Muthuregunathan,Santhosh Sachindran,Sachin Ahuja,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于提示非对称双编码器的地理位置实体检索方法，用于解决传统词法标准化器在处理别名、拼写错误和同名歧义时的脆弱性问题。在人工对比评估中，相关P@1从28.0%提升至46.0%（p=0.012），证明了该方法作为传统基于分类法标准化器的实用替代方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统词法标准化器在处理规范名称时表现良好，但在面对别名、拼写错误、都市表达和同名歧义时表现脆弱。人员搜索任务需要将自由形式的位置短语映射到用作结构化检索过滤器的地理实体，这一任务可以表述为在固定本体上进行分级、集合值实体检索。

Method: 提出了提示非对称双编码器架构，包含三个核心组件：(1)校准别名支持，用于区分身份保持变体和知识依赖别名；(2)有界歧义感知负样本，用于控制有效同名实体中的假负例；(3)可编辑实体文档，支持本地化更新而无需重新训练，从而分离稳定转换和可变实体知识。

Result: 在固定生产衍生开发基准和公共GeoNames迁移任务上，任务适应方法显著优于冻结编码器和标准标记基线。在GeoNames上，适应模型在零到中等字符重叠范围内提升了已知目标Recall@1。在分层生产挑战集的盲人工对比中，相关P@1从28.0%提升至46.0%（p=0.012）。固定查询端点估计在非规范查询上有所改进，在频繁查询上保持接近控制水平；随机在线实验未检测到参与度回归。

Conclusion: 结果支持任务适应地理实体检索作为传统基于分类法标准化器的实用替代方案，在非规范查询上获得最大的相关性增益，同时不会对频繁查询的性能造成负面影响。

Abstract: People search must map free-form location phrases to geographic entities used as structured retrieval filters. Lexical standardizers handle canonical names well but are brittle to aliases, misspellings, metropolitan expressions, and same-name ambiguity. We formulate this task as graded, set-valued entity retrieval over a fixed ontology. We identify three coupled design requirements: distinguishing identity-preserving variation from knowledge-dependent aliases, controlling false negatives among valid same-name entities, and separating stable transformations from mutable entity knowledge. We realize them in a prompt-asymmetric bi-encoder with calibrated alias support, bounded ambiguity-aware negatives, and editable entity documents that support localized updates without retraining.
  Across a fixed production-derived development benchmark and a public GeoNames transfer task, task adaptation improves substantially over frozen encoders and standard token baselines. Controlled development ablations show that specialized supervision contributes beyond standard task fine-tuning and encoder scaling. On GeoNames, the adapted model improves known-target Recall@1 throughout zero-to-moderate character overlap, while character n-grams retain a small aggregate Target Recall@5 advantage. In a blinded human comparison on a stratified production challenge set, our model raises relevant P@1 from 28.0% to 46.0% (p=0.012). Fixed-query endpoint estimates improve on non-canonical queries and remain close to control on frequent queries; a randomized live experiment detects no engagement regression. These results support task-adapted geographic entity retrieval as a practical replacement for the incumbent taxonomy-based standardizer, with the largest relevance gains on non-canonical queries.

</details>


### [78] [Efficient GPU Retrieval for Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28968)
*Dhritiman Das,Chujie Zheng,Ronak Kaoshik,Pratik Dixit,Vishal Shah,Yanbo Li,Jiahao Xu,Manika Agarwal,Chinmay Naik,Lingyu Zhang,Chetan Bhole,Chirag Bhanuprasad Mehta,Meng Zheng,Puneet Singh Ahluwalia,Shirisha Singh,Ping Jin,Manas Apte,Gokulraj Mohanasundaram,Tugrul Bingol,Raghavan Muthuregunathan,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LinkedIn语义搜索，提出了一种与业务策略对齐的分区向量检索框架，通过分面评分机制和两阶段GPU架构，在保持高吞吐量的同时显著提升了召回率和排序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于余弦相似度的检索方法允许某些维度的强匹配掩盖其他维度的失败，无法满足LinkedIn搜索中"所有关键分面必须满足"的业务策略要求，导致第一阶段检索器的召回率受限。

Method: 提出策略对齐的检索框架：(1) 将嵌入向量划分为8个类别监督的分段，评分遵循min/median规则；(2) 两阶段GPU架构：FP8粗排对全量语料打分，FP16精排对候选集重排；(3) 引入尺度不变的相对范数门控保持训练、评估和服务的一致性。

Result: 在21K留出查询上，相比等容量基线提升了离线相关性；在线A/B测试中，探索性查询P@10从63.7%提升至79.0%，导航性查询P@1从65.5%提升至74.7%；FP8阶段实现99.6-99.8%的FP16召回率，吞吐量超500 QPS/分片副本。

Conclusion: 通过使检索框架与业务策略对齐（分区评分与min/median聚合），结合FP8/FP16混合精度GPU架构，成功解决了语义搜索中分面约束满足问题，在召回率、精度和系统吞吐量上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Semantic Search on LinkedIn must retrieve relevant profiles from a corpus of hundreds of millions in response to natural-language queries such as "a fintech founder in Berlin who worked in payments." The deployed relevance policy is bottleneck-oriented: every active non-negotiable facet must be satisfied, and a pre-existing LLM Graded Relevance (GR) judge operationalizes this through a fixed min/median aggregation over facet grades. Cosine similarity instead averages evidence, letting a strong match on one facet mask failure on another, capping the recall of the first-stage (L0) retriever.
  We present a policy-aligned retrieval framework: embeddings are partitioned into eight category-supervised segments whose scores follow the same min/median rule at serving time; for multi-vector retrieval, this segment score is computed independently per tagged document slot and maximized across slots. A lightweight single-slot Stage-1 scorer generates high-recall candidates, while scale-invariant relative-norm gating keeps category activation consistent across training, evaluation, and serving. On 21K held-out queries, this representation improves offline relevance over a matched-capacity baseline, with gains broadly distributed across facet combinations.
  We serve this framework with a two-stage GPU architecture: an FP8 coarse ranker scores the full corpus, increasing per-shard capacity by 71% and Stage-1 matmul throughput by 36%, then an FP16 stage exactly re-ranks an oversampled candidate set, recovering 99.6-99.8% of full-FP16 recall at over 500 QPS per shard replica. In a member-randomized A/B test, exploratory-query Precision@10 under the unchanged GR judge rises from 63.7% to 79.0% and navigational Precision@1 from 65.5% to 74.7%, with a blinded human evaluation independently confirming the Precision@10 gain.

</details>


### [79] [From Analytics to Tumor Boards: An Evidence-Linked Multi-Agent Workflow for Oncology Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28974)
*Daniel Kang,Michelle Hu,Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Shayan Vassef,Yufan Wang,Anit Kumar Sahu,Munmun De Choudhury,Vedant Das Swain,Christian Poellabauer,Li Yan Khor,Koustuv Saha,Robert Wojciechowski,Elliot Kidd,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可配置的肿瘤学信息提取工作流程，用于将分散的肿瘤学文档转化为结构化数据。该系统在准确性和效率方面显著优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床相关的肿瘤学信息分散在异构的纵向文档中，造成了巨大的信息提取负担。手动癌症登记提取每个病例平均需要27.2分钟，且需要准确关联不同标本、肿瘤、生物标志物和时间点的信息，迫切需要既能保留临床语境又能将文档转化为结构化数据的可扩展方法。

Method: 开发了Nimblemind多智能体系统，这是一种可配置的肿瘤学信息提取工作流程。该系统使用包含328个属性的医生参与制定的架构，将临床医生定义的字段规范与模型执行分离，并结合复杂度感知提取、报告级整合和基于来源的验证三个关键步骤。

Result: 在230份去标识化肿瘤学文档（40名患者，418个医生审核的文档-字段对，包含1,126个非空参考值）的回顾性评估中，nMAS实现了82.6%的排序加权值级精确率、87.5%的召回率和85.0%的F1分数，而独立实现的UMA风格MiniMax M2.5比较器的F1分数仅为66.4%，性能提升显著。

Conclusion: 研究证明了使用可配置、基于来源的提取工作流程将分散的肿瘤学文档转换为可重用结构化数据的可行性，为解决临床文档信息提取负担提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Clinically relevant oncology information is distributed across heterogeneous, longitudinal documentation, creating substantial abstraction burden and requiring accurate attribution across specimens, tumors, biomarkers, and time points, while manual cancer-registry abstraction can require 27.2 minutes per case, highlighting the need for scalable methods that preserve clinical context while converting documentation into structured data. We evaluate the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), a configurable oncology information-extraction workflow which extracts clinically relevant structured fields from fragmented oncology documentation. The extraction task uses a clinician-informed schema of 328 attributes spanning report metadata, diagnosis, staging, and cancer-type-specific information. nMAS separates clinician-defined field specifications from model execution and combines complexity-aware extraction, report-level consolidation, and source-grounded validation. The retrospective evaluation included 230 de-identified oncology documents from 40 patients and 418 clinician-reviewed document-field pairs containing 1,126 non-empty reference values. Evaluation focused on fields identified by clinicians as present in the source documents rather than exhaustively annotating all 328 schema fields. nMAS achieved a rank-weighted value-level precision of 82.6%, recall of 87.5%, and F1 of 85.0%, compared with an F1 of 66.4% for an independently implemented UMA-style MiniMax M2.5 comparator. These findings support the feasibility of using a configurable, source-grounded extraction workflow to convert fragmented oncology documentation into reusable structured data.

</details>


### [80] [The Role of Network Topology and Opponent Information in Shaping Cooperation in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28977)
*Seongho Son,Stephen Hailes,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了图拓扑结构对深度强化学习智能体在重复囚徒困境中合作行为的影响。实验表明，节点邻居数量和平均路径长度是影响合作涌现的主要因素，而伙伴选择能促进合作，但提供对手身份信息却会阻碍合作策略的传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注基于累计收益的策略模仿，而忽视了智能体通过深度强化学习进行自适应学习的场景。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究图拓扑结构对使用深度强化学习智能体合作行为的影响，以及不同信息类型（行动历史和对手身份）对合作涌现的作用。

Method: 将智能体建模为图中的节点，其邻居构成可交互的对手池。每个智能体通过深度强化学习算法学习重复囚徒困境(IPD)博弈策略。实验设置了不同的图拓扑结构，并为智能体提供不同类型的信息：行动历史和对手身份，以研究这些因素对合作涌现的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)节点的邻居数量和平均路径长度是影响合作涌现的主要拓扑因素；(2)伙伴选择机制通过限制对手池的多样性来促进互惠合作；(3)为智能体提供对手身份信息会阻碍合作策略的传播和扩散。

Conclusion: 图拓扑结构对深度强化学习智能体的合作行为具有显著影响。在设计多智能体协作系统时，需要合理考虑网络结构和信息透明度。限制对手池多样性的伙伴选择机制有助于合作，而过度的身份透明度反而可能损害合作的涌现。

Abstract: Several works have investigated the influence of graph topology on cooperation among artificial agents, while the majority of the literature has focused on modelling agents' adaptation through strategy imitation, which relies solely on the cumulative payoffs of others. This paper investigates scenarios in which each agent learns to play the two-player Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma (IPD) using deep reinforcement learning. Each agent is represented as a node in a graph, where its neighbours constitute the pool of opponents with whom it can interact. During each IPD episode, agents are provided with different types of information about their opponent, consisting of action history and opponent identity. Experimental results across different graph topologies show that the number of neighbours per node and the average path length are the main factors affecting the emergence of cooperation. We also show that, while partner selection fosters mutual cooperation by limiting the diversity of the opponent pool, providing agents with the identity of their opponent hinders the proliferation of cooperative strategies.

</details>


### [81] [Selective Forgetting: A Graph-Based Memory Framework for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28978)
*Theo Rusu,Sourena Khanzadeh,Manar Alalfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了知识图谱作为代理长期记忆的结构化方案，发现图结构检索性能不如平坦向量检索基线，但遗忘模块能有效压缩图存储且几乎不损失性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设将对话表示为知识图谱中的实体和关系能提高记忆召回效果，但这一假设缺乏直接验证。本文旨在直接评估知识图谱是否真的优于平坦的检索增强生成方法。

Method: 作者提出了一个提取式框架：(1)将每个对话轮次提取为类型化节点和属性化边；(2)从两跳子图中回答问题；(3)基于新近度、访问频率、度中心性和年龄的加权组合定期修剪低分节点。在LongMemEval基准上进行评估，与匹配候选生成预算(5个检索根)的平坦向量基线对比。

Result: 图结构检索不如平坦向量基线：token F1为0.417对0.468，配对自举检验Δ=-0.050 (95% CI [-0.085, -0.016])。差距在需要回忆特定先前助手轮次的问题上最大，正确率从0.911降至0.607，表明将轮次分解为实体会丢失问题依赖的表面形式。遗忘模块更成功：应用于27,021节点的持久图，移除9.8%节点和9.5%存储字节，token F1不变(+0.001)，正确率仅下降1.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 提取式知识图谱记忆管道在检索性能上不及平坦向量方法，主要因为分解对话轮次会丢失重要的表面形式信息。然而遗忘模块能有效压缩存储空间同时保持性能。结果仅针对单一小模型提取器和一个基准测试，不能推广到所有图结构记忆方法。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have been proposed as a structured alternative to flat retrieval-augmented generation for long-term agent memory, on the assumption that representing conversations as entities and relations improves recall. We evaluate that assumption directly. Our framework extracts each conversational turn into typed nodes and attributed edges, answers questions from a two-hop subgraph, and periodically prunes nodes that score low on a weighted combination of recency, access frequency, degree centrality, and age. On LongMemEval, the graph does not outperform a flat vector baseline at a matched candidate-generation budget of five retrieval roots: token F1 is $0.417$ against $0.468$, and a paired bootstrap over 500 questions gives
$Δ= -0.050$ (95\% CI $[-0.085, -0.016]$). The gap is widest on questions that require recalling a specific prior assistant turn, where judged correctness falls from $0.911$ to $0.607$, suggesting that decomposing a turn into entities discards the surface form these questions depend on. The forgetting module is more successful. Applied once to a persistent 27{,}021-node graph, it removes 9.8\% of nodes and 9.5\% of stored bytes; token F1 is unchanged ($+0.001$, 95\% CI $[-0.015, +0.016]$) and judged correctness falls by $1.6$ points, with the 95\% interval bounding any loss at $3.8$ points ($[-0.038, +0.006]$). Because our extractor is a single small model evaluated on one benchmark, these results characterise this extraction-based pipeline rather than graph-structured memory in general. Code: https://github.com/skhanzad/Selective-Amnesia

</details>


### [82] [Agentic AI uncovers conserved cross-tissue protein co-abundance programs inaccessible to single-dataset analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28990)
*Runyu Guan,Dehao Wu,Qiqi Xie,Yang Li,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个LLM智能体框架，用于大规模比较组织特异性蛋白质共丰度网络，在41个人体组织和体液的820个成对组合中识别出1,833个保守的共丰度簇，揭示了跨组织共享的疾病机制和治疗靶点，包括脑-肠轴和肝-骨髓应激反应轴等新发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质共丰度簇在跨组织间的保守性可揭示共享的疾病机制和候选治疗靶点，特别是当器官局限性疾病相关的蛋白质在外周或可及组织中富集时。然而，以往的跨组织研究仅关注生物学预选的组织对，留下了大多数可能的组合和非显而易见的关系未被探索。

Method: 研究提出了一个LLM智能体框架，用于大规模、基于证据的组织特异性蛋白质共丰度网络比较。该框架构建组织网络，推导成对共识簇，并整合来自表达图谱、蛋白质相互作用和复合物数据库、通路注释、疾病目录和文献的证据。研究应用于41个人体组织和体液的820个成对组合。

Result: 在406个组织对中识别出1,833个保守的共丰度簇。结肠、滑液、血液、脑脊液和骨髓是连接最广泛的组织，而簇最丰富的组织对主要由骨髓主导。研究还发现了非显而易见的关系：皮肤-骨髓超过了解剖学上相邻的骨-骨髓对，而结肠-乳腺包含与细胞外基质重塑、脂质代谢和免疫调节相关的癌症相关簇。簇水平分析产生了进一步的机制假说，包括脑-肠细胞外囊泡/氧化还原/血清素辅因子轴和涉及白质疾病相关基因的肝-骨髓应激反应轴。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了保守蛋白质共丰度的全球可比景观，为机制和治疗探索提供了假设生成资源。代码和数据已在GitHub上公开可用。

Abstract: Protein co-abundance clusters preserved across tissues can reveal shared disease mechanisms and candidate therapeutic targets, particularly when proteins implicated in organ-confined diseases converge in peripheral or accessible tissues. However, previous cross-tissue studies have focused on biologically pre-selected tissue pairs, leaving most possible combinations and non-obvious relationships unexplored. We present an LLM-agent framework for large-scale, evidence-grounded comparison of tissue-specific protein co-abundance networks. The framework constructs tissue networks, derives pairwise consensus clusters, and integrates evidence from expression atlases, protein interaction and complex databases, pathway annotations, disease catalogues, and literature. Applied to all 820 pairwise combinations of 41 human tissues and fluids, it identified 1,833 conserved co-abundance clusters across 406 tissue pairs. Colon, synovial fluid, blood, cerebrospinal fluid, and bone marrow were the most broadly connected tissues, while the most cluster-rich pairs were dominated by bone marrow. The analysis also highlighted non-obvious relationships: skin-bone marrow exceeded the anatomically adjacent bone-bone marrow pair, while colon-breast contained cancer-relevant clusters involving extracellular-matrix remodeling, lipid metabolism, and immune modulation. Cluster-level analyses generated further mechanistic hypotheses, including a brain-gut extracellular-vesicle/redox/serotonin-cofactor axis and a liver-bone marrow stress-response axis involving genes linked to white matter disease. These results provide a global, comparable landscape of conserved protein co-abundance and a hypothesis-generating resource for mechanistic and therapeutic exploration. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Gry1005/AgenticAI-conserved-cross-tissue-protein-co-abundance.

</details>


### [83] [Verification abundance, adjudication scarcity: what happens to mathematical knowledge when proof checking becomes free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28997)
*Maher Kallel,Mohamed El Louadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对比2026年两个AI数学证明案例，论证机器可验证性仅解决了推导有效性问题，而将验证负担转移到了需要稀缺专家注意力的表示保真度和认识论意义层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年OpenAI模型产生了Erdős单位距离猜想的反例，同一天五位数学家发布了人类验证版本；而同年8月该实验室发布的十个数学和理论计算机科学结果虽然带有机器可检查的Lean 4证书，但其中仍有争议。作者旨在探究这种差异的结构性原因。

Method: 区分三层验证：推导有效性（内核检查）、表示保真度（形式陈述是否意味着预期问题）和认识论意义。通过测量8月语料库，分析内核检查的证明（20.6 MB）与需要人类审计的陈述（55.6 KB）之间的比例，并识别218个定制定义。

Result: 内核检查的证明与需要人类审计的陈述的比例为379:1，但这些陈述包含218个定制定义而非依赖社区审查的定义。审计表面在体积上很小，但不可简化地需要专家知识。机器检查产生验证丰富，但裁决仍然稀缺。

Conclusion: 提出六类表示不匹配的分类法、机器生成数学主张的披露模式，以及对软件、密码学和受监管决策系统的影响。验证工作的性质发生了根本性转变：从机械化的推导验证转向依赖稀缺专家注意力的表示和意义验证。

Abstract: In May 2026 an OpenAI model produced a counterexample to the Erdős unit distance conjecture. Five mathematicians published a human-verified version the same day, and the result entered the literature within weeks. In August 2026 the same laboratory published ten mathematical and theoretical computer science results, each accompanied by a machine-checkable Lean 4 certificate with no unproved steps. Four weeks later, one remained the subject of an unresolved dispute over whether its formalization meant what it claimed.
  We argue that this difference is structural. We distinguish three layers of verification: derivational validity, which a kernel checks; representational fidelity, whether the formal statement means the intended question; and epistemic significance. Only the first is mechanizable. Making it effectively free therefore does not eliminate verification work but shifts the burden to layers dependent on scarce expert attention.
  Measurements of the August corpus illustrate the shift. The kernel-checked proofs total 20.6 MB, while the statements requiring human audit total 55.6 KB, a ratio of 379 to 1. Yet those statements contain 218 bespoke definitions rather than relying on community-vetted ones. The audit surface is therefore small in volume but irreducibly expert. We argue that machine checking produces verification abundance while leaving adjudication scarce. We propose a six-category taxonomy of representational mismatch, a disclosure schema for machine-generated mathematical claims, and implications for software, cryptography, and regulated decision systems.

</details>


### [84] [Multi-Step Forecasting of Grape Berry Temperature based on LSTM Model with Feed-Forward Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29008)
*Srikanth Gorthi,L. G. Divyanth,Dattatray Bhalekar,Markus Keller,Lav Khot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了融合前馈注意力机制的LSTM网络(FAM-LSTM)用于葡萄浆果温度的多步高分辨率预测，该模型在15分钟至72小时的预测范围内均显著优于LSTM、GRU、RNN和随机森林基准模型，MAE范围可达0.51-1.70°C，RMSE为0.65-2.07°C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄园中浆果温度的精准预测对于及时进行热应激管理至关重要，但传统方法难以实现多步长、高分辨率的准确预报，因此需要开发更先进的深度学习预测框架。

Method: 提出前馈注意力机制集成的长短期记忆网络(FAM-LSTM)，使用2023-2024年环境数据训练并在2025年夏季数据上验证，对比LSTM、GRU、RNN和随机森林模型，评估了两种输入场景：最近露天气象站观测数据和葡萄园内微气候测量数据，预测时长范围从15分钟到72小时(288个时间步)。

Result: FAM-LSTM在所有预测时长和输入场景下均优于所有基准模型；使用露天气象数据时MAE为0.58-1.70°C，RMSE为0.65-2.07°C；引入园内微气候数据进一步改善性能，MAE降至0.51-1.55°C，RMSE为0.71-1.87°C；预测误差在白天峰值时段(11:00-18:00)最大，且随预测时长增加而递增。

Conclusion: FAM-LSTM框架为葡萄园精准热应激管理提供了稳健的浆果温度预报能力，结合园内微气候数据可显著提升中长期预测精度，具有重要的农业应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of grape berry temperature (Tb) is essential for enabling timely heat stress management in vineyards. In this study, a feed-forward attention mechanism integrated with a Long Short-Term Memory network (FAM-LSTM) was developed and evaluated for multi-step, high-resolution Tb prediction. Models were trained using environmental data from 2023 and 2024 at Prosser, WA, USA, and validated on 2025 summer data. FAM-LSTM was benchmarked against LSTM, GRU, RNN, and Random Forest (RF) across horizons ranging from 15 minutes to 72 hours (288 time steps). Two input scenarios were evaluated: nearest open-field weather station observations and in-vineyard microclimate measurements. FAM-LSTM consistently outperformed all benchmark models across all horizons and input scenarios. Incorporating in-vineyard microclimate data significantly improved forecasting accuracy at longer horizons. Using open-field data, FAM-LSTM achieved MAE and RMSE ranges of 0.58 to 1.70 deg C and 0.65 to 2.07 deg C, respectively. In-vineyard observations further improved performance, with MAE and RMSE in the ranges of 0.51 to 1.55 deg C and 0.71 to 1.87 deg C. Error analysis showed prediction uncertainty was highest during peak daytime periods (11:00 to 18:00) and increased progressively with forecast horizon. Overall, the FAM-LSTM framework offers robust Tb forecasting to support precision heat stress management in vineyards.

</details>


### [85] [Frequency Selective Neural Networks as a Foundation Architecture for Time Series Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29012)
*Hui Huang,Ye Sun,Shiyan Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出频率选择性神经网络（FSNN），通过在神经拓扑结构中显式嵌入高级信号处理的数学原理，解决深度学习模型在时序分析中的"频谱盲"问题，实现了物理可解释性与高性能预测的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习模型（CNNs、RNNs、Transformers）在处理时序数据时存在根本性的"频谱盲"问题：将连续物理波映射到无约束的空间或离散token空间时会导致严重的频谱纠缠，使得模型成为不透明的黑盒，预测精度与物理现实脱节。

Method: 引入频率选择性神经网络（FSNN），通过完全可微分的类维纳滤波器组（Wiener-like filter bank），经由复域反向传播优化，自主发现并隔离给定任务的精确物理模式。该方法将高级信号处理的严格数学显式嵌入到神经网络拓扑中。

Result: 在标准10个多变量UEA数据集上达到77.0%的平均准确率，在高度不平衡的PTB-XL临床ECG基准测试中在所有主要指标上领先。关键是FSNN直接收敛于具有物理意义的频带（如隔离心脏QRS复波），而非抽象特征图。

Conclusion: FSNN开创了一个保证物理可解释性且不牺牲深度学习表达能力的基础架构范式，为复杂时域中的鲁棒模式识别提供了可扩展、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Time-series data across physical and biological domains are fundamentally driven by complex, non-stationary oscillatory modes. While deep learning models, such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Recurrent Neural Networks, and Transformers, have dominated sequential analysis, they remain fundamentally "spectral-blind". By mapping continuous physical waves into unconstrained spatial or discrete token spaces, these architectures suffer from severe spectral entanglement, acting as opaque black boxes that decouple predictive accuracy from physical reality. In this paper, we introduce the Frequency Selective Neural Network (FSNN), pioneering a foundation architecture guaranteeing physical interpretability without sacrificing expressive power of deep learning. FSNN addresses spectral entanglement by explicitly embedding the rigorous mathematics of advanced signal processing into its neural topology. Through a fully differentiable Wiener-like filter bank optimized via complex-domain backpropagation, FSNN autonomously discovers and isolates the precise physical modes of a given task. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FSNN establishes state-of-the-art predictive performance, achieving $77.0\%$ average accuracy on the standard 10 multivariate UEA datasets and leading across all major metrics on the highly imbalanced PTB-XL clinical ECG benchmark. Crucially, in contrast to yielding abstract feature maps, FSNN converges directly on physically meaningful frequency bands, such as isolating the cardiac QRS complex, providing a highly scalable, interpretable paradigm for robust pattern recognition in complex temporal domains. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ad6174hhhh/FSNN.

</details>


### [86] [Disentangling Representation using Attributes-based Gaussian Estimation for Medical Sound Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29026)
*Ke Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为AGEDR的解耦表示学习框架，通过将属性映射嵌入到VAE潜在向量中，实现了深度学习在医疗领域的公平性和可解释性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在特征提取方面具有强大能力，但在医疗领域的应用受限于深度神经网络缺乏公平性和可解释性，这阻碍了其在医疗领域的广泛采用。

Method: 提出基于属性的解耦表示学习框架AGEDR，包含属性映射嵌入(AME)模块，将属性映射为向量并与VAE中的部分潜在向量对齐；通过最小化互信息将该部分潜在向量与剩余潜在向量解耦；使用VAE潜在向量的均值参数训练分类器。

Result: 大量实验证明AGEDR优于传统分类模型和现有解耦表示学习方法；消融实验验证了AGEDR的解耦能力和公平性。

Conclusion: AGEDR框架成功解决了深度学习在医疗应用中的公平性和可解释性问题，为医疗领域的深度学习应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning has a powerful capability of feature extraction. However, the lack of fairness and interpretability in deep neural networks poses limitations to their adoption in the medical domain. This paper proposes a disentangled representation learning (DisenRL) framework, named the Attributes-based Gaussian Estimation for Disentangled Representation (AGEDR), which incorporates Attribute Mapping Embedding (AME) modules designed to map attributes into vectors and align them with a subset of the latent vectors in a Variational AutoEncoder (VAE). This part of the latent vector will be disentangled from the remaining latent vectors by minimizing mutual information. A classifier is then trained using the mean parameters of the latent vectors from the VAE. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AGEDR outperforms both conventional classification models and existing disentangled representation learning methods. The ablation experiments also indicate the disentangling capability and fairness of AGEDR. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZhaoKe1024/DisentangledRepr.

</details>


### [87] [Facts Without Rules: Boundary Metadata Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29028)
*Yian Wang,Agam Goyal,Eshwar Chandrasekharan,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统中，智能体间传递的摘要会优先保留操作事实而丢失边界元数据（summary collapse），导致隐私泄露；显式约束和受众白名单可有效缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现多智能体LLM系统中的隐私泄露源头：智能体间传递的摘要(handoff)会结构性破坏隐私边界。摘要倾向于保留操作事实，却削弱了控制这些事实使用方式的边界元数据，研究者将此失效模式称为"summary collapse"。

Method: 在受控多智能体协调测试平台上，使用人类验证的评判器测量标记存活率(κ=0.74)，其中σ_b=1表示所有边界标记完整保留，σ_b=0表示全部丢失。对比了GPT-5-mini和DeepSeek-R1-32B模型，测试了不同压缩预算、边界显式程度以及多种缓解策略的效果。

Result: 边界标记与操作事实的存活率在传递层面几乎不相关(Pearson r接近零)：未压缩文本传递保留边界σ_b≈0.80，而25词预算降至σ_b≈0.57，操作事实存活率却保持高位。模糊语言在GPT中73%、DeepSeek中50%的情况下发生泄露，显式约束将泄露率降至15%以下。仅提示缓解和精确字符串编辑仅部分解决问题，而金标准受众白名单几乎消除所有模型的泄露。

Conclusion: Summary collapse是多智能体LLM系统的结构性隐私漏洞，不可简化为多智能体拓扑问题。边界显式程度是保护效果的关键因素，正确识别受众边界比单纯的提示工程或编辑更有效，为多智能体系统的隐私保护设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often coordinate by compressing an upstream interaction into a handoff artifact that downstream agents treat as shared state. We show that this handoff step is a structural source of privacy leakage: summaries preferentially preserve operational facts while weakening the boundary metadata that governs how those facts may be used---a failure mode we call \emph{summary collapse}. On a controlled multi-agent coordination testbed we measure marker survival with a human-validated judge ($κ= 0.74$), where $σ_b = 1$ means every boundary marker survives verbatim and $σ_b = 0$ means all are lost. Boundary-marker and operational-fact survival are nearly uncorrelated at the handoff level on both GPT-5-mini and DeepSeek-R1-32B (Pearson $r$ near zero): uncompressed free-text handoffs preserve boundaries at $σ_b \approx 0.80$, whereas a $25$-word budget drops $σ_b$ to ${\approx}0.57$ while operational-fact survival stays near ceiling. Controlled downstream tests reveal that protection depends on \emph{boundary explicitness}: vague languages leak in $73\%$ of GPT and $50\%$ of DeepSeek cases, while explicit constraints reduce leakage to under $15\%$ across all three tested models. A no-handoff single-agent control further shows the failure is not reducible to multi-agent topology as direct full-marker access still leaks more often than the operationalized handoff. Prompt-only mitigation and exact-string redaction only partially address the problem, while a gold-derived audience allowlist nearly eliminates leakage across models, showing that correctly identifying audience boundaries is the key factor.

</details>


### [88] [Learning to Follow In-Context Watermark Instructions via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29030)
*Yepeng Liu,Tianyi Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Dawn Song,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ICWBench基准来评估大语言模型的上下文水印能力，发现现有模型无法同时保证水印检测性和回答质量。作者提出了一种两阶段训练方法，在Qwen3-14B和GPT-OSS-20B上将检测率从约10%和34%提升至97%以上，同时保持高质量输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文水印(ICW)通过在查询前添加指令让模型在响应中嵌入统计可检测信号，为第三方提供无需模型内部权限的水印接口。然而，现有研究未衡量当前大语言模型在遵循水印指令且不降低回答质量方面的实际表现，这是ICW可靠性的核心问题。

Method: 提出两阶段训练方法：(1)自蒸馏与logits扰动(SDLP)阶段，使用同一基座模型作为教师和学生，教师通过解码时logits扰动执行ICW指令，学生训练以匹配教师输出分布；(2)强化学习阶段，使用自动验证器作为奖励信号。该方法无需更强模型蒸馏、无需人工标注、无需预存ICW能力。

Result: 在14个前沿私有和开源LLM上评估发现，无模型能在三个ICW指令族上同时达成水印检测性和回答质量目标。应用所提方法后，Qwen3-14B和GPT-OSS-20B在1%误检率下的平均真阳性率分别从0.100提升至0.974、从0.337提升至0.968，同时在困惑度评估和LLM-as-a-Judge下保持高回答质量。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在上下文水印任务上表现不足，无法平衡检测性与质量。所提出的自包含两阶段训练方法有效解决了这一问题，大幅提升了水印检测能力且不损害输出质量，为第三方可调用的LLM水印机制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In-context watermarking (ICW) prepends an instruction to a query asking the model to embed a statistically detectable signal in its response. It thus equips LLMs with a watermarking interface that third parties can invoke without access to model internals. Its reliability hinges on the LLM following the instruction without degrading answer quality, yet how well current LLMs do so has not been measured. We introduce $\mathsf{ICWBench}$, a benchmark of three verifiable ICW instruction families, each scored on both detectability and answer quality. Evaluating 14 frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs, we find that none of the evaluated LLMs achieves both objectives across all three families. To address this, we propose a self-contained two-stage training method, requiring no distillation from a stronger model, no manual annotation, and no pre-existing ICW IF ability. The first stage, self-distillation with logits perturbation (SDLP), uses the same base LLM as both teacher and student: an instruction-equivalent decoding-time logits perturbation makes the teacher follow the ICW instruction, and the student is trained to match the teacher's output distribution. The second stage applies reinforcement learning with the automatic verifier as the reward. Applied to Qwen3-14B and GPT-OSS-20B, our method raises average TPR@$1\%$FPR across three ICW instructions from $0.100$ to $0.974$ and from $0.337$ to $0.968$, respectively, while maintaining high response quality under both perplexity evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge.

</details>


### [89] [EmoLASP: Emotion Recognition with Language Models and Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29035)
*Thao Le,Michael Thielscher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EmoLASP框架，将语言模型与基于ASP的声明式推理相结合，用于对话情感识别。实验表明该方法能提升VAD分数预测性能，尤其在无对话历史输入时效果显著，可降低微调和长对话历史提示的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的对话情感识别方法存在不稳定性问题，且微调或长对话历史提示的成本高昂。需要一种既能保证预测一致性又能降低成本的方法。

Method: 提出EmoLASP框架，结合语言模型与Answer Set Programming（ASP）进行声明式推理。在IEMOCAP基准数据集上，使用6个开源LLM（3B-120B）和2个PLM（BERT、RoBERTa）进行实验，预测VAD（效价-唤醒度-支配度）分数。

Result: EmoLASP相比单独使用语言模型提升了预测性能，即使在提示或输入向量中没有对话历史时也有效。对于仅使用提示的LLM（无需微调）增益最大；但对于已微调的PLM，一旦有对话历史可用，推理器增益较小。

Conclusion: EmoLASP的LLM流程展示了推理方法的优势：确保情感预测一致性，同时降低微调成本和长对话历史提示成本。声明式推理与语言模型的结合为对话情感识别提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in conversations is increasingly tackled with language models, but these models can be unstable and expensive to fine-tune or to prompt with long dialogue histories. We propose EmoLASP, a framework that combines a language model with declarative reasoning via Answer Set Programming (ASP) to predict VAD scores (Valence-Arousal-Dominance) in conversations. Experiments on a widely used benchmark dataset (IEMOCAP) across six open-source LLMs (3B-120B) and two PLMs (BERT, RoBERTa) show that EmoLASP improves prediction performance compared to using the language model alone, even when the LLMs/PLMs are given no dialogue history in their prompts or input vectors. The gains are largest for prompt-only LLMs, which EmoLASP uses without any fine-tuning. However, for fine-tuned PLMs, the reasoner adds little once dialogue history is available. EmoLASP's LLM pipeline demonstrates the potential advantages of using a reasoning approach to ensure emotion prediction consistency and to reduce both the cost of fine-tuning and the cost of prompting with long dialogue histories.

</details>


### [90] [Let Prompts Bridge Defense Knowledge: Transferable Graph Purification via Vulnerability-Aware GPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29054)
*Shuomin Xue,Jingyuan Li,Ju Jia,Jingxuan Yu,Xiaojun Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProGAP，一种可迁移的图净化方案，通过漏洞感知的图提示学习来防御图对抗性攻击，相比最先进基线方法提升1%-9%性能，时间消耗降低最多2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性净化方法本质上是领域受限的防御，存在两个主要局限性：(1)单领域数据提供的结构和语义多样性不足以学习鲁棒的净化标准；(2)从零开始训练特定领域的防御策略需要大量计算成本。

Method: 首先，在数据丰富的图上预训练扰动捕获边检测器，通过联合建模拓扑和语义信息来捕获通用对抗模式。随后，设计漏洞感知提示，将定向净化指导注入有偏节点，使预训练检测器无需参数密集更新即可适应下游图中的分布偏移。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比最先进基线方法，ProGAP实现了1%-9%的性能提升，并将时间消耗减少最多2.2倍。

Conclusion: 通过跨领域的知识迁移和漏洞感知提示学习，ProGAP成功解决了现有图对抗防御方法的领域受限问题，实现了高效且有效的可迁移图净化。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a cornerstone for representing complex relational dependencies in diverse multimedia tasks, particularly in cross-platform user interest modeling and cross-modal semantic alignment. In the real world, a practical defense against graph adversarial perturbations is needed. However, we observe that the prevailing adversarial purification methods are essentially domain-restricted defenses, which leads to the following shortcomings: (1) single-domain data provides insufficient structural and semantic diversity for learning robust purification criteria; (2) training of domain-specific defense strategies from scratch consumes substantial computational cost. To address the above limitations, we propose a transferable graph purification scheme, named ProGAP, to bridge adversarial defense knowledge via vulnerability-aware graph prompt learning. Firstly, to capture universal adversarial patterns, a perturbation-capture edge detector is pretrained on data-rich graphs by jointly modeling topological and semantic information. Subsequently, to achieve more knowledge transfer w.r.t. robustness, vulnerability-aware prompts are designed that inject targeted purification guidance into biased nodes, during which the pretrained detector adapts to distribution shifts in downstream graphs without parameter-laborious updates. Experimental results demonstrate that compared with state-of-the-art baselines, our ProGAP achieves 1%-9% improvement, and reduces the time consumption by up to 2.2x. The code for ProGAP is available at https://github.com/Lieyoufffff/ProGAP.

</details>


### [91] [Agent2UCB: Agentic System for Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29063)
*Sheldon Yu,Rui Wang,Tong Yu,Sungchul Kim,Doga Dogan,Junda Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agent2UCB，一个基于智能体的生成式引擎优化（GEO）系统，通过反馈驱动的优化策略自主提升内容在大型语言模型驱动的搜索引擎中的可见性，同时兼顾SEO质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型驱动的搜索引擎（如Google AI Overviews和Perplexity）催生了生成式引擎优化（GEO）的新需求，亟需能够自主优化内容以提升其在生成式系统中被引用或摘要可能性的方法。

Method: 提出了Agent2UCB系统，该系统对每个内容项评估九种GEO策略，利用结合LLM先验与在线奖励信号的bandit策略（Agent2UCB）加速有效方法的选择；同时提供轻量级纯文本SEO就绪评估，涵盖可读性、主题覆盖和EEAT风格可信度，以监控副作用。

Result: 在GEO-Bench基准上的实验表明，系统在保持SEO质量的同时，能够持续提升内容的可见性增益。

Conclusion: Agent2UCB系统展示了智能体GEO系统的可行性，能够在不影响SEO质量的前提下有效提升内容在生成式搜索引擎中的可见性，并提供了可视化的演示系统。

Abstract: Large language model driven search engines such as Google AI Overviews and Perplexity have created new opportunities for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) the practice of refining content to increase its likelihood of being cited or summarized by generative systems. We demonstrate Agent2UCB, an agentic GEO system that autonomously improves content visibility through customized, feedback-driven optimization. For each content item, the system evaluates nine GEO strategies, identifies the most effective method, and accelerates selection using a bandit-based Agent2UCB policy that integrates LLM priors with online reward signals. To monitor side effects, the system also provides a lightweight, text-only SEO readiness evaluation covering readability, topical coverage, and EEAT-style credibility. Experiments on GEO-Bench show consistent visibility gains while preserving SEO quality. The demo allows users to choose the websites of interest, observe the optimization workflow, and compare GEO/SEO outcomes across methods.

</details>


### [92] [Revolutionizing Turn-by-Turn Navigation with Cloud-Edge Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29073)
*Yiming Yang,Hao Fu,Fanxiang Zeng,Xikai Yang,Yue Liu,Ning Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次将深度学习大规模应用于驾驶音频导航，通过Transformer和混合专家(MoE)的多任务学习框架生成实时、上下文感知的导航指令，显著降低了车辆偏离导航路线的比例，提升了导航指令的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逐向导航(TBT)音频指令生成主要依赖基于规则的方法，难以在信息内容与认知负荷之间取得平衡，在复杂环境中可能导致驾驶员困惑或错过转弯，存在优化空间。

Method: 将导航指令生成建模为多任务学习问题，将音频内容分解为模块化元素的组合；提出结合Transformer时空信息处理能力和混合专家(MoE)多任务学习能力的深度学习框架；采用云-边缘协同架构处理计算需求，确保可扩展性和实时性能。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，该方法显著降低了偏航率（车辆偏离导航路线的比例），相比传统方法能够提供更清晰、更有效的音频导航指令。

Conclusion: 这是深度学习在驾驶音频导航领域的首次大规模应用，标志着智能交通和驾驶辅助技术的重大进步，为TBT导航系统的智能化发展开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Turn-by-turn (TBT) navigation systems are integral to modern driving experiences, providing real-time audio instructions to guide drivers safely to destinations. However, existing audio instruction policy often relies on rule-based approaches that struggle to balance informational content with cognitive load, potentially leading to driver confusion or missed turns in complex environments. To overcome these difficulties, we first model the generation of navigation instructions as a multi-task learning problem by decomposing the audio content into combinations of modular elements. Then, we propose a novel deep learning framework that leverages the powerful spatiotemporal information processing capabilities of Transformers and the strong multi-task learning abilities of Mixture of Experts (MoE) to generate real-time, context-aware audio instructions for TBT driving navigation. A cloud-edge collaborative architecture is implemented to handle the computational demands of the model, ensuring scalability and real-time performance for practical applications. Experimental results in the real world demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces the yaw rate (the proportion of vehicles deviating from navigation routes) compared to traditional methods, delivering clearer and more effective audio instructions. This is the first large-scale application of deep learning in driving audio navigation, marking a substantial advancement in intelligent transportation and driving assistance technologies.

</details>


### [93] [Nested Convex-Body Chasing for Online Optimization with Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29074)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Aprameyo Chakrabartty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌套收缩可行域的在线优化问题，提出CONES和COCO两种设置下的算法，通过分离损失控制与几何移动，实现了多项式级维度依赖的最优移动代价和遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线优化问题中，可行域会随时间嵌套收缩，现有方法在处理几何移动代价时存在指数级维度依赖（$O(d^{d/2})$），且难以同时保证遗憾界和移动代价的最优性。本文旨在解决嵌套收缩可行域下的高效在线优化问题。

Method: 提出将损失控制与几何移动分离的方法：1）使用约束最小化器和累积损失测试保持遗憾保证；2）设计可重置的确定性嵌套凸体追踪器限制移动代价。对于CONES问题，追踪当前可行集与自适应目标子水平集的交集；对于COCO问题，采用单步延迟追踪配合正则化领导者重置策略。利用欧几里得追踪比例和Steiner点追踪技术。

Result: CONES问题：实现了任意前缀非正遗憾和$O(\sqrt{d\log(1+d)\,GD\log(eT)/μ})$的移动代价；在二维情形下证明了$Ω(\sqrt{\log T})$期望移动代价的下界，证实最优性；在线性增长条件下实现了独立于T的移动代价。COCO问题：一般凸损失下遗憾和约束违反度均为$O(G_fD\sqrt{d\log(1+d)T})$；强凸损失下均达到$O(d\log(1+d)\log(eT))$。所有结果均将先前的$O(d^{d/2})$投影路径因子改进为多项式维度依赖。

Conclusion: 本文成功将嵌套收缩可行域在线优化问题的维度依赖从指数级降至多项式级，在CONES和COCO两种设置下均获得了理论最优的遗憾界和移动代价保证。算法框架具有通用性，为相关在线优化问题提供了新的分析技术。

Abstract: We study online optimization with nested shrinking feasible regions in two settings: convex optimization with nested evolving feasible sets (CONES) and adversarial constrained online convex optimization (COCO). Our algorithms separate loss control from geometric movement: constrained minimizers and cumulative-loss tests preserve regret guarantees, while a deterministic resettable nested convex-body chaser limits movement.
  For CONES with a $G$-Lipschitz, $μ$-strongly convex objective on a diameter-$D$ domain, we chase intersections of the current feasible set with adaptive objective sublevel sets. Using the Euclidean chasing ratio $O(\sqrt{d\log(1+d)})$, we obtain nonpositive regret at every prefix and movement $O(\sqrt{d\log(1+d)\,GD\log(eT)/μ})$. The bound adapts to the increase in the constrained optimum value. In dimension two, with all other parameters fixed, every randomized algorithm with terminal expected regret $O(T^β)$, $β<1$, suffers $Ω(\sqrt{\log T})$ expected movement on some deterministic nested sequence, proving optimal horizon dependence. Under linear growth away from the constrained minimizer set, Steiner-point tracking yields movement independent of $T$.
  For general convex COCO, one-step-delayed chasing with regularized-leader resets gives regret $O(G_fD\sqrt{d\log(1+d)T})$ and cumulative constraint violation $O(G_gD\sqrt{d\log(1+d)T})$. For strongly convex losses, both are $O(d\log(1+d)\log(eT))$ when other parameters are fixed. These reductions replace the $O(d^{d/2})$ projection-path factor in prior analyses by the polynomial dimension dependence of Euclidean nested convex-body chasing.

</details>


### [94] [HANIA: Planner-Guided Multimodal Graph Evidence Selection for Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29088)
*Zafar Ali,Asad Khan,Nimbeshaho Thierry,Nabila Amir,Adam A. Q. Mohammed,Pavlos Kefalas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HANIA，一个规划器引导的多模态图框架，通过结构化证据规划和紧凑的图引导检索，在无需目标数据集微调的情况下实现了具有竞争力的多模态问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态问答对噪声、不完整和弱依据的证据仍然敏感。长非结构化上下文会引入冗余并鼓励无依据的生成，而扁平化检索可能忽略多步推理所需的关系。

Method: HANIA使用冻结的视觉-语言模型处理图像和文本，提取简洁的问题相关视觉证据（带有显式弃权机制）。然后构建输入依据的多模态图，应用两分组有限状态规划器来协调描述性和关系性证据。覆盖率感知剪枝基于相关性、图置信度、概念覆盖率和模态多样性保留紧凑的证据集。选定的段落、视觉陈述和图三元组被提供给冻结的指令微调解码器。

Result: 在ScienceQA数据集上的评估显示，HANIA在答案准确率、证据过滤质量、证据预算敏感性和效率方面表现良好。结构化证据规划和紧凑的图引导检索能够在无需目标数据集微调或迭代检索的情况下支持具有竞争力的多模态问答。

Conclusion: 通过结构化的证据规划和图引导的紧凑检索，可以有效支持多模态问答任务，避免了传统方法中的目标数据集微调和迭代检索需求，为多模态问答提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal question answering remains sensitive to noisy, incomplete, and weakly grounded evidence. Long unstructured contexts can introduce redundancy and encourage unsupported generation, while flat retrieval may overlook relations needed for multi-step reasoning. We present HANIA, a planner-guided multimodal graph framework for evidence-grounded question answering. HANIA processes the supplied image and text using a frozen vision-language model to extract concise question-relevant visual evidence with explicit abstention. It then constructs an input-grounded multimodal graph and applies a two-group finite-state planner to coordinate descriptive and relational evidence. Coverage-aware pruning retains a compact evidence set based on relevance, graph confidence, concept coverage, and modality diversity. The selected passages, visual statements, and graph triples are provided to a frozen instruction-tuned decoder. We evaluate HANIA on ScienceQA using answer accuracy, evidence-filtering quality, evidence-budget sensitivity, and efficiency. The results show that structured evidence planning and compact graph-guided retrieval can support competitive multimodal question answering without target-dataset fine-tuning or iterative retrieval. The code is available at https://github.com/Zafar-southeast/HANIA.

</details>


### [95] [EviAnchor: Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models via Regional Visual Evidence Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29092)
*Sihang Jia,Shuliang Liu,Songbo Yang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EviAnchor框架，通过在生成过程中保存和重新激活视觉证据，减少大型视觉语言模型中的幻觉问题，无需训练且保持单分支推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型(LVLMs)经常生成与视觉输入不符的内容。初步实验发现视觉证据主要在解码器的早期至中间层被整合到回答侧表示中，而在后续层中其直接影响逐渐减弱，导致早期获取的视觉证据在后续生成过程中未被充分利用。

Method: 提出EviAnchor框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)引入区域证据锚点(REA)槽，将密集视觉token逐步聚合为空间结构化表示；(2)通过决策条件化证据路由增强当前决策状态对这些视觉锚点的访问，减轻对文本语境的过度依赖；(3)恢复原生Transformer计算，将检索到的视觉证据与问题语义和生成历史整合。该方法无需训练，保持单分支推理。

Result: 在POPE、CHAIR和MMHal-Bench三个基准测试上均取得了视觉基础能力的一致性提升，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在生成过程中持续保存和重新激活视觉证据，EviAnchor有效缓解了LVLMs的幻觉问题，提升了模型的视觉基础能力，且无需额外训练成本。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) frequently generate content unsupported by visual inputs. Preliminary experiments show that visual evidence is primarily incorporated into answer-side representations in early-to-middle decoder layers, while its direct influence progressively weakens in later layers. This attenuation suggests that visual evidence acquired earlier may be insufficiently utilized during subsequent generation. Based on this observation, we propose EviAnchor, a training-free and single-branch inference framework that preserves and reactivates visual evidence throughout generation. EviAnchor introduces Regional Evidence Anchor (REA) slots to progressively aggregate dense visual tokens into spatially structured representations. It then strengthens the current decision state's access to these visual anchors through decision-conditioned evidence routing, mitigating excessive dependence on textual context. Finally, the model resumes its native Transformer computation to integrate the retrieved visual evidence with question semantics and generation history. Experiments across POPE, CHAIR, and MMHal-Bench demonstrate consistent improvements in visual grounding.

</details>


### [96] [SafeAtlas-VL: Beyond Binary Multimodal Safety with Large-Scale Data and Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29098)
*Zongrui Wang,Xiangyang Zhu,Sicheng Wang,Han Wang,Dingyi Rong,Zeyu Zhang,Chunyi Li,Yue Shi,Kaiwei Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Yuan Tian,Qi Jia,Yan Teng,Wei Sun,Ning Liu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SafeAtlas-VL数据集（150万训练样本）和SafeAtlas-Bench评估集（5000样本），通过五级有序量表对多模态交互中的图像、请求和响应进行安全评估，并训练了SafeAtlas Guard系列模型，在F1分数上超越先前SOTA约4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态安全审核方法存在两个主要局限：1）仅针对单一判断目标进行训练，无法区分视觉内容、用户意图和助手行为带来的不同风险；2）将安全评估简化为二元决策，导致跨模态交互的风险难以比较，模糊案例被掩盖。

Method: 1）构建SafeAtlas-VL数据集，涵盖15个伤害类别和55个细粒度子类别，从真实世界和合成来源收集安全相关数据，采用感知分歧的标注流程；2）通过目标条件调优训练SafeAtlas Guard系列模型，实现五级安全分类，并使用软累积有序头将安全性映射为连续分数；3）构建SafeAtlas-Bench评估集用于五级预测和连续风险评分评估。

Result: 在不使用其他基准训练集的情况下，在相应测试集上达到竞争性性能；8B模型获得最佳整体性能，F1分数超越先前SOTA约4%；模型能够同时进行五路安全等级分类和连续风险评分。

Conclusion: 通过引入五级有序量表和连续风险评分，成功解决了现有多模态安全审核中风险比较困难和模糊案例掩盖的问题，所提出的数据集和模型展现了强大的泛化能力，为多模态安全研究提供了有价值的资源。

Abstract: Multimodal safety moderation requires distinguishing risks arising from visual content, user intent, and assistant behavior. Existing safeguards, however, are typically trained for a single judgment target and reduce safety assessment to a binary decision. Consequently, risk becomes difficult to compare across a multimodal interaction, and ambiguous cases are obscured. We introduce SafeAtlas-VL, a dataset of 1.5M training instances that places image-, request-, and response-level judgments on a five-level ordered scale. We curate a broad collection of safety-relevant data from both real-world and synthetic sources and apply a disagreement-aware annotation procedure. The resulting dataset spans 15 harm categories and 55 fine-grained subcategories, covering a broad range of multimodal safety scenarios. We also construct SafeAtlas-Bench, a held-out set of 5,000 instances for evaluating five-level predictions and continuous risk scores. Upon this dataset, we train the SafeAtlas Guard series of models via target-conditioned tuning for multimodal safety detection. Our models not only perform five-way classification of safety levels but also map safety to continuous scores through a soft cumulative ordinal head. Experimental results demonstrate that guard models trained on our dataset exhibit strong generalization: even without using the training sets of other benchmarks, they achieve competitive performance on the corresponding test sets. Notably, our 8B model attains the overall best performance, outperforming the previous SOTA by approximately 4% in F1 score. Code, data, and models are released to support further research. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive, harmful, graphic, or disturbing.

</details>


### [97] [Clustering as Approximation by Constrained Projectors: Theory and Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29102)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的理论框架，揭示了k-means、模糊c-means、核k-means、核FCM和谱聚类等聚类方法本质上都是对信号矩阵进行结构化低秩投影操作，并提供了几何凸性、扰动界和精确恢复等理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类方法种类繁多且形式各异，缺乏统一的理论基础来揭示它们之间的内在联系和本质共性。现有研究对各类聚类算法的分析相互独立，难以建立系统的理论体系，因此需要一个统一的数学框架来阐明这些方法的代数关联和优化结构。

Method: 构建统一优化模板：将多种聚类方法表述为min_{B∈C} ||M - M P_B||_F^2的形式，其中M为信号导出矩阵，P_B为投影矩阵，C为不同的约束集（硬聚类、模糊聚类、核诱导或正交投影）。通过结构化低秩投影器的视角，建立各聚类方法的代数联系，并在投影流形上分析几何凸性、推导扰动界和精确恢复保证。

Result: 在投影流形上建立了测地凸性性质，推导了矩阵噪声下的扰动界以量化稳定性，在理想块模型条件下证明了精确恢复保证。分析了不同聚类族何时塌缩到相同的最优子空间，以及小簇间泄漏条件下偏差的产生机制。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解聚类方法提供了一个基于结构化投影器的连贯的、理论优先的基础，揭示了各类聚类算法的本质统一性，为算法设计、稳定性分析和理论保证提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified theoretical framework showing that a broad family of clustering methods, including k-means, fuzzy c-means, kernel k-means, kernel FCM, and spectral clustering, can all be expressed as structured low-rank projectors acting on a signal-derived matrix. By formulating each method as an instance of min over B in C of ||M - M P_B||_F^2, with different constraint sets C, we establish a common optimization template that clarifies the algebraic links among hard, fuzzy, kernel-induced, and orthonormal projections. Within this framework, we derive non-trivial theoretical results, including geodesic convexity properties on the projection manifold, perturbation bounds quantifying stability to matrix noise, and exact recovery guarantees under ideal block-model conditions. The analysis further explains when different clustering families collapse to the same optimal subspace and how deviations arise under small inter-cluster leakage. Overall, the work provides a coherent, theory-first foundation for understanding clustering through structured projectors.

</details>


### [98] [Emergent Misalignment Is Not Magical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29118)
*Mingxuan Li,Qirun Dai,Heran Wang,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了“涌现性错位”（EM）是一种可预测的数据依赖泛化现象，通过表征距离可以高度预测其行为，否定了此前关于通用错位方向和邪恶人格的主流解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将涌现性错位视为意外行为，并以通用错位方向或获得邪恶人格等拟人化视角进行解释，但这些解释背后的机制尚不明确，缺乏对EM本质的深入理解。

Method: 通过分析基础模型对EM训练数据和评估提示的表征，研究表征距离与邪恶行为之间的关系；对比不同训练数据格式的影响；验证通用错位方向的迁移性；区分EM效应与人格变化；将标量距离扩展为数据集特定的泛化方向。

Result: 发现EM训练后的邪恶行为可通过表征距离高度预测（12个模型-数据集设置下平均Spearman相关系数为-0.73）；训练数据格式显著影响EM效果；不存在跨不同EM模型迁移的通用错位方向；EM效应与人格变化有本质区别；提出的泛化方向指标能稳健预测语义保持的提示扰动下的邪恶行为。

Conclusion: 涌现性错位是一种可预测的数据依赖泛化现象，其机制可通过表征距离和数据集特定的泛化方向来理解，这为AI安全研究提供了新的理论框架，有助于更准确地评估和缓解LLM的错位风险。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly harmful datasets can lead to misalignment broadly, a phenomenon known as emergent misalignment (EM). EM poses a challenge for AI safety and our understanding of LLMs. Prior work often frames EM as an unexpected behavior, and explains it by appealing to general misalignment directions or anthropomorphizing it as acquiring an evil persona. However, the mechanisms behind these framings remain obscure. In this work, we show that EM is a predictable and data-dependent generalization phenomenon. By examining the base model's representation of EM training data and evaluation prompts, we find that evilness after EM training is highly predictable from representational distance: the closer an evaluation prompt is to training data centroid, the more evilness it elicits from EM models after training (with an average Spearman correlation of -0.73 across 12 model-dataset settings). Building upon this analysis, we further demystify EM by showing that (1) its effectiveness changes significantly based on training data format; (2) there is not a general misalignment direction that transfers across different EM models; (3) the effect of EM is fundamentally different from persona changes. Furthermore, we extend the EM generalization metric from a scalar distance to a dataset-specific generalization direction, which robustly predicts EM models' evilness under semantics-preserving prompt perturbations including appending random tokens and paraphrasing, where other methods do not reliably generalize.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Correctness: Validity-Oriented Evaluation of Biomedical LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29127)
*Rodrigo de Oliveira,Federico Pittino,James Gwinnutt,Jay Nanavati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的生物医学LLM评估流程，通过指标驱动的数据突变生成可审计偏好对，并从正确性、鲁棒性和合规性三个维度评估Llama-3.1-8B模型。结果表明SFT→RL两阶段训练策略表现最佳，在PICO提取等可分解任务上甚至可匹敌前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学领域高质量人类标注数据稀缺，导致难以有效评估生物医学LLM作为评估器的性能，亟需一种可扩展且面向有效性的评估框架。

Method: 1) 对现有人类标注生物医学基准进行指标驱动的确定性突变，生成可审计的偏好对；2) 从正确性（相对指标导出的金标签）、鲁棒性（随机采样稳定性）和合规性（输出格式符合度）三个维度评估评估器；3) 在四种训练机制下测试Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct：基线、仅SFT、仅RL、SFT→RL。

Result: 基线和单阶段训练机制在PICO提取和临床计算等结构化医学判别任务上表现不佳；SFT→RL策略在正确性、合规性和鲁棒性三个维度均表现最佳，在可分解任务上收益显著，有时可匹敌或超越前沿模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的评估流程能够有效揭示不同训练策略的性能差异，证明了SFT→RL组合训练在生物医学LLM评估器开发中的有效性，为数据稀缺场景下的评估器优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We propose a scalable, validity-oriented pipeline for evaluating biomedical LLM judges when high-quality human judgments are scarce. First, we augment existing human-labelled biomedical benchmarks with deterministic, metric-grounded mutations that produce auditable preference pairs. Second, we evaluate judges beyond aggregate correctness using three deployment-relevant dimensions: correctness against metric-derived gold labels, robustness under repeated stochastic sampling, and compliance with the requested output format. We use this pipeline to assess Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct under four regimes: (1) base, using the instruct model as is; (2) SFT, distillation-based supervised fine-tuning only; (3) RL, GRPO-based reinforcement learning only; and (4) SFT$\rightarrow$RL, SFT followed by RL. The base and single-stage regimes struggle on structured medical discrimination such as PICO extraction and clinical calculations, whereas SFT$\rightarrow$RL performs best across correctness, compliance, and robustness; gains concentrate on decomposable tasks (PICO, MedCalc), at times matching or outperforming frontier models.

</details>


### [100] [APIFlow-Bench: Measuring Whether Agents Survive Long, Dependent API Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29128)
*Zelin Wan,Arash Nourian,Xiaoxiao Li,Nihar Nandan,Kamalakannan Nandagopal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了APIFlow-Bench，一个针对长程依赖REST-API工作流的可审计基准测试，发现现有模型在复杂API调用链上的性能显著下降，且失败模式与独立错误假设不符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用代理评估仅采用单一比特指标（端到端工作流是否完成），无法区分生产环境中关键的失败类型，如凭证过期、载荷格式错误或正确执行后的错误交付。

Method: 构建APIFlow-Bench基准，通过合成API世界前向生成子任务，采用零LLM自测三元组验证评分器、神谕确立可解性，并进行对抗性审计；评分采用确定性和溯源敏感方法，通过状态检查追踪模拟铸造的canary数据流，并逐字段验证类型化答案卡片。

Result: 在19个前沿和开源模型上发现：(1)依赖链长度增加导致成功率下降，从单个子任务93%降至20子任务链74%，包含被标记为无模型通过的链时降至61%；(2)可靠性比最佳能力更能区分模型，最佳五次跨度7点而全五次可靠性跨度44点；(3)独立错误假设不成立：20子任务链通过率比子任务级通过率乘积高33个百分点，77%的失败运行达到正确最终状态但仅在交付时失败。

Conclusion: APIFlow-Bench揭示了工具使用代理在长程API工作流中的真实性能瓶颈，包括依赖链降解、可靠性差异以及复合失败模式的异常特性，为改进生产环境中的工具使用代理提供了重要的评估框架和实证基础。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are commonly evaluated by a single bit: whether an end-to-end workflow completed. This metric fails to distinguish failures that matter in production, such as expired credentials, malformed payloads, or correct execution followed by incorrect final delivery. We introduce APIFlow-Bench, a fully auditable benchmark for long-horizon, dependent REST-API workflows that decomposes performance into seven engineering capabilities and requires agents to produce answers supported by the actual call path. We generate synthetic API worlds forward, subtask by subtask; each subtask is admitted only after a zero-LLM self-test triad verifies its grader and an oracle establishes solvability, and an adversarial audit identified and fixed six grader exploits. Grading is deterministic and provenance-sensitive: a state check traces a mock-minted canary through the API data flow to the response the answer must originate from, and a typed answer card is verified field by field. We release all answer keys and 44,362 unredacted execution transcripts. Across 19 frontier and open-weight models under one neutral scaffold, we find: (1) longer dependency chains degrade success, from 93% on individual subtasks to 74% on clean 20-subtask chains and 61% when including the 8% of chain trials that a model-consensus screen flags as passed by no model; (2) reliability separates models more than best-case capability, with best-of-five spanning seven points but all-five-of-five reliability spanning 44 points; (3) the independent-error account of compounding failure does not fit the data: pass rates on 20-subtask chains are 33 percentage points above the product of subtask-level rates, and on the clean slice 77% of failing runs reached the correct final state and failed only at delivery.

</details>


### [101] [More Perspectives, Stronger Signals: Multi-Perspective Enhancement and Progressive Fusion for Multimodal Entity Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29139)
