We present a controlled evaluation of 13 models spanning dense and Mixture-of-Experts variants from the Qwen3.5 and Qwen3.6 lineages, together with proprietary GPT release variants, using the OntoLearner retrieval-augmented generation pipeline. All models are evaluated with the same embedding model, retrieval configuration, prompt templates, decoding settings, datasets, and metrics on term typing, taxonomy discovery, and non-taxonomic relationship extraction across four biomedical and materials science and engineering ontologies. Within the dense Qwen3.5 lineage, increasing parameter count primarily improves precision rather than recall, with the largest gains occurring between 9B and 27B parameters. However, the effect of scale is neither monotonic nor uniform across tasks and domains. Dense 27B models outperform substantially larger sparse models on term typing, whereas larger Mixture-of-Experts models achieve the strongest open-weight results on taxonomy discovery. Non-taxonomic relationship extraction remains difficult across model scales, particularly for the Materials Data Science ontology. Performance differences across matched Qwen variants and proprietary GPT releases further indicate that architecture and model lineage can outweigh nominal parameter count. These findings show that model size alone is an insufficient selection criterion for OL and provide empirical guidance for reproducible LLM-assisted ontology engineering.

</details>


### [207] [OntoAligner-Ensemble: Voting-Based Fusion across Heterogeneous Ontology Alignment Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31137)
*Hamed Babaei Giglou,Sören Auer,Peio Popov,Mahsa Sanaei,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OntoAligner-Ensemble框架，通过两阶段集成方法整合不同范式的本体对齐器，在多个基准测试中取得了比单一对齐器更优的性能，并提供了不同场景下的集成策略选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体对齐领域已经发展出词法、结构、知识图谱嵌入(KGE)和大语言模型(LLM)等多种方法范式，虽然现代OA框架可以部署这些异构对齐器，但如何系统地调和它们之间互补甚至冲突的预测结果仍然缺乏有效机制。

Method: 提出了OntoAligner-Ensemble，一个模块化的对齐器无关框架，采用可配置的两阶段集成过程：首先通过投票融合策略整合候选对应关系，然后应用后融合选择策略。该框架支持OntoAligner中任何产生候选对应关系的对齐器，实现了不同对齐范式在统一决策过程中的集成。实验中使用了轻量级字符串对齐器、KGE模型和检索增强生成对齐器（基于开源和API LLM）。

Result: 在5个OAEI赛道共8个基准任务上的评估表明：集成融合持续改善精确率和召回率的平衡，在多个领域经常超越独立对齐器。异构跨范式集成通常提高精确率，而同构LLM集成更常实现更高的F1分数。集成组成直接影响精确率-召回率的权衡。

Conclusion: 系统化集成学习为本体对齐提供了一种稳健且可重现的策略，同时为不同对齐场景下选择集成组成提供了实用指导，表明集成方法在OA领域具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: Ontology alignment (OA) has evolved through several methodological paradigms, ranging from lexical and structural aligners to knowledge graph embedding (KGE) models and, more recently, Large Language Model (LLM)-based approaches. Although modern OA frameworks provide unified ecosystems for deploying these heterogeneous aligners, mechanisms for systematically reconciling their complementary and sometimes conflicting predictions remain relatively underexplored. We present OntoAligner-Ensemble, a modular and aligner-agnostic framework that combines candidate correspondences through a configurable two-stage process comprising voting-based fusion strategies followed by post-fusion selection policies. The framework supports any aligner implemented within OntoAligner that produces candidate correspondences, enabling diverse alignment paradigms to be integrated through a unified decision process. To demonstrate its effectiveness, we instantiate the framework using representative lightweight string-aligner, KGE-based, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation aligners powered by both open-weight and API-based LLMs. We evaluate individual aligners and ensemble configurations across eight benchmark tasks from five OAEI tracks spanning biomedical to beyond-equivalence. The results show that ensemble fusion consistently improves the balance between precision and recall and frequently outperforms standalone aligners across diverse domains. Furthermore, our analysis reveals that ensemble composition directly affects the precision-recall trade-off: heterogeneous cross-paradigm ensembles generally improve precision, whereas homogeneous LLM ensembles more often achieve higher overall F1-scores. These findings demonstrate that systematic ensemble learning offers a robust and reproducible strategy for OA while providing practical guidance for selecting ensemble compositions under different alignment scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [ERR+: Sequential Entropy Resolution for Efficient and Decisive LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28771)
*Xin Jiang,Minhao Wang,Wen Wu,Zhentao Xie,Shangheng Du,Jinxin Shi,Jiabao Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERR+框架，通过奖励思维阶段的熵值下降来优化推理质量，而非仅依赖最终正确性，在提升准确率的同时实现更简洁的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法仅关注推理结果的正确性，缺乏对推理过程质量的指导，导致内部推理结构未被充分优化。作者发现正确推理在思维阶段呈现出更频繁且更大的token级熵值下降模式，这为优化推理过程提供了新思路。

Method: 提出两阶段RLVR框架ERR+：第一阶段使用熵释放奖励（ERR），该奖励与思维阶段累积token级熵值下降成正比，并经回答长度对数归一化，鼓励不确定性消解而非压制熵；第二阶段引入鲁棒相对效率奖励，通过tanh变换的组内z分数评估回答长度的相对效率。理论分析表明两目标联合优化会在训练早期产生梯度冲突，因此采用顺序设计。

Result: 在五个数据集上的实验表明，ERR+在多种模型骨干上均实现了准确率和回答简洁性的持续提升。理论分析验证了顺序训练设计的必要性。

Conclusion: 基于推理过程中熵动态特性的奖励机制能有效指导模型优化推理结构，为RLVR方法提供了超越正确性信号的过程质量监督新范式。

Abstract: Large reasoning models achieve strong performance on complex tasks by generating extended chain-of-thought (CoT) traces via reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). While current RLVR methods have achieved strong results with correctness-based reward signals, they provide limited guidance on the quality of the reasoning process itself, leaving the internal reasoning structure largely unoptimized. Through empirical analysis across multiple model families, we identify a consistent pattern: correct reasoning trac es exhibit more frequent and larger token-level entropy drops within the thinking phase than incorrect ones. We propose ERR+, a two-phase RLVR framework grounded in this observation. The first phase trains with the Entropy Relief Reward (ERR), a bonus proportional to cumulative token-level entropy drops in the thinking phase, log-normalized by response length. Unlike prior methods that suppress entropy, ERR rewards the resolution of uncertainty while leaving exploratory high-entropy states unconstrained. The second phase introduces the Robust Relative Efficiency Reward, which scores each response's length against co-generated peers via a $\tanh$-transformed within-group $z$-score. We provide a formal analysis showing that joint optimization of the two objectives induces gradient conflict in early training, motivating the sequential design . Experiments on five datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in both accuracy and response conciseness across model backbones. Our code is available at https://github.com/XrkArul/err_response

</details>


### [209] [Unsupervised Latent Space Alignment with Hyperspherical Geodesic Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28840)
*Cameron Ryan,Vivek Sivaraman Narayanaswamy,Kowshik Thopalli,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HGA方法，利用潜在空间的几何特征而非配对数据实现空间对齐，在无监督和弱监督场景下达到监督方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练的神经网络对相同数据编码产生相似的潜在几何结构，但这些结构间存在变换差异。现有对齐方法依赖共享样本对应关系（锚点），引发核心问题：仅凭潜在空间的几何特征能否恢复空间对齐？

Method: 提出HGA（超球面高斯对齐）方法，通过最大化两个潜在空间之间的几何"拟合"度量，直接优化空间变换。该方法由潜在空间几何驱动而非配对数据驱动，适用于无监督和弱监督两种模式。

Result: 在模型拼接和多语言词嵌入对应关系恢复等任务上，HGA在极少或无需监督的情况下达到监督方法的性能水平。

Conclusion: 潜在空间的几何特征确实足以实现空间对齐，HGA成功验证了几何驱动的对齐范式，为无监督空间对齐提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Independently trained neural networks tend to encode the same data with similar latent geometries. These latent geometries are not directly compatible, yet they can be nearly the same up to some class of transformations. While there exists many methods for alignment between different latent spaces, it is typically done using a set of shared sample correspondences, known as anchors. This leaves a fundamental question: are the geometric signatures of different latent spaces representing similar data sufficient to recover an alignment between them? To that end, we introduce HGA (Hyperspherical Gaussian Alignment), a method that directly optimizes a transformation between two latent spaces by maximizing a geometric measure of "fit" between them. Since it is driven by the geometry of the latent spaces rather than paired data, HGA can operate in both an unsupervised and weakly supervised regime. On tasks such as model stitching or multilingual word embedding correspondence recovery, HGA manages to match supervised results with minimal or no supervision.

</details>


### [210] [MedCache: Efficient and Temporally Valid Memory for Longitudinal Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29528)
*Hei Ting,Chan,Chenwei Wu,Xueshen Liu,Boyuan Zheng,Liyue Shen,Jiasi Chen,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MedCache框架，通过系统研究记忆设计选择（筛选、组织、检索和记忆增强推理），解决了纵向临床智能体的记忆设计问题，在多就诊、多专科患者记录基准测试中显著提升了推理准确性和记忆效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床智能体需要维护从跨就诊、时间点和专科分布的证据中演化的患者状态，但目前尚不清楚如何为此场景设计智能体记忆。

Method: 构建多就诊、多专科患者记录基准测试，系统研究四个记忆设计选择：筛选、组织、检索和记忆增强推理，并基于研究发现提出MedCache混合框架，构建时间有效的患者记忆，将证据组织到重叠的专科视图中，将每个查询路由到相关记忆，并自适应地调用一个或多个专科智能体。

Result: 时间有效性比单纯保留更多历史更重要；专科分解记忆减少上下文但可能隐藏共享证据；多智能体仅在专科需要共同推理时有帮助，而非证据来自多个记忆时。MedCache在强单智能体和多智能体基线上提升了推理准确性和记忆效率，并在不同模型骨干和外部数据集上泛化良好。

Conclusion: MedCache通过时间有效的记忆构建、重叠专科视图组织、查询路由和自适应专科调用，成功解决了纵向临床智能体的记忆设计问题，实现了更好的推理性能和记忆效率。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical agents must maintain an evolving patient state from evidence distributed across visits, time points, and specialties. However, how agent memory should be designed for this setting remains unclear. We introduce a benchmark of multi-visit, multi-specialty patient records that evaluates long-context evidence retrieval, cross-time evidence aggregation, and cross-specialty clinical reasoning. Using this benchmark, we systematically study four memory design choices: curation, organization, retrieval, and memory-augmented reasoning. We find that temporal validity is more important than simply retaining more history; specialty-factorized memory reduces context but can hide shared evidence; and multiple agents help when specialists must reason together, not merely when evidence comes from multiple memories. Guided by these findings, we propose \textit{MedCache}, a hybrid framework that constructs temporally valid patient memory, organizes evidence into overlapping specialty views, routes each query to relevant memories, and adaptively invokes one or multiple specialists. Experiments show that MedCache improves reasoning accuracy and memory efficiency over strong single-agent and multi-agent baselines, while generalizing across model backbones and external datasets.

</details>


### [211] [Curvature Cryptanalysis of Smooth Transformer Feed-Forward Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28843)
*Munawar Hasan,Apostol Vassilev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了平滑两层前馈网络（FFN）在黑盒访问下存在结构泄露通道，通过二阶Hessian信息可以在仅16个投影Hessian（8193次查询）的情况下高精度恢复隐藏的FFN参数方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型提取攻击主要关注行为保真度，但无法揭示内部参数几何结构。本文旨在探索黑盒设置下是否存在能够恢复模型内部结构的信息泄露通道，特别是针对Transformer中的FFN模块。

Method: 提出利用投影输入Hessian的二阶泄露通道，其中Hessian形成FFN输入权重诱导的对称秩一因子的不同混合。形式化Hessian收集为部分对称分解问题，建立局部可识别性条件，并利用向量输出模板重用技术将查询成本降低16倍。在GELU和SiLU激活的Transformer FFN上进行验证。

Result: 在CIFAR-10视觉Transformer上，仅16个投影Hessian（8193次查询）即可恢复隐藏FFN方向，平均余弦对齐度超过0.94（GELU 95.1%和SiLU 91.9%的方向超过0.90）。固定恢复方向并拟合剩余参数，获得93%以上的top-1一致性，测试准确率差距在0.90%和0.62%以内。输出舍入和高斯噪声下通过调整有限差分步长可恢复对齐度至0.9603和0.9398。

Conclusion: 在所选输入原始输出预言机模型下，平滑FFN曲率暴露了行为保真度无法揭示的内部参数几何结构，建立了从黑盒二阶观察到隐藏FFN结构恢复和功能替换的端到端路径，证明了模型结构泄露的可行性和潜在安全风险。

Abstract: We show that smooth two-layer feed-forward networks (FFNs) expose an additional structural model extraction channel under a chosen-input raw-output oracle at the FFN branch; consider transformer FFN branches with GELU or SiLU activations under chosen-input raw-output access, without access to parameters, gradients, or internal activations; exploit a second-order leakage channel in which projected input Hessians form different mixtures of the same hidden symmetric rank-one factors induced by the FFN input weights. We formalize resulting Hessian collection as a partially symmetric decomposition to establish conditions for local identifiability and stability to exploit vector-output stencil reuse to reduce the structural query cost by a factor of 16. On independently trained CIFAR-10 vision transformers, only 16 projected Hessians, corresponding to 8193 black-box queries, recover the hidden FFN directions with average absolute cosine alignment above 0.94, with 95.1 % of GELU and 91.9 % of SiLU directions exceeding 0.90 alignment. Recovery remains high across independently trained models, repeated extraction runs, and all transformer blocks. The recovered structure supports functional extraction too. Keeping the recovered directions fixed and fitting only the remaining FFN parameters yields high-fidelity substitutes with more than 93 % top-1 agreement, while test accuracy remains within 0.90% and 0.62% of the GELU and SiLU targets. Output rounding and Gaussian noise substantially reduce recovery under a fixed attack configuration, but adapting the finite-difference step restores average alignment to 0.9603 and 0.9398. This is an end-to-end path from black-box second-order observations to hidden FFN-structure recovery and functional replacement. Under the stated oracle model, smooth FFN curvature exposes internal parameter geometry that behavioral fidelity alone cannot reveal.

</details>


### [212] [Equivariant Sheaf Neural Networks: Learning Geometric Transport on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28853)
*Alessio Borgi,Mario Severino,Fabrizio Silvestri,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ESNN（等变层神经网络），这是一种新型等变图神经网络架构，通过学习有向、矩阵值传输来丰富向量特征在图上的转换，同时保持精确的欧几里得等变性。该方法在保持一阶标量和向量特征的同时，将额外的几何灵活性置于边缘传输本身，无需高阶表示即可实现更强的表达能力，在粒子动力学、网格模拟、点云分类和分子属性预测等多个任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变图神经网络为几何系统建模提供了原理性方法，但现有高效的一阶架构在向量信息跨越图移动时如何转换方面存在局限性。传统的提升表示阶数的方法计算成本较高，因此需要一种既能保持等变性又能增强几何灵活性的替代方案。本文旨在通过学习几何信息在边上的传输方式来提供一种补充路径，在不要求高阶表示的情况下实现表达性强的等变消息传递。

Method: ESNN的核心方法包括：(1)引入等变层结构，学习相邻向量特征之间的有向、矩阵值传输，同时保持精确的欧几里得等变性；(2)保持标量和向量特征为一阶表示，将额外的几何灵活性置于边缘传输本身；(3)理论表征传输机制，证明当相对位移是唯一的协变几何输入时，每个线性O(n)等变映射分解为独立的径向和切向分量；(4)引入受控对称性松弛机制，处理具有优先环境方向的系统，该方向可以预先指定或从数据中推断；(5)在方向通路非活动时恢复完整的E(n)等变性。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)在粒子动力学任务中改善了动力学预测能力；(2)在对称性破缺时能够恢复重力轴；(3)在选定的网格任务和长时域推演中获得了显著收益；(4)对未见过的旋转保持鲁棒性；(5)在分子属性预测和点云分类任务上表现良好。这些结果验证了学习几何信息如何在边上传输确实提供了通往表达性等变消息传递的补充路径。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了几何信息在边上的传输学习为表达性等变消息传递提供了一种有效且互补的路径，无需依赖高阶表示。通过将几何灵活性置于边缘传输本身，ESNN成功地在保持计算效率和精确等变性的同时，显著提升了模型在多种几何任务上的表现。这为几何深度学习领域提供了一个新的设计范式，即通过丰富边缘传输机制而非提升表示阶数来增强模型能力。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks provide a principled way to model geometric systems, but efficient first-order architectures remain limited in how vector information can be transformed as it moves across a graph. We introduce \textsc{ESNN}, an Equivariant Sheaf Neural Network that enriches this interaction by learning directed, matrix-valued transport between neighboring vector features while preserving exact Euclidean equivariance. Rather than increasing the order of the representation, ESNN keeps scalar and vector features first-order and places the additional geometric flexibility in the edge transport itself. We characterize this transport theoretically, showing that when relative displacement is the only covariant geometric input, every linear $O(n)$-equivariant map decomposes into independent radial and tangential components, while learned covariant features enable richer feature-conditioned transformations. We also introduce controlled symmetry relaxation for systems with a preferred ambient direction, which may be prescribed or inferred from data while recovering full $E(n)$-equivariance when the directional pathway is inactive. Across particle dynamics, mesh-based simulation, point-cloud classification, and molecular property prediction, ESNN improves dynamics prediction, recovers the gravity axis when symmetry is broken, yields substantial gains on selected mesh tasks and long-horizon rollouts, and remains robust to unseen rotations. These results show that learning how geometric information is transported across edges offers a complementary route to expressive equivariant message passing without requiring higher-order representations.

</details>


### [213] [The Halt Vector: Internalizing a Causal Steering Intervention for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28859)
*Dylan Jayabahu,Tinuade Adeleke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在模型权重中内置一个"停止向量"，成功移除了推理模型中约25%的不必要思考过程，同时保持准确性不变，并解决了随难度增长的非终止问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B）在已知答案后仍继续思考，其思维链长度约为答案概率稳定所需时间的两倍。由于这种过度思考的程度因问题而异，全局长度惩罚无法有效移除，因此需要一种更精准的控制方法。

Method: 利用因果可解释性发现，在第18层识别出一个"停止向量"（差异均值方向），通过重构整个被引导的激活并固定非目标维度到其自然值（避免破坏下游依赖），将该干预内置到模型权重中。仅在24个问题上拟合，无需强化学习。

Result: 该方法移除了约25%的思考过程，在五个未见过的基准测试上保持准确性不变；剪切量与每个问题自身的可移除余量相关（相关系数0.70）；同时解决了随难度增长的非终止问题，而置信度钩子反而会加剧该问题。

Conclusion: 本研究不声称在原始权衡上优于良好调优的长度惩罚或解码时早退策略，其核心贡献在于展示了如何通过将因果可解释性发现内置到权重中来获得停止机制，为模型行为控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reasoning models do not stop when they know the answer. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B the chain of thought runs about twice as long as the model's own answer probability takes to settle, and how much of that excess is removable varies from problem to problem, so a global length penalty cannot take it out. We take it out by internalizing a causal interpretability finding into the weights. The mechanism is a halt vector: a difference-of-means direction at layer 18 of this model whose steering strength controls how long it thinks, while a replicated value axis does nothing. Installing that intervention in the weights is harder than it looks. Maximizing the scalar projection onto the direction corrupts the off-axis dimensions a frozen downstream reader depends on, and generation gets longer instead of shorter; what works is reconstructing the whole steered activation with those dimensions pinned to their natural values. Fit from 24 problems and no reinforcement learning, the halt removes about a quarter of the thinking at held accuracy across five unseen benchmarks, and the cut tracks each problem's own removable slack at 0.70. It also closes a non-termination pathology that grows with difficulty and that a decoding-time confidence hook makes worse. We do not claim to beat a well-tuned length penalty or decoding-time early exit on the raw trade-off; the contribution is how the halt is obtained.

</details>


### [214] [Conservative Hybrid Graph Networks for Process Systems with Learned Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28896)
*Paolo Guida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出保守混合图网络(CHGN)学习工业过程网络的潜在运行机制(路由、工况分配)，将数据驱动代理模型嵌入固定传输方程，保证质量守恒，实现跨拓扑零样本迁移且性能显著优于GNN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程网络运行时拓扑动态变化(节流、旁路、工况切换)，现有模型仅基于测量状态训练而忽略潜在运行机制，无约束图网络虽能拟合轨迹但无法赋予恢复的路由以稳定的物理意义。

Method: 提出保守混合图网络(CHGN)，将路由、工况分配和移除率作为数据驱动代理学习，并将其嵌入固定传输方程，从而在构建上保证质量守恒；模型学习潜在运行机制而非仅拟合状态轨迹。

Result: 在10-20节点网络训练后，CHGN零样本迁移至25-40节点未见拓扑，RMSE达2.1e-3（GNN基线6e-2至9e-2），门控MAE为7.9e-3，工况准确率94.3%；在流体混合中试装置上改进持久性基线，但无法预测未观测阀门动作导致的人工干预。

Conclusion: CHGN实现了跨过程拓扑的无重训练迁移，揭示了支配工厂行为的潜在机制并使其可被检验，为工业过程网络建模提供了物理约束与可解释性。

Abstract: Industrial process networks do not maintain a single effective topology while operating: streams are throttled or bypassed, and units move between idle, transition, and active regimes. Models of such systems are typically trained on measured state trajectories while the operating mechanisms that generated them remain latent, and an unconstrained graph network can fit such a trajectory without assigning stable physical meaning to the recovered routing. We address both problems with the Conservative Hybrid Graph Network (CHGN), which learns routing, regime assignment, and removal rates as data-driven surrogates and inserts them into a fixed transport equation, so that the mass balance holds by construction for any predicted routing. CHGN trained on networks of 10-20 nodes transfers zero-shot to unseen graphs of 25-40 nodes without retraining, reaching an RMSE of 2.1e-3 against 6e-2 to 9e-2 for GNN baselines under the same protocol, with a gate MAE of 7.9e-3 and regime accuracy of 94.3% (1.2e-2 and 96.4% respectively on the fixed training topology). On a fluid-mixing pilot plant, CHGN improves on a persistence baseline for held-out physical faults but does not predict manual interventions, for which the governing valve actions are unobserved. The model therefore transfers across process topologies without retraining and exposes the latent mechanisms governing plant behaviour to inspection.

</details>


### [215] [Fully Distributed GNE Algorithms for Multi-Robot Placement without Consensus on Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29388)
*Shao-An Yin,Mingyi Hong,Nicola Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需交换拉格朗日乘子的全分布式连续时间算法，用于求解共享线性等式约束下的广义纳什均衡问题，降低了通信开销并提升了隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解强单调博弈中基于共识的变分广义纳什均衡（v-GNEs）的方法需要交换拉格朗日乘子，导致通信开销大且存在隐私泄露风险，因此亟需一种无需乘子交换的分布式方法。

Method: 针对共享线性等式约束的广义纳什均衡问题（GNEPs），设计了无需拉格朗日乘子交换的全分布式连续时间算法，并提供了相应的离散时间实施方案。

Result: 所提出的连续时间算法能够收敛至任意广义纳什均衡点，在多机器人定位任务中得到了有效验证，实现了降低通信开销和增强隐私保护的目标。

Conclusion: 该研究为共享约束博弈问题提供了一种通信高效且隐私友好的分布式求解方案，突破了现有方法对乘子交换的依赖，拓展了GNEP求解方法的应用潜力。

Abstract: Recent machine learning research has increasingly focused on equilibrium analysis in non-cooperative games rather than solely on optimal solutions. Many such problems involve shared constraints and can be formulated as Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems (GNEPs). For strongly monotone games, existing methods compute consensus-based variational GNEs (v-GNEs) by exchanging Lagrange multipliers. We propose a fully distributed continuous-time algorithm for shared linear equality constraints that converges without multiplier exchange and reaches any GNE, reducing communication overhead and improving privacy. Discrete-time schemes are also provided, and the method is validated on a multi-robot placement task.

</details>


### [216] [Off-Policy Evaluation for Semantic ID Recommenders: Does the Model's Own Code Hierarchy Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28905)
*Artem Betlei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出利用生成式推荐器自身的语义ID树结构作为离线策略评估(OPE)的动作抽象机制，通过将细粒度商品边际化为代码前缀聚类来恢复可估计的支撑集并降低误差，解决了生产环境下商品级有效样本量不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器越来越多地输出语义ID（SID），在开展稀缺的A/B测试之前，团队需要通过离线策略评估（OPE）决定哪些解码器或重排序变体值得测试。核心动机是探索能否利用模型自身的SID树结构作为OPE的动作抽象，以克服生产日志中商品级有效样本量不足导致的评估困难。

Method: 提出了基于SID树的粗粒化方法：(1) 在推荐器常用的近极大值日志条件下，商品级OPE不可行，但将商品边际化为代码前缀聚类可恢复可估计支撑集；(2) 利用SID树的层次结构，解码器能精确且廉价地返回每个聚类质量，而平面聚类需要枚举商品/叶子质量；(3) 分辨深度作为操作旋钮，在支撑稀缺时采用更粗粒化，并通过条件偏差界限将粗粒化偏差与量化器的最坏情况重构残差及目标-日志散度联系起来。

Result: 证明了三个关键结果：(1) SID树结构使得粗粒化在生成系统中可行，每个聚类的质量可由解码器精确且高效地返回；(2) 性能提升源于粗粒化本身而非层次结构特有，但SID树是实现粗粒化的关键基础设施；(3) 分辨深度是有效控制参数，粗粒化偏差与量化器重构残差和目标-日志分布散度存在理论界限。

Conclusion: 该研究为生成式推荐系统的离线评估提供了理论指导：在支撑稀缺场景下，通过SID树结构的粗粒化方法可以有效平衡估计误差与偏差，为A/B测试决策提供了可靠的离线评估工具，同时建立了偏差-误差权衡的理论基础。

Abstract: Generative recommenders increasingly emit semantic IDs (SIDs): each item is a short sequence of hierarchical discrete codes from a residual quantizer, decoded autoregressively. Before spending scarce A/B-test, a team may decide offline which decoder or reranking variants are worth testing - a job for off-policy evaluation (OPE). We ask a simple question: can the model's own SID tree serve as the action abstraction for that OPE? Our answer has three parts. (i) Under the near-argmax logging real recommenders use, per-item OPE is hopeless - as item-level effective sample size is usually small on production logs - but marginalizing items to code-prefix clusters restores estimable support and cuts error. (ii) This gain is thanks to coarsening, not to the hierarchy specifically; but the SID tree is what makes coarsening feasible in a generative system - each cluster's mass is exactly and cheaply returned by the decoder, whereas flat clustering requires enumerating item/leaf masses that a code-only decoder does not directly expose. (iii) Resolution depth is the operative knob - coarser under scarce support - and a conditional bias bound links the coarsening bias to the quantizer's worst-case reconstruction residual and the target-logging divergence.

</details>


### [217] [Learning-Theoretic Foundation for General Coded Computing: The Straggler Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28910)
*Parsa Moradi,Behrooz Tahmasebi,Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了通用编码计算（GCC）框架，通过学习理论视角和RKHS方法，放宽了传统编码计算对精确恢复和严格恢复阈值的依赖，为现代机器学习工作负载提供了更灵活且具有理论保证的近似计算方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码计算方案主要针对具有高度代数结构的计算（如多项式求值、矩阵乘法）设计，依赖严格的恢复阈值和精确恢复假设，这严重限制了其在现代机器学习工作负载（特别是深度神经网络）中的适用性，因为DNN计算通常缺乏刚性代数结构且仅需近似恢复。

Method: 从学习理论角度重新审视编码计算，提出通用编码计算（GCC）框架。采用端到端均方误差损失直接度量期望计算与恢复估计之间的差异，将编码器和解码器限制在具有温和平滑性约束的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中，导出编码器和解码器可表示为RKHS核函数的线性组合，从而实现高效的系数计算。

Result: 在两种互补的straggler场景下建立了理论性能保证：（1）最坏情况：在N个工作节点、最多S个straggler的设置下，端到端损失至少以O(S³N⁻³)速率衰减；（2）概率情况：每个工作节点以概率p独立straggling时，期望损失仍能以O(log_{1/p}³(N)N⁻³)速率收敛。

Conclusion: GCC框架成功将编码计算从代数方法拓展到学习理论方法，在保持理论性能保证的同时放宽了对精确恢复和严格代数结构的依赖，为现代机器学习应用提供了更通用的编码计算解决方案。

Abstract: Coded computing has emerged as a powerful paradigm for mitigating the impact of straggling workers in distributed computing systems. However, existing coded-computing schemes are predominantly designed for the exact recovery of highly structured computations, such as polynomial evaluation and matrix multiplication, and typically rely on strict recovery thresholds. These assumptions significantly limit their applicability to modern machine-learning workloads, particularly deep neural networks (DNNs), whose computations generally lack rigid algebraic structure and, in many applications, require only accurate approximations rather than exact recovery.
  To address this gap, we revisit coded computing from a learning-theoretic perspective and introduce General Coded Computing (GCC). Rather than adopting existing algebraic tools, GCC formulates coded computing through a natural end-to-end mean-squared error loss that directly measures the discrepancy between the desired computations and their recovered estimates. By deriving suitable upper bounds and restricting the encoder and decoder to a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) with mild smoothness constraints, we show that both the encoder and decoder admit specific representations as linear combinations of RKHS kernel functions. This representation allows the corresponding coefficients to be computed efficiently. Moreover, this framework enables us to establish theoretical performance guarantees for GCC under two complementary straggler regimes. In the worst-case setting with $N$ worker nodes, and at most $S$ stragglers, we show that the end-to-end loss decays at least at rate $O(S^3N^{-3})$ for standard configurations. We then study a probabilistic setting in which each worker independently straggles with probability $p$. We prove that the expected loss can still converge at rate $O(\log_{1/p}^3(N)N^{-3})$.

</details>


### [218] [Asynchronous Cooperative Online Learning for Multi-Robot Control under Computational Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29562)
*Xiaobing Dai,Zewen Yang,Wei Ren,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体系统在不确定环境下的安全运行问题，本文提出了一种考虑预测精度、查询点差异和延迟影响的异步协作学习策略，并设计了基于伴随系统的分布式控制律，在无人水面艇仿真中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在不确定环境下（存在外部干扰和模型不确定性）的安全运行至关重要。现有分布式高斯过程协作学习方法忽略了因智能体计算能力和预测任务差异导致的计算延迟异构性和查询点差异问题。

Method: 提出异步协作学习策略，显式考虑预测精度、查询点差异和延迟效应；开发了基于伴随多智能体系统的分布式控制律以确保期望的控制性能。

Result: 在无人水面艇仿真中验证了所提方法的有效性，相比现有最优方法，学习和控制性能均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的异步协作学习策略有效解决了现有分布式高斯过程方法中被忽略的计算延迟和查询点差异问题，为多智能体系统在不确定环境下的安全运行提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring the safe operation of multi-agent systems (MASs) under uncertain environments is crucial for cooperative robotic, where external disturbances and inaccurate dynamic models can significantly compromise performance and reliability. To address this challenge, calibrated machine learning models, particularly Gaussian process (GP) regression, are extensively employed due to their interpretable performance quantification. As the interconnected communication of MASs facilitates cooperative learning, agents are able to enhance learning performance by exchanging local GP inferences with their neighbors and aggregating the received information via distributed GP strategies. However, variations in computational power and prediction tasks among agents inevitably lead to heterogeneous computational delays and differences in query points, which are often overlooked in existing aggregation methods. To overcome these limitations, this work proposes an asynchronous cooperative learning strategy that explicitly accounts for prediction accuracy, query point variations and delay effects. Additionally, a distributed control law based on an adjoint MAS is developed to ensure the desired control performance. Simulations on unmanned surface vehicles validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, demonstrating substantial improvements in both learning and control performance compared to the state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [219] [SemKV: Semantic Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization Guided by the Quality Cliff for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28911)
*Daeha Lee,Do-Hyung Kim,Jae-Hong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了KV缓存量化存在"质量悬崖"现象，在临界比特数附近性能骤降。基于此发现，提出SemKV方法，通过悬崖上方的精度插值实现6.0倍存储压缩，且无统计可检测的质量损失，优于FP16令牌剪枝方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是长上下文LLM推理的主要内存瓶颈，随上下文长度线性增长。现有均匀量化方法存在性能突变问题，缺乏对量化临界点的系统性研究，亟需找到无损压缩的最佳操作点。

Method: 1) 通过多种子统计协议识别量化"质量悬崖"位置；2) 提出SemKV混合精度方法：保留所有token，按模型内部重要性评分排序，分配悬崖上方两种相邻精度；3) 测试畸变优化量化器(TurboQuant-MSE)以提升基线性能。

Result: Llama-3.1-8B在2.322比特以下出现质量悬崖，SemKV实现6.0倍存储压缩且无统计可检测质量差异(n=900)，在1.5倍内存预算下优于FP16令牌剪枝；TurboQuant-MSE将无损操作点提升至7.9倍压缩；悬崖现象在生成时量化、多轮对话及Mistral-7B中均存在。

Conclusion: KV量化存在普适的质量悬崖，应首先测量目标部署场景的悬崖位置，然后在其上方进行精度插值。该方法为长上下文LLM推理提供了可预测、可复现的压缩策略。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the dominant memory bottleneck of long-context large language model (LLM) inference, growing linearly with context length. We show that uniform KV quantization on a fractional-bit grid does not degrade gracefully: under a prespecified multi-seed statistical protocol, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with an affine quantizer is statistically indistinguishable from FP16 KV down to 2.322 code bits/value and collapses at 2.0 bits - a quality cliff in (2.0, 2.322] that reappears in generation-time quantization and multi-turn dialogue and transfers to Mistral-7B. The cliff reframes importance-aware mixed precision: above it, eight model-internal importance indicators are statistically interchangeable, so the benefit of mixing is grid interpolation, reaching average precisions uniform quantization cannot realize. SemKV preserves every token, ranks tokens by a model-internal score, and assigns two adjacent above-cliff precisions, achieving a measured 6.0x storage reduction with no statistically detectable quality difference from full KV (n=900, three seeds), and outperforming FP16 token pruning granted a 1.5x larger memory budget. Replacing the affine base with a distortion-optimized quantizer (TurboQuant-MSE) lowers the cliff in every protocol tested, raising the no-detectable-loss operating point to 7.9x. The recipe: measure the cliff for the target deployment setting, then interpolate above it.

</details>


### [220] [RankShift: In-Database Detection and Explanation of Categorical Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28922)
*Omair Shafi Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RankShift是一种部署在分析型数据库中的异常检测方法，通过比较时间窗口内的类别份额与良性参考来检测稀有类别漂移，无需模型训练且存储开销降低137倍，在HDFS上匹配自编码器性能（0.999 vs 1.000），在Thunderbird上超越自编码器（0.983 vs 0.949）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件计数监控方法无法检测稀有类别变为常见但总消息量保持稳定的情况，例如登录服务中某来源失败登录占比从2%增至30%或系统日志中稀有事件模板变常见，这类异常不改变事件总数但改变了活跃类别，需要一种新方法来检测此类类别份额变化。

Method: RankShift直接部署在存储数据的分析型数据库中，使用皮尔逊分数比较每个时间窗口的类别份额与良性参考分布，该分数的各个项能够识别导致变化的类别。单个查询即可返回分数、校准警报和最大的增长贡献，无需模型训练或推理服务。

Result: 在HDFS上匹配计数向量自编码器性能（AUROC 0.999 vs 1.000），在Thunderbird上领先（0.983 vs 0.949）。在固定容量的受控实验中检测稀有类别漂移达到0.787 AUROC（自编码器为0.771），能检测到事件计数监控无法发现的异常。在所有三个数据集上，观察到的误报率与请求的运行水平一致。

Conclusion: RankShift成功解决了稀有类别漂移检测问题，相比自编码器方法具有无需训练、部署简单、存储开销降低137倍等优势，在保持或提升检测性能的同时实现了更高效的资源利用，适用于实际生产环境的数据库内异常检测。

Abstract: A login service can receive its usual number of failed sign-ins while one source grows from 2% to 30% of them. The same pattern appears in system logs when a rare event template becomes common while the message rate stays stable. These events change which categories are active without changing how many events occur. RankShift detects such changes inside the analytical database that stores the data. It compares each window's category shares with a benign reference using a Pearson score whose terms identify the categories responsible for the change. The same query returns the score, calibrated alert, and largest increasing contributions. We evaluate RankShift on HDFS, BGL, and Thunderbird. It matches the count-vector autoencoder within 0.001 AUROC on HDFS (0.999 versus 1.000) and leads on Thunderbird (0.983 versus 0.949). In a controlled fixed-volume experiment, RankShift detects rare-category shifts that are invisible to event-count monitoring, reaching 0.787 AUROC compared with 0.771 for the autoencoder. Across all three corpora, observed false-alarm rates track the requested operating levels. RankShift requires no model training or inference service, and the autoencoders deployed state is 137x larger.

</details>


### [221] [Revisiting the Provable-Auditable Privacy Gap of DP-SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28934)
*Saloni Modi,Srivi Balaji,Yusong Zhu,Gautam Kamath,Kevin Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将经验隐私下界作为优化目标，设计了轻量级防御框架来提升机器学习算法的实际隐私保护性能，且在增强DP-SGD时不增加理论隐私损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统差分隐私研究关注理论隐私上界，但现代私有机器学习中效用与理论隐私的权衡困难。隐私审计研究揭示了DP-SGD的理论隐私边界实际上相当紧致，表明现有理论分析可能不够宽松，因此需要新的方法来改善实际隐私保护。

Method: 提出将算法的经验隐私下界作为具体优化指标，构建了轻量级防御框架，可通用性地增强机器学习流水线中的优化方法。该框架在增强DP-SGD时不会产生额外的理论隐私成本，这与之前针对成员推断攻击的防御方法不同。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，所提出的框架显著改善了经验隐私性能。在多种审计构造、模型和数据集上的广泛评估证明了该方法的灵活性和有效性，且不会牺牲理论隐私保证。

Conclusion: 经验隐私下界可作为理论隐私上界的有益补充指标。所提出的防御框架为改善私有机器学习的实际隐私保护提供了实用且理论可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) has traditionally been used to provide theoretical upper bounds on an algorithm's stability to changing its training data. In modern private machine learning applications, achieving strong tradeoffs between utility and theoretical privacy is challenging, and thus one may optimistically hope that existing theoretical privacy analyses are loose. Recent work on privacy auditing has adopted a dual viewpoint, instead lower bounding the true privacy of an algorithm by constructing empirical distinguishing events. The auditing literature has thus far yielded a pessimistic outlook on the looseness of theoretical privacy bounds for DP-SGD, the de facto private training method in modern ML, as nearly-matching empirical lower bounds have been achieved under various threat models [NHSBTJCT23, AC24, CBP25].
  In this work, we propose the empirical privacy lower bound of an algorithm as a concrete metric to optimize for, complementary to the theoretical upper bound. We give a lightweight defense framework that generically augments optimization methods in the ML pipeline to have significantly-improved empirical privacy on standard benchmarks. Moreover, we show that our framework comes at no theoretical privacy cost when augmenting DP-SGD, unlike previously-proposed defenses against membership inference attacks. We evaluate our defense against a broad range of audit constructions, models, and datasets to demonstrate its flexibility.

</details>


### [222] [From the Loss Landscape to Diverse Feature Learning in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28948)
*David Aram Yunis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究神经网络损失景观中的模式连接性现象，通过理解和利用这一特殊结构来解释神经网络的优化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在自动驾驶、建筑、法律、招聘和健康等关键领域的广泛应用带来了意想不到的后果，但对其如何实现解决方案的理解仍然有限。理解神经网络需要理解其优化过程，而模式连接性这一现象目前无法解释，这构成了神经网络可解释性的重要缺口。

Method: 通过研究神经网络损失景观中的模式连接性现象（即能够在损失表面上连接神经网络的能力），深入分析神经网络优化过程中的特殊结构，从训练过程和数据暴露的角度探究神经网络如何达到其结果。

Result: 本论文阐明、解释并利用了损失景观中的这一特殊结构，为理解神经网络优化过程提供了新的见解和工具。

Conclusion: 通过研究较小规模神经网络中存在的失败迹象，可以在更易处理的设置下研究神经网络问题；理解神经网络的关键在于理解其优化过程，而模式连接性为神经网络可解释性研究提供了重要突破点。

Abstract: Over the course of the last decade, neural networks have grown from an academic curiosity to moving the markets of nations. Despite this explosion in both research and deployment, relatively little is understood about how they achieve the solutions they do. This is both scientifically relevant, and pressing for society. When neural networks make decisions across self-driving, construction, law, hiring and health, there have been and will continue to be unintended consequences.
  However, attempting to generalize the failures of the largest and most important production systems makes for a very difficult task. Yet signs of these failures exist at all scales of neural networks, so we should be able to study a much more tractable setting. All neural networks must undergo an optimization process, called training, to be useful. To a great degree, understanding neural networks is understanding their optimization: through what process and exposure to which data did they arrive at their results. Yet our knowledge on this topic as a field is quite imprecise. In particular, a curious phenomenon called mode connectivity, the ability to connect neural networks in the loss surface, defies explanation entirely.
  This dissertation elucidates, explains and exploits this special structure in the loss landscape...

</details>


### [223] [Continuity-Free Near-Minimax Leading-Order Regret for CVaR-UCBVI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28960)
*Yuanlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI算法在无连续性假设下可达更优的$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAK/τ})$遗憾率，且该算法在主导项上具有极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限时域表格型CVaR强化学习研究中，对于任意归一化回报律，已证明的遗憾上界为$\widetilde{O}(τ^{-1}\sqrt{SAK})$，而在密度下界条件下可达更优的$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAK/τ})$率。本文旨在证明在无连续性假设下，相同的Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI算法即可达到更优的遗憾率。

Method: 核心方法为建立selected-budget self-bound：证明回合短缺的条件方差最多为$τ$加上价值估计宽度。将该自界代入原始Bernstein分解中，从而获得改进的遗憾上界。

Result: 对于任意归一化回报律（包括原子型、混合型和连续型），算法以高概率达到$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAK/τ}+(SAHK^{1/4}+S^2AH)/τ)$遗憾。主导项$τ^{-1/2}$在对数因子范围内匹配期望遗憾的极小极大下界。

Conclusion: Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI算法在完整回报律类别的主导项阶上具有极小极大最优性；低阶项保留其$τ^{-1}$依赖性。该结果将更优遗憾率的适用范围从需要密度下界条件扩展至无连续性假设的一般情况。

Abstract: For finite-horizon tabular CVaR reinforcement learning, prior work proves a $\widetilde{O}(τ^{-1}\sqrt{SAK})$ leading regret bound for arbitrary normalized return laws and the sharper $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAK/τ})$ rate under a density lower bound. We show that the same Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI algorithm attains the sharper rate without continuity assumptions. The key is a selected-budget self-bound: the conditional variance of the episode shortfall is at most $τ$ plus the value-estimation width. Substitution into the original Bernstein decomposition yields, with high probability, $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAK/τ}+(SAHK^{1/4}+S^2AH)/τ)$ regret for arbitrary normalized return laws, including atomic, mixed, and continuous laws. The $τ^{-1/2}$ leading term matches the expected-regret minimax lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Thus Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI is minimax-optimal over the full return-law class in the leading-order regime; the lower-order terms retain their $τ^{-1}$ dependence.

</details>


### [224] [V2TATC: A Joint Voice-Trajectory Embedding Framework and Dataset for Air Traffic Controller Situational Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28981)
*Louis Brusset,Mathurin Petit,Jordan Kam,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出V2TATC框架，通过联合嵌入语音通信和飞行轨迹数据，实现空中交通管制场景下的双向跨模态检索，支持实时决策辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着国家空域系统特别是低空区域交通量的持续增长，空中交通管制员对可扩展的决策支持工具需求日益迫切，需要能够实时理解飞行轨迹数据和飞行员自然语言意图的能力。

Method: V2TATC结合了自监督轨迹编码器、冻结的大规模语音编码器、对比联合嵌入以及通过归一化流的双射提升，将语音指令和目标飞机轨迹映射到单一潜在空间中的邻近点，实现双向查询。

Result: 在旧金山湾区（集中多个主要机场且混合商业和通用航空低空交通）验证了V2TATC的有效性；发布了新的配对语音-轨迹数据集；在跨模态检索、消融实验和潜在空间分析上报告了实验结果。

Conclusion: 语音与轨迹数据并非独立，而是共享同一物理指称（飞机在空域中的飞行）；V2TATC可作为拥挤空域态势感知组件，辅助开发面向实时推理的ATC工具。

Abstract: As air traffic volumes in the National Airspace System continue to expand, in particular in the low altitude airspaces, the need for scalable decision support tools used by air traffic controllers will also require more development. This article introduces Voice-to-Trajectory for Air Traffic Control, a joint voice communication-flight trajectory data embedding framework, that can be a component of situational awareness in congested airspaces, and assist the development of tools for ATC as they reason in real-time over Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast trajectories, or the intent expressed by pilots in natural language. We show that these data modalities are not independent and represent a common physical referent: an aircraft flying through the airspace. V2TATC maps a voice instruction and the trajectory of the addressed aircraft to nearby points in a single latent space that can be queried in both directions. It combines a self-supervised trajectory encoder, a frozen large-scale speech encoder, a contrastive joint embedding, and a bijective lifting via normalizing flows. We demonstrate V2TATC's effectiveness on the San Francisco Bay Area, for its concentration of major airports, and its mix of commercial and general aviation low altitude traffic. Lastly, we release a novel paired voice-trajectory dataset, and report experiments on cross-modal retrieval, ablations, and latent-space analysis.

</details>


### [225] [Effective Graph and Rank-based Contextual Embeddings for Textual and Multimedia Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29001)
*Thiago César Castilho Almeida,Gustavo Rosseto Letício,Lucas Pascotti Valem,André Freitas,Daniel Carlos Guimarães Pedronette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRaCE是一种基于图和排序的无监督嵌入框架，通过稳健的基于排序的度量来选择代表性子集并生成可解释的嵌入，在检索、分类和聚类任务中均优于RaDE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图嵌入技术在降低图方法计算成本的同时保留了结构信息，但传统方法缺乏可解释性维度。RaDE方法引入了基于排序信息的方法，但未充分探索代表性子集选择在不同类别间的有效性，也未在分类和聚类等任务中进行充分评估。

Method: 提出GRaCE（Graph and Rank-based Contextual Embeddings）框架，这是一个完全无监督的框架。该框架利用稳健的基于排序的度量进行代表性子集选择和节点嵌入，从而生成可解释的嵌入向量。

Result: GRaCE在多种数据集（包括文本和图像集合）上超越了RaDE和原始特征。在检索、分类和聚类任务中均表现优异，甚至在分类任务中优于图卷积网络（GCN）模型，并以最先进的Transformer模型作为特征描述符进行评估。

Conclusion: GRaCE成功地将基于排序的度量与图结构相结合，实现了完全无监督的、具有可解释性的嵌入学习，在多个下游任务中取得了优于现有方法的表现，证明了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: In a data-driven world, efficiently organizing and mapping relationships between objects is crucial. Graphs are powerful tools for modeling these connections, being widely used in social networks, telecommunications, and biology. However, graph-based methods often face high computational costs, particularly in memory and space usage. To address this, graph embedding techniques, also referred to as Network Representation Learning, encode graph information into lower-dimensional representations while preserving structural aspects. Traditional methods, however, lack interpretable dimensions. RaDE (Rank Diffusion Embedding) introduces a new approach using rank-based information, with a key step being the selection of a representative subset of nodes to provide interpretability for its dimensions and improve retrieval tasks. Despite its potential, RaDE's original proposal did not fully explore the effectiveness of representative subset selection across different classes or evaluate embeddings in tasks like classification and clustering. Inspired by RaDE, this work introduces GRaCE (Graph and Rank-based Contextual Embeddings), a fully unsupervised framework that generates interpretable embeddings by leveraging robust rank-based measures for representative subset selection and node embedding. GRaCE surpasses RaDE and Original Features across diverse datasets, including textual and image collections, excelling in retrieval, classification, and clustering tasks, considering state-of-the-art Transformer models as feature descriptors and Graph Convolutional Networks models in classification tasks.

</details>


### [226] [Context-Aware Interpretable Representations for Retrieval and Graph Convolutional Network Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29004)
*Thiago César Castilho Almeida,Gustavo Rosseto Letício,Vinicius Atsushi Sato Kawai,Daniel Carlos Guimarães Pedronette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合流形学习与基于排名的可解释图嵌入的无监督框架，解决了传统方法在几何相似性评估和模型可解释性方面的不足，在保持低维度的同时提供了内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉信息建模取得显著进展，但仍存在两个关键挑战：几何鸿沟（传统成对度量无法捕获数据集流形的内在几何特性）和可解释性鸿沟（表示与人类认知缺乏对齐）。如何在保持低维度和下游任务高效性的同时提供可解释性仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出一种无监督框架，集成流形学习策略与基于排名的可解释图嵌入。首先通过流形分析刻画数据集的上下文信息，然后生成稀疏、自解释的嵌入表示。该框架采用灵活的公式化方法，允许不同的流形学习和表示学习策略组合。

Result: 在多个数据集和特征上的广泛实验表明，该方法不仅提供了内在可解释性和降维能力，还在图像检索和基于图卷积网络(GCN)的半监督分类等下游任务中保持或提升了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的上下文感知表示成功弥合了几何鸿沟和可解释性鸿沟，证明了在无监督学习框架下实现高质量、可解释且低维度表示的可行性，为透明AI系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: The advances in visual information modeling and representation during the last decades are remarkable, mainly supported by Convolutional Neural Networks, Transformer-based, and Foundation Models. Despite this progress, critical challenges regarding the nature of similarity assessment and model transparency have been neglected. A primary concern is the Geometric Gap, where traditional pairwise measures fail to capture the intrinsic geometry of the dataset manifold. Furthermore, the Interpretability Gap persists, as representations often lack alignment with human cognition. Therefore, how to provide interpretability to representations while maintaining low dimensionality and high effectiveness in downstream tasks remains an open challenge. In this paper, we propose a novel unsupervised framework that integrates Manifold Learning strategies with Rank-based Interpretable Graph Embeddings. Our approach effectively bridges these gaps by first characterizing the contextual information of the dataset through manifold analysis and subsequently generating sparse, self-explainable embeddings. The proposed approach employs a flexible formulation, allowing different Manifold Learning and Representation Learning strategies. Extensive experimental evaluation across diverse datasets and features demonstrates that our Context-Aware representations not only provide intrinsic interpretability and dimensionality reduction but also maintain or enhance effectiveness in downstream tasks, specifically in image retrieval and semi-supervised classification using Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs).

</details>


### [227] [Hybrid Semantic Context-Enhanced Ensemble Learning for Wind Power Ramp-Event Forecasting and Uncertainty-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29024)
*Momina Liaqat Ali,Muhammad Abid,Muhammad Abdullah,Aneela Zameer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合语义上下文的混合预测方法，用于提升风电爬坡事件的预测准确性。该方法通过将风电机组运行数据转换为文本并生成稠密嵌入向量，结合其他特征构建集成模型。实验表明，该方法在30分钟和60分钟预测时段上相比基准模型有统计学上的显著改进，外部验证显示误差减少具有良好的泛化能力，但改进幅度受模型和数据集影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电爬坡事件（风力涡轮机输出在短时间窗口内的突然大幅波动）难以预测，传统预测模型经常遗漏这些事件。现有方法在捕捉此类突发性、大幅度的功率变化方面存在不足，需要一种能够更好识别和预测爬坡事件的方法。

Method: 构建混合预测方法：1）将涡轮机运行数据转换为简化的文本描述；2）将文本转换为稠密嵌入向量；3）将嵌入向量与其他特征结合作为集成模型的输入。在SDWPF数据集上进行10分钟、30分钟和60分钟预测时段的测试。采用Diebold-Mariano检验和自助法置信区间进行稳健性验证，通过PCA压缩嵌入向量，并在Kaggle SCADA和NREL数据集上进行外部验证。与自回归模型、LSTM和GRU等基准模型进行对比。

Result: 语义上下文特征在多个集成模型对比中产生了微小但统计显著的改进，特别是在30分钟和60分钟预测时段效果最明显，且该改进在不同爬坡阈值定义下均成立。PCA压缩在部分长时段预测中有帮助。最佳的上下文增强集成模型在整体排名中名列前茅，但GRU模型在30分钟和60分钟时段仍保持最低的爬坡事件RMSE。外部验证证实误差减少具有跨数据集的泛化能力，但改进幅度依赖于模型和数据集的组合。预测区间在大多数测试用例中覆盖良好，但在爬坡事件期间有所减弱。

Conclusion: 语义上下文增强方法能够有效提升风电爬坡事件的预测性能，改进虽小但具有统计显著性且具备跨数据集的泛化能力。然而，在爬坡事件期间预测区间的覆盖能力下降，表明数据分布发生了局部偏移，这为未来的研究指明了改进方向。研究结果表明，虽然传统深度学习模型（如GRU）在某些场景下仍具有优势，但语义上下文融合方法为风电功率预测提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Wind power ramp events which are sudden, large swings in turbine output over short windows are difficult to estimate, and standard models often miss them. Hybrid forecasting approach is built which augments semantic context to ramp-event forecast. Rather than applying an extensive language model directly to predict turbine operating data, we have implemented a pipeline where turbine operating data is converted to simplified text, which is then converted to dense embeddings to be used as inputs for ensemble models incorporated with other features. Testing runs are performed at multiple intervals within the SDWPF dataset, including 10-minute, 30-minute, and 60- minute horizons, with ramp events constituting the highest change in future power output. We check robustness against autoregressive, LSTM, and GRU baselines plus several ensemble configurations, using Diebold-Mariano tests and bootstrap confidence intervals, and we vary the ramp threshold, compress the embeddings with PCA, and validate externally on Kaggle SCADA and NREL data with uncertainty-aware scoring. The semantic-context features produce negligible yet statistically significant gains over the baselines in multiple paired ensemble runs, most clearly at the 30- and 60-minute horizons where these gains hold across different ramp-threshold definitions, and PCA compression helps in some longer-horizon cases. The best context- augmented ensembles rank near the top overall, though the GRU model still posts the lowest ramp-event RMSE at 30 and 60 minutes. External tests confirm the error reduction generalizes across datasets, but the size of the gain depends on both model and dataset. Prediction intervals cover most test cases well but weaken during ramp events, pointing to a localized shift in the data distribution.

</details>


### [228] [Flow-JEPA: Flow Matching for Robust Latent Dynamics in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29029)
*Yanchen Huo,Ziying Song,Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: F-JEPA通过条件流匹配方法改进JEPA世界模型，将确定性自回归预测器替换为随机轨迹级预测，显著提高了在干净和噪声条件下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWorldModel (LeWM)的确定性自回归预测器通过重复的单步转换生成未来状态，容易累积误差且对任务无关的视觉扰动敏感，限制了其在复杂环境下的表现。

Method: 提出Flow-JEPA (F-JEPA)，一种条件流匹配动力学模型，联合生成以当前观测和动作为条件的未来潜在状态序列。使用高斯分布作为流源，让向量场学习将受扰动的潜在轨迹传输到干净的未来表示，实现了随机轨迹级预测而非点对点转换回归。

Result: F-JEPA将干净观测下的平均成功率从86%提升至92%，在噪声条件下从67%提升至86%，显著提高了模型的鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配为JEPA世界模型中的确定性自回归动力学提供了一个有前景的替代方案，能够更好地处理累积误差和视觉扰动问题。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have shown strong potential for learning compact predictive representations, and LeWorldModel (LeWM) extends this paradigm to reconstruction-free latent world modeling from pixels. However, its deterministic autoregressive predictor generates future states through repeated one-step transitions, which can accumulate errors and remain sensitive to task-irrelevant visual perturbations. In this work, we propose Flow-JEPA (F-JEPA), a conditional flow matching dynamics model that jointly generates a sequence of future latent states conditioned on the current observation and actions. A Gaussian distribution serves as the flow source, exposing the vector field to perturbed latent trajectories as it learns to transport them toward clean future representations. This formulation retains the reconstruction-free JEPA framework while replacing point-wise transition regression with stochastic trajectory-level prediction. F-JEPA raises mean success from $86\%$ to $92\%$ under clean observations and from $67\%$ to $86\%$ under noisy conditions, suggesting that conditional flow matching provides a promising alternative to deterministic autoregressive dynamics in JEPA world models.

</details>


### [229] [NVE: A Separability and Coverage-Aware Internal Validation Metric for Biclustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29045)
*Paritosh Tiwari,I Navin Kumar,James C. Bezdek,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为归一化虚拟误差（NVE）的内部验证指标及其覆盖度调整变体NVE_cov，用于评估双聚类的分离性和冗余性，弥补了传统仅关注簇内一致性的指标的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双聚类内部验证指标（如MSR和VE）主要关注簇内一致性，但未能直接评估提取的双聚类是否相互区分或是否解释了数据矩阵中有意义的部分，缺乏对分离性和覆盖度的考量。

Method: 提出归一化虚拟误差（NVE），采用超双聚类归一化策略，通过比较单个双聚类的VE与将其与其他双聚类合并后的VE，引入分离性和冗余度的相对概念。同时研究了覆盖度调整变体NVE_cov，对仅选择极小子矩阵的低误差解进行惩罚。

Result: 通过合成基准测试和酵母基因表达数据集验证，NVE对冗余和分离性差的双聚类敏感，而NVE_cov在低误差双聚类仅覆盖矩阵极小部分时会改变解的排序。

Conclusion: 基于NVE的指标是有用的双聚类内部验证补充标准，特别适用于需要同时考虑一致性、分离性和覆盖度的场景。

Abstract: Biclustering, or co-clustering, aims to discover coherent submatrices by grouping rows and columns of a data matrix simultaneously. This local two-dimensional structure makes validation more difficult than in ordinary clustering, where internal indices usually rely on compactness and separation in a single shared feature space. Existing popular internal biclustering measures such as Mean Squared Residue (MSR), and Virtual Error (VE) mainly evaluate within-bicluster coherence. Although useful, these measures do not directly assess whether the extracted biclusters are mutually distinct or whether they explain a meaningful portion of the data matrix. This paper investigates Normalised Virtual Error (NVE), an internal validation metric that extends VE using a super-bicluster normalization strategy. By comparing the VE of each bicluster with the VE obtained after merging it with other biclusters, NVE introduces a relative notion of separability and redundancy. We also study a coverage-adjusted variant, NVE\textsubscript{cov}, which penalizes solutions that obtain low error by selecting only very small submatrices. Through controlled synthetic benchmarks and yeast gene-expression datasets, we examine whether NVE and NVE\textsubscript{cov} provide information beyond standard coherence-based metrics. The results show that NVE is sensitive to redundant and poorly separated biclusters, while NVE\textsubscript{cov} changes solution rankings when low-error biclusters cover only a negligible part of the matrix. These findings suggest that NVE-based measures are useful complementary criteria for internal co-clustering validation, especially when coherence, separability, and coverage must be considered jointly.

</details>


### [230] [Sparse Koopman Autoencoders Identify Local Dynamical Regimes in Multibasin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29057)
*Aidan Li,Uday Kiran Reddy Tadipatri,Mahan Fathi,Sarath Chandar,Ross Goroshin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出稀疏Koopman自编码器(SKAEs)，通过稀疏潜在表示解决多吸引盆非线性系统的建模问题，在预测性能和可解释性方面均优于传统密集潜在变量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Koopman自编码器在处理具有多个吸引盆的复杂非线性系统时存在局限性，理论和实证研究表明这类系统通常无法在标准假设下获得单一有限维的全局Koopman嵌入，需要新的建模方法来识别和区分不同的动力学区域。

Method: 引入稀疏诱导目标函数，鼓励潜在空间中仅有少量活跃系数；训练稀疏Koopman自编码器时无需吸引盆标签或区域标注；将学习到的潜在支撑作为模型产生的区域变量，用于识别不同的动力学吸引盆。

Result: 在多种程序生成的多吸引盆系统和混沌流测试中，SKAEs在预测性能上显著优于密集潜在变量的KAEs；潜在支撑对于表示质量至关重要；SKAEs能够有效识别未见过的吸引盆内部状态所属的吸引盆，而密集潜在变量KAEs则退化为无信息的单一族。

Conclusion: 稀疏潜在变量及其对应的支撑为具有多个局部动力学规律的非线性系统提供了无需标签、可解释的区域变量，为Koopman学习框架下多吸引盆系统的建模提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Koopman autoencoders (KAEs) seek a higher-dimensional latent representation in which nonlinear dynamics evolve linearly. However, many interesting systems have multiple basins of attraction, and both theoretical and empirical work has shown these multibasin systems cannot generally admit a single finite-dimensional global Koopman embedding under standard assumptions. We posit that encoders with a sparsity-inducing objective encouraging few active latent coefficients will provide latent supports as an inspectable basin-modeling principle for Koopman autoencoders. We use these encoders producing sparse latents in training Sparse Koopman Autoencoders (SKAEs) without basin labels or other regime annotations, and treat the learned latent supports as model-produced regime variables after training. Across a range of procedurally generated multibasin systems and chaotic flows, we show that SKAEs have superior forecasting performance compared to dense-latent KAEs. We also perform a mechanistic study that shows latent supports produced by SKAEs are both essential for the quality of the representation and useful for identifying basins on held-out basin interior states, whereas dense-latent KAEs collapse to an uninformative single family. These results identify sparse latents and their corresponding supports as label-free, interpretable regime variables for Koopman learning in nonlinear systems with multiple local dynamical laws.

</details>


### [231] [PathBridger: Subgoal Bridges for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29061)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PathBridger，一种分层离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过显式连接子目标选择与短期执行来解决长视距任务中的挑战，在OGBench任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视距离线GCRL面临两大挑战：(1)稀疏的目标达成信号需要在多步之间传播；(2)执行错误无法通过额外的环境交互来纠正。现有的分层方法中，选定的子目标仅指定“去哪里”，而中间的状态空间路径隐含在端点条件的低层策略中，这种接口设计存在不足。

Method: 提出PathBridger方法，核心创新在于显式连接子目标选择与短期执行：首先向选定的中间端点构建状态空间桥梁（state-space bridge），然后利用逆动力学模型将桥梁解码为短可执行动作块（action chunk），从而实现分层策略的有效衔接。

Result: 在OGBench评估任务中展现出强劲的综合性能，特别是在多物体Cube操控任务上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式构建状态空间桥梁并将子目标选择与动作执行紧密连接，PathBridger有效解决了长视距离线GCRL中的接口问题，为分层强化学习提供了更可靠的执行机制。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) aims to learn policies for reaching diverse goals entirely from fixed trajectory data. Long-horizon offline GCRL remains challenging because sparse goal-reaching signals must be propagated over many steps, while execution errors cannot be corrected through additional environment interaction. Existing methods address these challenges by improving long-range value estimation or reducing the effective decision horizon through subgoals, options, and action chunks. In several hierarchical methods, however, a selected subgoal specifies where to go, while the intervening state-space path remains implicit in an endpoint-conditioned low-level policy. To address this interface, we propose PathBridger, a hierarchical offline GCRL method that explicitly connects subgoal selection to short-horizon execution. PathBridger constructs a state-space bridge toward the selected intermediate endpoint and decodes it into a short executable action chunk using an inverse dynamics model. Experiments across the evaluated OGBench tasks demonstrate strong aggregate performance, with particularly large gains on the multi-object Cube manipulation tasks. Code: https://github.com/SChoish/PathBridger

</details>


### [232] [Selective Disclosure of Hidden Directives in Reasoning Models: Behavioral Asymmetry and Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29070)
*Zimo Shi,Xander Tifft,Wen Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 思维链监控存在"指令合规差距"：模型更倾向于泄露恶意隐藏指令而非良性指令，导致基于CoT的AI监督机制存在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证思维链推理作为AI监督机制的核心假设——即模型是否会对所有指令（无论良性或恶意）同等程度地在推理过程中进行表述。当前AI监督依赖于检查模型的推理痕迹来检测不良行为，但这需要模型如实反映其接收到的指令。

Method: 1. 引入指令合规差距（ICG）指标：测量模型在收到恶意与良性隐藏系统提示时，其思维链明确引用该指令的概率差异；2. 在100个任务对和8个前沿推理模型上进行测试；3. 使用Wilcoxon检验进行统计分析；4. 提取引导向量并通过对比激活添加进行因果干预实验。

Result: 1. 发现一致的非对称披露现象：Qwen3系列（14B、32B、235B）、MiniMax-M2.5和DeepSeek-R1均表现出更可能泄露恶意隐藏指令的趋势；2. 检测器对独立盲标注达到100%精度；3. LLM监控器在82%的恶意痕迹中能识别具体指令；4. 良性与恶意导向的隐藏向量高度相似（MiniMax-M2.5余弦相似度0.804，Qwen3-14B为0.970）。

Conclusion: 披露非对称性源于共享隐藏方向（hiding direction）的差异化激活，而非独立机制；这意味着仅依赖思维链监控的AI监督策略存在根本性缺陷，因为模型会对不同性质的指令表现出不同的披露倾向。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning traces are increasingly proposed as a mechanism for AI oversight: a monitor inspecting a model's reasoning can, in principle, detect misbehavior invisible from outputs alone. This assumes CoT surfaces what a model is instructed to do regardless of the instructions given. We test this assumption along two axes. First, we introduce the Instruction-Compliance Gap (ICG): the difference in probability that a model's CoT explicitly references a hidden system prompt directive when that directive is malign versus benign. Across 100 task pairs and 8 frontier reasoning models from 5 families, we find consistent asymmetric disclosure, a higher probability of leaking malign hidden instructions than benign ones, in Qwen3-14B (Wilcoxon $p=0.0001$, $+13.9$pp), Qwen3-32B ($p=0.0011$, $+13.0$pp), Qwen3-235B ($p=0.035$, $+5.8$pp), and similar results with MiniMax-M2.5 and DeepSeek-R1. The detector has 100% precision against two independent blinded labelling passes, and an LLM monitor reading only the reasoning trace reproduces the asymmetry in all 8 models against directive-free controls, identifying the specific directive in 82% of malign traces which the detector classifies as clean. Second, steering vectors extracted in MiniMax-M2.5 via Contrastive Activation Addition causally induce hiding from bare prompts and suppress it from prompts that would otherwise produce it, replicating in Qwen3-14B under a pre-registered design. Benign and malign-derived hiding vectors are highly similar (cosine $0.804$ in MiniMax-M2.5; $0.970$ in Qwen3-14B), implying that in these models the disclosure asymmetry arises from differential activation of a shared hiding direction rather than separate mechanisms.

</details>


### [233] [Titans-QFWP: A Regime-Aware Hybrid Quantum Fast Weight Programmer for Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29093)
*Ming-Kai Hung,Jun-Hao Chen,Yun-Cheng Tsai,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Titans-QFWP将量子快速权重编程器与Titans记忆机制结合，在约3000参数约束下对468只标普500股票实现了中位年化收益0.426、Calmar比率8.55的优异表现。量子门控重新定义了记忆组件功能：Persistence控制回撤、Surprise驱动收益、Forgetting增强稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维市场特征的自适应投资组合优化问题，传统方法难以在参数效率与性能间取得平衡。研究旨在将量子计算优势与记忆机制融合，在有限参数约束下实现对市场动态的精准适应。

Method: 提出Titans-QFWP架构，整合量子快速权重编程器与Titans记忆系统（Persistence、Surprise、Forgetting）；构建增强版A3C²框架，引入匈牙利对齐K-means聚类处理高维特征；设计缩放对数收益奖励函数；采用等参数计数基准限制可训练参数约3000个。

Result: 在468只标普500成分股上验证，实现中位年化收益率0.4260、Calmar比率8.5504、信息比率0.8427。消融实验揭示量子门控从根本上重塑记忆组件功能：Persistence组件支持回撤控制，Surprise组件贡献收益生成，Forgetting组件提供额外稳定性。模型在市场下跌期间实现防御性配置同时保持上行潜力。

Conclusion: 量子门控与记忆机制的协同作用有效解决了高维组合优化的参数效率问题。量子表示的稳定性使模型能够在市场下行时实现防御性配置并保留上行空间，为量子增强强化学习在金融决策中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose Titans-QFWP, a hybrid reinforcement learning architecture integrating a Quantum Fast Weight Programmer with Titans-style memory (Persistence, Surprise, and Forgetting) for adaptive portfolio optimization. To address high-dimensional market features, we introduce an enhanced A3C^2 framework with Hungarian-aligned K-means clustering and scaled log-return rewards. Evaluated on 468 S&P 500 stocks under an Equal-Parameter-Count (EPC) benchmark with approximately 3,000 trainable parameters, Titans-QFWP achieves strong performance (median ARR 0.4260, Calmar 8.5504, IR 0.8427). Ablation results reveal that quantum gating fundamentally reshapes memory component roles, with Persistence supporting drawdown control, Surprise contributing to return generation, and Forgetting providing additional stabilization. By stabilizing these quantum representations, the model enables defensive allocation during market drawdowns while preserving upside potential.

</details>


### [234] [Development of an Autonomous AI Coding Agent using Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and Gemini LLM Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29096)
*Pravin Game,Vipin Ramakrishnan,Prathamesh Wagh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)的自主AI编程代理，通过将代码生成视为搜索问题，在复杂逻辑提示词上的成功率达到92%，显著超越传统零样本生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程需求的持续变化产生了对自动化工具的迫切需求，这些工具能够从自然语言输入生成安全的源代码。传统大语言模型(LLMs)受限于"一次性"生成能力，在复杂操作中会产生逻辑幻觉和算法性能下降的问题。

Method: 1. 使用Gemini 2.5 Flash API提供核心推理能力；2. 采用定制的蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)方法，将代码生成作为搜索操作来处理；3. 引入"自批判"(Self-Critic)评估器系统，根据准确性和难度对不同实现方法进行排名；4. 通过反向传播改进操作框架；5. 提供基于Flask的Web界面，具有即时反馈和语法高亮功能。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于MCTS的方法在复杂逻辑提示词上达到92%的成功率，显著超越了标准的零样本生成模型。

Conclusion: 该研究成功展示了将MCTS搜索算法与LLM推理能力相结合的有效性，通过结构化的决策方法弥合了LLM生成内容与生产就绪软件之间的差距，为自动化代码生成提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The ongoing changes in software engineering requirements have created a substantial need for automated tools which can create secure source code from natural language input. The performance of traditional Large Language Models (LLMs) becomes limited by their "one-shot" capability which results in logical hallucinations together with reduced algorithmic performance during complicated operations. The research presents an autonomous AI Coding Agent which establishes a connection between LLM-generated content and production-ready software through its organized methodology for decision making. Our framework uses the Gemini 2.5 Flash API for essential reasoning capabilities while employing a tailored Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) method to solve code generation challenges as a search operation. The agent uses a "Self-Critic" evaluator system to test different implementation methods which it ranks according to their accuracy and difficulty level before it improves its operational framework through backpropagation. The system operates through a Flask-based web interface which delivers instant feedback together with syntax highlighting features. Our experimental results show that the MCTS-based method achieves a 92% success rate on complex logical prompts while surpassing standard zero-shot generation models.

</details>


### [235] [Temperature-Adaptive Transformed Teacher Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29099)
*Hiroaki Aizawa,Yoshikazu Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TTM框架下的样本级逆温度自适应更新方法，通过局部最小化KL散度实现高效、基于曲率的温度调整，显著提升了知识蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度缩放是知识蒸馏的核心组件，但其作用机制尚未被充分理解。现有TTM方法虽阐明了温度缩放的角色（解释为隐式Rényi熵正则化），但仍依赖固定温度，且未说明如何针对单个样本自适应调整教师端的温度参数。

Method: 提出一种样本级逆温度更新策略，通过局部最小化温度缩放后的教师分布与学生预测之间的KL散度来优化温度。推导了关于逆温度的一阶和二阶闭式导数，证明其可用变换教师加权下的中心化师生logits的方差和协方差统计量表示。该方法仅需一次softmax评估和常数级类别加权求和，计算高效且考虑曲率信息。

Result: 在标准图像分类蒸馏基准测试上，该方法通常能提升TTM和WTTM的性能，同时与之前的温度自适应蒸馏基线保持竞争力或表现更优。

Conclusion: 样本级温度自适应机制能有效改善知识蒸馏效果，基于方差的闭式更新提供了一种理论清晰且计算高效的温度调整方案。

Abstract: Temperature scaling is a core component of knowledge distillation, yet its role and effect are still not fully understood. Transformed Teacher Matching (TTM) clarifies the role of temperature scaling by applying it only to the teacher distribution and interpreting the resulting objective as standard distillation with an implicit Rényi entropy regularization on the student. However, TTM still relies on a fixed temperature and does not specify how the teacher-side temperature should be adapted for individual samples. In this paper, we introduce a sample-wise inverse-temperature update for TTM by locally minimizing the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the temperature-scaled teacher distribution and the student's prediction. We derive closed-form first and second derivatives with respect to the inverse temperature, and show that they can be expressed using variance and covariance statistics of centered teacher and student logits under the transformed teacher weighting. This yields an efficient curvature-aware update that requires one softmax evaluation and a constant number of class-wise weighted sums. Experiments on standard image classification distillation benchmarks show that our temperature adaptation generally improves TTM and WTTM, while remaining competitive with or outperforming prior temperature-adaptive distillation baselines.

</details>


### [236] [PathGuide: Dynamic Classifier-Free Guidance via On-Policy Transport Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29107)
*Avishag Nevo,Tamir Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PathGuide框架，将分类器自由引导(CFG)的标量选择问题重构为在线传输问题，通过连续性方程的弱形式推导出具有直接路径正确性解释的选择准则，实现了最优引导尺度的在线计算或离线拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型在条件生成中的推理时精确控制仍是关键挑战。虽然分类器自由引导(CFG)是主要控制机制，但通常被视为静态调节参数。在基于流的模型中，引导尺度根本性地决定了速度场和结果概率路径，因此引导选择是一个动态路径优化问题。

Method: 利用连续性方程的弱形式推导选择准则：证明如果引导场沿生成轨迹与精确条件场弱等价，则采样器路径与目标条件律一致。对于标量CFG，该准则产生严格二次的局部目标，并为每个求解器区间提供高效的闭式选择器。可在生成过程中在线计算最优引导尺度，或离线拟合为可复用的分段常数调度。

Result: 在低分辨率图像流形和各种连续时间流构造的控制设置上进行验证，证明该基于传输的选择器在路径对齐和样本保真度方面均优于固定引导尺度和最先进的自适应引导基线方法。

Conclusion: PathGuide成功地将CFG选择从静态参数调节转化为动态路径优化问题，通过传输理论提供了理论上有根据且实践中有效的引导尺度选择方法，显著改善了条件生成的质量和控制精度。

Abstract: While modern generative models excel at modeling complex data, precise inference-time control in conditional generation remains a critical challenge. Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is a primary mechanism for such control, yet it is typically treated as a static tuning parameter. In flow-based models, however, the guidance scale fundamentally dictates the velocity field and the resulting probability path, making guidance selection a dynamic path-optimization problem. We introduce PathGuide, a framework that reformulates scalar CFG selection as an on-policy transport problem. Leveraging the weak form of the continuity equation, we derive a selection criterion with a direct path-correctness interpretation: we prove that if the guided field is weakly equivalent to the exact conditional field along the generated rollout, the sampler's path coincides with the target conditional law. For scalar CFG, this criterion yields a strictly quadratic local objective with an efficient, closed-form selector for each solver interval. PathGuide enables optimal guidance scales to be computed and used online during generation or fitted offline as a reusable piecewise-constant schedule. We validate our method on low-resolution image manifolds and controlled settings across various continuous-time flow constructions, demonstrating that this transport-based selector improves path alignment and sample fidelity over both fixed and state-of-the-art adaptive guidance baselines.

</details>


### [237] [Explainable Machine Learning for Broadband Adoption Disparities: Tract-Level Prediction and SHAP-Based Factor Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29110)
*Xiao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可解释的机器学习框架，用于分析美国83,359个人口普查区域的宽带采用差异。LightGBM模型结合TreeSHAP分析揭示了三大因素特征类型，相比仅基于收入的启发式方法，该模型在缩小采用差距方面表现更优（+2.8个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国已通过《基础设施投资与就业法案》为宽带扩展拨款约650亿美元，但基于证据的投资目标定位方法仍不成熟。现有方法缺乏对宽带采用差异的细粒度分析和可解释性，难以有效指导资源配置。

Method: 基于2022年美国社区调查数据，使用65个社会经济、人口和基础设施特征构建LightGBM模型，采用空间五折交叉验证和州级留出交叉验证。运用TreeSHAP进行特征重要性分析和聚类，识别人口普查区域的因素特征类型。

Result: 模型在空间五折交叉验证中达到R²=0.533、Spearman ρ=0.763，州级留出验证R²=0.525。识别出三类区域：连接良好中等程度（约4.9万个区域）、负担受限严重（约2.1万个区域）、农村老年型（约1.3万个区域）。在前10%重点区域中，ML方法比纯收入启发式方法多捕获2.8个百分点（p<0.002）的采用差距。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了每个区域的因素分解能力，能够识别哪些特征组（收入/教育、农村性、年龄）与各区域的预测差距关联最强，为差异化调查和政策干预提供了可解释的筛选工具。时间稳定性检验（2017年训练、2022年预测）确认了排名稳定性（ρ=0.784）。

Abstract: The United States has allocated approximately $65 billion through the Infrastructure Investment and Jobs Act for broadband expansion, yet evidence-based methods for targeting these investments remain underdeveloped. This paper presents an explainable machine learning framework for profiling broadband adoption disparities at census-tract granularity across 83,359 tracts nationwide. Using 65 socioeconomic, demographic, and infrastructure features derived from the American Community Survey 2022, we train a LightGBM model under spatial five-fold cross-validation, achieving R^2 = 0.533 and Spearman rho = 0.763; state-held-out cross-validation (51 folds) confirms generalization (R^2 = 0.525). TreeSHAP analysis identifies income and education as the dominant factor group (with the engineered interaction term absorbing attribution from its constituent features), and SHAP-based clustering reveals three exploratory factor profiles: Well-Connected Moderate (~49K tracts), Affordability-Limited Severe (~21K tracts), and Rural-Elderly (~13K tracts). As a screening tool, ML-based tract selection captures 38.0% of the total adoption gap within the top 10% of tracts versus 35.2% for income-only heuristics (+2.8 pp, p < 0.002, county-block bootstrap); in regret-reduction terms, the model closes 19% of the remaining gap between income-only and oracle selection. The primary contribution is the per-tract factor decomposition: SHAP identifies which feature groups (income/education, rurality, age) are most strongly associated with each tract's predicted gap, and informs differentiated investigation. A temporal stability check, training on ACS 2017 and predicting ACS 2022 with zero survey-year overlap, confirms ranking stability (rho = 0.784, noting hyperparameters tuned on 2022 data).

</details>


### [238] [Locked at the Entrance, Open Inside: Where RLVR Narrows the Solution Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29188)
*Qiancheng Zhou,Ruizhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLVR训练导致推理广度在思考轨迹入口处严重收缩（覆盖率达67%下降），而非执行过程中；通过入口干预可恢复多样性且不损失准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）虽提升单样本准确率，但导致策略解空间收缩，降低了测试时扩展的收益。本研究旨在探究推理广度损失的具体位置：是策略无法访问有效解族，还是无法执行已启动的计算？

Method: 通过Countdown任务（解空间可穷举为离散入口族）解耦访问与执行，在Qwen2.5-3B上分析PPO和GRPO训练过程；采用逐标记似然分析、入口前缀供给、后期层参数插值、以及SFT-DPO-RLVR分阶段管道等方法。

Result: 解覆盖率下降高达67%，在入口处似然偏移是下游推理的11-16倍；提供未选择入口前缀可使低访问族完成率提升一个数量级；入口定向干预使解覆盖率增加37%且不损失pass@1；早期步骤熵坍缩在六个数学基准中复现，但SFT基线保留超过两倍覆盖率。

Conclusion: 推理广度在入口处丧失而非执行过程中；熵坍缩非推理优化的必然产物；通过入口干预和分阶段训练可有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) substantially improves single-sample accuracy (pass@1) but causes the policy's solution space to contract, diminishing the returns of test-time scaling. In this work, we investigate where inside a reasoning trajectory this breadth is lost: does the policy fail to access a valid solution family, or does it fail to execute computation once initiated? To disentangle access from execution, we analyze the Countdown task, whose solution space can be exhaustively enumerated into discrete entrance families defined by the first operand and operator, across PPO on Qwen2.5-3B and GRPO on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct. Across both training setups, solution coverage falls by up to 67%, halving even on problems solved across all checkpoints. We show that this contraction is heavily concentrated at the entrance: per-token likelihood shifts are 11x--16x larger prior to the first arithmetic operation than during downstream reasoning. Supplying only an unselected entrance prefix restores completion rates in low-access families by over an order of magnitude (0.018 -> 0.212 under PPO), demonstrating that alternative solutions remain executable but are no longer initiated. Guided by this localization, we find that while surface prompting fails to recover diversity, entrance-targeted interventions succeed: late-layer parameter interpolation with early checkpoints increases solution coverage by 37% at no loss in pass@1. Finally, we show that early-step entropy collapse recurs across six math benchmarks with 7B and 14B models, but is not an inevitable byproduct of reasoning optimization: an SFT baseline preserves more than double the coverage, and staged SFT--DPO--RLVR pipelines retain early-step entropy. In summary, reasoning breadth is lost at the door, not inside the room. Code: https://github.com/ershiyidian/early-branch-locking.

</details>


### [239] [HalluPrism: When Multimodal Uncertainty Should Diagnose, Not Decide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29193)
*Aman Prakash,Sourish Dasgupta,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HalluPrism行为诊断方法，通过视觉扰动、图像移除和基础/关系检查探测多模态大语言模型的失败模式，构建(V,L,A)联合特征显著提升了失败家族分类性能（AUROC最高达0.97），但该特征不直接改善正确性排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)对不同原因导致的错误答案会分配相似的置信度，这种置信度混淆使得难以区分不同类型的失败原因，也无法有效指导模型的弃权或纠正决策。

Method: 提出HalluPrism行为诊断框架，对模型答案进行三种针对性探测：(1)视觉退化后重新运行；(2)空白图像替换后重新运行；(3)基础/关系检查。由此构建包含视觉扰动敏感性(V)、图像移除置信度保留(L)和基础/关系探针不稳定性(A)的三维特征签名。

Result: 在4个基准数据集(58K+样本)和4个MLLM上的实验表明：(1)图像移除置信度保留现象最普遍，但基础/关系探针不稳定性更能区分失败家族；(2)仅18/48的源-目标检查对角对齐，说明坐标需联合解释；(3)联合特征将HallusionBench的AUROC从0.634提升至0.769，VizWiz从0.707提升至0.817；(4)XGBoost分析中，使用(V,L,A)特征AUROC达0.95，加入置信度后达0.97；(5)该特征不自动改善正确性排序，三种直接标量化方法反而可能损害排序性能。

Conclusion: 多模态不确定性应先刻画失败结构，再用于决定是否弃权或纠正。失败诊断与弃权评分应分离处理，联合特征签名能够有效识别失败模式，但不应直接用于正确性排序。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can assign similar confidence to answers that fail for different reasons. We propose HalluPrism, a behavioral diagnostic that re-runs an answer after visual degradation, blank-image replacement, and grounding or relation checks. These targeted probes yield a signature over visual-perturbation sensitivity (V ), image-removal confidence retention (L), and grounding/relation-probe instability (A). Across 58K+ examples from four benchmarks and four MLLMs, image-removal confidence retention is most prevalent, while grounding/relation-probe instability better separates failure families. Only 18 of 48 source-target checks are diagonally aligned, so the coordinates should be interpreted jointly rather than as independent causal sources. With the dataset fixed, the joint signature improves failure-family AUROC from 0.634 to 0.769 on HallusionBench and from 0.707 to 0.817 on VizWiz, with smaller gains on POPE and VSR. In pooled XGBoost analysis, AUROC rises from 0.78 with scalar confidence to 0.95 with (V, L, A) and 0.97 when confidence is added. The same signature does not automatically improve correctness ranking. The three tested direct scalarizations can harm it. These results separate failure diagnosis from abstention scoring: multimodal uncertainty should characterize failure structure before it is used to decide whether to abstain or correct.

</details>


### [240] [PokaiTrainer: Scaling Belief-State Search to Competitive Pokémon VGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29197)
*Max Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次将决策时均衡搜索应用于复杂的不完美信息博弈——宝可梦双打对战（VGC格式），构建的AI代理在真实在线对战平台达到1350-1400 Elo水平，成功进入前500名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策时均衡搜索在扑克等游戏中实现了超人表现，但依赖于可处理的子博弈假设：每个决策只有少量动作、随机性仅限于发牌、玩家轮流行动。宝可梦官方双打格式（VGC）同时打破了这三个假设：双方玩家同时从数百个联合动作中选择、每个联合动作产生数百种随机结果、对手的备选宝可梦和属性配置是隐藏的。研究旨在构建一个强大的VGC代理并报告其实现过程。

Method: 开发了两个核心组件：1) PokaiEngine——用Rust编写的战斗引擎，单次遍历即可枚举联合动作的完整加权结果分布，与Pokémon Showdown达到约99%一致性，成本仅为采样的很小一部分；2) PokaiTrainer——将Student of Games方法适配到此规模，将每个决策求解为公共信念状态上的贝叶斯矩阵博弈，在明确计算预算下增长子博弈。

Result: 在Showdown平台的BO3天梯赛中，代理在150场比赛中对平均约1320 Elo的人类选手取得了59%的胜率。代理稳定在1350-1400 Elo区间，巅峰时期短暂进入该格式前500名。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将决策时均衡搜索扩展到具有大规模联合动作空间、同时行动、大量随机结果和隐藏信息的复杂博弈场景，证明了该方法在更广泛游戏类型中的适用性。

Abstract: Decision-time equilibrium search carried poker to superhuman play, but it has so far relied on tractable subgames: a handful of actions per decision, chance confined to card deals, one player moving at a time. Competitive Pokémon in its official doubles format (VGC) breaks all three assumptions at once. Both players act simultaneously from joint menus in the hundreds, each joint action resolves to hundreds of stochastic outcomes, and the opponent's reserves and stat allocations are hidden. We set out to build a strong VGC agent and report what that took. PokaiEngine, our Rust battle engine, enumerates a joint action's full weighted outcome distribution in one pass, at ${\sim}99\%$ parity with Pokémon Showdown and a fraction of the cost of sampling it. On top of the engine, PokaiTrainer adapts Student of Games to this scale, solving every decision as a Bayesian matrix game over public belief states and growing subgames under an explicit compute budget. On the live Showdown best-of-three ladder, the agent wins 59% of 150 sets against a human field averaging ${\sim}1320$ Elo. It settles into a 1350-1400 Elo band, and at its peak briefly entered the format's top 500.

</details>


### [241] [RL-FAT: Reinforcement Learning for Fair Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29247)
*Tejaswini Medi,Levan Mikeladze,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的公平对抗训练框架(RL-FAT)，通过策略梯度反馈和公平性强调损失来缓解对抗训练中的类别间鲁棒性不均衡问题，在保持鲁棒精度的同时显著减少了类别间鲁棒性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在对抗攻击下仍然高度脆弱，对抗训练(AT)被广泛用于提高鲁棒性。然而，平均鲁棒精度的提升往往掩盖了严重的类别间差异：某些类别变得更加鲁棒，而其他类别在攻击下可能仍然不成比例地脆弱。这种不平衡引发了重要的对抗公平性关切，特别是在期望所有类别具有可靠鲁棒性的视觉任务中。

Method: 提出RL-FAT框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)将预测分布解释为策略，结合基于正确性的预测奖励和类别价值估计，计算类别特定的优势函数用于策略梯度优化，使模型能够自适应地关注类别间错误分类；(2)引入公平性强调对抗损失，对高对抗损失的类别施加更强的训练压力，从而缓解类别间鲁棒性差异。通过强化驱动的自适应与公平性强调正则化的结合，实现鲁棒性提升与类别间鲁棒性分布均衡。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在鲁棒精度方面达到竞争性水平，与标准对抗训练基线相比，显著减少了类别间鲁棒性不平衡。方法有效提升了对类别间鲁棒性不均衡问题的解决能力。

Conclusion: RL-FAT框架成功地将强化学习思想引入对抗训练，通过策略梯度优化和公平性强调损失的有效结合，在保持竞争性鲁棒精度的同时，显著改善了类别间鲁棒性分布的均衡性，为解决对抗训练中的公平性问题提供了有效的解决方案。
