We further determine two more values of the weighted selection profile $F_w(d,m,n)$: at the near-threshold budget, $F_w(d,m,(m+1)d-1)=1+\frac{1}{dm^2}$, and at the spanning budget, $F_w(d,m,d)=d+1$ for every $m$, while $F_w(d,m,n)=\infty$ for $n<d$. For the smallest open intermediate cell $(d,m)=(2,2)$ we prove $F_w(2,2,3)\in[13/8,15/8]$ and $F_w(2,2,4)\in[5/4,3/2]$, reduce the conjectured exact values $13/8$ and $5/4$ to a finite moment problem on the circle with at most seven atoms, and establish strong structural evidence for the conjecture. The upper-bound techniques (a fixed-basis conic compression lemma, a determinant-facet rigidity theorem for maximal certificates, and sharp sparsification lemmas for zero-mean weighted point systems) are of independent interest. As a byproduct we correct an erroneous claim circulating in a recent unrefereed preprint, exhibiting an explicit dataset with $m=2$ on which no weighted selection of $2d$ points recovers the optimal loss. All results are new only for $m\ge 2$; the scalar case $m=1$ is due to Hanneke et al.

</details>


### [313] [Multivariate Scientific Data Compression with Learned Cross-Variable Latent Decorrelation and Autoregressive Entropy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30262)
*Liangji Zhu,Anand Rangarajan,Sanjay Ranka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAESAR-LDAR是一种面向多变量科学数据的有损压缩方法，通过正交变换解耦潜在空间的全局跨通道依赖，结合因果自回归先验建模局部空间结构，两者互补以提升压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟产生的多物理场数据具有异构统计特性和复杂依赖关系，但现有学习方法往往对各场进行独立编码或仅使用共享编码器，未能显式建模潜在空间中残留的结构信息，限制了压缩效率。

Method: 在共享CAESAR-V骨干网络上集成两种机制：(1) 基于矩阵指数参数化的可训练正交变换，重组并解耦对齐潜在通道间的依赖，且变换严格可逆；(2) 因果自回归层次先验，捕捉变换后残留的局部空间结构；并通过统一的残差校正阶段严格控制重建误差容限。

Result: 在燃烧、气候和湍流数据集上的实验表明：当潜在空间存在显著线性跨通道依赖时，正交变换收益大；当残留结构以局部/空间特性为主时，自回归建模更有效。两者结合在所有评估数据集上达到最优或接近最优的率失真性能。正交变换计算开销低，自回归编码存在较大吞吐权衡。

Conclusion: 提出多变量科学数据压缩的实用设计原则：当潜在空间中可测得全局跨通道依赖时优先利用，并用局部概率上下文作为广泛数据域下的互补机制，两者协同可实现更优压缩效果。

Abstract: Scientific simulations generate collections of physical fields with heterogeneous statistics and dependencies, yet learned compressors often encode those fields independently or rely on a shared encoder without explicitly modeling the structure that remains in latent space. We present CAESAR-LDAR, an error-controlled multivariate learned compressor that augments a shared CAESAR-V backbone with two complementary mechanisms: a trainable orthogonal transform that reorganizes dependence across aligned latent channels, and a causal autoregressive hierarchical prior that captures local spatial structure left after transformation. Orthogonality is maintained through a matrix-exponential parameterization, making the transform exactly invertible without an additional penalty. A common residual-correction stage is applied uniformly to all variants to enforce the requested reconstruction tolerance.
  Experiments across combustion, climate, and turbulence data show that the two mechanisms are useful in different regimes. Latent decorrelation helps most when substantial linear cross-channel dependence survives the nonlinear encoder, whereas autoregressive modeling remains effective when the remaining structure is primarily local or spatial. Their combination provides the strongest or near-strongest rate-distortion performance across the evaluated datasets. The global transform adds little computational overhead, while autoregressive coding introduces a larger throughput tradeoff. More broadly, the results suggest a practical design principle for multivariate scientific compression: exploit global cross-channel dependence when it is measurably present in latent space, and use local probabilistic context as a complementary mechanism across a wider range of data regimes.

</details>


### [314] [BCPPO: Bachelier-Inspired Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization for Tail-Risk-Aware Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30283)
*Dongsheng Hou,Yanqiao Chen,Yuhan Rui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BCPPO方法，通过critic间的预测分歧来衡量不确定性，并将其转化为平滑的策略更新惩罚，在保持部署简便性的同时实现了更好的风险-收益权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束强化学习方法存在局限性：期望成本约束无法阻止罕见高成本事件，高置信度下的蒙特卡洛CVaR梯度噪声大，而建模结果分布的critic方法增加了复杂性。

Method: 提出BCPPO（Bachelier启发的约束近端策略优化）方法。使用独立初始化的成本预测网络（critics），通过随机样本掩码训练产生分歧（disagreement），该分歧标记了对训练数据中状态-动作区域和critic训练敏感的预测。利用Bachelier公式将这种分歧转化为平滑的策略更新惩罚，该惩罚梯度不改变critics，因此时序差分critic学习不受影响。饱和感知控制器调整平均成本惩罚，防止累积误差增长。部署时仅保留策略网络。

Result: 在175次运行实验中（共享任务、成本、预算、训练步数和评估种子），没有任何基线方法在任何任务中同时获得更高的平均回报和更低的平均CVaR。在Push1任务上，BCPPO相比所有基线方法具有不低于的回报和不高于的CVaR，且至少有一个严格提升。

Conclusion: BCPPO在奖励回报、对critic预测不确定性的谨慎处理，以及仅策略网络部署的简便性之间实现了实用的平衡，为约束强化学习提供了有效的新方法。

Abstract: Expected-cost constraints can still permit rare, high-cost events. Monte Carlo conditional value at risk (CVaR) gradients can be noisy at high confidence, whereas critics that model an outcome distribution add complexity. We propose BCPPO (Bachelier-Inspired Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization), a proximal policy optimization (PPO) method. Separately initialized cost-prediction networks (critics), trained with random sample masks, produce disagreement that marks predictions sensitive to which state-action regions occur in the training data and to critic training. A Bachelier formula for the expected amount above a reference level converts this disagreement into a smooth policy-update penalty. Gradients from this penalty do not alter the critics, so temporal-difference (TD) critic learning is unchanged. A saturation-aware controller adjusts the mean-cost penalty and stops accumulated error from growing while that penalty is clipped. Deployment retains only the policy network. The disagreement penalty is neither a tail-event probability nor a guaranteed error bound, and it provides no safety guarantee. Across 175 runs with shared tasks, costs, budgets, training steps, and evaluation seeds, no comparator attains both higher mean return and lower mean CVaR than BCPPO in any task. On Push1, BCPPO has no lower return and no higher CVaR than every comparator, with at least one strict gain. These results support a practical balance among reward, caution around cost predictions that vary across trained critics, and policy-only deployment.

</details>


### [315] [CateKV: On Sequential Consistency for Long-Context LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30295)
*Haoyun Jiang,Haolin Li,Jianwei Zhang,Fei Huang,Qiang Hu,Minmin Sun,Shuai Xiao,Yong Li,Junyang Lin,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CateKV方法，通过识别注意力头的一致性模式来选择性压缩KV缓存，在保持准确性的同时显著降低内存使用和加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理长上下文任务时面临巨大的内存需求和推理延迟挑战，现有的KV缓存机制在长序列场景下资源消耗过大。

Method: 发现部分注意力头存在序列一致的注意力模式，提出基于变异系数的算法识别这些一致性头；设计CateKV混合KV缓存方法，对一致性头仅保留关键token信息以减少缓存大小，对自适应头保留大部分KV对以确保精度。

Result: 在长上下文基准测试中，CateKV在保持与全注意力相当准确性的同时，单样本输入场景下内存使用降低最多2.72倍、解码加速2.18倍，批处理场景下吞吐量提升3.96倍。

Conclusion: 通过区分不同注意力头的特性进行差异化KV缓存管理，可以有效平衡内存效率与模型性能，为长上下文LLM的高效部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in handling long-context tasks, but processing such long contexts remains challenging due to the substantial memory requirements and inference latency. In this work, we discover that certain attention heads exhibit sequential consistency in their attention patterns, which can be persistently identified using a coefficient-of-variation-based algorithm. Inspired by this observation, we propose CateKV, a hybrid KV cache method that retains only critical token information for consistent heads, thereby reducing KV cache size and computational overhead, while preserving the majority of KV pairs in adaptive heads to ensure high accuracy. We show the unique characteristics of our algorithm and its extension with existing acceleration methods. Comprehensive evaluations on long-context benchmarks show that, while maintaining accuracy comparable to full attention, CateKV reduces memory usage by up to $2.72\times$ and accelerates decoding by $2.18\times$ in single-sample inputs, and boosts throughput by $3.96\times$ in batch scenarios.

</details>


### [316] [Tail-Replay: Escaping the Curse of Linear Attention in Prefix Caching for Hybrid LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30310)
*Yirui Liu,Ruoling Qi,Xuaner Wu,Penghang Liu,Jian Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tail-Replay通过回放前缀末尾短片段重建线性注意力状态，在混合大语言模型中实现了不受限的token级前缀重用，以极低的回放预算获得了接近全量预填充的质量和显著的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合大语言模型中，全注意力层的KV缓存支持任意位置访问，而线性注意力层的循环状态无法回滚到任意前缀边界，现有方法通过存储循环状态检查点解决此问题，但限制了前缀重用只能在离散边界进行。这导致前缀缓存无法实现灵活的token级重用，影响了推理效率。

Method: 提出Tail-Replay机制，核心洞察是将线性注意力（如Gated DeltaNet）视为输入前缀的结构化有损压缩：门控循环更新会逐渐衰减早期输入的贡献。因此匹配前缀的循环状态可以通过仅回放该前缀的短近邻后缀来很好地近似。该方法缓存完整的全注意力KV缓存，但省略循环状态检查点；在缓存命中时，通过回放匹配前缀的短后缀来重建线性注意力状态。

Result: 在三个基于Gated DeltaNet的混合模型上进行评估（LongBench和RULER基准）。仅使用5-10%的回放预算，在LongBench和RULER上保持了92.8-99.9%的全量预填充质量。在服务效率方面，评估了8K、16K、32K多个匹配前缀长度下的首token时间加速比，加速随前缀长度增长，在32K时达到9.1-14.3倍加速。

Conclusion: Tail-Replay成功实现了混合大语言模型中不受限的token级前缀重用，通过利用线性注意力机制的衰减特性，以极低的计算开销实现了高质量的状态重建，显著提升了长上下文推理效率，为混合架构的部署提供了实用的缓存解决方案。

Abstract: Hybrid large language models interleave full-attention layers with linear-attention layers to reduce the cost of long-context inference. This structure complicates prefix caching: full-attention key-value caches are token-addressable, whereas linear-attention layers maintain recurrent states that cannot be rolled back to arbitrary prefix boundaries. Existing hybrid prefix caching methods address this mismatch by storing recurrent-state checkpoints. As a result, token-level matches are directly usable only at positions aligned with stored checkpoints, constraining prefix reuse to a discrete set of boundaries. We present Tail-Replay, a prefix caching mechanism that enables unconstrained token-level prefix reuse in hybrid large language models. The key insight is that linear-attention mechanisms such as Gated DeltaNet can be viewed as a structured, lossy compression of the input prefix: gated recurrent updates progressively attenuate the contributions of earlier inputs. Consequently, the recurrent state of a matched prefix can be well approximated by replaying only a short, recent suffix of that prefix. Tail-Replay exploits this property by caching the exact full-attention key-value cache while omitting recurrent-state checkpoints. On a cache hit, it reconstructs the linear-attention states by replaying a short, recent suffix of the matched prefix. As a result, the reuse boundary is determined by the shared tokens rather than by recurrent-state checkpoints. We evaluate Tail-Replay on three Gated DeltaNet-based hybrid models using the LongBench and RULER benchmarks. With only a 5--10\% replay budget, it retains 92.8--99.9\% of full-prefill quality on LongBench and RULER. For serving efficiency, we evaluate time-to-first-token speedups across multiple matched-prefix lengths---8K, 16K, and 32K. The speedup grows with prefix length, reaching $9.1$--$14.3\times$ over full prefill at 32K.

</details>


### [317] [Context Staircase: Signature-Aligned Dynamics of Token Embeddings under Small Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30315)
*Junjie Yao,Liangkai Hang,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了语言模型中词嵌入的学习遵循"上下文阶梯"规律：从简单的低阶统计特征逐步学习到复杂的高阶上下文特征，并通过梯度流分析解释了这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型通过梯度训练从随机初始化学习词嵌入，但嵌入结构如何动态形成的机制尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示嵌入结构的演化规律及其与数据统计特征和模型架构的关系。

Method: 1. 定义"概率签名"概念，将嵌入结构与token条件标签和上下文分布相关联；2. 通过小初始化下的梯度流分析，推导前馈和自注意力架构的嵌入演化方程；3. 在真实语言模型训练中验证观察结果。

Result: 发现"上下文阶梯"现象：训练早期嵌入首先学习低阶、无上下文的简单统计特征，随后逐步学习涉及越来越多上下文token的高阶复杂特征。这些嵌入结构对任务学习和语义结构融入嵌入空间起重要作用。

Conclusion: 提供了数据统计特征和架构共同塑造语言模型词嵌入的动态解释，揭示了数据统计空间中的隐式偏置：训练从简单的低阶统计关系向复杂的上下文依赖关系渐进发展。

Abstract: Token embeddings are the basic representational units that connect discrete tokens with continuous computation in language models. Although modern language models learn embeddings from random initialization through gradient-based training, the dynamical mechanism by which meaningful embedding structures emerge remains unclear. In this work, we identify that the evolving embedding structures are closely related to token-conditioned label and contextual distributions, which we formalize as probability signatures. We observe a progressive learning process, which we term Context Staircase: embeddings learn the low-order statistic signatures of the data before the high-order ones. More specifically, we observe that early in training they align with the simplest, context-free signature linking a token to its label, and as training proceeds, they progressively reflect signatures involving more and more context tokens. We then analyze the gradient flow of embeddings under small initialization to explain this phenomenon, deriving embedding evolution equations for feed-forward and self-attention architectures. We further extend these observations to real language-model training. Finally, we show that these embedding structures play an important role in both task learning and the incorporation of semantic structure into the embedding space. Overall, our results provide a dynamic explanation of how data statistics and architecture jointly shape token embeddings in language models, and reveal an implicit bias in the space of data statistics: training proceeds from simpler, low-order statistical relations toward increasingly complex, context-dependent ones.

</details>


### [318] [Online Estimation of Dynamic Origin-Destination Matrices Using Reinforcement Learning with Link-Flow Propagation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30317)
*Donggyu Min,Dong-Kyu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合链路流传播引导(LFPG)和近端策略优化(PPO)的在线动态OD矩阵估计方法(LFPG-RL)，通过将聚合的链路流误差转化为OD特定的优势函数修正，解决了传统强化学习方法在处理变化目标链路流轨迹时的标量反馈模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线动态OD矩阵估计需要根据当前观测和网络状态估计OD需求，但后续观测和随机动态网络加载结果存在不确定性。虽然强化学习已应用于此问题，但由于策略离线训练在线部署，需要处理变化的目标链路流轨迹。相同OD需求向量可能需要不同调整，导致传统标量反馈存在模糊性。

Method: 提出LFPG-RL方法，将链路流传播引导(LFPG)集成到近端策略优化(PPO)中。LFPG结合链路流误差敏感性和各OD-时间需求分量对模拟链路流的贡献，将聚合的误差失配转化为OD特定的优势函数塑造，用于PPO的演员网络更新。部署时策略仅需单次前向传播。采用链路传输模型和随机路径选择建模墨尔本干线网络。

Result: 在250个工作日轨迹的15分钟链路流数据上进行评估，LFPG-RL在保留测试轨迹上达到RMSE为4.69、MAPE为20.15%、Pearson相关系数为0.995的性能表现。

Conclusion: LFPG-RL相比现有方法是一种更高效、准确的在线OD需求校准方法，有效解决了传统强化学习方法在处理变化目标链路流轨迹时的反馈模糊问题，为在线动态OD矩阵估计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Online dynamic origin-destination (OD) matrix estimation (DODE) calibrates time-dependent OD demand to reproduce observed link-flow trajectories. In online, OD demand should be estimated from current observations and propagated network states while subsequent observations and stochastic dynamic network loading (DNL) outcomes remain uncertain. Recently, reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising alternative, reducing computational burden by replacing iterative algorithms while being applicable to stochastic environments. However, because the policy is trained offline and deployed online, it must handle varying target link-flow trajectories; since each target trajectory defines the link-flow error used in the reward, the same OD demand vector can require different adjustments, making conventional scalar feedback ambiguous. To address this gap, this study proposes LFPG-RL, which integrates link-flow propagation guidance (LFPG) into proximal policy optimization (PPO). LFPG combines link-flow error sensitivities with the contribution of each OD-time demand component to simulated link flows, transforming aggregate mismatch into OD-specific advantage shaping for PPO actor updates. At deployment, the policy requires only a single forward pass. LFPG-RL is developed and evaluated on 250 weekday trajectories of 15-min link-flow data from a Melbourne arterial network modeled by a link transmission model with stochastic route choice. On held-out trajectories, LFPG-RL achieved an RMSE of 4.69, MAPE of 20.15%, and Pearson correlation of 0.995. These results support the contention that our method is a more efficient and accurate online OD demand calibration method compared to existing ones.

</details>


### [319] [Generative multi-domain transfer learning for fault detection in data-scarce wind turbines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30323)
*Stefan Jonas,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于StarGAN的多域映射方法，解决新部署风力发电机数据稀缺导致的故障检测难题。通过将数据稀缺风电机的SCADA数据映射为多个数据丰富风电机的数据特征，利用预训练正常行为模型实现故障检测，仅需2周数据即可显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正常行为模型在风力发电机故障检测中表现良好，但这些无监督异常检测模型需要充足的正常训练数据。在数据稀缺场景（如新部署风机）下，模型故障检测性能较差。现有研究缺乏针对数据稀缺风电机的有效解决方案。

Method: 提出基于StarGAN的多域生成式映射方法，将数据稀缺风电机SCADA测量数据映射为多个数据丰富风电机的数据特征。在转换过程中保持运行状态，使得数据稀缺域中发生的故障能够被数据丰富域的可靠预训练正常行为模型检测。采用集成融合策略，并提出了用于训练时检测性能不佳的代理指标。

Result: 在严重数据稀缺情况下，该方法可生成与大规模代表性数据集训练模型相当的异常分数。当可用训练数据少于2周时，持续优于稀缺数据训练的模型。仅用2周累积训练数据，异常分数相似度平均比传统微调高16%，比单源域映射高10%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了多域映射在训练数据不具代表性情况下的风力发电机故障检测潜力。该方法为解决新部署风电机数据稀缺问题提供了有效途径，同时也揭示了多域映射面临的挑战，为无监督模型选择提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Normal behavior models have shown promise for reliable fault detection in wind turbines. However, these unsupervised anomaly detection models require sufficient fault-free training data to learn the normal operation behavior of turbines. Under data scarcity, for example in newly deployed wind turbines, these models may result in poor fault detection performance. In this work, we propose a multi-domain generative domain mapping approach based on Star Generative Adversarial Networks (StarGAN) to improve fault detection on data-scarce wind turbines. Our model maps SCADA measurements from a data-scarce turbine to resemble those of several data-rich turbines. By preserving the operational state during translation, faults occurring in a data-scarce domain can be mapped and detected by reliable pre-trained normal behavior models of data-rich domains. Highlighting the benefits of an ensemble fusion strategy, we show that under severe data scarcity our method can produce anomaly scores comparable to models trained on large representative datasets. Our approach can consistently outperform models trained on scarce data when less than 2 weeks of training data are available. With just 2 weeks of accumulated training data, we achieve an anomaly score similarity that is, on average, +16% higher than conventional fine-tuning, and +10% higher than single-source domain mapping. As a step towards unsupervised model selection, we propose a proxy metric that detects poor performance at training time, despite an absence of anomalies. Our study presents the potential and challenges of multi-domain mapping for wind turbine fault detection under unrepresentative training data.

</details>


### [320] [Learning PDE Time-Stepping with Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30328)
*Esha Saha,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经细胞自动机(NCA)的可训练代理模型，用于学习PDE的长时间动力学。该模型学习局部、均匀的更新规则而非一次性映射整个场，在五个典型PDE的长时间预测任务中，多数情况下获得了最低的相对误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PDE数值求解器在不同初始条件下重复求解时计算成本高昂，这促使了学习型代理模型的需求。现有方法在长时间预测中存在精度问题，需要开发能够准确捕捉PDE长时间动力学的新型代理模型。

Method: 提出基于神经细胞自动机(NCA)的代理模型，该模型不采用一次性将整个初始场映射到完整轨迹的方法，而是学习一个小的、局部的、均匀的更新规则，在每个网格单元上相同且重复地应用，这种设计反映了微分算子的局部性特性。

Result: 在五个典型PDE（热传导、平流、Burgers方程、Allen-Cahn方程和Fisher-KPP方程）上进行了基准测试，评估时间域扩展到训练时间域的两倍。与PDE-Net、改进的物理信息神经网络(PINN)和傅里叶神经算子(FNO)三个基线方法相比，该模型在大多数实验中获得了最低的长时域相对误差。

Conclusion: 基于NCA的代理模型通过模仿微分算子的局部性，能够有效学习PDE的长时间动力学，在长时间外推预测任务中展现出优于现有方法的性能，为PDE求解提供了一种高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Classical numerical solvers for partial differential equations (PDEs) are computationally expensive to solve repeatedly across varying initial conditions, motivating the need for learned surrogates. In this paper, we propose a trainable Neural Cellular Automata (NCA) based surrogate model for learning long time PDE dynamics. Rather than mapping an entire initial field to a full trajectory in one shot, our proposed model learns a small, local, homogeneous update rule that is applied identically and repeatedly at every grid cell, mirroring the locality of differential operators. We benchmark this framework against three baselines: PDE - Net, a modified physics-informed neural network (PINN), and a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO), on five canonical PDEs (heat, advection, Burgers, Allen - Cahn, and Fisher - KPP), evaluated at temporal domain two times beyond the training temporal domain. The proposed model achieves the lowest long-horizon relative errors on the majority of the experiments.

</details>


### [321] [Coarse composition suffices: tabular in-context learning for multi-activity antimicrobial peptide profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30337)
*Raunak Kumar,Anuj Pal,Dhruvi Solanki,Parikshit Pareek,Juhi Singh,Jitin Singla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种简单高效的序列特征与TabPFN相结合的方法，在ESCAPE多标签抗菌肽预测基准上达到77.8% mAP-5，超越此前最佳72.1%，证明了简单方法可胜过复杂的结构条件深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗菌肽多标签活性预测方法依赖昂贵且难调参的多模态结构条件深度模型，本文旨在探索简单高效的序列方法是否能够达到或超越复杂方法的效果。

Method: 构建序列特征管道：提取330个可解释序列描述符（包括全局理化标量等），结合TabPFN表格基础模型进行上下文预测；采用标签幂集和概率分类器链两种多标签策略，无需梯度训练和超参数搜索。

Result: 在ESCAPE数据集（82,359条肽段，五个标签）上，标签幂集TabPFN达到mAP-5=77.8%，较此前最佳72.1%提升5.7个百分点；概率分类器链是首个在所有五个标签上同时匹配或超越已发表最佳平均精度的方法；在远缘同源物上提升最大（序列一致性<30%时+11.2分）；仅需10个全局理化标量即可恢复91%的全特征性能。

Conclusion: 简单序列方法在多标签抗菌肽活性预测中能够超越复杂结构条件模型，无需预测结构即可达到优异性能；显式建模标签依赖性对稀有活性有利，并支持从部分正证据中排序下一步实验活动。

Abstract: Antimicrobial peptides (AMPs) often act against multiple pathogen classes, making multi-label activity prediction a more realistic screening target than binary antimicrobial classification. The ESCAPE benchmark formalizes this setting, but leading approaches typically rely on multimodal, structure-conditioned deep models that are costly to train and tune. We show that a simple, sequence-only pipeline can match and surpass these methods by combining 330 interpretable sequence descriptors with TabPFN, a tabular foundation model that performs in-context prediction in a single forward pass without gradient-based training or hyperparameter search. On ESCAPE (82,359 peptides; five labels), a label-powerset TabPFN model achieves mAP-5 = 77.8%, improving on the previously best reported 72.1%. A probabilistic classifier chain is the first method to match or exceed the best published average precision on each of the five labels simultaneously. The gains persist under the prior state-of-the-art single-fold training protocol, indicating they are not a training-set-size artefact, and are largest for remote homologues (+11.2 points below 30% sequence identity). Ablations further show that predicted structure is unnecessary at inference and that performance is not driven by any single descriptor family: ten global physicochemical scalars recover 91% of full-feature performance. Finally, explicitly modelling label dependence yields targeted benefits for scarce activities and supports ranking which activity to assay next from partial positive evidence.

</details>


### [322] [Beyond Churn: Predicting Financial Fragmentation in Retail Banking with Temporal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30364)
*Ananyaa Chopra,Brandon Xu,Brendan Yuen,Lauren Zung,Sarabroop Aulakh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种预测零售银行客户资金碎片化的端到端时序机器学习系统，在客户完全流失前识别其资金向外转移行为，主要分类器测试AUC达0.823，top 1%客户的预测精度达95.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零售银行客户流失预测将客户离弃视为终端二元事件，但客户关系通常更早通过存款、投资和日常活动向外部金融机构部分转移而弱化。这种先行状态被定义为"金融碎片化"，预测此状态可为客户挽留提供更早的干预窗口。

Method: 基于大型零售银行匿名多源数据，使用595,220个客户月度观测样本和346个工程特征（包含客户画像、余额、产品关系、资金流向、宏观经济和竞争对手活动），构建四阶段XGBoost级联模型，依次预测：（1）90天内是否发生外部流出；（2）预期金额；（3）来源产品；（4）目标金融机构。

Result: 主要分类器测试精度-召回曲线下面积（AUC）达0.823；验证集选定阈值下精度86.4%、召回率75.1%、F1分数0.803；按碎片化得分降序排列，top 1%客户预测精度95.3%，top 5%捕获78.7%的流出案例；金额模型94.9%预测落在相邻金额桶内；目标预测27类macro-F1达0.81，来源产品预测加权F1达0.92。

Conclusion: 通过将分析焦点从终端流失提前至资金迁移阶段，该方法为客户挽留决策支持提供了可操作的、可解释的、经济导向的实用基础，实现了从被动响应到主动预防的转变。

Abstract: Retail banking attrition is usually represented as a terminal binary event, even though client relationships often weaken earlier through partial movements of deposits, investments, and recurring activity to external financial institutions. This paper defines that preceding state as financial fragmentation and presents an end-to-end temporal machine-learning system for predicting it before complete disengagement. Using anonymized multi-source data from a large retail bank, the framework predicts whether a valid external transfer or investment event will occur within 90 days. The study uses 595,220 client-month observations, with 346 engineered features combining monthly client profiles, balances, product relationships, prior flow-of-funds behavior, macroeconomic conditions, and competitor activity. A four-stage XGBoost cascade estimates (1) whether an external outflow will occur within 90 days, (2) the expected amount, (3) the originating product, and (4) the destination financial institution. The primary classifier achieved a test precision-recall area under the curve of 0.823. At the validation-selected threshold, it produced 86.4% precision, 75.1% recall, and an F1 score of 0.803. Ranking test observations in descending Stage 1 fragmentation score, the top 1% of clients yielded 95.3% precision, while the top 5% captured 78.7% of observed outflow cases. The amount model placed 94.9% of predictions within an adjacent amount bucket. Destination prediction reached a macro-F1 of 0.81 across 27 classes; source-product prediction achieved a weighted F1 of 0.92. By moving the analytical focus from terminal churn to earlier fund migration, the proposed approach provides a practical foundation for proactive, explainable, and economically informed client-retention decision support.

</details>


### [323] [Mode Connectivity Beyond Classifiers: Evidence from Generative and Contrastive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30366)
*Chengzheyi Yao,Yongzhao Zhang,Yongding Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次将模式连通性从分类模型扩展到生成模型（DDPM）和对比模型（NanoCLIP），提出架构感知的连接构建算法，成功发现了这些复杂模型中独立训练模式间的低损失连续路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模式连通性研究主要局限于分类器模型，现代复杂生成和对比模型是否存在类似的几何性质仍是开放性问题。

Method: 针对DDPM和CLIP的独特架构，提出架构感知的连接构建算法。

Result: 首次在DDPM和NanoCLIP中成功发现独立训练模式间的模式连通性。

Conclusion: 为理解现代生成和对比模型损失景观的几何性质提供了新视角。

Abstract: The loss landscape of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) exhibits highly complex and non-convex properties. Recent studies have revealed the phenomenon of mode connectivity, demonstrating that independently trained network modes can be connected via a continuous low-loss path. However, existing mode connectivity research is predominantly confined to classifier-based models, leaving it an open question whether similar geometric properties exist in modern complex models. In this paper, we extend the boundaries of mode connectivity to generative and contrastive domains (specifically DDPM and NanoCLIP). Addressing the unique architecture of DDPM and CLIP, we propose an architecture-aware connection building algorithm. Extensive empirical results demonstrate for the first time that we successfully discover mode connectivity between independently trained DDPM and NanoCLIP modes. Our work provides a novel perspective for understanding the geometric properties of the loss landscapes in modern generative and contrastive models.

</details>


### [324] [Beat-Synchronous Tokenization for ECG Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30367)
*Ahmed Sameh,Nolan Wilson,Max Enderlein,Yogatheesan Varatharajah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于心跳同步的token化方法，替代传统固定时间窗口分片，在保持性能的同时显著减少序列长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的ECG模型通常使用固定时间窗口对波形进行token化，这种方法会将心跳结构分割在不同的token边界上，破坏了心脏跳动的完整生理结构。

Method: 比较了固定分片方法与三种心跳对齐策略：重采样心跳、自适应池化心跳、以及带有R-R间隔信息的重采样心跳。在两个数据集上进行实验：PTB-XL（10秒12导联诊断分类）和Icentia11k（60秒单导联心律分类）。

Result: 在PTB-XL上，重采样心跳token达到最高macro AUROC（0.8945），AUPRC接近最佳固定分片方法（0.7414），序列长度从100个token降至11.2个。在Icentia11k上，心跳同步token化获得与固定分片相当的AUPRC，且跨运行稳定性更好。

Conclusion: 保持形态的心跳token化方法是固定时间分片的紧凑且有竞争力的替代方案，能够在大幅减少序列长度的同时保持分类性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based electrocardiogram (ECG) models commonly tokenize waveforms into fixed temporal patches. Though convenient, fixed patching can split heartbeat structures across token boundaries. We study beat-synchronous tokenization as a physiologically grounded alternative, comparing fixed patches with three beat-aligned strategies: resampled beats, adaptive pooled beats, and resampled beats augmented with R--R interval information. Experiments span two settings: 10-second 12-lead diagnostic classification on PTB-XL after MIMIC-IV-ECG masked pretraining, and 60-second single-lead rhythm classification on Icentia11k after patient-level contrastive pretraining. On PTB-XL, resampled beat tokens achieve the highest mean macro Area Under the ROC Curve (AUROC; 0.8945) and nearly match the best fixed-patch macro Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC; 0.7414), reducing average sequence length from 100 to 11.2 tokens. On Icentia11k, beat-synchronous tokenizers obtain comparable AUPRC to fixed patching with better stability across runs. These results suggest morphology-preserving beat tokenization is a compact, competitive alternative to fixed temporal patching.

</details>


### [325] [Convergence rates for the RMSprop optimizer with full control of the hyperparameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30382)
*Steffen Dereich,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为RMSprop优化算法提供了首个非渐近误差估计，其误差常数相对于步长、二阶矩衰减参数β和正则化参数ε均一致有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应随机梯度下降方法（如RMSprop、Adam、AdamW）在AI系统中被广泛应用，但其误差估计一直存在开放问题：在凸随机优化问题中，能否提供误差常数相对于超参数不爆炸且一致有界的误差估计？这些方法涉及多个超参数，包括正则化参数ε（防止除零）和二阶矩衰减参数β，缺乏对超参数一致有界的理论保证。

Method: 通过建立RMSprop二阶矩过程的合适逆矩界作为核心创新点，对RMSprop过程的目标函数停止评估的期望进行上界分析。证明适用于所有可允许的步长选择、β参数和ε∈[0,1]（包括ε=0的情况）。

Result: 将RMSprop的目标函数评估误差上界分解为三部分：随训练时间指数衰减的初始化项、阶为γ_n的随机逼近余项、以及阶为(1-β)²的记忆误差项。误差常数在所有可允许超参数选择下一致有界。非渐近误差估计对每个梯度步n=1,2,3,...均成立，且所有误差常数显式给出。

Conclusion: 本质上解决了RMSprop的一致有界误差估计问题，为凸随机优化中自适应SGD方法的理论分析提供了重要突破。所提出的逆矩界技术是分析此类方法的新工具，可能适用于其他自适应优化器的理论分析。

Abstract: Popular adaptive stochastic gradient descent (SGD) methods to train artificial intelligence (AI) systems include the RMSprop, the Adam, and the AdamW optimizers, where the adaptivity parts in Adam and AdamW basically just coincide with RMSprop. Such adaptive methods involve several hyperparameters including the regularization parameter $ε$ (which ensures that one does not divide by 0 and is often chosen to be very close to zero such as $10^{-8}$ in PyTorch by default) and the second moment decay parameter $β$ (which is often chosen to be very close to $1$ such as 0.99 (RMSprop) and 0.999 (Adam and AdamW) in PyTorch by default). Despite the high relevance of such methods, it remains an open research problem to provide error estimates for such methods with the error constants being not exploding but uniformly bounded with the respect to the hyperparameters, even in the situation of convex stochastic optimization problems.
  It is the key contribution of this work to essentially solve this problem for RMSprop. Specifically, we bound the expectation of the stopped evaluation of the objective function at the RMSprop process from above by the sum of an initialization term that decays exponentially in the training time, a stochastic approximation remainder of order $γ_n$, and a memory error of order $( 1 - β)^2$ with the error constants being uniformly controlled over all admissible choices of the step sizes, the second moment decay parameter $β$ and the regularization parameter $ε\in[0,1]$ (also covering $ε=0$). Our non-asymptotic error estimates hold not just for all sufficiently large n but hold for every gradient step $n=1,2,3,...$ with all error constants being explicitly specified. The key innovative new feature in the proof of our analysis are suitable inverse moment bounds for the second moment process in RMSprop.

</details>


### [326] [RSLM: Training-Free Vector Quantization for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30384)
*Rastislav Lenhardt,Teodora Dobos,Thomas Vecchiato,Jiri Isa,Igor Ginzburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RSLM，一种无需训练的向量量化编解码器，将嵌入压缩至每维度1-4比特，在保持或提高召回率的同时降低大规模近似最近邻搜索系统的内存成本、带宽和复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模近似最近邻（ANN）搜索系统通常使用粗粒度分区过滤候选，通过近似评分缩小候选集，然后使用高精度表示（通常≥8比特/维度）重新评分最佳候选。这导致高内存成本和带宽消耗。研究旨在在保持或提高召回率的前提下，显著降低内存需求和系统复杂度。

Method: 提出了RSLM（旋转缩放Lloyd-Max）编解码器族，是一种无需训练的向量量化方法。核心创新包括：1) 残差向量编码方案，对近似评分和重评分阶段均使用残差向量而非完整向量；2) 修正最终重构向量的L₂范数而非仅残差，替代了各向异性损失等复杂方案；3) 高性能实现采用块级级联快速Walsh-Hadamard变换（FWHT）、AVX SIMD优化码本以及缩放因子的隐写编码实现完美缓存行对齐。

Result: 成功将嵌入向量压缩至每维度2-4比特（相比传统≥8比特/维度），在多个基准数据集上保持或提高了召回率。相比通用量化方法，获得了更有利的质量-大小权衡。系统实现了线性的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: RSLM编解码器通过残差化和范数修正，有效解决了最大内积搜索（MIPS）对向量范数的敏感性问题。该方法简单高效，无需训练即可实现显著压缩，同时保持或提升召回率，为大规模ANN搜索系统提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: By introducing RSLM (Rotated Scaled Lloyd-Max), a family of training-free vector quantization codecs compressing embeddings to 1--4 bits per dimension, we reduce memory cost and memory bandwidth of a typical large-scale Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search system, while reducing its complexity and keeping or improving recall across multiple benchmark datasets. State-of-the-art systems filter candidates using coarse partitions, approximately score them to narrow the set, and then rescore the best with higher precision representations (often >=8 bits per dimension). Our relativized codecs can bring this down to 2--4 bits per dimension.
  We use the properties of the ANN system to encode residual vectors instead of full vectors, both for the approximate scoring phase and the rescoring phase. Since Maximum Inner Product Search (MIPS) is very sensitive to vector norms, we correct the $L_2$ norms of quantized vectors. Our major innovation is that we correct the $L_2$ norm of the final reconstructed vector rather than just the residual. Our rescaling replaces more complicated schemes, such as Anisotropic loss. The residualization scheme gives us a more favorable quality vs size trade-off than generic quantization methods.
  Our high-performance implementation leverages a block-wise cascaded Fast Walsh-Hadamard Transform (FWHT) with linear-like complexity, AVX SIMD-optimized codebooks, and a steganographic encoding of scaling factors for perfect cache-line alignment.

</details>


### [327] [DASC: Decay-Aware State Compression for Hybrid Linear-Attention Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30386)
*Yanqi Yu,Pingwei Sun,Jianchao Tan,Tao Zhang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Yao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DASC方法，通过分析不同注意力头和通道的信息保留时间尺度差异，对混合线性注意力模型的循环状态检查点进行压缩，在保持性能的同时大幅降低内存压力和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合线性注意力架构虽然减少了KV缓存增长，但其原位循环状态更新导致前缀重用需要状态检查点，存储这些检查点增加了内存压力，导致更多驱逐和重复预填充，影响了推理效率。

Method: 提出Decay-Aware State Compression (DASC)：分析Gated DeltaNet (GDN)和Kimi Delta Attention (KDA)的衰减结构，识别不同头和通道的保留视野差异；选择长期保留的状态单元，打包成不规则状态检查点布局；在张量并行推理引擎中平衡压缩状态检查点；重用时通过零填充或有界后缀刷新恢复省略单元。

Result: 在Kimi-Linear上，保守的DASC配置压缩KDA循环状态检查点2.63倍，性能接近完全缓存；在固定状态检查点内存预算下，平均TTFT降低42.6%，输入吞吐量提升68.4%；更大压缩比下，后缀刷新能恢复大部分精度损失。Qwen with GDN展现相似的质量-效率趋势，验证了方法从通道级KDA到头级GDN的扩展性。

Conclusion: DASC有效利用了不同注意力头和通道的信息保留时间尺度差异，实现了状态检查点的高效压缩，在保持推理质量的同时显著提升了吞吐量和降低了延迟，为混合线性注意力模型的高效部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Hybrid linear-attention architectures have recently scaled to large open-weight models, offering quality competitive with full attention while substantially reducing key/value (KV) cache growth. However, their in-place recurrent-state updates complicate cache management: prefix reuse requires state checkpoints alongside full-attention KV, while storing state checkpoints in full increases memory pressure, leading to more evictions and repeated prefill. By analyzing the decay structure of Gated DeltaNet (GDN) and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA), we find that different heads and channels retain prefix information over markedly different timescales, which we term \emph{retention horizons}. This variation suggests substantial compression potential in persistent state checkpoints. Building on this observation, we introduce \emph{Decay-Aware State Compression} (DASC), which derives retention horizons from model weights, selects long-horizon state units, and packs them into a ragged state checkpoint layout. To integrate efficiently with tensor-parallel inference engines, DASC furtherly balances compressed state checkpoints across TP ranks. On reuse, DASC either zero-fills omitted units or refreshes them from a bounded suffix with additional compute cost. Across retrieval and end-to-end reasoning benchmarks on Kimi-Linear, conservative DASC configurations remain close to full caching while compressing KDA recurrent state checkpoints by $2.63\times$. Under fixed state checkpoint memory budgets, the resulting capacity gains reduce mean Time to First Token (TTFT) by 42.6\% and improve input throughput by 68.4\%. At larger compression ratio, suffix refresh recovers much of the accuracy lost to more aggressive omission, at the cost of additional replay computation. Qwen with GDN exhibits a similar quality--efficiency trend, showing that DASC extends from channel-wise KDA to head-wise GDN.

</details>


### [328] [Uncertainty of Vision Medical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30390)
*Haoxu Huang,Narges Razavian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了医学领域专用与通用视觉基础模型在不确定性量化方面的表现，发现领域专用预训练能产生更准确的点预测和更高效的共形预测，强调了模型选择和双重不确定性评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险医疗领域对机器学习系统的不确定性估计有严格要求。传统基于概率输出的点预测缺乏形式化覆盖保证，而共形预测能提供有限样本有效性保证的预测集合。目前缺乏对预训练方法、数据规模和领域对点预测和区域预测不确定性量化影响的系统性研究。

Method: 研究比较了医学领域专用视觉基础模型与通用领域视觉基础模型，在视网膜、组织病理学和胸部X光数据集上进行评估。应用多种校准技术，系统分析预训练策略、数据规模和领域对点预测校准和共形预测效率的影响。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1)在高质量领域专用数据集上结合自监督学习的预训练，比通用领域预训练产生更好的点预测校准效果；(2)标准重新校准方法无法完全消除不同数据源训练模型之间的不确定性差异；(3)领域专用基础模型能实现更高效的共形预测。

Conclusion: 研究强调了模型选择的重要性，以及整合点预测和区域预测以增强医学AI系统可靠性和可信度的必要性。提出在医学视觉基础模型发展中需要采用整体方法进行不确定性量化，确保AI决策的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Accurate uncertainty estimation is essential for machine learning systems de- ployed in high-stakes domains such as medicine. Traditional approaches primarily rely on probability outputs from trained models (point predictions), which provide no formal guarantees on prediction coverage and often require additional calibra- tion techniques to improve reliability. In contrast, conformal prediction (region prediction) offers a principled alternative by generating prediction sets with finite- sample validity guarantees, ensuring that the ground truth is contained within the set at a specified confidence level. In this study, we explore the impact of pre-training approach, dataset scale and domain on both point and region-level uncertainty quantification, by studying domain-specific vision medical foundation models vs. general domain vision foundation models. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation across foundation models trained on retinal, histopathological, and Chest X-Rays data, applying various calibration techniques. Our results demonstrate that (1) pre-training on higher-quality domain-specific datasets along with self-supervised learning leads to better-calibrated point predictions than general domain pre-training, (2) stan- dard re-calibration methods alone cannot fully mitigate uncertainty discrepancies across models trained on different data sources, (3) domain-specific foundation model can lead to more efficient conformal prediction. These findings highlight the importance of careful model selection and the inte- gration of both point and region prediction to enhance the reliability and trust- worthiness of medical AI systems. Our work underscores the need for a holistic approach to uncertainty quantification in recent development of medical vision foundation model, ensuring robust and interpretable AI-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [329] [Foundation Models Meet Agriculture: Challenges Beyond Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30392)
*Vishal Nedungadi,Xingguo Xiong,Marc Rußwurm,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统评估发现地球观测基础模型在农业任务中表现不佳，主要受限于模态差异和任务空间复杂性，需开发领域适配的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球粮食安全和可持续气候行动日益依赖可靠的农业监测，而地球观测基础模型虽在遥感领域表现优异，但在农业应用中表现异常不佳，需探究其根本原因。

Method: 系统评估两个关键瓶颈：对两个地球观测基础模型、一个表格数据基础模型和传统监督学习基线进行基准测试，涵盖七个真实农业数据集（产量预测、物候估计、作物分类）。识别预训练-部署模态差距，并沿五个结构轴形式化农业任务空间。

Result: 发现两个关键问题：(1)预训练-部署模态差距——农业任务常需非图像数据模态，地球观测基础模型架构上无法处理，而表格数据基础模型更自然；(2)当前模型难以可靠泛化，导致模型排名在不同评估设置下高度不稳定。

Conclusion: 揭示了通用架构与专业农业下游数据之间的摩擦，为开发下一代领域感知基础模型提供了战略路线图。

Abstract: Global food security and sustainable climate action increasingly rely on robust, scalable agricultural monitoring. Earth observation foundation models have emerged as powerful, label-efficient tools across general remote sensing domains, yet early attempts to deploy them for agricultural applications have yielded surprisingly poor results. We hypothesize that this performance gap stems from the extreme heterogeneity of agricultural landscapes and the inherent inability of current earth observation foundation models to adapt to task-specific nuances. In this work, we systematically evaluate two critical bottlenecks hindering the deployment of foundation models in agricultural tasks, benchmarking two earth observation foundation models, a foundation model designed for tabular data, and conventional supervised baselines across seven real-world agricultural datasets spanning yield prediction, phenology estimation, and crop classification. First, we identify a pretraining-deployment modality gap: agricultural downstream tasks frequently require diverse, non-imagery data modalities that earth observation foundation models are architecturally unequipped to ingest, while a foundation model built for tabular data handles this heterogeneity more naturally. Second, we formalize the agricultural task space across five structural axes to demonstrate why current models fail to generalize reliably, resulting in highly unstable model rankings across evaluation settings. By characterizing these structural and modal gaps, our insights highlight the friction between general-purpose architectures and specialized agricultural downstream data, providing a strategic roadmap for developing the next generation of domain-aware foundation models.

</details>


### [330] [TopGQ: Fast GNN Post-Training Quantization Leveraging Topology Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30394)
*Dain Kwon,Kanghyun Choi,Hyeyoon Lee,Sunjong Park,Seoyong Lee,Sukjin Kim,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TopGQ框架，通过双轴尺度吸收和拓扑感知分组大幅降低GNN量化开销，在保持精度的同时将量化时间缩短一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN量化方法存在显著的量化开销问题，严重限制了其在实际场景中的应用，亟需高效的量化解决方案。

Method: 提出双轴尺度吸收方法，通过将一个维度合并到邻接矩阵实现沿内外维度的激活量化；引入TopPIN作为节点局部结构代理，用于在量化过程中对具有相似拓扑结构的节点进行分组。

Result: TopGQ将量化时间降低一个数量级，同时有效保持了模型精度。

Conclusion: TopGQ框架成功解决了GNN量化中的开销问题，为GNN在实际场景中的部署提供了可行的量化方案。

Abstract: Existing GNN quantization methods suffer from considerable quantization overhead, which severely limits their practical usage in real-world scenarios. To this end, we present TopGQ, an accurate post-training GNN quantization framework, alleviating redundant quantization overhead. We propose dual-axis scale absorption, which enables activation quantization along both the outer and inner dimensions by merging one into the adjacency matrix. On top of that, we introduce TopPIN, a proxy for nodes' local structure, and use it to group nodes with similar topology during quantization. Experimental results show that TopGQ reduces quantization time by an order of magnitude while preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [331] [Locally-Guided Actor-Critic: Training a Goal-conditioned Actor with a Subgoal-aware Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30406)
*Olivier Serris,Stéphane Doncieux,Olivier Sigaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LG-AC方法，通过显式地将价值估计器条件化于中间目标序列来解决目标链问题，在需要复杂目标链的任务中实现了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标条件强化学习在稀疏奖励和长视野任务中表现不佳。现有方法存在以下问题：RIS方法中的正则化项在低维中间目标下会导致目标链问题；基于势能的奖励塑造(PBRS)会在终止状态产生欺骗性奖励；而移除欺骗性奖励的RS方法则牺牲了PBRS的理论保证。

Method: 提出了局部引导演员评论家(LG-AC)方法：(1)显式地将价值估计器条件化于完整的中间目标序列；(2)将价值函数表示为子目标条件价值函数之和，从而实现密集的后见重标记。与RS方法中中间奖励隐含在塑造信号中不同，LG-AC显式地奖励智能体到达中间目标。

Result: 在具有挑战性目标链要求的任务中进行了评估，实验结果显示：动作正则化或奖励塑造在特定情况下会产生低性能，而LG-AC方法在所有任务中均实现了最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式条件化价值估计器于中间目标序列并使用子目标条件价值函数的求和表示，LG-AC有效解决了目标链问题，为目标条件强化学习中的长视野任务提供了一个有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning struggles with long horizons when rewards are sparse. While a planner can provide subgoals to guide a low-level policy, its use at test time may introduce practical subgoal management difficulties. An alternative paradigm utilizes a high-level planner to assist learning, while the policy remains conditioned only on the final goal, enabling planner-free deployment. Among these methods, Reinforcement Learning with Imagined Subgoals (RIS) introduces a regularization term that encourages the policy to take the same actions for the final goal as it does for an intermediate goal. This regularization, however, may lead to goal-chaining issues when intermediate goals are low-dimensional. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) translates plans into an additional reward while ensuring that the optimal policy remains unchanged. Yet, it can generate deceptive rewards in terminal states. We study these failure cases and first propose an alternative reward shaping method (RS) that removes these deceptive rewards at the expense of theoretical guarantees of PBRS. Similar to this RS variant, we then propose another method named Locally-Guided Actor Critic (LG-AC) that rewards the agent for reaching intermediate goals. Unlike RS, where intermediate rewards are implicit in the shaping signal, we explicitly condition a value estimator on the full sequence of intermediate goals but represent the value function as a sum of subgoal-conditioned value functions, enabling dense hindsight relabeling. We evaluate all these methods in tasks with challenging goal-chaining requirements and empirically highlight specific cases in which either action regularization or reward shaping yield low performance, while LG-AC achieves the best overall performance across tasks.

</details>


### [332] [No Equivariant Architecture Covers All Equivariant Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30417)
*Tīkun Ông*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了等变多头自注意力（MHSA）的结构，证明任何通过多项式参数化实现精确等变的固定MHSA架构必然导致表达能力损失，并以$D_4$群为例展示了等变分量数量的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络在处理具有对称性的数据时具有重要应用，但MHSA作为Transformer的核心组件，其等变性质尚未被完整理解。现有工作通过参数化方法构建等变MHSA，但缺乏对其表达能力的理论分析，因此需要研究等变MHSA的完整结构及其表达能力限制。

Method: 1. 从理论上完整刻画等变MHSA的结构：证明了群$G$只能通过对头聚类进行置换来作用，且QK和OV矩阵需满足与群作用相关的等变约束；
2. 分析无约束MHSA的等变轨迹（equivariance locus）的代数结构，证明其由众多Zariski不可约分量组成；
3. 证明任何单一架构至多覆盖一个不可约分量，从而必然损失表达能力；
4. 以$D_4$群作用于正则表示的$C$个副本为例，具体计算不可约分量的数量下界。

Result: 1. 给出了等变MHSA的完整刻画定理；
2. 证明了通过多项式参数化无约束MHSA参数来实现精确等变的任何固定架构都会导致表达能力损失；
3. 对于$G=D_4$作用于$C$个正则表示副本作为token特征空间的情况，证明了8个注意力头时有$Ω(C^{64})$个不可约分量。

Conclusion: 等变MHSA的等变轨迹在约化参数空间中形成了由极多Zariski不可约分量组成的并集，任何单一的参数化架构无法同时覆盖多个分量，这从根本上限制了多项式参数化方法构建等变MHSA的表达能力。该结论为设计等变Transformer架构提供了重要的理论指导。

Abstract: We give a complete characterization of equivariant multi-head self-attention (MHSA): if an MHSA layer is equivariant to a symmetry group $G$, then $G$ can only act by permuting head-clusters, with QK and OV matrices satisfying an equivariance constraint tied to the group action. As a consequence, we prove that any fixed MHSA architecture that achieves exact equivariance by polynomially parameterizing unconstrained MHSA parameters inevitably leads to expressivity loss within the class of equivariant maps: the equivariance locus of unconstrained MHSA forms a union of extremely many Zariski-irreducible components in a reduced parameter space, and any single architecture covers at most one. For $G=D_4$ acting on $C$ copies of the regular representation as the token feature space, we show that there are $Ω(C^{64})$ components for eight attention heads.

</details>


### [333] [Confounding Masquerading as Improvement: A Systematic Evaluation of Offline Reinforcement Learning for Stroke Antithrombotic Treatment in a 129,000-Patient Registry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30442)
*Kihun Rhee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了44,894例急性缺血性卒中患者的离线强化学习方法，发现标准的FQE评估存在奖励嵌入混杂问题，导致虚假的策略改进估计；经过完全去混杂处理后，策略改进不再具有统计学显著性，表明现有离线RL方法在临床决策优化上的效果被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究报道离线强化学习策略在临床结果上优于医生决策，但这些发现可能受到方法学问题的干扰。本文旨在系统评估不同离线RL算法和奖励设计在实际临床数据中的表现，识别潜在的混杂因素。

Method: 使用44,894例2018年后急性缺血性卒中患者的全国性注册数据，进行五个离线RL算法家族和14种奖励设计的系统性部分交叉评估；采用Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE)进行策略评估；识别并处理奖励嵌入混杂问题；使用DML启发的GBM奖励残差化和完全去混杂方法；进行2×2因子分析、T-learner分析和直接复发分析。

Result: 标准FQE显示表面策略改进+0.0069，添加早期神经恶化惩罚后增至+0.0101；但发现终端奖励混杂占观察信号变化的218.6%，去除后甚至超过零假设；经过DML启发的GBM奖励残差化后，FQE估计降至+0.0033(p=0.132)，完全去混杂后为+0.0025(p=0.291)；FQE诊断、T-learner分析和直接复发分析均显示无临床意义的聚合改进；1年mRS因子分析复制了衰减结果。

Conclusion: 离线强化学习在临床决策优化中存在奖励嵌入混杂问题，导致对策略改进效果的高估；经过适当的方法学修正后，现有方法未能证明优于医生决策的临床有意义改进；提供了六步评估检查清单以指导未来研究；NIHSS分层异质性分析可为前瞻性试验设计提供假设。

Abstract: Recent offline reinforcement learning (RL) studies report policies that outperform physician decisions on clinical outcomes. We conduct a systematic, partially crossed evaluation of five offline RL algorithm families and 14 reward designs in 44,894 post-2018 acute ischemic stroke patients from a nationwide registry (N = 129,033).
  Standard Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) yields an apparent policy-improvement estimate of +0.0069; adding an Early Neurological Deterioration penalty increases it to +0.0101. We identify reward-embedded confounding, in which a proxy terminal reward encodes baseline severity and prognosis as well as treatment efficacy. A 2 x 2 factorial analysis finds that terminal reward confounding accounts for 218.6% of the observed signal change, so its removal overshoots the null.
  After DML-inspired GBM reward residualization, the FQE estimate attenuates to +0.0033 (p = 0.132), and full deconfounding yields +0.0025 (p = 0.291). FQE-based diagnostics, T-learner analyses, and direct recurrence analyses converge away from a clinically meaningful aggregate improvement. A 1-year mRS factorial analysis replicates the attenuation. We provide an empirically motivated six-step evaluation checklist. NIHSS-stratified heterogeneity is hypothesis-generating for prospective trial design; hospital-level disagreement does not persist after full reward deconfounding.

</details>


### [334] [PRIME: Mitigating Subgroup Optimization Competition in Shared CTR Top Networks with Plug-in Residual Input-Conditioned Mixture of Expert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30449)
*Heng Yao,Siyun Hou,Tianying Liu,Yulou Shu,Yong He,Chuan Yuan,Kaibin Qiu,Guowei Chen,Jiayu Zhao,Chao Yu,Ke Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出CTR模型中不同语义子群体共享单一MLP会导致梯度竞争，提出PRIME方法，通过低秩残差专家混合模型实现输入条件化，在13种CTR架构上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTR模型的顶层网络通常采用所有样本共享的单一多层感知机，导致异构用户、物品和上下文子群体更新相同参数。学习信号对齐性弱，使得聚合梯度成为竞争方向间的折中。通过实验验证，语义子群体间的梯度余弦相似度比随机分组降低0.23-0.37，证实了这种竞争问题的存在。

Method: 提出PRIME（Plug-in Residual Input-conditioned Mixture of Experts）：1）以Dense映射为锚点，采用零残差初始化确保训练初始时与基线完全匹配；2）输入依赖的路由权重控制低秩专家进行样本特定的logit校正；3）采用多袋聚合和EMA负载偏置稳定条件估计，在保持Dense路径和优化稳定性的同时增加输入依赖的容量。

Result: 在Avazu和Criteo数据集的13种CTR架构上进行评估：中位配对AUC增益分别为+0.0022和+0.0066，LogLoss分别降低0.0011和0.0081。在FiBiNET和DCNv2上，PRIME在全部10个种子级别的AUC比较中均优于APG，且使用更少参数和更低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 函数保持的条件残差能够在保留Dense路径及其优化稳定性的同时，有效增加输入依赖的容量，为解决CTR模型中的参数竞争问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Click-through rate (CTR) models vary in feature-interaction design, yet their top networks usually remain a single multilayer perceptron shared by all examples. Heterogeneous user, item, and context subgroups therefore update the same parameters; weakly aligned learning signals make the aggregate gradient a compromise among competing directions. We study the competition on Avazu with 4 models and 4 semantic fields. Across all architectures, semantic subgroups show lower Top-NN gradient cosine similarity than random groups matched by sample size and label ratio, with reductions of 0.23-0.37.
  This competition motivates input-conditioned experts, but directly replacing an established Dense mapping changes its initial function, sharing pattern, and capacity, obscuring the source of gains. We introduce PRIME (Plug-in Residual Input-conditioned Mixture of Experts), a Dense-anchored mixture of low-rank residual experts. PRIME anchors the original prediction and uses zero-residual initialization to match the Dense baseline exactly at training onset. Input-dependent routing weights low-rank experts for example-specific logit corrections; multi-bag aggregation and EMA load biases stabilize conditional estimation.
  We evaluate PRIME on held-out Avazu and Criteo test sets across 13 CTR architectures and five paired seeds. Median paired AUC gains are +0.0022 and +0.0066, with LogLoss reductions of 0.0011 and 0.0081, respectively. On FiBiNET and DCNv2, PRIME outperforms APG in all ten seed-level AUC comparisons while using fewer parameters and lower inference latency on both backbones. These results show that function-preserving conditional residuals add input-dependent capacity while preserving the Dense path and its optimization stability. Code is available at https://github.com/YH-learning/PRIME.

</details>


### [335] [Self-Supervised Pretext Tasks for Infant Cry Analysis: A Controlled Comparison and a Cautionary Result on Donateacry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30456)
*Luigi Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定预算下比较了六种自监督预训练任务用于婴儿哭声分析。重构目标在哭声检测中表现优异（AUC 0.988），但在哭声原因分类任务上所有编码器均表现不佳。发现现有文献报告的高准确率源于评估协议中的数据泄露问题，去除泄露后模型表现接近随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴儿哭声分析领域缺乏公平的自监督学习方法比较研究，且现有基准测试中存在评估协议缺陷。研究旨在统一框架下比较不同预训练任务，并揭示现有文献中数据泄露导致的虚假高性能问题。

Method: 在固定预算（1.17M参数编码器、115小时授权验证的公开预训练音频）下比较六种自监督预训练任务。采用两种评估协议：主体划分（避免数据泄露）和片段划分。引入数据增强技术，使用声码器说话人扰动和噪声混合进行二十倍数据扩充。

Result: 哭声检测：重构目标占优，掩码频谱图编码器线性探测达0.988 AUC。哭声原因分类：所有编码器表现接近随机（0.38-0.54 macro AUC），领域适应和端到端微调无改善。去除数据泄露后，文献报告的90%+准确率降至85.2%（接近83.8%多数类基线），该任务有效样本量为婴儿数量而非片段数。

Conclusion: 哭声原因分类任务的瓶颈在于标签质量而非模型容量。现有文献中的高准确率源于评估协议中的数据泄露。在无泄露的评估框架下，数据增强对跨主体泛化无帮助，该任务需要更多独立婴儿样本而非更多片段。

Abstract: We compare six self-supervised pretext tasks for infant cry analysis under a fixed budget, meaning the same compact encoder of 1.17M parameters, the same 115 hours of license-verified public pretraining audio, and the same evaluation protocol for every candidate. On cry detection the reconstructive objectives dominate, and a linear probe over a masked-spectrogram encoder reaches 0.988 AUC with subject-wise splits even though the encoder never observed a cry during pretraining. On cry-reason classification over donateacry, the de facto public benchmark for cry reasons, every encoder performs at chance (0.38 to 0.54 macro AUC over 5 classes), and neither domain adaptation on 1.8 hours of real cries nor end-to-end fine-tuning moves the result. Since a frozen HuBERT-base with 80 times more parameters shows the same pattern, the bottleneck must sit in the labels and not in model capacity. We then reproduce the 90\%+ accuracies of the donateacry literature on our own system by changing nothing but the evaluation protocol: clip-wise splits raise accuracy to 85.2% (barely above the 83.8% majority-class baseline), and applying augmentation before splitting raises it to 97.9%, matching the reported state of the art, from the same model that measures 0.49 macro AUC under subject-wise splits. Under leakage-free splits, a twentyfold augmentation of the labeled set (vocoder speaker perturbation and noise mixing, 21 hours) leaves cross-subject AUC unchanged: for this task the effective sample size is the number of infants. We release code, seeds and per-clip license manifests.

</details>


### [336] [Learning Where Outcomes Change:Credit-Addressable Reasoning for Multimodal Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30457)
*Jiani Guo,Junjie Wang,Jie Wu,Pengxiang Zhao,Dongdong Zhang,Shaohan Huang,Yujiu Yang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Code-CoT和CE-GRPO方法，通过可寻址代码表示和事件级强化学习优化多模态几何推理，在九个基准上平均准确率达76.04%，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在多模态几何推理中存在两个关键问题：(1)自由形式的推理轨迹掩盖了决定答案的关键决策，缺乏可解释性；(2)轨迹级强化学习将单一的终端奖励信号分配到整个响应序列，无法精确识别和优化关键推理步骤。这导致模型难以进行长链条、高依赖性的多模态推理。

Method: 提出"信用可寻址推理"原则，使推理过程中的语义单元同时定义学习过程中的信用分配位置。具体实现包括：(1)Code-CoT：将视觉关系表示为行可寻址的可执行代码，保留几何图形信息，将推理组织为类型化事件；(2)CE-GRPO：利用结构先验和类型归一化熵选择事件边界，从共享前缀采样完整续写，将结果差异转化为局部优势信号，实现精确的事件级信用分配。

Result: 在九个几何推理基准测试中，CE-GRPO平均准确率达76.04%，分别比Qwen3-VL-8B和轨迹级GRPO高出8.09和3.43个百分点。随着中间事件数量增加，其相对优势更加显著，验证了表示-优化协同设计在长链条、高依赖性多模态推理中的价值。

Conclusion: 通过将可执行代码表示与事件级强化学习相结合，实现了表示与优化的协同设计，有效解决了多模态几何推理中的信用分配问题，为长链条依赖推理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Multimodal geometry reasoning requires VLMs to extract precise visual relations and preserve them through multi-step deduction. Existing free-form traces obscure the decisions that determine the answer, and trajectory-level reinforcement learning distributes a single terminal signal across the entire response. We introduce credit-addressable reasoning, in which the semantic units exposed during inference also define where learning compares alternatives and assigns credit. We instantiate this principle with Code-CoT, which retains the diagram, represents visual relations as line-addressable executable code, and organizes reasoning into typed events, and CE-GRPO, which selects event boundaries using structural priors and type-normalized entropy, samples complete continuations from shared prefixes, and converts outcome differences into localized advantages. Across nine geometry benchmarks, CE-GRPO achieves an average accuracy of 76.04, outperforming Qwen3-VL-8B and trajectory-level GRPO by $8.09$ and 3.43 points, respectively. Its relative advantage increases with the number of intermediate events, demonstrating the value of representation--optimization co-design for long, dependency-heavy multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [337] [ToxLens: A Reproducible Graph-Learning Framework for Leakage-Aware, Uncertainty-Calibrated Molecular Toxicity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30472)
*Magnus H. Strømme,Alex G. C. de Sá,David B. Ascher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ToxLens，一种针对11种毒性终点的多任务图学习框架，通过泄漏控制的数据划分策略，在测试集上实现MCC 0.44、AUROC 0.83的预测性能，并支持毒性结构警示的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分子毒性预测基准测试往往因训练集和测试集之间存在结构相似的分子而高估模型的实际效用，这种数据泄漏问题导致模型性能被夸大，影响了模型在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 提出ToxLens框架，包含保守的化学数据整理、球体排除过滤、泄漏感知的UMAP-HDBSCAN数据划分、并行图编码器和全局特征编码器的晚期拼接融合、温度缩放的蒙特卡洛dropout结合保形预测集、适用域分析，以及SHAP引导的毒性团发现与遮挡控制。

Result: 在泄漏控制的测试集上，五种子软投票集成模型达到MCC 0.44、AUROC 0.83、AUPRC 0.58，在所有11个终点上均超越四种ECFP4浅层基线模型。消融实验显示全局特征编码通路重要，晚期拼接融合优于门控融合和FiLM融合变体。SHAP引导的遮挡分析和共识子图挖掘产生了44个符合反事实标准的结构假设。

Conclusion: ToxLens通过严格的泄漏控制策略提供了更可靠的毒性预测性能评估，模型校准和判别能力随与训练域相似度提升而改善，同时能够自动发现具有可解释性的毒性结构警示，为毒性预测的实际应用提供了更可信的工具。

Abstract: Molecular toxicity prediction is increasingly used to prioritise compounds before experimental testing, but conventional benchmark performance can overstate practical utility when structurally related molecules occur across training and test folds. We introduce ToxLens, a reproducible multi-task graph-learning framework for 11 toxicity endpoints spanning Ames mutagenicity, acute oral toxicity, hERG inhibition, and Tox21 nuclear-receptor and stress-response assays. The workflow combines conservative chemical curation, sphere-exclusion filtering, a leakage-aware UMAP-HDBSCAN split, parallel graph and global-feature encoders joined by late concatenation, temperature-scaled Monte Carlo dropout with conformal-style prediction sets, applicability-domain analysis, and SHAP-guided toxicophore discovery with occlusion controls. On the leakage-controlled test fold, a five-seed soft-voting ensemble achieved a Matthews correlation coefficient score of 0.44, an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve score of 0.83, and an area under the precision-recall curve score of 0.58. It exceeded four ECFP4-based shallow baselines on all 11 endpoints under the same split and validation-based threshold-selection protocol. Controlled ablations showed that the global pathway was important, whereas late concatenation outperformed the tested gated and feature-wise linear modulation fusion variants. Conformal-style prediction sets revealed substantial endpoint-specific variation in set efficiency, and discrimination and calibration improved with similarity to the training domain. Retraining on fixed published Tox21 Challenge and TDA folds produced competitive, but not uniformly state-of-the-art, performance. SHAP-guided occlusion and consensus subgraph mining yielded model-derived structural hypotheses, 44 of which contained at least one occurrence that passed the predefined counterfactual criteria.

</details>


### [338] [Measuring Memory and Generalization as Separable Geometric Channels: The Topo^2 Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30487)
*Zhanbo Zhang,Ming Liu,Qing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Topo^2测量框架，利用持久同调H1结构将噪声标签下的泛化与记忆分离，发现记忆是可测量、可分离、可逆的拓扑层，并建立了记忆-泛化的定量定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在噪声标签上训练时会同时泛化干净数据并记忆翻转标签，现有研究将二者混淆为单一容量压力，缺乏对记忆与泛化关系的清晰理解与测量手段。

Method: 提出Topo^2框架，利用表示空间的持久同调H1结构分离出类内流形通道（训练停止点的函数）和跨类通道（记忆翻转样本的单调读出）；设计FM0干预方案（从epoch 0对翻转样本零损失）以分离泛化与记忆。

Result: 验证了分级定律：(L2)FM0分离方案在9/9设置中有效；(L1)类内通道呈现中阶上升特性；(L3)环形构造恒等式；TLS记忆-泛化拓扑分层定律表明记忆具有因果可加性、锚定性、可逆性和可计费性，记忆成本定律的有效斜率系数C约0.38。

Conclusion: 该框架将"记忆"从模糊概念转化为可测量、可分离、可逆的拓扑层，揭示了记忆与泛化的因果关系和定量规律，并提供了可证伪的边界条件。

Abstract: Deep networks trained on noisy labels simultaneously generalize on clean data and memorize flipped labels. These are usually conflated as pressures on one capacity. We present Topo^2, a measurement framework that makes them causally separable, measurable, and law-governed. Persistent-homology H1 structure of the representation space separates into a within-class manifold channel (a function of the training stopping point) and a cross-class channel (a monotone readout of memorized flipped samples). An intervention, the FM0 prescription (zero loss on flipped samples from epoch 0), reaches each setting's generalization ceiling while memorizing essentially nothing. Within the framework we establish a law set with graded evidence: (L2) FM0 separation prescription (9/9); (L1) the within-channel as a training-position function (mid-rise 6/6; convergence-back CIFAR 3/3, SVHN 2/3); (L3) a ring-construction identity (definitional, not a law); and TLS (memory-generalization topological layering): memory is causally additive, anchored (silencing clean collapses the representation), invertible (stripping memory restores near-ceiling generalization), and quantitatively billable (the memorization cost law, effective slope coefficient C ~ 0.38 at the reference capacity: CIFAR-10 0.3801 / SVHN 0.3806 / CIFAR-100 0.384 / VGG 0.3715, capacity-dependent in general and traced to clean-sample feature displacement). We also publish the framework's boundaries: a falsification ledger of nine dead ends, and an instrument-vindication section that excludes six families of global statistics as explanations of the within-channel. The framework turns "memorization" from an ill-defined capacity into a measurable, separable, invertible topological layer.

</details>


### [339] [When the Martingale Never Stops Firing: Anytime-Valid Gating on Real Forecast Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30502)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究揭示，基于可交换性假设的anytime-valid监控方法在非独立同分布的真实时序数据上会产生严重的虚警问题，进而提出需要针对依赖数据的监控方法增加零校准控制和机制追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统越来越多地依赖统计监控器来决定干预时机，anytime-valid推理为此提供了理论基础。然而，现有监控方法依赖可交换性假设，而该假设在监控器最需要发挥作用的环境——依赖数据和学习-监控闭环中——最难满足，且很少被实际测量。因此，作者旨在评估该假设在实际部署中的有效性，并分析假设失效带来的后果。

Method: 作者设计了一个预先指定的案例研究：使用基于共形测试鞅的监控器来控制Kalman适配器对冻结时序基础模型的在线更新。实验在两个场景下进行评估：满足可交换性的合成数据流和真实预测数据流。监控器基于Ville不等式保证虚警概率上限，在α=0.05的水平下进行检验。作者还引入了Huber风格的门控机制作为对比方案，以分析系统行为。

Result: 在可交换的合成数据流上，监控器在60次运行中最多仅触发1次，符合理论预期。但在真实数据流上，135次干净流运行全部触发（135/135），虚警率达到100%。这种频繁触发会激活漂移响应，而门控滤波器反而放大了原本要抑制的瞬态误差。作者进一步分析发现，Huber式门控机制在无数据集特定调参的情况下，可将隔离尖峰衰减降低一个数量级，且该组件本身不作有效性声明。

Conclusion: 可交换性假设在真实部署环境中经常失效，导致anytime-valid监控方法产生严重虚警问题，甚至产生适得其反的效果。因此，针对依赖数据提出的anytime-valid方法应当配套零校准控制和机制追踪，以确保其在实际应用中的可靠性。同时，不应盲目依赖理论上限，而应通过实证评估检验假设前提是否满足。

Abstract: Machine learning systems are increasingly corrected while they run, and the decision of when to intervene is increasingly delegated to statistical monitors. Anytime-valid inference promises evidence that can be acted on at any moment, exactly the guarantee this setting needs, and it is moving from theory into deployed monitoring. Conformal test martingales are the change-detection instrument, and Ville's inequality caps their false-alarm probability on exchangeable data. The guarantee is conditional. A deployment inherits it only if the stream it monitors behaves exchangeably. The premise is hardest to satisfy where these monitors are most useful, on dependent data and inside loops where the monitor modifies the learner whose scores it reads. It is also rarely measured. We measure it in a pre-specified case study, where such a monitor gates the online updates of a Kalman adapter correcting frozen time-series foundation models on five forecasting streams. On exchangeable synthetic streams, the same implementation fires in at most 1 of 60 runs. On the real streams, at alpha = 0.05, 135 of 135 clean-stream runs fired. The construction does not explain the firing; the failure comes from the deployed score stream itself. Repeated fires hold the gate's drift response active, and the gated filter amplifies the very transient it was designed to prevent. The component worth keeping makes no validity claim. Huber-style gating of the filter's own updates cuts isolated-spike degradation by an order of magnitude with no dataset specific tuning. Anytime-valid methods proposed for dependent data should therefore be accompanied by null-calibration controls and mechanism traces.

</details>


### [340] [Tensor Methods for Language Models: From Token Representation to Training, Adaptation, Inference, Compression, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30505)
*Matvei Tarasov,Salman Ahmadi-Asl,Andre L. F. de Almeida,Andrzej Cichocki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于张量方法在大语言模型中应用的综述，通过七阶段生命周期分类法和组件视角组织张量方法，提供统一符号和理论基础，并引入压缩-实现差距指标$\rho_{\rm gap}$来衡量理论内存减少与实际系统级加速之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型由token表示、权重、适应更新、缓存和激活等结构化高维对象构成，其多线性结构在传统矩阵中心视角下未被充分利用。张量分解和张量网络为这种结构提供了原则性的代数语言，但现有文献常将其视为孤立的压缩机制。需要系统性地组织张量方法，将其作为统一的结构原理。

Method: 通过两个互补视角组织张量方法：1）七阶段生命周期分类法，涵盖token化、嵌入、预训练、适应、压缩、推理和可解释性；2）组件视角，涵盖嵌入、注意力和前馈网络。提供统一符号和理论基础，分析各Transformer组件的张量化策略，对比各生命周期阶段的方法，明确评估协议和模型规模的差异。同时关联相邻效率技术和概率张量网络。

Result: 提供了张量化语言模型的结构化切入点，明确了参数节省何时能合理转化为内存效率、计算效率或可解释性。引入$\rho_{\rm gap}$指标衡量理论内存减少与实测系统级加速之间的压缩-实现差距，连接了张量方法与相邻效率技术。

Conclusion: 通过将张量化视为共同的结构原理，本综述阐明了张量方法在LLMs中的系统性应用框架，揭示了参数压缩到实际系统效率转化的条件，为大语言模型的结构化优化提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are built from structured high-dimensional objects such as token representations, weights, adaptation updates, caches, and activations, whose multilinear structure is underexploited by the conventional matrix-centric view. Tensor decompositions and tensor networks provide a principled algebraic language for this structure, yet the literature often treats them as isolated compression mechanisms. This survey organizes tensor methods for LLMs through two complementary views: a seven-stage lifecycle taxonomy covering tokenization, embeddings, pre-training, adaptation, compression, inference, and interpretability, and a component view covering embeddings, attention, and feed-forward networks. We provide unified notation and theoretical foundations, analyze tensorization strategies for individual Transformer components, and compare methods at each lifecycle stage while making differences in evaluation protocols and model scales explicit. We further connect tensor methods to neighboring efficiency techniques and probabilistic tensor networks. Finally, we synthesize open challenges and introduce $ρ_{\rm gap}$, a metric for the compression-realization gap between theoretical memory reduction and measured system-level speedup. By treating tensorization as a common structural principle, the survey provides a structured entry point to tensorized language models and clarifies when parameter savings can plausibly translate into memory efficiency, computational efficiency, or interpretability. The GitHub page dedicated to this paper is accessible at \href{https://github.com/ma-tt-a/awesome-tensor-methods-for-llms}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [341] [Trajectory-Initialized Neural Double Q-Routing for Large-Scale Overhead Hoist Transport Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30512)
*Cheng Gu,Qiusheng Zhao,Anbang Liu,Shaochong Lin,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neural Double Q-routing方法，通过神经网络替代传统Q表并采用离线预训练+在线优化的策略，在大型OHT系统中显著降低任务完成时间，尤其在150-200台车辆的高负载场景下表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模工业机器人车队共享受限的物理基础设施，车辆行驶时间受安全间距、交叉口通行、下游阻塞和站点竞争等时变交通成本影响。传统静态最短路径路由无法考虑这些动态因素，而表格Q路由虽然能在线适应，但需要独立学习每个目的地-节点-动作价值，限制了稀疏路由场景下的信息共享，且启动性能对不准确的价值估计敏感。

Method: 提出Neural Double Q-routing方法：1) 用共享的状态-动作价值网络替代按目的地索引的Q表；2) 通过在混合仿真生成的路由轨迹上进行return-to-go回归实现网络热启动；3) 在线采用Double-Q更新、局部拥塞校正和事件分层结构化经验回放进行优化。

Result: 在9个匹配的车队规模-到达率设置（100、150、200台OHT）中，相比表格Double Q-routing平均完成时间降低0.8%-8.8%。在6个150和200台OHT设置中达到所有对比方法中的最低平均完成时间（100台OHT设置下Dijkstra仍最优）。在9个设置中有8个完成的任务数量保持在表格Double Q-routing的1%范围内，8个设置中95分位完成时间下降。在两个匹配的启动场景中，离线初始化使完成任务数增加最多23%，尾部完成时间减少最多15%。

Conclusion: Neural Double Q-routing成功解决了传统表格Q路由在稀疏路由场景下信息共享受限和启动性能敏感的问题，在高负载场景下显著优于传统方法，同时保持了稳定的任务完成能力和更低的尾部延迟，验证了神经网络近似与离线预训练+在线优化框架的有效性。

Abstract: Large-scale industrial robot fleets share constrained physical infrastructure, making vehicle travel times dependent on safety separation, intersection access, downstream blocking, and station contention. We study this problem in overhead hoist transport (OHT) systems, a representative ceiling-mounted material-handling system used in semiconductor fabs. Static shortest-path routing cannot account for these time-varying traffic costs, whereas tabular Q-routing adapts online but learns each destination--node--action value independently, limiting information sharing across sparsely visited routing contexts and making startup behavior sensitive to inaccurate value estimates. We propose Neural Double Q-routing, which replaces destination-indexed tables with a shared state--action value network. The network is warm-started through return-to-go regression on mixed simulator-generated routing trajectories and then refined online using Double-Q updates, local congestion correction, and event-stratified structured replay. Across nine matched fleet-size--arrival-rate settings with 100, 150, and 200 OHTs, the proposed framework reduces mean completion time relative to tabular Double Q-routing by $0.8\%$--$8.8\%$. It achieves the lowest mean completion time among all compared methods in the six 150- and 200-OHT settings, whereas Dijkstra remains best in the three 100-OHT settings. Completed-task counts remain within $1\%$ of tabular Double Q-routing in eight of nine settings, and 95th-percentile completion time decreases in eight settings. In two matched startup scenarios, offline initialization increases the number of completed tasks by up to $23\%$ and reduces tail completion time by up to $15\%$.

</details>


### [342] [PAC: Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum for Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30528)
*Yuanqiang Yu,Yanzhao Zheng,Zhentao Zhang,Tianze Xu,Chao Ma,Jihuai Zhu,Jiashun Liu,Xinle Deng,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAC方法，结合优势信号和奖励增益实现在线课程学习，显著提升LLM强化学习后训练的样本效率和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练管道依赖固定或人工设计的任务混合，忽略了任务有效性随训练进度动态变化的特性。现有在线课程方法仅通过更新幅度定义可学习性，忽视了更新是否真正带来奖励提升，导致rollout预算错误分配到产生大但无效更新的任务上。

Method: 提出PAC（Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum），结合两个任务级别信号：1）优势派生的可学习性，衡量任务能诱导的策略更新幅度；2）近期奖励增益，显示这些更新是否提升了任务性能。采用贝叶斯Thompson Sampling控制器，在GRPO训练期间基于这些信号在任务间分配rollout。

Result: 在多层次推理和多领域推理两种设置下评估，PAC以更少的rollout步数达到可比验证分数，在两种设置下均实现比随机采样和基于优势的课程学习基线更高的最终平均值。

Conclusion: 联合追踪优势信号和实际奖励增益，为LLM后训练提供了有效的在线课程学习策略，证明了同时考虑策略更新潜力和实际性能提升的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is used to improve the reasoning abilities of LLMs, while training data span heterogeneous tasks. However, most RL post-training pipelines rely on fixed or manually designed task mixtures, even though task usefulness changes as training progresses. Online curriculum methods often define learnability by update magnitude, ignoring whether the update translates into reward gains, which can misallocate rollout budget toward tasks with large but ineffective updates. We propose PAC, a Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum for multi-task RL of LLMs that combines two task-level signals: advantage-derived learnability, which measures the magnitude of the policy update a task can induce, and recent reward gains, which show whether those updates have improved task performance. A Bayesian Thompson Sampling controller uses these signals to allocate rollouts across tasks during GRPO training. We evaluate PAC under two settings: a multi-level reasoning setting and a multi-domain reasoning setting. PAC improves sample efficiency and final performance: it reaches comparable validation scores with fewer rollout steps and achieves higher final averages than random sampling and advantage-based curriculum baselines in both settings. These results show that jointly tracking advantage signals and actual reward gains yields an effective online curriculum for LLM post-training.

</details>


### [343] [Q-Strata: Hierarchical Bit Allocation for Mixed-Precision Quantization of Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30564)
*Deokjae Lee,Sihun Chu,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Q-Strata，一种面向MoE模型的混合精度量化双层分配器，通过在块内使用代理排序、在模型级直接优化目标，解决了MoE模型参数空间巨大的分配难题，在多个模型上显著降低了量化后的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度量化(MPQ)需要为每个线性层分配不同位宽以最小化质量损失，但在Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)模型中，每个专家模块都包含线性层，导致分配空间远大于密集模型。现有方法要么在块内统一预算下分配，要么通过可加代理跨块分配，都无法直接优化模型级目标，忽视了块间耦合关系。

Method: 提出Q-Strata双层分配器：内层阶段使用低成本代理对块内分配进行排序，缓存每个块在细粒度预算下的帕累托前沿候选方案；外层阶段将搜索简化为每个块设定一个预算值，直接在组装的量化模型上优化模型级目标函数，有效捕获块间耦合关系。

Result: 在Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct、Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite三个模型上，Q-Strata在低位宽机制下均实现了比统一位宽GPTQ及现有先进MoE MPQ方法(MxMoE和GEMQ)更低的WikiText2困惑度。

Conclusion: Q-Strata通过双层优化框架成功解决了MoE模型的混合精度量化分配问题，相比现有方法能够更有效地捕获块间耦合关系，在实际模型上取得了优越的性能表现，为大规模MoE模型的高效量化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization (MPQ) assigns a different bitwidth to each linear layer of a large language model (LLM) to minimize the quantization-induced quality loss under a fixed budget, but Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models contain these layers in every expert of every MoE block, so the allocation space grows far larger than in a dense model. Existing methods either allocate within each block under a uniform per-block budget, or allocate across blocks through an additive proxy, and neither directly optimizes a model-level objective over the choices that couple the blocks. We propose Q-Strata, a bi-level allocator that ranks within-block assignments with a cheap proxy and allocates across blocks with a model-level objective evaluated on the assembled quantized model. Its inner stage caches a Pareto frontier of candidates per block over finely spaced budgets, leaving the outer stage to set one budget per block instead of a bitwidth for every linear layer. With the search reduced to one budget per block, the outer stage optimizes this model-level objective directly, capturing the inter-block coupling that additive proxies miss. On Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct, Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B, and DeepSeek-V2-Lite, Q-Strata consistently achieves lower WikiText2 perplexity than uniform-bitwidth GPTQ and the state-of-the-art MoE MPQ methods MxMoE and GEMQ in the low-bit regime. The code is available at https://github.com/snu-mllab/Q-Strata/tree/main.

</details>


### [344] [Collapsibility of Performance Metrics in Clinical Predictive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30568)
*João Matos,Ben Van Calster,Richard D. Riley,Paula Dhiman,Gary S. Collins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了预测AI中15个常用性能指标的可折叠性，发现AUC、校准截距、校准斜率、期望校准误差和Nagelkerke R²这5个指标不可折叠，可能导致公平性评估产生误导性结果，建议明确报告指标的可折叠性质以提高透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体层面的预测AI性能评估可能掩盖子群体间的性能差异。虽然公平性评估通常依赖于子群体性能分析，但某些性能指标具有不可折叠性，即整体性能值不等于各子群体值的加权平均，这一问题需要深入研究。

Method: 研究15个性能指标的可折叠性。对于可折叠指标，将其表示为分层特定值的线性组合；对于不可折叠指标，提供受辛普森悖论启发的反例作为形式化反证。重点关注接收者操作特征曲线下面积（AUC）。

Result: AUC、校准截距、校准斜率、期望校准误差和Nagelkerke R²这5个指标不可折叠；O:E比率、对数损失、Brier分数、准确率、F1分数等10个指标可折叠。AUC不可折叠是因为当子群体共存时，它分解为群体内和跨群体AUC项，导致整体值可能落在子群体特定AUC范围之外。

Conclusion: 性能指标的不可折叠性对报告、模型评估和公平性评估具有重要影响，可能产生子群体与整体性能间的虚假差异，从而误导公平性评估。明确承认并报告性能指标的可折叠性质，可提高公平性评估的可解释性和透明度。

Abstract: Background: Population level assessments of predictive artificial intelligence (AI) can conceal performance disparities across subgroups. Fairness evaluations commonly rely on performance analyses across subgroups. However, some performance metrics are non-collapsible, meaning that the overall population performance value does not equal the weighted average of subgroup specific values.
  Objective: To examine the collapsibility properties of commonly reported performance metrics in predictive AI, with a focus on the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC, also known as c-statistic).
  Methods: We investigate the collapsibility of 15 performance metrics, either by expressing each metric as a linear combination of its stratum specific values or, where non-collapsible, by providing a counterexample inspired by Simpson's paradox as a formal disproof.
  Results: Five performance metrics (AUC, calibration intercept, calibration slope, expected calibration error, and Nagelkerke R^2) are shown to be non-collapsible, and ten (O:E ratio, logloss, Brier score, accuracy, F1-score, true positive rate, true negative rate, positive predictive value, negative predictive value, and net benefit) are shown to be collapsible. The AUC is shown to be non-collapsible because it decomposes into within- and cross-group AUC terms when subpopulations coexist, such that its overall value may fall outside the range of subgroup specific AUCs.
  Conclusions: Non-collapsibility of performance metrics has important consequences for reporting, model appraisal, and fairness evaluation. It can generate spurious differences between subgroup and overall performance, which may mislead fairness evaluations. Explicitly acknowledging and reporting the collapsibility properties of performance metrics improves both the interpretability and transparency of fairness assessments.

</details>


### [345] [The Safety Relay in Roleplay Jailbreaks: A Component-Resolved Causal Analysis of Harm Recognition and Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30585)
*Md Mokarram Chowdhury,Ernie Chang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机械可解释性分析揭示了角色扮演越狱攻击如何通过破坏安全机制来诱导大语言模型产生有害内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被训练为遵循指令同时拒绝有害请求，但角色扮演越狱攻击特别令人担忧：有害请求可见于由角色、场景和任务组成的角色扮演包装中，模型却可能遵从。研究旨在理解这种上下文如何逆转拒绝行为以及哪些元素促成了这种逆转。

Method: 研究采用机械可解释性方法，在两个基准测试、三个模型家族和四个自编包装器上进行实验。通过比较有无包装器的匹配有害和良性请求，追踪从请求到最终提示状态的隐藏状态对比，通过受控反事实隔离包装器操作，干预其激活方向，并几何分解有效方向。

Result: 发现三个关键结果：(1)成功攻击保留了请求时的有害-良性区分，但拒绝相关表达在答案开始处减弱，称为"安全中继衰减"；(2)完整构建角色扮演和场景框架对攻击成功有因果贡献，移除相关激活变化可恢复拒绝；(3)这些效应主要共享内部结构，大部分修复可通过模型普通拒绝机制的组件复现，场景框架保留较小的模型依赖成分。

Conclusion: 研究解释了角色扮演如何在保留有害证据的情况下产生遵从行为，并为未来安全措施确定了具体目标：维持从伤害识别到拒绝的连接。这为理解和防御越狱攻击提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to follow instructions while refusing harmful requests. Jailbreaks exploit this balance to elicit content a model would ordinarily reject. Roleplay jailbreaks are especially concerning: the harmful request can remain visible inside a roleplay wrapper made of a persona, scenario, and task, yet the model may comply. We use mechanistic interpretability to determine how this context reverses refusal and which elements contribute to the reversal.
