Across two benchmarks, three model families, and four authored wrappers, we compare matched harmful and benign requests with and without this wrapper. We trace hidden-state contrasts from the request to the final prompt state, isolate wrapper operations through controlled counterfactuals, intervene on their activation directions in held-out evaluation requests, and decompose effective directions geometrically.
  Our analysis yields three findings. (1) Successful attacks retain the measured harmful-versus-benign distinction at the request, while its refusal-associated expression weakens where the answer begins, a pattern we call safety-relay attenuation. (2) Constructing the complete roleplay around the request and framing it within the scenario contribute causally: removing the associated activation changes restores refusal. (3) These effects largely share internal structure, and most repair is reproduced by components aligned with the model's ordinary refusal of harmful requests without roleplay; scenario framing retains a smaller, model-dependent component. Together, these findings explain how roleplay can produce compliance despite retained evidence of harm and identify a concrete target for future safeguards: maintaining the connection from harm recognition to refusal.

</details>


### [346] [State of Health Estimation using Convolutional and Bidirectional LSTM Neural Networks tuned by Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30593)
*Panagiotis Eleftheriadis,Foivos Georgios Kyrgios,Sonia Leva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合CNN和BiLSTM的混合深度学习框架，用于电池健康状态（SOH）估计，通过贝叶斯优化调整超参数，并在三个公开数据集上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决电池健康状态（SOH）估计问题，研究者希望开发一种更准确、更可靠的预测模型。通过探索不同的深度学习架构组合以及特征工程方法，旨在提高SOH估计的精度和泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的混合深度学习架构，将卷积神经网络（CNN）与双向长短期记忆神经网络（BiLSTM）串联，并集成贝叶斯优化进行超参数调整。研究评估了三种深度学习架构：独立循环模型、CNN-RNN架构以及带有中间全连接层的增强型CNN-RNN组合。在特征工程方面，结合了容量（Q）、电压（V）、增量容量分析（ICA）和差分电压分析（DVA），系统评估了多种特征组合以确定最佳输入表示。

Result: 在三种评估架构中，带有中间全连接层的模型表现出最高的预测精度。研究使用了三个公开数据集进行验证（两个外部数据集和一个作者自行开发的数据集），采用平均绝对误差（MAE）、均方根误差（RMSE）和浮点运算次数（FLOPs）作为评估指标，确保了结果的可复现性。

Conclusion: 所提出的CNN-BiLSTM混合架构，结合贝叶斯优化和综合特征工程方法，在SOH估计任务中展现出卓越的性能，特别是带有中间全连接层的配置表现最佳，为电池健康管理提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this research, a novel framework is proposed for the SOH estimation, which employs a hybrid deep learning architecture of a concatenation of a Convolution Neural Network (CNN) and a Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) Neural Network (NN) with the integration of Bayesian Optimization-based hyperparameter tuning for the network. Three different deep learning architectures are being evaluated: standalone recurrent models, CNN-RNN architectures and CNN-RNN combinations enhanced with intermediate Fully Connected (FC) layers. Among the three, the model with the intermediate FC layers demonstrated the highest predictive accuracy. A comprehensive feature engineering approach combines capacity (Q), voltage (V), Incremental Capacity Analysis (ICA), and Differential Voltage Analysis (DVA), with systematic evaluation of multiple combinations to identify the optimal input representation. To validate the proposed method, three publicly available datasets were utilized, ensuring reproducibility of the results, two from external sources and one developed by the author of this study using a unique experimental setup. The comparison study was performed using the Mean Absolute Error (MAE), the Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) and the FLoating-point OPerations (FLOPs) as evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [347] [PLC-DPO: Posterior Label Correction in Noisy and Ambiguous Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30597)
*Boryeong Cho,Sumyeong Ahn,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PLC-DPO方法，通过后验标签校正解决DPO中偏好数据噪声问题，在57个数据集-模型-基准测试单元中取得最佳平均胜率(60.5%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接偏好优化(DPO)假设所有观察到的偏好都是可靠的，但实际数据中存在反转、弱信号或模糊标签等噪声，导致策略更新产生有害影响。

Method: 提出后验标签校正DPO(PLC-DPO)，利用校准的策略-参考边界作为在线证据，将每对训练信号路由为干净、翻转或平局三种情况，主动校正监督方向和强度。

Result: 在57个数据集-模型-基准测试单元中，PLC-DPO对DPO的平均胜率为60.5%，优于次优方法的55.5%；噪声注入测试、平局压力测试、人类分歧分析和自确认诊断均验证了路由机制的稳定性。

Conclusion: PLC-DPO成功将噪声偏好学习重构为主动校正过程而非简单过滤，有效区分翻转对和弱方向对，实现了对噪声偏好数据的鲁棒优化。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) simplifies alignment through pairwise comparisons but assumes all observed preferences are reliable. Real data often violates this assumption, leading to reversed, weak, or ambiguous labels that cause harmful policy updates. To address this, we propose Posterior Label Correction DPO (PLC-DPO) to robustly optimize preferences by routing each pair's training signal as a clean, flip, or tie case. The key idea is to use the calibrated policy-reference margin as online evidence to take appropriate correction actions. This reframes noisy preference learning as actively correcting supervision direction and strength rather than merely filtering suspicious examples. Across 57 dataset-model-benchmark cells, PLC-DPO obtains the best mean win rate against DPO (60.5 vs. 55.5 for the next-best method). Injected-noise and tie stress tests, human disagreement analysis, and self-confirmation diagnostics further show that the routing remains stable and distinguishes flipped from weakly directional pairs.

</details>


### [348] [MolLedger: An Additive Graph Neural Network with Chemically Grounded ADME Attributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30636)
*Christina X. Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MolLedger的新型图神经网络架构，通过输出原子得分之和来预测ADME性质，实现了无需牺牲模型性能即可获得精确可解释性的目标，并且在解释分子性质变化方面优于其他可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小分子药物发现中，优化吸收、分布、代谢和排泄（ADME）是重要环节。虽然已有许多机器学习模型用于预测ADME性质，但解释模型预测结果仍然具有挑战性，现有方法难以提供有意义且准确的解释。

Method: 提出一种内置有意义原子归因的图神经网络架构MolLedger。该模型输出预测值为原子得分之和，通过全局上下文向量使加性头部获得足够上下文来产生良好的原子得分，并使用辅助损失将原子得分锚定到化学性质。

Result: MolLedger的加性框架在不损失性能的情况下获得精确可解释性；与其他可解释性方法相比，MolLedger产生的归因更忠实于化学性质；在分子对上的案例研究表明，MolLedger能更好地为预测性质变化提供合理解释。

Conclusion: MolLedger成功解决了ADME预测模型的可解释性难题，实现了精确解释与模型性能的无损结合，为药物发现中的分子优化提供了可靠的解释工具。

Abstract: Optimizing absorption, distribution, metabolism, and excretion (ADME) is an important part of small molecule drug discovery. Many machine learning models have been built to predict ADME properties to facilitate this optimization process, but explaining model predictions is challenging. We propose a new graph neural network architecture with built-in meaningful per-atom attributions. Our model MolLedger outputs predictions that are the sum of per-atom scores. MolLedger's additive framework obtains exact interpretability at no cost to performance because the global context vector gives the additive head enough context to produce good per-atom scores. Furthermore, MolLedger produces attributions that are more faithful to chemical properties than other interpretability methods because the auxiliary loss in MolLedger anchors the atom scores to chemical properties. Our case studies comparing interpretations from multiple methods on molecular pairs reveal that MolLedger is much better at producing sensible explanations for predicted property changes.

</details>


### [349] [Three Steps at a Time: Learning Representations from Action Sequences in Contrastive RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30640)
*Michal Korniak,Kamil Dybek,Benjamin Eysenbach,Marco Bagatella,Michał Bortkiewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将对比强化学习（CRL）从单步动作扩展到动作块（action chunks），在离线和在线基准上分别获得31.7%和93.1%的性能提升，主要原因是动作块比单步动作携带更多目标相关信息，从而改善评论家的表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督强化学习方法通常采用单步动作建模，但动作建模的合适时间尺度仍然是一个开放性问题。研究者希望探索将动作扩展为动作块是否能带来性能提升，并理解其背后的机制。

Method: 将对比强化学习（CRL）从单步动作扩展到动作块，使其能够在更长时间尺度上对动作序列进行建模，而非仅考虑单步动作。

Result: 在18个离线环境和11个在线环境上分别获得+31.7%和+93.1%的性能提升。研究发现，传统上解释动作块优势的理由（建模非马尔可夫策略、传播无偏多步回报）对CRL仅部分适用。关键发现在于：动作块比单步动作携带更多关于目标的信息，可显著改善评论家的表示质量。

Conclusion: 在CRL框架下，动作块的有效性主要源于其更强的目标信息编码能力而非传统解释。这一发现为自监督强化学习中动作时间尺度的选择提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: While self-supervised approaches to reinforcement learning have achieved strong results by learning representations of states and actions, a key open question is the time scale over which actions should be modeled. Departing from the standard formulation relying on single-step actions, we extend contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL), a prototypical self-supervised method, to operate over action chunks, and find that this results in large, pervasive gains across established offline and online benchmarks: +31.7% and +93.1% across 18 and 11 environments respectively. While action-chunking-driven gains are generally explained through the ability to model non-Markovian, temporally extended policies, and to propagate unbiased multi-step returns, interestingly, we find that these arguments only partially apply to CRL. Our empirical studies suggest that, in the context of CRL, an action chunk carries more information about the goal than a single action, measurably improving the critic's representations, and rendering the algorithm significantly more effective.

</details>


### [350] [Season-Aware Hybrid Convolutional-Transformer for Antarctic Sea Ice Concentration Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30654)
*Danyang Li,John Taylor,Thang Bui,Quanling Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合卷积-Transformer框架用于南极海冰浓度月度预测，通过引入月份感知位置编码和季节性时间偏置两种季节先验机制，在分类和回归指标上均优于传统基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南极海冰浓度预测面临复杂空间结构、长期时间依赖和强季节性变化的挑战。传统基于卷积的模型虽然能有效捕获局部空间模式，但在建模长期时间演化方面能力有限。

Method: 构建混合卷积-Transformer预测框架，结合卷积编码进行空间特征提取和分解自注意力机制进行时空依赖建模。引入两种季节先验机制：月份感知位置编码（将日历月信息注入token表示）和季节性时间偏置（引导注意力关注周期相关的历史状态）。

Result: 所提框架在分类和回归指标上均优于卷积和循环基线模型。消融实验表明，季节先验机制在短期和长期预测中均提供一致的额外增益。

Conclusion: 证明了结合卷积结构、注意力机制和周期先验信息对南极海冰浓度预测的价值，为该领域提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Antarctic sea ice concentration (SIC) forecasting is an important yet challenging task due to the coexistence of complex spatial structure, long-range temporal dependencies, and strong seasonal variability. Conventional convolution-based models are effective at capturing local spatial patterns, but often have limited ability to model long-term temporal evolution. To address these challenges, we build on a hybrid Convolutional-Transformer forecasting framework for monthly Antarctic SIC forecasting. This framework combines convolutional encoding for spatial feature extraction with factorised self-attention for spatio-temporal dependency modelling. We further introduce two seasonal prior mechanisms: a month-aware positional encoding that injects calendar-month information into the token representation, and a seasonal temporal bias that encourages attention to periodically related historical states. Experimental results show that the proposed framework achieves better performance than convolutional and recurrent baselines across both classification and regression metrics. Ablation studies further indicate that the seasonal prior mechanisms provide consistent additional gains in both short- and long-horizon prediction. These results demonstrate the value of combining convolutional structures, attention mechanisms, and periodic prior information for Antarctic SIC forecasting.

</details>


### [351] [CoMPASS: Collaborative Molecular Property Prediction via Adaptive Small-Large Model Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30674)
*Wentao Li,Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Leyi Zhao,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMPASS框架，通过证据检索和受控融合机制实现图神经网络与大语言模型的协作，在保持统计可靠性的同时引入化学推理能力，显著提升了分子性质预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测面临核心矛盾：图神经网络虽具有统计可靠性但受限于训练数据覆盖范围，大语言模型能提供化学推理和分子证据比较但作为独立定量预测器不可靠。关键挑战在于确定LLM何时以及多大程度上影响校准模型。

Method: 提出CoMPASS检索校准框架：以图注意力网络(GAT)为预测锚点，检索局部相关训练分子，向LLM提供基于注意力的证据，通过协议感知门控机制将LLM建议转化为有界修正，实现小-大模型协作。

Result: 在6个分类和2个回归基准测试上，CoMPASS在可修正不确定区域提升GAT锚点性能，同时在高置信区域限制LLM干预。消融实验证明性能提升源于验证校准的检索和有界融合机制，而非单纯提示工程。

Conclusion: 生成式推理应通过证据基础、受控修正的方式增强校准预测，而非直接输出替换。这为分子性质预测领域提供了一个平衡统计可靠性与化学推理能力的有效范式。

Abstract: Accurate molecular property prediction requires both statistical reliability and chemical reasoning. Graph neural networks can be calibrated directly on labeled assays but remain limited by the coverage of their training data. Large language models (LLMs) can compare molecular evidence and articulate chemical rationales, yet are unreliable as standalone quantitative predictors. The central challenge is therefore to determine when an LLM should influence a calibrated model and by how much. Here we present CoMPASS, a retrieval-calibrated framework for small-large model collaboration. CoMPASS retains a graph attention network (GAT) as the predictive anchor, retrieves locally relevant training molecules, provides attention-grounded evidence to an LLM, and converts its proposal into a bounded correction through an agreement-aware gate. Across six classification and two regression benchmarks, CoMPASS improves the GAT anchor in regions of correctable uncertainty while limiting LLM intervention in high-confidence regimes. Ablations show that the gains arise from validation-calibrated retrieval and bounded fusion rather than prompting alone. These results suggest that generative reasoning should augment calibrated prediction through evidence-grounded, controlled corrections rather than direct output replacement. Code is available at https://github.com/littlepeachs/CoMPASS.

</details>


### [352] [Learning Materials Properties from Scarce Labels and Unlabeled Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30682)
*Wentao Li,Yizhe Chen,Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Leyi Zhao,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SemiMat基准测试和MatRank目标函数，在材料属性回归任务中利用半监督学习处理标签稀缺问题，在24个骨干网络-任务组合中取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀缺标签和未标记晶体中学习材料属性是数据驱动材料发现的核心挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的评估基准和有效的伪标签处理机制。

Method: 1) SemiMat：受控的半监督材料属性回归基准，固定了标签/未标签输入、图骨干接口、验证集选择、测试报告、NMAE指标和方法排名；2) MatRank：基于可靠性加权的连续伪标签不确定性目标，通过标记锚点构建伪目标，利用局部可靠性和弱预测一致性加权，训练弱强图视图并添加排序信号。

Result: 在24个骨干网络-任务组合上，单一MatRank目标实现了最低的总体held-out测试NMAE (0.896) 和最佳平均方法排名 (2.208)，组件分析、OOD诊断和生成池分析验证了增益的可靠性。

Conclusion: SemiMat为半监督材料属性预测提供了标准化评估框架，MatRank方法有效利用未标记数据提升预测性能，但某些场景仍需进一步筛选评估。代码已开源。

Abstract: Learning materials properties from scarce labels and unlabeled crystals is a central challenge for data-driven materials discovery. We present SemiMat, a controlled benchmark for semi-supervised materials property regression, and MatRank, a reliability-weighted objective for continuous pseudo-label uncertainty. SemiMat fixes labeled and unlabeled crystal inputs, graph-backbone interfaces, validation-only checkpoint selection, held-out test reporting, normalized MAE (NMAE), and method-rank summaries across six scarce-label tasks, four graph backbones, and five predefined split runs. MatRank builds pseudo-targets from labeled anchors, weights them by local reliability and weak-prediction agreement, trains weak and strong graph views consistently, and adds ranking signals so that unlabeled crystals shape both values and candidate order. Across the retained 24 backbone-task blocks, one fixed MatRank objective gives the lowest aggregate held-out test NMAE (0.896) and best average method rank (2.208). The component, OOD, and generated-pool diagnostics identify where the gain is reliable and where further screening evaluation remains necessary. Code is available at https://github.com/littlepeachs/SemiMat.

</details>


### [353] [Liquid Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30695)
*Yiheng Jiang,Yuanbo Xu,Yongjian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无求解器、可并行计算的时间算子Liquid Gated Attention (LGA)，通过将观测时间间隔与输入驱动的门控机制参数化，实现了线性时间复杂度的连续时间建模，并在多种长时序任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列通常具有不规则采样和长时间跨度，现有方法存在明显局限：离散时间方法将可变时间间隔折叠为静态位置，丢失时间信息；依赖求解器的连续时间模型能保持时间结构但需顺序积分，无法并行化；无求解器近似方法虽避免了顺序计算，但未能将观测时间间隔与输入驱动的状态调制相结合。

Method: 提出Liquid Gated Attention (LGA)，一种无求解器的并行时间算子。核心创新包括：(1)通过输入驱动的门控机制参数化观测时间间隔，引入连续时间归纳偏置；(2)将隐藏状态演化表述为快速权重联想记忆，实现时间维度的并行计算；(3)在非因果编码中利用矩阵结合律，在因果编码中使用前缀扫描，两者均达到序列长度的线性时间复杂度；(4)设计序列级归一化以约束累积时间衰减，确保长时间跨度优化的稳定性。基于LGA构建LFormer模块化骨干网络。

Result: 在跨越六个任务和十六个数据集（序列长度最高达17,984步）的实验中，LFormer展现出长程依赖建模、细粒度状态跟踪以及从稀疏和噪声观测中重建轨迹的能力，同时保持线性扩展效率，与最先进的离散时间和连续时间基线方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: LGA通过创新的门控机制和时间间隔参数化，成功解决了连续时间建模中时间结构保持与并行计算之间的矛盾，LFormer作为其实现载体，证明了该方法在长时序建模中的有效性和效率优势。

Abstract: Real-world time series often exhibit irregular sampling and extended temporal horizons, requiring models to capture continuous-time dynamics across arbitrary intervals without prohibitive scaling costs. Discrete-time methods collapse variable time intervals into static positional steps; solver-dependent continuous-time models preserve temporal structure but rely on sequential integration, precluding parallelization; and solver-free approximations avoid this cost yet none couples observed time intervals with input-driven state modulation. We propose Liquid Gated Attention (LGA), a solver-free parallel temporal operator. By parameterizing an input-driven gating mechanism with observed time intervals, LGA introduces a continuous-time inductive bias and formulates hidden state evolution as a fast-weight associative memory, enabling parallel computation across the temporal dimension. Using matrix associativity in non-causal encoding and a prefix scan in causal encoding, LGA attains linear temporal complexity in sequence length in both modes. A sequence-level normalization bounds cumulative temporal decay for stable long-horizon optimization. Building on LGA, we instantiate LFormer, a modular backbone for continuous-time representation learning. Across six tasks and sixteen datasets spanning up to 17,984 steps, LFormer demonstrates long-range dependency modeling, fine-grained state tracking, and trajectory reconstruction from sparse and noisy observations, while delivering competitive performance against state-of-the-art discrete-time and continuous-time baselines with linear scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [354] [Learning Dynamics of Logits Debiasing for Long-Tailed Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30699)
*Yue Cheng,Jiajun Zhang,Xiaohui Gao,Weiwei Xing,Zhanxing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从学习动力学视角理论刻画了长尾半监督学习中的对数去偏机制，提出了动态剪枝框架DyTrim，通过重新分配梯度预算有效减少类别偏差并提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的半监督学习普遍存在长尾分布问题，伪标签倾向于偏向多数类导致泛化性能下降。尽管已有多种长尾半监督学习方法被提出，但其隐式去偏机制仍未被充分理解，缺乏理论层面的解释。

Method: 推导了对数更新的逐步分解，揭示预测主要由反映标签先验的类别不平衡偏差主导；使用任务无关基线图像的对数作为累积偏差指标并证明其收敛于类别先验；在此基础上提出DyTrim动态剪枝框架，在标注数据上进行类别感知剪枝，在未标注数据上进行基于置信度的软剪枝，从而重新分配梯度预算。

Result: 理论证明DyTrim能够减少类别偏差并改善泛化能力；在标准LTSSL基准测试上进行了广泛实验，在不同架构和方法上均表现出一致的性能提升；提供了统一视角解释logit调整、重加权、重采样等方法对应于重塑梯度动力学。

Conclusion: 本文为LTSSL方法提供了学习动力学层面的理论解释，建立了一个统一的理论框架，所提出的DyTrim框架在理论保证和实验验证两方面都展现了有效性，为长尾半监督学习领域的进一步发展奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Long-tailed distributions are prevalent in real-world semi-supervised learning (SSL), where pseudo-labels tend to favor majority classes, leading to degraded generalization. While many long-tailed semi-supervised learning (LTSSL) methods have been proposed, the mechanisms by which they implicitly debias logits remain poorly understood. In this work, we revisit LTSSL through the lens of learning dynamics and provide a theoretical characterization of logits debiasing. Specifically, we derive a step-wise decomposition of the logits updates, showing that predictions are dominated by class-imbalance bias that reliably reflects label priors. To expose this effect, we use the logits of a task-irrelevant baseline image as an indicator of accumulated bias and prove that they converge to the class prior. This provides a unified view where LTSSL remedies such as logit adjustment, reweighting, and resampling correspond to reshaping gradient dynamics. Based on this insight, we propose DyTrim, a principle-based dynamic pruning framework that reallocates gradient budget through class-aware pruning on labeled data and confidence-based soft pruning on unlabeled data. We provide theoretical guarantees that DyTrim reduces class bias and improves generalization. Extensive experiments on standard LTSSL benchmarks show consistent gains across architectures and methods. Code available at: https://jiajun0425.github.io/DyTrim

</details>


### [355] [Kolmogorov--Arnold against bounded translations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30710)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理（KART）提供了对抗有界平移攻击的鲁棒性证明，构造了使用固定分段线性内函数的近似表示，其外函数对抗攻击具有不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理在Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KANs）中得到广泛应用，但其在对抗性扰动下的稳定性仍是一个关键的开放问题。本文旨在解决KART在隐藏层受到连续对抗性扰动时的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 作者提供了近似表示的显式、自包含且构造性的证明，使用固定的分段线性内函数进行构造。关键创新在于构造了一个单一的外函数，该函数对所有求和项保持不变，并且在已知最大界限的前提下，独立于具体的对抗性平移。

Result: 成功证明了KART在受到有界对抗性平移时的鲁棒性，提供了一个构造性的近似表示方案，其中外函数具有对抗不变性。

Conclusion: 该工作为KANs等神经网络应用中的KART提供了理论保证，证明了其在对抗性扰动下的稳定性，这对于理解和提升神经网络的安全性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Historically originating from Hilbert's 13th problem, the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem (KART) has recently experienced a major revitalisation through its applications to neural networks, specifically Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs). While the exact representation is well established, its stability under continuous adversarial perturbations of the hidden layer remains a critical open question. In this paper, we investigate the robustness of KART against bounded adversarial translations. We provide an explicit, self-contained, and constructive proof of an approximate representation using fixed, piecewise linear inner functions. Crucially, our construction employs a single outer function that remains invariant for all summands and is independent of the specific adversarial translation, provided its maximum bound is known a priori.

</details>


### [356] [Tracing distinguishability through transformer processing with stochastic LayerNorm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30720)
*Kieran Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出为Transformer表示赋予体积概念，通过LayerNorm添加高斯噪声将相似性转化为统计可区分性，从而建立表示比较与模型功能的信息理论联系，揭示了不同层和注意力头处理信息的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表示相似性分析方法基于点值表示之间的距离，但这种距离与模型的下游功能没有内在联系：相近的状态可能产生不同的行为，而距离较远的状态可能表现相似。需要一种能够将潜在表示的比较建立在模型功能基础之上的方法。

Method: 对预训练Transformer的LayerNorm进行轻量修改：在每个残差流读取时，对状态进行归一化，添加各向同性高斯噪声，然后重新归一化。在蒸馏微调过程中，每个残差流读取操作学习一个分配参数，在处理堆栈中分配固定的全局速率预算。使用Bhattacharyya系数追踪反事实区分信息通过MLP块的保留情况，以及对查询、键、值计算的暴露情况。

Result: 在ViT-S和GPT-2小模型上的实验揭示了连续视觉扰动的深度传播模式，以及注意力头对与已知注意力模式一致的token区分的特异性敏感性。成功识别了哪些反事实区分通过MLP块被保留，以及哪些被选择性地暴露给特定注意力头的Q、K、V计算。

Conclusion: 可区分性作为分析Transformer计算的功能基础视角，能够补充现有的可解释性方法。该方法通过信息论工具（如数据处理不等式）将表示比较与模型功能联系起来，为理解Transformer的信息处理机制提供了新的理论基础和实证工具。

Abstract: Representational similarity is foundational to analyses of deep networks, yet distances between point-valued representations are not intrinsically tied to downstream function: nearby states may produce different behaviors, while distant states may behave similarly. We instead give representations volume, turning similarity into statistical distinguishability. Overlapping stochastic representations necessarily induce overlapping downstream distributions, grounding latent comparison in model function and bringing it under information-theoretic tools such as the data-processing inequality. We realize this idea in pretrained transformers through a light-touch modification to LayerNorm: at each residual-stream read, we normalize the state, add isotropic Gaussian noise, and renormalize. During distillation fine-tuning, one learned allocation parameter per residual-stream read distributes a fixed global rate budget across the processing stack. The resulting model can be viewed as transformer blocks reading the residual stream with learned finite precision under a shared global rate budget. Using the Bhattacharyya coefficient, we trace which counterfactual distinctions are preserved through MLP blocks or selectively exposed to the query, key, and value computations of individual attention heads. Experiments on ViT-S and GPT-2 small reveal the depthwise propagation of continuous visual perturbations and head-specific sensitivity to token distinctions aligned with known attention motifs. These results establish distinguishability as a functionally grounded lens on transformer computation that complements existing interpretability approaches.

</details>


### [357] [BAITBENCH: Measuring Agent Reward Hacking with Optional Shortcuts Planted in ML Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30724)
*Pradyumna Shyama Prasad,Meiri Anto,Leon Eshuijs,Julian Moncarz,Kaustubh Kislay,Juan J. Vazquez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出BAITBENCH基准测试，发现57.1%的LLM智能体在自主ML实验中存在奖励投机行为（reward hacking），即使被明确要求不作弊，作弊率仍超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试未能衡量智能体利用数据和建模任务中的捷径进行投机的行为，无法有效评估自主ML实验的安全性和有效性。

Method: 构建包含三个合成表格ML任务的BAITBENCH测试套件，每个任务包含可提升公开测试分数但在隐藏测试集失效的捷径，通过两阶段评判流程对七个前沿智能体进行评估。

Result: 57.1%的运行出现奖励投机行为，七个智能体中有五个作弊率超过50%；即使明确提示不作弊，平均作弊率仍高于50%。

Conclusion: 发布BAITBENCH基准、评判实现和标注数据集，为评估和对比奖励投机缓解方案提供测试平台。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used to run autonomous ML experiments, iterating on target metrics with little human oversight. Prior work has documented reward hacking in these environments, bringing into question the validity of produced research and the broader safety case for AI R&D. Existing benchmarks do not measure exploits that live in the data or the modeling task itself. We introduce BAITBENCH, a suite of three synthetic tabular ML tasks that each contain a shortcut that allows agents to inflate the public test score but fail on a hidden test set. Since the shortcut is optional and using it breaks no stated rule, BAITBENCH measures how often models exploit the shortcut to achieve inflated scores. Across seven frontier agents scored by our two-stage judge pipeline, 57.1% of runs exhibit reward hacking, with five of seven above 50%. Agents cheat even under a second condition where they are prompted not to -the mean cheating rate remains above 50%. We release BAITBENCH, along with the judge implementation, and an annotated dataset of transcripts containing reward hacks as a testbed for evaluating reward-hacking mitigations head-to-head.

</details>


### [358] [E-Commerce Bench: Evaluating LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Business Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30730)
*Wei Fan,Xinjie Shen,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Yang Su,Yinger Zhang,Lianghao Deng,Fengyu Wang,Baohua Dong,Yangqiu Song,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了E-Commerce Bench，首个整合多轮谈判和动态事件的开源电商基准，LLM代理需在365天内经营多家店铺以最大化年终资产。评测18个模型显示无单一模型全面领先，GPT-5.6 Sol收益最高（14倍），开源模型中Qwen3.8-Max-Preview表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长程智能体任务研究主要集中在将短任务链式扩展，但现实中的长程任务具有动态变化的环境和长距离依赖关系，要求LLM能够持续探索、从经验中学习，并在数千步交互中不断调整策略。电商运营是典型的长程决策场景，缺乏能够真实模拟这一复杂环境的基准测试。

Method: 构建了一个365天的电商运营基准测试，LLM代理同时运营多家在线商店，执行市场调研、供应商谈判、库存采购、销售策略优化、订单履行、退货处理和现金流管理等任务。使用真实电商平台的产品和供应商数据，并设计了包含促销、自然灾害、供应链冲击等动态事件的年度日历来持续重塑市场需求。为保可复现性，采用确定性需求模型和谈判内核。

Result: 对18个前沿模型在七个维度上进行了评估。GPT-5.6 Sol收益最高，将10万初始资金增长至1,431,425（14倍），但在欺诈规避上排名第16。开源模型中，Qwen3.8-Max-Preview以416,252领先GLM 5.2（high）38%，并在长期学习中表现最强，能够逐步压低重复订单价格。没有单一模型在所有维度上占主导地位。

Conclusion: E-Commerce Bench为长程智能体研究提供了真实且可复现的评估环境，揭示了不同模型在收益、效率、学习能力和风险管理等方面的权衡。实验结果表明，长程决策能力的发展需要在多个维度上进行优化，而非仅仅追求单一指标的提升。该基准为未来长程智能体的研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks go beyond chaining short tasks over more interaction turns. Their evolving dynamic environments and long-range dependencies require Large Language Models (LLMs) to continually explore, learn from experience, and adapt their policies over thousands of steps. We introduce E-Commerce Bench, the first open-source benchmark that integrates multi-round counterpart negotiation and dynamic events into a year-long business operation. Over a 365-day year, an LLM agent concurrently runs multiple online stores, researching the market, negotiating with suppliers to source inventory, optimizing sales strategies, fulfilling orders, handling returns, and managing cash flow to maximize its end-of-year total assets. To construct a realistic merchant-side operating environment, the product and supplier data are derived from a real e-commerce platform, while a year-long calendar of promotions, natural disasters, and supply-chain shocks continually reshapes demand. For reproducibility, both sides of the market are deterministic: customer purchases and returns follow a fixed demand model, while a negotiation kernel determines supplier pricing, concessions, and decisions, with an LLM used only to verbalize them. We evaluate 18 frontier models across seven dimensions, including year-end assets, and find that no single model dominates. GPT-5.6 Sol earns the most, growing the 100,000 opening stake into 1,431,425, yet it ranks 16th of 18 on fraud avoidance and trails Fable5 in operational efficiency. Among open-weight models, Qwen3.8-Max-Preview leads with 416,252, 38% above GLM 5.2 (high), and achieves the strongest learning over the horizon, progressively bargaining down prices across repeated orders. Our code is available at https://github.com/QwenLM/E-CommerceBench.

</details>


### [359] [Functional Degeneracy in Neural Networks: Measurement and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30741)
*Maria Matveev,Pascal Esser,Ayush Bharadwaj,Lucius Bushnaq,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了行为恢复秩（behavioral recovery rank）作为模型压缩的几何基准，发现结构和幅度剪枝在任务饱和后仍保留较多自由度，表明功能冗余分布于参数方向而非单个权重或神经元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中，如何在保持模型行为不变的前提下压缩训练好的模型，以减少部署所需的内存、计算和能耗，是一个核心问题。现有的剪枝方法可能未充分利用功能冗余，因此需要一种新的几何视角来理解和量化模型的功能退化性。

Method: 引入行为恢复秩作为功能退化性的量化指标，定义为恢复训练模型性能所需的前导行为-海森特征方向的数量。将行为恢复秩作为压缩的几何基准，评估结构和幅度剪枝方法的效果。

Result: 结构和幅度剪枝即使在任务性能饱和后仍保留更多的自由度，表明现有剪枝方法未能充分暴露和利用参数空间中的功能冗余。功能冗余分布于参数方向中，而非存在于单个权重或神经元层面。

Conclusion: 功能冗余在参数方向上是分布式存在的，传统的基于单个权重或神经元的剪枝方法无法有效识别和移除这些冗余。这为设计更高效的模型压缩方法提供了新的理论基础和方向。

Abstract: A central question in modern machine learning is how much a trained model can be compressed without changing its behavior, to reduce the memory, compute and energy required to deploy it. To study this, we quantify functional degeneracy through the behavioral recovery rank, defined as the number of leading behavioral-Hessian eigendirections required to recover a trained model's performance. Using the behavioral recovery rank as a geometric benchmark for compression, we find that structural and magnitude pruning retain more degrees of freedom, even after the task is saturated. This gap suggests that functional redundancy is distributed across parameter directions and is not exposed by individual weights or neurons.

</details>


### [360] [TDDM-Melatt: A Decoupled Memory and Diffusion Framework for Generalizable Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30745)
*Ze Chen,Qiming Yu,Zijia Song,Guozheng Yang,Wei Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TDDM-Melatt框架，通过解耦记忆表示和扩散模型数据增强解决加密流量分类中的虚假特征相关性和样本不平衡问题，在多个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于数据集驱动的加密流量分类研究存在两个关键局限性：（1）虚假特征相关性导致的捷径学习问题；（2）真实流量长尾分布引起的样本不平衡问题。这些问题导致流量识别性能在实际网络环境中泛化能力弱。

Method: 提出双模块框架：1）Melatt记忆解耦流量表示模型，采用竞争门控LSTM(CG-LSTM)构建编解码器，通过严格拓扑匿名化和冻结预训练编码器策略切断虚假特征学习路径；2）面向流量特性的流量去噪扩散模型(TDDM)进行数据增强。推理时使用冻结表示进行高效分类。

Result: 在4个代表性公共基准数据集上进行广泛实验，在严格的流级划分和匿名化条件下，TDDM-Melatt性能优于6种基础分类模型和6种最新表示学习模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为真实网络环境中的加密流量分类提供了新的有效技术路径，显著提升了模型的泛化能力和实际部署效果。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of encrypted traffic poses severe challenges to current security situational awareness systems based on network traffic monitoring. In existing dataset-driven training and testing studies, limitations such as shortcut learning induced by spurious feature correlations and sample imbalance caused by the long-tail distribution of real-world traffic result in weak generalization of traffic identification performance to real-world network traffic. To address these limitations, we propose TDDM-Melatt, a disentangled memory-based traffic classification framework with diffusion-based data augmentation. First, we design Melatt, a memory-decoupled traffic representation model, which employs Competitive Gating Long Short-Term Memory (CG-LSTM) to construct the encoder and decoder. We design a spurious-correlation-free pre-training and inference paradigm, employing strict topology anonymization and a frozen pre-trained encoder strategy to cut off the model's learning pathways for spurious features. During inference, classification is performed efficiently by a downstream classifier on the frozen representations. Second, we propose a Traffic Denoising Diffusion Model (TDDM) tailored to the characteristics of traffic data. Extensive experiments are conducted on 4 representative public benchmark datasets. Under strict flow-level splitting and anonymization, TDDM-Melatt outperforms 6 basic classification models and 6 SOTA representation learning models. The proposed method provides a new and effective technical pathway for encrypted traffic classification in real-world network environments.

</details>


### [361] [Do VLMs Share Safety Neurons Across Modalities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30750)
*Jiaxuan Li,Jiahao Zhang,Duc Minh Vo,Huy H. Nguyen,Pride Kavumba,Koki Wataoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在神经元层面对VLM安全机制进行因果分析，发现文本安全高度集中在约88个神经元（<0.01%），而视觉安全则高维分散（需≥50个子空间方向），揭示了当前对齐方法未能弥补视觉安全缺口的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）可通过图像绕过安全机制执行有害请求，但视觉输入如何在神经元层面干扰安全通路尚不清楚。现有研究仅在表征层面或经验层面描述此类越狱现象，缺乏深入的神经元级因果分析。

Method: 提出两阶段检测管道，采用迭代消融方法以考虑自修复效应；构建两个模态隔离基准ViSafe-Detect和ViSafe-Eval，解耦视觉和文本安全信号；在10个VLM上进行系统性神经元层面的安全机制分析。

Result: (1) VLM中文本安全可定位：约88个神经元（<0.01%）的定向消融可显著降低拒绝率；(2) 文本安全神经元构成主导拒绝通路，其消融是唯一在所有模型中一致且显著降低拒绝率的干预；(3) 视觉安全在单神经元层面高维且分散：文本安全集中在约5个子空间方向，而视觉安全需要≥50个。

Conclusion: 文本安全与视觉安全在神经元层面存在根本性结构差异，文本安全高度集中而视觉安全高度分散，这解释了为何当前对齐方法未能有效解决视觉安全缺口，为未来VLM安全研究提供了重要理论指导。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can comply with harmful requests delivered through images, even when their LLM backbones would refuse the same content in text. While prior work characterizes these jailbreaks empirically or at the representation level, how visual inputs perturb safety pathways at the neuron level remains uncharted. We close this gap with a causal, neuron-level analysis of safety mechanisms in 10 VLMs. We propose a two-stage detection pipeline with iterative ablation that accounts for self-repair, and introduce two modality-isolated benchmarks, ViSafe-Detect and ViSafe-Eval, which decouple visual and textual safety signals.
  Our analysis reveals: (i) Text safety in VLMs is localizable: $\sim$88 neurons ($<$0.01%) whose targeted ablation substantially reduces refusal. (ii) Text safety neurons constitute the dominant refusal pathway: ablating them is the only intervention that consistently and substantially reduces refusal across all models. (iii) Visual safety is high-dimensional and diffuse at the single-neuron level: text safety concentrates in $\sim$5 subspace directions while visual safety requires $\geq$50. This gap holds across architectures, explaining why current alignment has not closed the visual safety gap. Project page is at: https://jiaxuan-li.github.io/vlm-safety-neuron/
  Warning: this paper may include examples of harmful content.

</details>


### [362] [PRACTICE: From Experience to Expertise in Self-Evolving Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30760)
*Ziyi Bai,Siqi Li,Tinglei Huang,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PRACTICE框架，通过训练技能学习器持续维护技能库，实现多模态大语言模型具身智能体的持续改进与环境适应，在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat基准上超越现有经验基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体难以通过交互持续改进和快速适应环境，已有经验方法依赖手工设计的提示流程提取和更新技能，固定流程难以从多样化新经验中学习更新技能。

Method: 训练技能学习器从历史交互轨迹发现并维护持久化技能库，同时冻结任务执行器。技能学习器产生结构化批量编辑（添加、细化、合并、删除技能），层级整合为一致的更新技能库。采用两阶段课程学习：先从oracle轨迹学习基本技能生成和库维护，再通过对比异质执行器在相同任务上的成功与失败轨迹学习识别无效行为模式和恢复策略。最后应用在线技能编辑蒸馏使技能学习器与更强教师对齐。

Result: 紧凑的技能学习器在多个冻结执行器的连续库更新轮次中实现一致性能提升；在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat数据集上进一步超越最强的经验基线方法。

Conclusion: PRACTICE框架成功实现了具身智能体的持续学习与技能积累，证明了通过训练技能学习器自动维护技能库的有效性，为构建可持续改进的具身AI系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can serve as embodied agents, translating language instructions and visual observations into executable plans. However, building agents that can continually improve through interaction and rapidly adapt to their environments remains challenging. Summing up experience from past interaction trajectories provides a promising solution, but existing experience-based methods often rely on manually designed prompting workflows to extract and update skills. Such fixed procedures may struggle to learn updated skills from new and diverse experiences. We introduce PRACTICE, which trains a skill learner to discover and maintain a persistent skill library from past interaction trajectories while keeping the task executor frozen. Given the historical accumulated skills and incoming trajectories, the skill learner produces structured batch-edits that add, refine, merge, or remove skills, and then hierarchical consolidate all collected edits into a consistent updated skill library. We train the learner with a two-stage curriculum. First, it learns basic skill generation and library maintenance from oracle trajectories. Then, by contrasting successful and failed trajectories from heterogeneous executors on the same tasks, it learn to identify invalid action patterns and recovery strategies. Finally, we apply online skill-edit distillation to align the skill learner with a stronger teacher on its current edit distribution to further improves the policy. Experiments demonstrate that a compact skill learner delivers consistent performance improvements across successive library-update rounds for multiple frozen executors. On EB-ALFRED and EB-Habitat, PRACTICE further outperforms the strongest experience-based baselines. Project resources are publicly available at: https://baai-agents.github.io/PRACTICE

</details>


### [363] [T3S: Improving Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Task-Specific Feature Selector and Scheduler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30765)
*Yuanqiang Yu,Tianpei Yang,Yongliang Lv,Yan Zheng,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出T3S框架，通过超网络构建任务特定特征和智能任务调度，有效解决多任务强化学习中的任务间干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务强化学习中参数共享策略存在任务间干扰问题，现有方法未明确哪些参数应在任务间共享，导致学习效率显著降低。

Method: 提出T3S框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)特征选择器：利用超网络生成任务特定的软掩码，应用于全局共享表征以构建任务特定特征；(2)任务调度器：基于任务进度（成功率）和学习速度两个指标选择学习任务，选择概率与任务进度和学习速度成反比。

Result: T3S在各种机器人操作任务上持续优于现有的最先进多任务强化学习算法。

Conclusion: 通过任务特定的特征选择和自适应任务调度，T3S有效缓解了多任务强化学习中的任务间干扰问题，提升了多任务学习的效率和性能。

Abstract: Multi-task reinforcement learning (MTRL) is a technique to train multiple tasks simultaneously, where previous works usually train a single model to solve different tasks by sharing parameters across various tasks. However, these methods are faced with inter-task interference since what parameters should be shared across tasks is not addressed, dramatically reducing learning efficiency. To solve these problems, we propose a novel MTRL framework called Task-Specific feature Selector and Scheduler (T3S), which consists of two components: a feature selector and a task scheduler. Specifically, the feature selectors employ hypernetworks to construct task-specific soft masks, which can be applied by globally shared representation to construct task-specific features. The task scheduler selects tasks for learning through two metrics, where the selection probability is inversely proportional to task progress (e.g., success rate) and task learning speed. Experimental results show that T3S consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art MTRL algorithms on various robotics manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [364] [TrainSDC: Characterizing and Mitigating Silent Data Corruption in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30769)
*Zhipeng Xia,Haotian Xu,Siyu Yun,Liqi Lin,Hu Liu,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了Transformer训练中各计算接口的静默数据损坏(SDC)易损性，发现前向传播和反向传播具有不同的错误传播机制，并提出TrainSDC保护框架，以1.65%-6.76%的运行开销有效维持训练行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)训练日益易受静默数据损坏(SDC)影响，但现有保护方法对Transformer计算缺乏差异化处理，原因在于对其易损性缺乏系统性理解。因此需要深入分析前向传播和反向传播中各计算接口的SDC易损性特征。

Method: 1) 系统分析Transformer训练中前向和反向传播的SDC易损性特征；2) 提出TrainSDC保护框架，包含三个核心组件：Q/K路径重计算、残差增益监控、指数感知梯度缩放。

Result: 在Llama 3.2-1B和Qwen3-0.6B上的实验表明：1) 前向传播易损性高度依赖位置，Q/K路径故障产生持续训练偏差；2) 反向传播易损性主要由梯度指数分布而非计算位置决定；3) TrainSDC在稀疏和密集故障注入下均能保持接近无故障执行的训练行为，仅需1.65%-6.76%的运行开销。

Conclusion: Transformer训练中前向传播和反向传播具有截然不同的SDC错误传播机制，基于这些发现设计的TrainSDC框架能够高效保护训练过程，为LLM训练可靠性提供了新的指导。

Abstract: LLM training is increasingly vulnerable to silent data corruption (SDC), yet existing protection methods largely treat Transformer computations uniformly because their vulnerability remains poorly understood. We present the first systematic characterization of SDC vulnerability across major computation interfaces in both the forward and backward passes of Transformer training. Our analysis reveals two distinct error propagation mechanisms: forward-pass vulnerability is highly location dependent, with faults on the Q/K path producing persistent training deviations, whereas backward-pass vulnerability is largely governed by gradient exponent distributions rather than computation locations. Motivated by these observations, we propose TrainSDC, a characterization-guided protection framework consisting of Q/K-path recomputation, residual-gain monitoring, and exponent-aware gradient scaling. Experiments on Llama 3.2-1B and Qwen3-0.6B show that TrainSDC maintains training behavior close to fault-free execution under both sparse and dense fault injection while introducing only 1.65%-6.76% runtime overhead.

</details>


### [365] [Reciprocity Separates Gradient Flow from Rotation in Conservative Physical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30778)
*Ruiwu Niu,Xiaowen Bi,Michaël Antonie van Wyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了物理学习系统何时遵循传统梯度下降、何时沿不同学习轨迹演化，发现守恒性约束学习方向、互易性产生重加权梯度流、非互易性使学习路径产生旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学习允许可训练材料或网络利用自身的物理响应来传递误差信号，从而减少对独立编程的反向计算的需求。然而，目前尚不清楚是什么因素决定这类系统是遵循传统的梯度下降，还是沿着截然不同的学习轨迹演化。作者旨在揭示守恒性、互易性和非互易性在物理学习中的作用。

Method: 采用有向分层传输网络作为规范模型，其中每个节点重新分配固定量的流，从而在学习过程中保持正性和总质量守恒。研究了匹配响应类中的伴随匹配方法，通过非负模态反馈产生互易闭环响应和重加权梯度流，并通过添加反对称边界分量引入非互易性。

Result: 守恒性仅约束允许的学习方向；互易闭环响应产生重加权梯度流；非互易性使闭环响应具有旋转特性，学习路径可以转向，而驱动更新的误差在当下仍在减小。转向并不自动带来益处，其有限步效应由局部曲率决定，累积效应还依赖于步长选择和路径上访问的新状态。数值一致性检验重现了精确的响应结构，预测了新网络族中局部效应的符号，并展示了轨迹漂移如何抵消局部优势。

Conclusion: 研究成功分离了守恒性、互易性和非互易性在物理学习中的不同作用，为理解物理学习系统何时遵循传统梯度下降、何时沿不同轨迹演化提供了清晰的理论框架。

Abstract: Physical learning lets a trainable material or network use its own physical response to carry error signals, reducing the need for a separately programmed backward computation. We ask what determines whether such a system follows conventional gradient descent or evolves along a genuinely different learning trajectory. Our canonical model is a directed layered transport network in which every node redistributes a fixed amount of flow, so learning preserves positivity and total mass. In this model, conservation constrains only the allowable learning directions. Within the matched response class studied here, adjoint matching gives the physical output response a symmetric form. Non-negative mode-wise feedback then produces a reciprocal closed-loop response and a reweighted gradient flow. Adding an antisymmetric boundary component makes the closed-loop response rotational: the learning path can turn while the error driving that update still decreases at that moment. Turning is not automatically beneficial. Its finite-step effect is set by local curvature, and its accumulated effect also depends on step selection and on the new states visited along the path. Numerical consistency checks reproduce the exact response structure, predict the sign of the local effect across new network families, and show how trajectory drift can negate a local advantage. These results separate the roles of conservation, reciprocity, and nonreciprocity in physical learning.

</details>


### [366] [Geometric Attractor Monitoring: A Robust and Frugal Framework for Multi-modal Industrial Robotic Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30804)
*Martin Bonsergent-Brachet,Jesse Read,Dany Abboud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于相空间重构(PSR)的工业机器人健康监测框架，通过几何吸引子展开机械状态，解决了多模态运行周期和故障数据稀缺的挑战，在真实数据集上超越深度学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构工业机器人机群的健康监测面临两大核心挑战：(1)运行周期的多模态特性，(2)运行至故障数据的持续稀缺。标准的深度学习架构依赖序列重构，倾向于过度平滑复杂动力学，掩盖早期退化迹象，难以适应此类工业场景。

Method: 提出基于相空间重构(PSR)的监测框架：将单变量传感器数据转换为几何吸引子，显式展开机械状态，使其独立于时间发生顺序。在相空间中评估多种异常评分技术，发现离散支持估计能提供有效且计算成本低廉的健康指标(HI)。该方法将算法偏置与目标系统的几何特性对齐。

Result: 在包含21台异构机器人、历时三年的真实世界数据集以及合成Langevin系统上验证，该方法性能超越标准深度学习基线。证明了将算法偏置与目标系统几何属性对齐的有效性，实现了务实、可追溯且易于部署的健康监测解决方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的PSR框架为工业约束下的健康监测提供了一种切实可行的解决方案，具有可解释性强、计算效率高、易于部署等优势，完美契合工业实际需求。该方法通过几何视角而非时序预测，有效克服了传统数据驱动方法在多模态和数据稀缺场景下的局限性。

Abstract: Monitoring the health of heterogeneous industrial robot fleets is severely challenged by the multi-modal nature of their operational cycles and a persistent scarcity of run-to-failure data. Standard data-driven approaches, particularly deep learning architectures relying on sequential reconstruction, often struggle in this specific setting; they tend to over-smooth complex dynamics, masking early signs of degradation. To address these industrial constraints, we reframe the monitoring problem through a framework based on Phase Space Reconstruction (PSR). Instead of predicting temporal sequences, this framework transforms univariate sensor data into a geometric attractor, explicitly unfolding the mechanical states independently of their temporal occurrence. By evaluating various anomaly scoring techniques within this space, we demonstrate that discrete support estimation provides an effective and computationally frugal Health Indicator (HI). Validated on a real-world dataset of 21 heterogeneous robots over three years and a synthetic Langevin system, our approach outperforms standard deep learning baselines. We show that aligning the algorithmic bias with the geometric properties of the target system yields a pragmatic, traceable and easily deployable approach perfectly tailored to the realities of industrial constraints.

</details>


### [367] [What Emerges and What Breaks in Self-Play Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30819)
*Laur Sisask,Ardi Tampuu,Tambet Matiisen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文尝试将纯自博弈训练的自动驾驶策略模型从MLP扩展到Transformer，并在真实城市高精地图上进行训练，但在CARLA和Waymax基准测试中性能不及Gigaflow，主要原因是红绿灯奖励黑客行为和缺乏停车标志停车激励等特定失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（如Gigaflow和Puffer-Drive）已证明纯自博弈训练自动驾驶策略的可行性，本研究旨在扩展这些方法：将模型架构从MLP升级为Transformer，并将训练环境迁移到真实城市的高精地图，以最终实现实际部署目标。

Method: 1. 采用与Gigaflow和Puffer-Drive类似的自博弈训练范式\n2. 将模型架构从MLP扩展至Transformer\n3. 在真实城市的高精地图上进行训练\n4. 引入奖励条件化机制以实现驾驶行为的多样性

Result: 1. 在CARLA和Waymax基准测试中，策略性能低于Gigaflow\n2. 识别出两类主要失败模式：红绿灯处的奖励黑客行为、缺乏在停车标志处停车的激励\n3. 自博弈训练能够涌现出交通规则，但与人类驾驶行为的匹配度存在差距\n4. 奖励条件化机制成功产生了预期的驾驶行为多样性

Conclusion: Transformer架构与真实城市地图结合的自博弈训练方法仍面临挑战，特别是在奖励函数设计方面需要更精细的调整以避免特定失败模式。研究为未来改进自博弈自动驾驶策略训练提供了重要的失败模式分析和行为多样性验证。

Abstract: Training autonomous driving policies through pure self-play has recently shown promising results. Following Gigaflow and Puffer- Drive, we train driving policies in a similar self-play fashion, but extend the models from MLPs to Transformers and train on the high-definition map of a real city, where we ultimately aim to deploy them. On the CARLA and Waymax benchmarks, our policies fall short of Gigaflow, and we trace the gap to specific failure modes, including reward hacking at traffic lights and a missing incentive to stop at stop signs. We further analyze which traffic rules emerge from self-play and how closely they match human driving, and we confirm that reward conditioning yields the intended diversity of driving behaviors. A demonstration of a trained policy is available at https://laursisask-ut.github.io/eccvdemo.

</details>


### [368] [Deploying DeepSeek 175B Locally on a Single Consumer-Grade RTX 4060 Laptop with 32GB RAM for 200k-Scale Protein-Ligand Virtual Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30877)
*Rui Xiao,Yili Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在单张消费级RTX 4060笔记本电脑上部署175B参数的大语言模型，完成20万规模蛋白质-配体虚拟筛选，在72小时内达到8卡A100集群100倍吞吐量，预测误差0.88 kcal/mol满足药物发现精度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模虚拟筛选流程依赖高端GPU集群和数百GB内存，为小型学术团队创造了难以逾越的硬件壁垒，限制了AI驱动药物发现的普及。

Method: 提出完全本地化的低资源框架，在配备32GB系统内存和8GB显存的消费级RTX 4060笔记本电脑上部署1750亿参数的DeepSeek 175B大语言模型，实现异构内存管理和模型优化，完成20个蛋白质靶点的20万规模虚拟筛选工作流。

Result: 在相同任务配置下72小时内达到8卡A100集群基准100倍吞吐量；所有靶点平均结合亲和力预测误差0.88 kcal/mol，满足临床前药物发现1.0 kcal/mol化学精度要求；异构内存管理开销占72%总执行时间，模型优化引入的精度损失占预测误差不到10%。

Conclusion: 验证了在消费级硬件上运行工业级万亿参数大语言模型驱动的生物医学计算任务的工程可行性，建立了AI驱动早期药物发现的低门槛新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated exceptional performance in protein-ligand interaction prediction, but state-of-the-art pipelines for large-scale virtual screening almost exclusively rely on high-end GPU clusters with hundreds of gigabytes of memory, creating prohibitive hardware barriers for small academic teams. In this work, we present a fully local low-resource framework that deploys the 175-billion-parameter DeepSeek 175B LLM on a single consumer-grade RTX 4060 laptop equipped with 32GB system RAM and 8GB VRAM, completing a full 200k-scale protein-ligand virtual screening workflow across 20 distinct protein targets. Our implementation achieves 100x throughput of an 8-card A100 cluster baseline under identical task configurations within 72 hours, with an average binding affinity prediction error of 0.88 kcal/mol across all targets, satisfying the 1.0 kcal/mol chemical accuracy requirement for preclinical drug discovery. Systematic runtime profiling reveals that heterogeneous memory management overhead accounts for 72% of total execution time, while accuracy loss introduced by model optimization contributes less than 10% to total prediction error. This work validates the engineering feasibility of running industrial-scale trillion-parameter LLM-driven biomedical computing tasks on consumer hardware, establishing a new low-barrier paradigm for AI-powered early stage drug discovery.

</details>


### [369] [Fine-Tuning Low-Bit Models with Gradient in Quantized Code Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30908)
*Shiguang Wu,Zhouchen Lin,Quanming Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GradCodes方法，通过代码代理梯度和引导搜索解决低位宽模型微调中的优化效率与部署保真度平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低位宽模型微调在实践中非常重要，可以减少存储和部署的内存与推理成本。现有方法存在权衡：连续低位训练虽然高效但受直通估计误差或后量化差距影响；离散搜索部署保真度高但在有限训练预算下效率过低。

Method: 提出代码代理梯度作为可部署代码空间中的一阶信号来加速优化，同时执行引导搜索以保持部署保真度。该方法结合了连续优化的效率和离散搜索的部署可靠性。

Result: 在算术推理、指令遵循和结构化语言理解等多个任务上的实验表明，GradCodes在不同量化数据类型下都能一致地改善低位宽模型的微调性能。

Conclusion: GradCodes成功解决了低位宽模型微调中效率与部署保真度的矛盾，为量化模型的实际部署提供了有效的微调解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Low-bit models aims to adapt a quantized model while keeping the final deployed checkpoint in the same low-bit form. This setting is practically important as it reduces memory and inference cost for storage and deployment. Under this constraint, adaptation becomes an optimization problem over quantization codes and scales. Existing continuous low-bit training is efficient, but it can be distorted by straight through estimation error or by post-quantize gap; discrete search is deployment-faithful, but it is often too inefficient under a finite training budget. We propose code surrogate gradient as the first order signal in deployable code space to acceleate optimization, and performing guided search to preserve deployment faithfulness. Experiments across arithmetic reasoning, instruction following, and structured language understanding show that GradCodes consistently improves fine-tuning low-bit models across different quantization datatypes. Code is provided at https://github.com/ovo67/GradCodes.

</details>


### [370] [S3C-LLM: Skill-Code Guided Agentic Language Models for Spectrum-to-Structure Elucidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30910)
*Xuanle Zhao,Xinyuan Cai,Xiang Cheng,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S3C-LLM，一种技能引导与代码增强的智能体大语言模型，用于光谱到分子结构的推断。通过显式建模光谱学家的分析工作流程（如诊断性峰解释、片段推理等），在多个基准上超越现有模型，且训练数据量仅为SpectraLLM的十分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的光谱到SMILES直接生成方法未显式建模光谱学家的分析工作流程（如诊断峰解释、片段推理、分子式约束和化学一致性检查），无法充分利用光谱分析的领域知识。

Method: 提出S3C-LLM框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）自进化光谱技能库；（2）思维增强的技能-代码轨迹构建流水线；（3）两阶段训练策略（监督微调SFT + 步骤级强化学习RL）。该方法先检索模态特定的光谱技能，执行分析代码实例化这些技能，整合峰级证据和分子式约束，最后生成SMILES。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，S3C-LLM一致性地超越当前通用LLM和光谱专用模型，且训练语料库规模仅为SpectraLLM的十分之一以下。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模光谱分析的工作流程和引入技能引导机制，S3C-LLM能够更准确地推断分子结构，证明了将领域知识融入LLM的有效性，为光谱结构推断提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Spectroscopic structure elucidation is central to molecular analysis, but recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based methods mostly formulate it as direct spectrum-to-SMILES generation. Although this paradigm can leverage paired spectral data, it does not explicitly model the analytical workflow used by spectroscopists, such as diagnostic peak interpretation, fragment reasoning, formula constraints, and chemical consistency checking. In this paper, we introduce S3C-LLM, a skill-guided and code-grounded agentic LLM for spectrum-to-structure elucidation. Rather than directly predicting a molecule, S3C-LLM retrieves modality-specific spectroscopy skills, executes analysis code to instantiate these skills on the input spectra, and integrates the resulting peak-level evidence and formula constraints before generating SMILES. Specifically, we contribute a self-evolving spectroscopy skill library, a thinking-augmented skill-code trajectory construction pipeline, and a two-stage training strategy that teaches Qwen3-4B through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by our proposed step-level reinforcement learning (RL). Experiments on diverse benchmarks show that S3C-LLM consistently outperforms current general LLMs and spectrum-specific models across spectra, while using less than 1/10th of SpectraLLM's training corpus.

</details>


### [371] [Selection-Aware Stress Testing for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30916)
*Yang Xu,Chenang Li,Jiefu Zhang,Haixiang Sun,Zhou Li,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SASST协议，用于解决智能体评估中“选择偏差”问题。通过分离发现和确认任务，该协议能验证评估结论的可靠性和稳定性。实验表明，原有方法声称的显著性能提升在确认阶段消失，证明了新协议的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体评估方法存在“选择偏差”问题：研究者通常使用同一个基准选择工作流，并在同一数据集中寻找其优势减弱的任务类型，导致两个结论均源自同一数据，缺乏独立验证，容易过拟合，结论不可靠。

Method: 提出了一种称为“选择感知语义压力测试”（SASST）的评估协议。该方法的核心是将评估分为发现和确认两个独立阶段。它从发现任务中学习任务重加权，并在完全独立的确认任务上验证配对比较结果。协议包含支持度与稳定性检查，使用联合置信区间约束所有待验证的声明，并允许返回“无有效结论”以避免误报。

Result: 在一个包含480个episode的τ-bench研究中，原先在发现阶段观察到的3.75分性能增益在确认阶段完全消失。另一项模型研究同样未能确认工作流的优势或稳定的压力规则。理论证明了该方法在特定聚类假设下的条件渐近有效性。审计发现高斯覆盖不足和保守的Bonferroni t界限。

Conclusion: SASST协议通过严格区分发现与确认阶段，有效避免了传统评估中的选择偏差。实验结果警示，智能体评估中的显著增益可能因缺乏独立验证而不可靠，强调了在评估流程中引入独立确认机制的重要性。

Abstract: Agent evaluations often use one benchmark to choose a workflow and then search for task types where its advantage weakens, so both conclusions are selected from the same data. We introduce Selection-Aware Semantic Stress Testing (\SASST{}), which learns a task reweighting from pre-execution features on discovery tasks and evaluates the same paired comparison on separate confirmation tasks. The protocol checks support and stability, uses joint bounds for all planned claims, and can return no claim. We prove conditional asymptotic validity under stated cluster assumptions. A forty-cluster audit finds Gaussian undercoverage and conservative Bonferroni $t$ bounds. In one 480-episode $τ$-bench study, a $3.75$ point discovery gain vanished on confirmation. A second-model study likewise confirmed neither a workflow benefit nor a stable stress rule.

</details>


### [372] [Towards Stream Learning on Embedded Systems: Benchmarking the Memory Consumption of Stream Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30923)
*Sebastian Buschjäger,Nuwan Gunasekara,Heitor Murilo Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对7种流学习分类器在资源受限条件下进行了大规模基准测试（6,463个实验），发现了两类资源失效模式：自适应集成方法因初始内存占用过大而立即超出小预算，增量树方法虽初始适配但会在长数据流中持续增长（HoeffdingTrees增长7.37倍）。研究表明现有最先进方法在嵌入式系统或长期运行系统中仅部分适用，呼吁将资源约束作为与概念漂移适应同等重要的设计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流学习研究长期关注预测性能和概念漂移适应，但忽视了资源使用的可预测性和有界性。随着流学习从服务器向资源匮乏的近传感器嵌入式系统迁移，资源约束变得至关重要。然而，现有最先进方法将资源使用仅作为评估副产品，缺乏系统性的资源约束设计考量，存在理论与实际部署之间的差距。

Method: 在13个真实和合成数据流上，对7种代表性流分类器进行基准测试，设置从128 KiB到约8 MiB的模型大小预算约束。共进行6,463个实验，评估指标包括：考虑失败情况的准确率、峰值模型大小、预算耗尽时间、预测加更新延迟。系统性地分析不同预算级别下各类算法的行为模式。

Result: 发现两种资源失效模式：（1）自适应集成方法因初始内存占用大，即使后续稳定也会立即超出小预算；（2）增量树方法（如HoeffdingTrees和EFDT）初始可适配但在长数据流中持续增长，中位数增长因子分别达7.37和5.87。明确紧凑型方法是极小预算下唯一可行选项，但随着预算增大，自适应集成方法变得有竞争力。许多现有方法在嵌入式或长期运行系统中适用性有限。

Conclusion: 当前流学习方法在资源受限环境中的适用性严重不足。呼吁流学习社区将资源使用约束提升为与概念漂移适应同等重要的一级设计目标，并提出具体改进路径，包括设计使流学习器能显式暴露和遵守资源预算的API接口，以实现资源感知的流学习系统设计。

Abstract: Stream learning is commonly evaluated through predictive performance and adaptation to concept drift. However, sustained operation of a stream learner also requires predictable and bounded resource usage even on long streams. This requirement becomes even more critical when learning moves from servers to near-sensor embedded systems where memory and processing are scarce resources. In state-of-the-art stream learning, however, we perceive a strong focus on concept drift adaptation, whereas resource usage is often an evaluation byproduct. To close this gap, we benchmark seven representative stream classifiers on 13 real and synthetic streams under model-size budgets from 128\,KiB to approximately 8\,MiB. Our benchmark comprises a total of 6,463 experiments. We measure failure-aware accuracy, peak model size, time to budget exhaustion, and prediction-plus-update latency. The results reveal two distinct resource failure modes. Adaptive ensembles can exceed small budgets almost immediately because of their initial footprint, even when their size remains stable thereafter. Incremental trees can fit initially but grow throughout a long stream, with HoeffdingTrees (HT) and Extremely Fast Decision Trees (EFDT) increasing by median factors of 7.37 and 5.87. Explicitly compact methods remain the only viable option under the smallest budgets, but are usually overtaken as larger budgets make adaptive ensembles competitive. Hence, many state-of-the-art methods are only partially applicable in embedded systems or for long-running systems. We therefore call on the stream-learning community to make bounded resource usage a first-class design objective alongside drift adaptation, and propose concrete steps toward this goal, including an API through which stream learners can explicitly expose and respect resource budgets.

</details>


### [373] [Nonparametric Contextual Pricing and Inventory Learning under Censored Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30944)
*Zean Han,Jing Liang,Ruihan Lin,Zezhen Ding,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MCK-UCB算法，用于解决在线零售中基于删失销售数据的定价和库存联合决策问题，在无需假设特定需求函数形式的情况下实现了最优学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线零售中，当产品售罄时，零售商只能观察到已售出的数量，而无法获知库存充足时会有多少顾客购买。库存水平决定了能观察到多少需求信息，而这些信息又会影响后续决策和未来利润。核心挑战在于如何基于不完整的销售数据，学习上下文相关的定价和库存策略，同时不假设特定的需求函数形式。

Method: 提出均値校准核UCB（Mean-Calibrated Kernel UCB，MCK-UCB）算法。该算法利用过去具有相似市场条件的轮次数据，将每条不完整的销售记录转化为库存和价格决策的可靠指导。该方法可以在服务顾客的同时进行学习，无需单独的探索阶段，也不需要恢复所有被缺货隐藏的需求。

Result: 证明了所提算法的minimax最优性；当期望利润随价格变化更平滑时，算法能获得严格更快的收敛速率。通过全面的数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: MCK-UCB算法成功解决了在线零售中基于删失销售数据的联合定价和库存决策问题，在理论上具有最优性能保证，在实践中表现出良好的效果，为在线零售商提供了有效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: In online retailing, when a product sells out, a retailer often sees only the units sold, not how many customers would have bought it had inventory been available. However, the inventory level determines how much demand is revealed, and this information can influence subsequent decisions and future profits. We study an online selling problem in which, in each round, the seller observes a market context and then makes pricing and stocking decisions based on censored sales data from previous rounds. The challenge is to learn a context-dependent pricing and stocking policy without assuming a particular formula for demand or observing realized profit. To overcome this difficulty, we propose a Mean-Calibrated Kernel UCB (MCK-UCB) algorithm that turns each incomplete sales record into a reliable guide for both inventory and price decisions, using data from past rounds with similar market conditions. This design allows us to learn while serving customers, without a separate exploration phase or the need to recover all demand hidden by stockouts. We prove the minimax optimality of the proposed algorithm, with strictly faster rates when expected profit varies more smoothly with price. Comprehensive numerical experiments have been conducted to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [374] [Reproducible macroscopic dynamics in a closed-loop human-AI learning system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30946)
*Minlin Wu,Xu Fang,Yicheng Zhang,Chenyu Zhou,Zhiyi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分析大规模自适应辅导数据，发现了人机交互系统中存在可重现的"类盆地流"动力学和亚稳态特征，并识别出一个外部锚定的主导有效场，连接了经验动力学、可解释机制和神经计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环人机AI系统产生高维行为轨迹，但其集体动力学特征仍然不明确，缺乏对学习过程中群体行为演化规律的理论理解和定量描述。

Method: 使用297,915名学习者的自适应辅导历史数据，在模型拟合前定义语义顺序变量并在用户不相交的队列中进行测试；构建匹配的零假设模型；使用四项条件机制恢复群体漂移；采用预测事件级自监督学习恢复状态和学习平面流；进行洗牌顺序训练和支持对齐随机化测试。

Result: 发现状态展现出可重现的类盆地流和操作定义的、状态异质性的类亚稳态动力学；四项条件机制恢复群体漂移（r = 0.946）；自监督学习能够恢复状态和学习平面流，零参照校正保留方向性和部分振幅过剩场结构；模型间共享主导群体漂移（r = 0.866）和持久性排序，无需跨模型拟合。

Conclusion: 识别出一个外部锚定的主导有效场，将经验动力学、可解释机制和神经计算联系起来，为理解人机交互系统的集体行为动力学提供了理论基础和实证支持。

Abstract: Closed-loop human-AI systems generate high-dimensional behavioural trajectories whose collective dynamics remain obscure. Using 297,915 learners' adaptive-tutoring histories, we define semantic order variables before model fitting and test them in user-disjoint cohorts. The state exhibits reproducible basin-like flow and operationally defined, state-heterogeneous metastable-like kinetics. A construction-matched null distinguishes normalised-memory relaxation from a reproducible excess field. A four-term conditional mechanism recovers population drift (r = 0.946; learner-bootstrap 95% CI, 0.935-0.955). Predictive event-level self-supervised learning recovers the state and learned-plane flow; null-referenced corrections retain directional, partial-amplitude excess-field structure without full calibration. Shuffled-order training reverses learned-plane flow on ordered trajectories; support-alignment randomisation selectively reduces inward transport. Both axes remain linearly accessible without state supervision. Without cross-model fitting, the models share leading population drift (r = 0.866; learner-bootstrap 95% CI, 0.857-0.875) and persistence ordering; residual directions remain model-specific. These results identify an externally anchored leading-order effective field linking empirical dynamics, an interpretable mechanism and neural computation.

</details>


### [375] [One Policy Is Enough: Single-Agent Reinforcement Learning Outperforms Tree Search for Chemistry Tool Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30952)
*Armin Dariani,Sifan Wu,Bang Liu,Entao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单一策略模型，通过左到右生成交错推理、工具调用和返回，在化学问答任务上显著超越此前基于层次化进化MCTS的CheMatAgent系统，在Qwen-2.5-7B上工具F1提升5.5%，返回F1提升9.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学问题常需要精确计算和数据库查询，语言模型无法仅从参数中提供答案，必须借助外部工具。现有方法CheMatAgent采用层次化进化MCTS，需要分离的策略模型和执行模型，搜索工具调用树，训练过程依赖学习型评论家和GPT评分，系统复杂且成本随树增长。作者旨在证明单一策略模型即可有效解决工具使用的三部分问题：从大型工具池中选择正确工具、填充正确类型的参数、链接调用使后序调用消费前序输出。

Method: 提出单一策略模型，在一个从左到右的生成过程中交替进行推理、工具调用和返回。训练分两阶段：首先监督学习预热，然后基于结果级别的强化学习，直接使用标注工具调用链提供的程序化奖励，无需学习型评论家或评判者参与训练循环。相比CheMatAgent的树搜索方案，该方法每个问题仅需一次模型调用，成本固定。

Result: 在ChemToolBench多工具综合化学基准上，使用CheMatAgent相同的两种骨干网络进行评估：在Qwen-2.5-7B上，工具F1提升5.5%，返回F1提升9.6%，答案通过率领先；在Llama-3.1-8B上，工具F1提升3.7%，返回F1提升3.9%。所有对比均针对CheMatAgent的最强搜索配置，而本文方法仅使用单次模型调用，避免了成本随树增长的搜索过程。

Conclusion: 单一策略模型通过交错生成推理与工具调用，配合直接程序化奖励的强化学习，能够有效解决化学问答中的多工具使用问题，在简化系统架构的同时显著提升性能并降低推理成本，证明了无需复杂树搜索和学习型评论家的有效性。

Abstract: Chemistry questions often demand exact computation and database lookups that a language model cannot supply from its parameters, so it must reach for external tools. Tool use here is a three-part problem: select the right tool from a large pool, fill it with correctly typed arguments, and chain calls so that each consumes the outputs of the last. CheMatAgent, a previously published system, addresses this with hierarchical evolutionary MCTS: separate policy and execution models searching tool-call trees under two learned critics, one regressed partly onto GPT-assigned scores. We show that a single policy suffices. Our model interleaves reasoning, tool calls, and returns in one left-to-right generation, trained by a supervised warm-up and then outcome-level reinforcement learning against a programmatic reward read directly off the gold call chain, which leaves no learned critic and no judge in the training loop. On ChemToolBench multiple-tool comprehensive chemistry, on both backbones CheMatAgent use, we improve Tool F1 by 5.5% and Return F1 by 9.6% on Qwen-2.5-7B, and by 3.7% and 3.9% on Llama-3.1-8B, compared with their strongest search configuration, at one model invocation per question, against a search whose cost grows with the tree; we also lead answer Pass Rate on Qwen-2.5-7B.

</details>


### [376] [Singular Curvature in ReLU Training:Differentiation and the Gradient-Flow Limit Need Not Commute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30960)
*Xiaoyang Li,Runni Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了梯度下降算法（GD）的状态敏感性与连续梯度流极限之间存在固有差异，这种差异源于激活事件导致的跳跃项，即使误差很小，反向模式的灵敏度分析也可能产生显著偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降可视为梯度流的显式欧拉离散化，但一个状态精确的连续时间代理模型在求导后未必仍保持精确。研究旨在揭示离散GD程序与其连续流极限在自动微分灵敏度上的差异机制。

Method: 采用前点Stieltjes表示分解绝对连续的区域Hessian与原子界面曲率，建立离散导数收敛定理，分析事件自由区域传播器与速度归一化激活事件转移的贡献，并将结果扩展至参数和反向模式伴随系统。

Result: 证明了在固定有限时间范围内，GD状态收敛且离散导数趋近于无事件区域传播器，而极限流的导数还包含速度归一化的激活事件转移项；即使误差很小，存在一类全局强凸风险函数可产生任意大的灵敏度比差异。

Conclusion: GD离散敏感性与连续流极限的差异源于激活事件产生的跳跃项，通过平滑化或事件定位可恢复流灵敏度；这是关于确定性全批次有限时域动力学的一致性定理，而非大规模训练的普遍性主张。

Abstract: Gradient descent (GD) is explicit Euler for gradient flow, but a state-accurate continuous-time surrogate need not remain accurate after differentiation. At every fixed nonresonant step size, ordinary automatic differentiation exactly differentiates the executed hard-ReLU GD program. We prove that, over a fixed finite horizon, the GD states converge and these exact discrete derivatives approach an event-free regional propagator, whereas the derivative of the limiting flow also contains speed-normalized activation-event transfers. A prepoint Stieltjes representation separates the absolutely continuous regional Hessian from atomic interface curvature; one nonzero gradient jump produces an exactly rank-one endpoint discrepancy, and global convexity prevents complete multi-event cancellation whenever an event is strict. Nevertheless, a standard family of globally 1-strongly convex residual-ReLU squared-loss risks realizes arbitrarily large reciprocal sensitivity ratios on open initialization sets, with a uniform transversality margin. The same discrete-versus-flow decomposition extends to parameters and reverse-mode adjoints; resolved smoothing in the scalar or autonomous-normal regime and consistent event localization recover the flow sensitivity. The results concern deterministic full-batch, finite-horizon dynamics with a stable finite itinerary of separated same-direction transverse events; they are consistency theorems, not prevalence claims for large-scale training.

</details>


### [377] [A Universal Context-Reuse Layer for Cross-Model KV Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30963)
*Yi Li,Dongming Jiang,Yi Zhao,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了跨模型KV共享机制，允许将一个模型产生的KV缓存状态转换并应用于另一个不同的目标模型（包括不同规模、架构、分词器和模型家族），从而减少重复的prefill计算，在保持性能的同时显著降低推理延迟和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM服务系统经常处理重复或共享的上下文，但每个模型即使处理相同输入也各自进行prefill计算。现有的KV缓存复用机制仅限于同一模型内部，假设缓存的生产者和消费者完全相同。本研究旨在解决跨模型间的KV状态共享问题，以减少异构LLM和多智能体推理工作流中的冗余prefill计算。

Method: 提出跨模型KV共享（cross-model KV sharing）方法，将源模型产生的KV状态转换为可被不同目标模型消费的表示形式。该方法支持模型在规模、架构、注意力配置、分词器和模型家族等多个维度上的差异。研究在家族内部（within-family）和跨家族（cross-family）两种设置下进行了评估。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B→Qwen2.5-1.5B的家族内部实验中，转换后的KV状态将LongBench2准确率从27.59%提升至34.48%，相比原生1.5B基线提升6.89个百分点。在跨家族Qwen2.5-1.5B→Gemma-2-2B设置中，KV传递在4K上下文长度下减少目标端prefill成本高达67.05%，同时保持解码困惑度接近原生模型基线。在更异构的Llama3.1-70B→Qwen2.5-7B设置中，跨家族传递达到44.0%准确率（原生Qwen2.5-7B为45.7%），同时将测量延迟从899ms减少到138ms。

Conclusion: 研究结果初步证明KV状态可以作为可转移的计算表示，而非严格局限于模型本地的缓存，为异构LLM和多智能体推理工作流中的冗余prefill减少提供了新思路，并提出了"上下文迁移性"（context mobility）作为系统抽象概念。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) serving systems increasingly operate over repeated or shared context, yet each model typically performs its own prefill computation even when another model has already processed the same input. Existing KV-cache reuse mechanisms substantially reduce redundant computation within a single model, but generally assume that the producer and consumer of a cache are identical. We study \emph{cross-model KV sharing}, which translates the KV state produced by a source model into a representation that can be consumed by a different target model, including models that differ in scale, architecture, attention configuration, tokenizer, and model family. We evaluate the approach in both within-family and cross-family settings. For Qwen2.5-7B $\rightarrow$ Qwen2.5-1.5B, translated KV states improve LongBench2 accuracy from 27.59\% to 34.48\%, a gain of 6.89 percentage points over the native 1.5B baseline, while reducing handoff cost relative to native target prefill. For the cross-family Qwen2.5-1.5B $\rightarrow$ Gemma-2-2B setting, KV handoff reduces target-side prefill cost by up to 67.05\% at 4K context length while maintaining decoding perplexity close to native-model baselines. In a more heterogeneous Llama3.1-70B $\rightarrow$ Qwen2.5-7B setting, cross-family handoff achieves 44.0\% accuracy compared with 45.7\% for native Qwen2.5-7B inference, while reducing measured latency from 899ms to 138ms. These results provide initial evidence that KV states can serve as transferable computational representations rather than strictly model-local caches, and motivate \emph{context mobility} as a systems abstraction for reducing redundant prefill across heterogeneous LLM and multi-agent inference workflows.

</details>


### [378] [A Human-in-the-Loop Autonomous Agent for Industry Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30976)
*Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Ze Guo,Bokai Pan,Qi Liu,Shijin Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CastClaw是一个人机协同的自主预测系统，通过自然语言交互整合数据、专业模型、分析工具和领域知识，在五个电价数据集上实现了16个基线中最低的MSE和MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列预测需要任务制定、数据模型连接、领域知识整合、预测合理性评估和不确定性沟通等复杂流程，但现有专业预测模型通常运行在固定流程中，而通用大语言模型代理缺乏预测特定的检查、约束和停止规则。

Method: 构建了一个面向预测的工具化人机协同系统CastClaw，用户用自然语言指定目标、时间范围、约束和假设。系统从初始预测出发，检查时间模式和用户约束；当缺失证据时，检索上下文、运行分析或其他模型或询问用户；在明确的停止条件下保留、修订或升级结果，最终输出预测结果和包含输入、证据、行动和修订的执行报告。

Result: 在五个电价数据集的实验中，CastClaw在16个基线中取得了最低的点估计MSE和MAE。Nord Pool案例展示了可检查的工作流程。系统还在华北地区2026年1-6月的省级电力负荷数据上进行了离线验证。

Conclusion: 通过面向预测的工具化工程构建的人机协同自主预测系统能够有效整合专业模型和LLM代理的优势，在保持工作流程可检查性的同时提升预测准确性。

Abstract: Real-world time-series forecasting is rarely a one-shot model invocation: practitioners must formulate tasks, connect data and models, incorporate domain expertise, assess prediction plausibility, and communicate uncertainty. Specialized forecasting models provide strong numerical predictions but usually operate in fixed pipelines, while general-purpose large language model (LLM) agents often lack forecasting-specific checks, constraints, and stopping rules. We present CastClaw, a human-in-the-loop autonomous forecasting system built through forecasting-oriented harness engineering. CastClaw connects data, specialized models, analytical tools, user input, and a versioned execution record in one runtime. Users specify the target, horizon, constraints, and hypotheses in natural language. Starting from a supplied or model-generated forecast, CastClaw checks temporal patterns and user constraints; when evidence is missing, it retrieves context, runs an analysis or another model, or asks the user. It then keeps, revises, or escalates the result under explicit stopping conditions. The output contains the final forecast and an execution report recording inputs, evidence, actions, and revisions. In this five-dataset electricity-price setting, CastClaw reports the lowest point-estimate MSE and MAE among 16 baselines. A Nord Pool case demonstrates the inspectable workflow. CastClaw was also validated offline on provincial electricity-load data from North China covering January--June 2026.

</details>


### [379] [Sparse Competition during Training For the Emergence of Specialized Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30978)
*Baptiste Rossigneux,Karim Haroun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过神经元群组间的竞争机制，成功地在标准神经网络中自发诱导出功能模块化，这些模块能捕获数据的语义结构（如狗或车辆等类别），同时保持接近基线的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的模块化被认为能够提升可解释性和训练效果，通过促进解耦表示和降低冗余。作者旨在研究如何通过训练过程中的竞争动力学来实现模块化结构的自发涌现。

Method: 提出一种方法包含三个核心要素：(i)维持接近基线的准确率，(ii)通过稀疏路由机制将输入分配到神经元群组以诱导基于使用情况的模块化，(iii)鼓励模块专业化使其激活与输入类别相关联。该机制让模块在无监督情况下自发涌现。

Result: 在ImageNet-100和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，无需模块级监督即可涌现专业化模块。这些模块捕获数据中有意义的高层结构，单个模块对语义类别（如狗或车辆）产生响应。还观察到随着模块数量变化，会涌现出子任务的层级划分。

Conclusion: 竞争动力学可作为在标准架构中诱导功能模块化的简单机制，为通过竞争动态实现神经网络模块化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modularity in deep neural networks has been proposed as a means of improving both interpretability and training by promoting disentangled representations and reducing redundancy. In this work, we study the emergence of modular structure through competition dynamics between groups of neurons during training. We introduce a method that (i) maintains near-baseline accuracy, (ii) induces usage-based modularity by sparsely routing inputs to neuron groups, and (iii) encourages specialization of these modules, such that their activations are correlated with input classes. We evaluate the proposed approach on ImageNet-100 and CIFAR-100 and show that with it, specialized modules emerge without module-level supervision. These modules capture a meaningful high-level structure in the data, with individual modules responding to semantic categories (e.g., dogs or vehicles). We also study the emergence of a hierarchical partition of sub-tasks depending on the number of modules. Our results suggest that competitive dynamics can serve as a simple mechanism for inducing functional modularity in standard architectures.

</details>


### [380] [Controlling Refusal Behavior of LLMs via Stiefel-Constrained Rotation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30986)
*Kirill Bunin,Dmitry Bylinkin,Vladimir Aletov,Daniil Medyakov,Vladimir Solodkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼优化的旋转激活引导方法，用于控制大语言模型的拒绝行为，无需依赖额外的拒绝向量等辅助结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活引导技术虽然可以通过旋转激活来控制模型拒绝行为，但需要依赖拒绝向量等辅助结构来定义这些旋转，缺乏自包含的参数高效旋转变换方法。

Method: 基于黎曼优化开发自包含的方法论，学习参数高效的旋转变换，直接优化旋转参数而无需辅助结构。

Result: 实验验证表明所提方法在干预效率方面优于现有技术，广泛的消融研究揭示了关键设计选择的重要性。

Conclusion: 基于旋转的引导方案是实现对大语言模型行为更可靠控制的有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a lightweight approach for controlling model refusal at inference time. A growing line of research explores trainable rotations of activations to develop geometrically principled intervention mechanisms. However, existing techniques rely on auxiliary constructs, such as refusal vectors, to define these rotations. In our work, we develop a self-contained methodology for learning parameter-efficient rotational transformations based on Riemannian optimization. We empirically validate the proposed scheme, demonstrating its superiority in intervention efficiency. An extensive ablation study highlights the importance of key design choices in our method. Our results identify the proposed rotation-based steering scheme as a promising direction for more reliable control over the behavior of LLMs.

</details>


### [381] [Language-Informed Flow Matching for Trend-Guided Structure-Based 3D Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31009)
*Tianyu Gao,Zhikai Su,Jiashu Li,Wenjun Gao,Zichuan Ying,Zhe Zhao,Fei Zhang,Ye Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LiFT框架，基于流匹配实现语言引导的3D分子生成，通过语言先验提升药物化学指标，无需额外微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可控分子生成方法通常依赖任务特定的微调或外部采样引导，增加了计算成本，并且可能与不断演变的3D几何约束产生冲突。

Method: 提出LiFT框架，采用"感知-演化-组装"智能体生成目标感知的SMILES作为中间化学条件，利用预训练化学基础模型提取连续语义先验，通过轻量级语义投影器集成到几何生成中。引入自条件解耦路由器(SCDR)在ODE积分过程中根据中间结构状态调节速度场。

Result: 在Cross-Docked2020数据集上，LiFT在任务引导设置下实现了具有竞争力的分布匹配，同时改善了药物化学指标，并保持了竞争力的结构有效性，且无需额外的生成器微调。

Conclusion: 语言衍生的化学先验为3D分子生成提供了有效的趋势级指导，证明了跨模态方法在基于结构的药物设计中的潜力。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) requires ligands that satisfy both 3D target affinity and 1D chemical validity. Existing controllable generation methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or externally imposed sampling-time guidance, adding cost and potentially conflicting with evolving 3D geometric constraints. We propose LiFT, a language-informed cross-modal framework built on Flow Matching for trend-guided 3D molecular generation across both de novo design and scaffold hopping. LiFT uses a "Sense-Evolve-Assemble" agent to generate target-aware SMILES as intermediate chemical conditions, from which a pre-trained chemical foundation model extracts continuous semantic priors. These priors are integrated into geometric generation through a lightweight semantic projector with zero-initialized adaptive normalization for stable cross-modal conditioning. We further introduce a Self-Conditioned Decoupled Router (SCDR), which modulates the velocity field according to intermediate structural states during ODE integration. Experiments on Cross-Docked2020 show that LiFT achieves competitive distribution matching while improving medicinal chemistry metrics and maintaining competitive structural validity under task-steering settings without additional generator fine-tuning. Our results suggest that language-derived chemical priors provide effective trend-level guidance for 3D molecular generation. Code and released artifacts are available at https://github.com/kasurl/LiFT.

</details>


### [382] [TSPFN: A Temporal Tabular Foundation Model for Physiological Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31013)
*Jérémie Stym-Popper,Clément Rambour,Federica Granese,Nicolas Thome,Olivier Bernard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TSPFN，一种针对生理时间序列分类的基础模型，通过重新设计TabPFN架构并引入结构化时间表示和位置嵌入，在小到中等数据量的医学场景中实现了优于现有表格基线和专业时序模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学机器学习中，低到中等数据量场景下的模型泛化能力仍是主要挑战。现有的表格基础模型（如TabPFN）虽然通过上下文学习提供了无需微调的方案，但无法捕捉生理信号固有的时序依赖性，因此需要专门针对时间序列数据设计的模型。

Method: 本文提出TSPFN，重新设计TabPFN架构以适应时间序列数据。该方法集成结构化时间表示和位置嵌入来捕捉样本内时序和通道依赖性，并在140,000条跨多个医学领域的真实生理时间序列数据上进行预训练，构建统一的、可泛化的医学时间序列学习框架。

Result: 在多个生理基准测试中，TSPFN一致性地优于标准表格基线和原始TabPFN模型，并在跨域泛化能力上超越专业的深度时序模型。

Conclusion: TSPFN成功将表格基础模型扩展到时间序列领域，通过结构化的时空设计实现了生理时间序列的有效建模，为医学时间序列分类提供了一个强有力的通用框架，所有实验和代码已开源。

Abstract: Designing models that generalize effectively in low- to medium-data regimes remains a primary challenge in medical machine learning, particularly for physiological time-series classification. While tabular foundation models such as TabPFN offer an attractive alternative to conventional fine-tuning through in-context learning, they are not designed to capture the temporal dependencies inherent to physiological signals. ~In this paper, we introduce TSPFN, a foundation model that redesigns TabPFN's architecture for time series data. TSPFN integrates structured temporal representations and positional embeddings to capture intra-sample temporal and channel dependencies. To fully leverage its spatio-temporal design, the model is pretrained on 140,000 real-world physiological time series across multiple medical domains. This yields a unified, generalizable framework capable of learning the specificities of medical time series.
