Experiments across diverse physiological benchmarks demonstrate that TSPFN consistently outperforms standard tabular baselines and TabPFN, and achieves superior cross-domain generalization compared to specialized deep time-series models. All our experiments, ablation studies, and pre-processing scheme are publicly available at https://github.com/Jeremstym/TSPFN

</details>


### [383] [Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31036)
*Jiale Kang,Ziyin Yue,Zheng Zhan,Yangyi Huang,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NoRA方法，通过对LoRA的下投影矩阵进行归一化，在无需额外参数或推理开销的情况下，显著提升了模型训练的收敛速度、性能和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA被广泛用于参数高效的模型适配，但其训练动态的正则化方法尚未得到充分探索。由于LoRA将上投影初始化为零，其早期优化动态主要受下投影控制，这为改进训练稳定性提供了切入点。

Method: 提出归一化低秩适配方法，在训练过程中对下投影矩阵进行归一化。进一步证明该归一化可仅在初始化时应用，从而改进标准LoRA而无需在训练过程中重复归一化。

Result: 在预训练、监督微调和强化学习任务中，NoRA一致性地加速了收敛过程，提升了性能和训练稳定性，并缓解了灾难性遗忘问题，且无需额外可训练参数或推理时计算开销。

Conclusion: NoRA是一个简单且广泛适用的LoRA增强方法，通过简单的归一化策略有效改善了LoRA的训练动态，在多种学习场景下均表现出色，具有很高的实用价值。

Abstract: While low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for parameter-efficient model adaptation, how to regularize its training dynamics for stable and effective optimization remains underexplored. Because LoRA initializes the up-projection to zero, its early optimization dynamics are largely governed by the down-projection. Building on this observation, we introduce Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation (NoRA), a simple yet effective method that normalizes the down-projection matrices during training. We further show that the same normalization can be applied only at initialization, improving standard LoRA without requiring repeated normalization throughout training. Across pretraining, supervised finetuning, and reinforcement learning, NoRA consistently accelerates convergence, improves performance and training stability, and mitigates catastrophic forgetting. These benefits require neither additional trainable parameters nor inference-time computation, making NoRA a simple and broadly applicable enhancement to LoRA.

</details>


### [384] [Rotational Equivariance in Machine Learning: A Comprehensive Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31045)
*Peter Lippmann,Fred A. Hamprecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于3D数据机器学习中旋转等变性的综合性教程，系统介绍了从几何直观到数学基础再到现代等变架构的完整知识体系，旨在降低该领域的学习门槛并帮助实践者在不同方法间做出选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理、材料科学和3D计算机视觉等应用中，模型的预测结果不应依赖于坐标系的任意选择。旋转等变性通过要求输入的旋转导致输出相应变换来从数学上捕获这一需求，这是3D数据处理中的重要结构原则。

Method: 教程从物理和几何直观出发，逐步构建几何深度学习、群论和表示理论的必要数学工具。具体包括：欧几里得图上的消息传递、群作用与表示、球谐函数、Wigner矩阵、张量积和Clebsch-Gordan分解，并解释这些组件如何构建现代等变架构。随后系统梳理了三种主要的旋转等变策略：群卷积、内部张量表示和基于正则化的方法。

Result: 本教程建立了从基础数学概念到实际模型设计的清晰连接，统一了通常以不同形式语言表达的思想，通过对不同方法优劣的清晰讨论为实践者选择合适方法提供了指导，成功降低了旋转等变性这一主题的学习门槛。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地介绍旋转等变性的理论基础和实现策略，本教程为理解和应用等变深度学习方法提供了完整的学习框架，有助于推动该技术在3D数据相关领域的应用和发展。

Abstract: Rotational symmetry is one of the most important structural principles in machine learning on 3D data. In applications ranging from physics and materials science to 3D computer vision, predictions should not depend on an arbitrary choice of coordinate frame. Rotational equivariance captures this requirement mathematically by enforcing that a rotation of the input induces a corresponding transformation of the model output. This tutorial provides a comprehensive introduction to rotational equivariance, starting from the physical and geometric intuition behind coordinate independence and building up the necessary machinery from geometric deep learning, group theory, and representation theory. We introduce message passing on Euclidean graphs, group actions and representations, spherical harmonics, Wigner matrices, tensor products, and Clebsch-Gordan decomposition, and explain how these ingredients give rise to modern equivariant architectures. We then survey the principal strategies for incorporating rotational equivariance in deep learning, including group convolutions, internal tensorial representations, and canonicalization-based methods, and discuss their practical strengths and limitations. The tutorial aims to lower the barrier to the subject by connecting the underlying mathematics to practical model design, by unifying ideas that are often expressed in different formal languages, and by helping practitioners choose among competing approaches through a clear discussion of their trade-offs.

</details>


### [385] [Does On-Policy Distillation Really Distill? From Noisy Teacher to Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31046)
*Yi Ding,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在策略蒸馏（OPD）的有效性主要源于抑制低概率token而非教师指导，提出了无监督方法OPSA，在AIME24上相比基线提升35.41分，相比OPD提升16.77分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略蒸馏（OPD）作为强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）的替代方案，提供密集的token级监督。然而，教师模型对学生生成的轨迹评分时，这些轨迹对教师而言本质上是离策略的，导致监督的可靠性及学生改进的来源不明确。作者旨在探究OPD的实际工作机制及其有效性的真正来源。

Method: 1. 定量分析OPD训练过程中教师监督的噪声，发现噪声普遍存在且随教师规模增加而加剧
2. 分析学生策略对噪声的不敏感性
3. 研究学习集中在低对数概率token的现象
4. 提出在策略自适应（OPSA）方法：无监督方法，使用熵自适应负优势，向高熵位置分配更强学习信号，抑制尾部token并在头部token间均匀重分配概率质量

Result: 1. OPD主要通过抑制低对数概率token起作用，而非依赖教师指导
2. 使用单个固定负优势可达到教师提供优势的性能，表明教师并非必需
3. OPSA方法相比Qwen3-1.7B基线在AIME24上Avg@32提升35.41分（相对增益263%），在三个基准测试上Pass@32翻倍以上
4. OPSA在AIME24 Avg@32上超越OPD 16.77分
5. 跨模型家族和任务的广泛实验验证了方法的有效性和泛化性

Conclusion: OPD的有效性主要源于抑制低概率token而非教师指导，这一发现促成了无监督方法OPSA的提出。OPSA通过熵自适应负优势实现更高效的学习，无需教师监督即可达到或超越OPD的性能，为模型蒸馏和自改进提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers dense token-level supervision as an alternative to the sparse outcome-level advantages of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, the teacher scores student-generated trajectories that are inherently off-policy for it, so the reliability of its supervision, and hence the source of the student's improvement, remains unclear. We quantitatively analyze teacher supervision during OPD training and find substantial noise whose prevalence increases with teacher scale. Surprisingly, the student policy is insensitive to such noise, converging to comparable performance regardless of whether noisy supervision is retained or removed. Does OPD distill at all? By analyzing what drives its gains, we find that learning concentrates on low log-probability tokens, and using a single fixed negative advantage matches the performance of teacher-provided ones. This suggests that OPD works largely by suppressing low log-probability tokens, which requires no teacher. These findings motivate On-Policy Self-Adaptation (OPSA), a supervision-free method using entropy-adaptive negative advantages. It assigns stronger learning signals to high-entropy positions, suppressing tail tokens, and evenly redistributing probability mass among head tokens. Compared with the base \texttt{Qwen3-1.7B}, OPSA improves Avg@32 by 35.41 points on AIME24, corresponding to a 263\% relative gain, and more than doubles Pass@32 across all three benchmarks. It also outperforms OPD by 16.77 points in Avg@32 on AIME24. Extensive experiments and analyses across model families and tasks further demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [386] [Universal Transformers for Circuit Computations: Perfect Length Generalization in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31067)
*Takuya Ito,Ruchir Puri,Murray Campbell,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Learning generalizable algorithmic computations remains a challenge for neural networks, as reflected in persistent failures on compositional and length generalization benchmarks. We present a provably correct, transformer parameterization (with only 280 learnable parameters for Boolean algebra tasks) capable of learning and evaluating problems of any depth or length. We assume inputs are fully parenthesized, well-formed expressions. Our approach conceptualizes algorithmic tasks as circuit models embedded in transformers, enabling depth-1 circuit reduction in a single forward pass. To achieve depth generalization, we introduce a positional encoding that tracks each gate's depth within the circuit, enabling the model to identify evaluable subexpressions at each iteration via masked hard attention, with $O(n)$ per-iteration complexity via linear attention. Combined with an autonomous halting criterion, the model terminates after $d$ iterations for problems of depth $d$, yielding $O(n \cdot d)$ total complexity. We show that training on shallow problem instances (depth 1 and depth 2) effectively recovers interpretable parameters that {\em snap} into place, resulting in exact length generalization. Though we establish that our construction provably evaluates Boolean expressions -- a universal symbolic computation -- of arbitrary length perfectly, in other experiments we also demonstrate that our transformer variant can learn and generalize perfectly (100% accuracy) on other common length generalization benchmarks, including modular arithmetic and ListOps.

</details>


### [387] [A Model with No Head and Many Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31069)
*Nikita Koriagin,Yaroslav Aksenov,George Bredis,Gleb Gerasimov,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Soft Latent Thinking方法，用轻量投影器替代语言模型头部，在连续嵌入空间中进行自回归推理，避免离散词元生成，在提升推理质量的同时降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在每一步解码时都需要通过大规模词汇表头部投影隐藏状态，计算成本高昂，且强制所有推理过程必须表达为离散词元，限制了推理的表达能力。

Method: 提出Soft Latent Thinking方法，在推理过程中使用轻量投影器替代语言模型头部，实现在连续嵌入空间中的自回归展开（rollout），使推理步骤保持连续状态而非离散化词元。

Result: 在DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B和LLaMA-3.2-3B上的实验表明，该方法在所有k值上均一致提升pass@k指标，同时降低了思维链推理中的每步计算量，在所有软思考方法中实现了最高的pass@32分数。

Conclusion: 证明了在连续空间中进行有效推理是可行的，无需离散词元生成，为提升大语言模型推理效率提供了一种有效的新途径。

Abstract: Large language models decode by projecting hidden states through a large vocabulary head at every step. This operation is computationally costly and forces all reasoning to be expressed in discrete tokens. We introduce Soft Latent Thinking, a method that replaces the LM head during reasoning with a lightweight projector, enabling autoregressive rollout in embedding space where reasoning steps remain continuous rather than tokenized. Experiments on DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B and LLaMA-3.2-3B show that Soft Latent Thinking consistently improves pass@k across all k while reducing per-step compute during chain-of-thought. Our method achieves the highest pass@32 among all soft-thinking approaches, demonstrating that effective reasoning can be carried out in continuous space without discrete token generation.

</details>


### [388] [Sycophantic Agreement Transfers with Neutral Data via Contrastive Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31079)
*Camila Blank,Zhuofan Ying,Christopher Potts,Peter Hase,Jing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，广泛使用的对比偏好优化目标会意外导致语言模型产生"阿谀赞同"行为，这种行为从教师模型转移到学生模型，且信号分散在整个数据集中，难以通过过滤特定样本来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿谀赞同（模型过度肯定用户而牺牲事实准确性）是模型对齐中的已知问题，但对其如何从模型训练中产生的理解有限。研究者希望揭示这一行为的产生机制，特别是与广泛使用的偏好优化方法之间的关系。

Method: 使用OLMo 3后训练流水线，研究对比偏好优化目标（包括DPO和其他6种偏好优化目标）与阿谀赞同行为的关系。通过分析多个模型家族的教师-学生模型对，测量教师模型的阿谀赞同率对数比与学生模型阿谀赞同率之间的相关性。进一步通过探针数据归因和logit线性选择等方法分析偏好数据。

Result: 发现了教师模型阿谀赞同率与学生模型阿谀赞同率之间的强相关性，证明这种行为可从教师模型转移至学生模型。该现象不仅存在于DPO，还出现在其他6种偏好优化目标中。阿谀赞同信号分散在整个数据集中而非集中在特定样本，每个样本表面上看都是中性的，没有明确的阿谀赞同实例。基于探针的数据归因或logit线性选择的过滤方法无法在不删除大量数据的情况下缓解阿谀行为。

Conclusion: 用于生成偏好数据的教师模型可能与对齐训练目标以意想不到的方式交互，导致阿谀赞同等不良行为的泛化。这表明在模型对齐过程中需要更仔细地考虑教师模型选择和训练目标设计，以避免产生潜在有害的行为模式。

Abstract: Sycophantic agreement refers to a behavior in which language models excessively affirm the user, often at the cost of factual accuracy. Although sycophantic agreement is a well-known failure of model alignment, there is limited understanding of how it emerges from model training. In this work, we demonstrate that sycophantic agreement can emerge as an unintended consequence of widely used contrastive preference optimization objectives. Using the OLMo 3 post-training pipeline, we show that, for various pairs of teacher models across three families, there is a strong correlation between the log-ratio of the teacher model sycophantic agreement rates and the resulting student model sycophantic agreement rate. We further demonstrate that this unintended transfer is not limited to DPO but also occurs across 6 other preference optimization objectives. To understand whether this effect can be attributed to particular training examples, we analyze the preference data and find that the sycophancy signal is diffused across the entire dataset rather than concentrated in a sparse set of examples: each example appears neutral, i.e., there are no explicit instances of sycophantic agreement, and filtering based on probe-based data attribution or logit-linear selection fails to mitigate sycophancy without removing a large portion of the dataset. Overall, our findings suggest that the teacher models used to generate preference data can interact with alignment training objectives in unexpected ways, generalizing to undesirable and potentially harmful behaviors like sycophantic agreement.

</details>


### [389] [Stress-Testing Efficient Responsible-AI Evaluation: When Compute Savings Change Benchmark Conclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31108)
*Ahmed El Kady,Aravind Narayanan,Rehana Noorani,Yani Ioannou,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过测试批处理、量化和基准缩减等高效评估方法，发现这些方法会改变模型行为结论，高效评估应被视为需要验证有效性的测量干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效评估方法虽然能降低评估成本，但很少有人测试这些方法是否会影响关于模型行为的结论的稳定性。本文旨在对责任AI基准测试中的结论稳健性进行压力测试，检验高效评估方法是否会改变原本的结论。

Method: 在BBQ和BBQ-V数据集上评估三个密集模型和专家混合模型，测试七种条件：批处理、量化、基准缩减及其组合。将准确率、偏差严重程度、推理质量、子组行为、子集成员稳定性、运行时间和GPU能耗与完整的BF16基准进行全面对比，而非仅关注聚合准确率。

Result: 较大批处理在准确率上与基准相差0.35个百分点以内，子组变化较小，六个模型-数据集设置中有五个降低了能耗。INT8量化基本保持质量但能耗是基准的1.79-4.26倍。INT4量化导致较大且依赖模型和上下文的变化。缩减的基准提供最一致的节省，但非常小的子集对保留哪些项目更敏感。

Conclusion: 高效评估方法应被视为一种测量干预，其有效性必须在基准测试旨在支持的各项结论中进行验证，不能仅依赖聚合准确率的保持作为充分标准。

Abstract: Efficient evaluation changes the protocol used to support claims about model behavior, yet it is rarely tested whether those claims remain stable after the evaluation itself is made cheaper. We stress-test conclusion robustness in responsible-AI benchmarking by evaluating three dense and mixture-of-experts models on BBQ and BBQ-V under seven conditions spanning batching, quantization, benchmark reduction, and their combinations. Rather than treating preserved aggregate accuracy as sufficient, we compare accuracy, bias severity and prevalence, reasoning quality, subgroup behavior, subset-membership stability, runtime, and measured GPU energy against a full-benchmark BF16 baseline. Larger batching keeps accuracy within 0.35 percentage points of baseline and produces comparatively small subgroup changes, while reducing energy in five of six model--dataset settings. INT8 largely preserves quality but uses 1.79--4.26$\times$ baseline energy. INT4 causes larger, model- and context-dependent changes. Reduced benchmarks provide the most consistent savings, but very small subsets are substantially more sensitive to which items are retained. Efficient evaluation should therefore be treated as a measurement intervention whose validity must be checked across the conclusions the benchmark is intended to support. Our project website is https://vectorinstitute.github.io/sustainable-rai-evaluation/ and the code is available at https://github.com/VectorInstitute/sustainable-rai-evaluation.

</details>


### [390] [On the Complexity of the Compatibility Problem for Succinctly Encoded Conditional Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31120)
*Guy Emerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了使用算术电路简洁编码的条件分布的兼容性问题在计算上是不可行的：非零概率情况下为co-NP完全，允许零概率情况下为PSPACE完全，且存在兼容的条件分布无法用简洁的方式表示联合分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究机器学习中概率模型隐含的权衡问题。模型通常以条件概率形式进行预测，但一对条件分布p(x|y)和p(y|x)可能不存在与之兼容的联合分布p(x,y)。在高维设置（如神经网络模型）中，条件分布通常用简洁电路表示，需要研究这种简洁表示下的兼容性问题。

Method: 形式化并研究兼容性问题的简洁版本，将条件分布编码为算术电路而非概率表。针对非零概率和允许零概率两种情况，通过计算复杂性理论分析问题的难度，区分多种兼容性概念，并在多项式层次不坍缩的假设下分析联合分布的表示问题。

Result: 对于简洁电路表示的条件分布，兼容性问题在计算上不可行：当所有概率非零时，问题是co-NP完全的；当概率可以为零时，问题的多个版本是PSPACE完全的。此外，在多项式层次不坍缩的假设下，存在兼容的简洁条件分布，其联合分布无法用简洁方式表示。

Conclusion: 这些结果对概率建模和机器学习具有重要含义：在高维设置中使用简洁表示的条件分布时，验证其兼容性在计算上是困难的，甚至兼容的条件分布对可能不存在简洁的联合分布表示，这为实际应用中的模型设计和验证带来了根本性挑战。

Abstract: The motivation for this paper is the investigation of the trade-offs implicit in probabilistic models used in machine learning. Models are often used to make predictions in the form of conditional probabilities. However, a pair of conditional distributions p(x|y) and p(y|x) may not be compatible with any joint distribution p(x,y). Given two such conditionals, determining if there exists a compatible joint is known as the compatibility problem. For discrete random variables, when the conditionals are encoded as probability tables, the compatibility problem has a known solution, which is computationally tractable. In this paper, we formalise and study a succinct version of the problem, encoding conditional distributions as arithmetic circuits. This is applicable to practical applications of probabilistic modelling in high-dimensional settings, including neural network models. We show that, for succinct circuit representations of conditionals, the compatibility problem is intractable. In the case that all probabilities are non-zero, the problem is co-NP-complete. In the case that probabilities can be zero, we give examples to demonstrate that several notions of compatibility can be distinguished, and we prove that multiple versions of the problem are PSPACE-complete. Furthermore, we show that, assuming the polynomial hierarchy does not collapse, there exist compatible succinct conditionals whose joint cannot be expressed succinctly. Implications of these results for probabilistic modelling and machine learning are discussed.

</details>


### [391] [Sharp Approximation Rates for Neural Networks with Affine Latent Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31157)
*Shijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了在潜在维度 M 和网络参数预算 P 的权衡中，固定维度的潜在空间足以在增加网络预算时实现逼近误差趋近于零。对于仿射生成器和全连接ReLU架构，在逼近α-Hölder函数类时，最优最坏一致逼近误差的精确阶为(P·min{M,P})^{-α/d}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多参数高效方法（如超网络、低维参数化、参数高效适应和模型压缩）通过从低维潜在表示生成大型神经网络的参数。理解潜在维度M和网络参数预算P之间的权衡，对于刻画这些方法的表达效率具有基础性意义。然而，这种权衡的精确数学刻画尚未明确。

Method: 研究仿射生成器和全连接ReLU架构下的理论性质。具体方法是对架构Φ（满足P_Φ ≤ P）和仿射生成器G: R^M → R^{P_Φ}进行联合优化，分析在[0,1]^d上α-Hölder函数单位球上的最优最坏一致逼近误差，其中0 < α ≤ 1。

Result: 证明了最优最坏一致逼近误差具有精确阶(P·min{M,P})^{-α/d}。这一结果表明，即使固定维度的潜在空间，在增加网络预算时也足以实现逼近误差趋近于零。

Conclusion: 该工作为参数高效方法提供了坚实的理论基础，揭示了潜在维度与网络预算之间的根本权衡关系。理论结果表明这些方法在表达效率方面具有良好的扩展性质，为实际应用提供了理论指导和保证。

Abstract: Many parameter-efficient methods generate the parameters of a large neural network from a low-dimensional latent representation. Given an architecture $Φ$ with $P_Φ$ parameter slots, we write $\boldsymbolθ_f=\mathcal{G}(\boldsymbolξ_f)$, where $\mathcal{G}\colon\mathbb{R}^M\to\mathbb{R}^{P_Φ}$ is a parameter generator and $\boldsymbolξ_f\in\mathbb{R}^M$ is a latent representation of the target function $f$. The architecture $Φ$ and the generator $\mathcal{G}$ are shared across the entire target class, while each target $f$ is represented by its own latent vector $\boldsymbolξ_f$, with $Φ_{\mathcal{G}(\boldsymbolξ_f)}$ approximating $f$. This framework encompasses hypernetworks, low-dimensional parameterizations, parameter-efficient adaptation, and model compression. Understanding the tradeoff between the latent dimension $M$ and the network budget $P$ is therefore fundamental to characterizing the expressive efficiency of these methods. We study this tradeoff for affine generators and fully connected ReLU architectures. More precisely, optimizing jointly over architectures $Φ$ satisfying $P_Φ\leq P$ and affine generators $\mathcal{G}:\mathbb{R}^M\to \mathbb{R}^{P_Φ}$, we prove that the optimal worst-case uniform approximation error over the unit ball of $α$-Hölder functions on $[0,1]^d$, where $0<α\leq1$, has the sharp order $ \bigl(P\min\{M,P\}\bigr)^{-α/d}. $ In particular, our result shows that even a fixed-dimensional latent space suffices to achieve vanishing approximation error as the network budget increases.

</details>


### [392] [Constant Individual Regret in General Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31166)
*Mingyang Liu,Gabriele Farina,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ECHO-OFTRL算法，首次在完全信息反馈下消除了N玩家正则形式博弈中个体后悔值对时间视界T的依赖，实现了O(poly(N, log m_max))的后悔值上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解耦的无后悔动态为均衡提供了去中心化路径，但现有保证中个体后悔值仍然存在对时间视界T的多对数依赖。本文旨在消除这种依赖，为有限N玩家博弈提供更优的理论保证。

Method: 引入ECHO-OFTRL算法：将乐观跟随正则化领导者（OFTRL）与EMA级联相结合以实现高阶乐观（ECHO），其中EMA为指数移动平均。该算法受现代滤波器设计启发，采用新型乐观机制，具有确定性和完全解耦特性。

Result: 对于任意时间视界T≥1，算法同时保证博弈中每个玩家的后悔值上界为O(poly(N, log m_max))，完全消除了对T的依赖，其中m_max为最大动作集大小。

Conclusion: ECHO-OFTRL成功实现了与时间视界T无关的后悔值保证，为去中心化博弈均衡提供了更优的理论基础，其高阶乐观机制为后续研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Uncoupled no-regret dynamics provide a decentralized route to equilibrium, but prior guarantees for individual regret retain a polylogarithmic dependence on the horizon. We remove this dependence for every finite $N$-player normal-form game under full-information feedback. We introduce \emph{ECHO-OFTRL}: optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OFTRL) equipped with an EMA cascade for high-order optimism (ECHO), where EMA denotes exponential moving average. The algorithm is deterministic and fully uncoupled. If $m_{\max}$ denotes the largest action-set size, then, simultaneously for every horizon $T\geq1$, it guarantees that each of the $N$ players in the game incurs regret upper bounded by $O(\textrm{poly}(N, \log m_{\max}))$. Our algorithm leverages a new form of optimism inspired by modern filter design.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [393] [Goal Staying Makes Sum-of-Costs Anonymous Multi-Agent Path Finding NP-Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28658)
*Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了目标停留型匿名多智能体路径规划(AMAPF)与智能体消失型AMAPF之间存在复杂度边界：前者最小化总代价(SoC)是NP难问题，后者存在多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明智能体消失型AMAPF在最小化最大完成时间、总距离和总代价(SoC)等目标下存在多项式时间网络流算法。本文旨在研究标准的目标停留型AMAPF（智能体到达目标后停留）是否具有相同性质。

Method: 1. 通过扩充标准时间扩展流模型，加入目标停留约束来形式化SoC最小化问题；
2. 证明所得线性规划松弛非整数性；
3. 通过从3-SAT问题归约，证明目标停留型AMAPF中SoC最小化问题的NP困难性。

Result: 证明了标准目标停留型AMAPF中SoC最小化问题是NP难的，与其多项式时间可解的智能体消失型变体形成对比。由此确定了AMAPF问题的一个清晰复杂度边界：问题复杂度由已完成智能体是否停留在目标位置决定。

Conclusion: 本文建立了AMAPF问题复杂度边界理论：智能体是否在到达目标后停留决定了SoC最小化问题的计算复杂度。这一发现澄清了目标停留与智能体消失两种变体之间的本质差异，为后续算法设计和理论研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Anonymous Multi-Agent Path Finding (AMAPF) admits polynomial-time network-flow algorithms for several objectives, including makespan, total distance, and sum-of-costs (SoC) when agents disappear upon reaching goals. We show that standard goal-staying AMAPF is fundamentally different. We first formulate SoC minimization by augmenting the standard time-expanded flow model with goal-settlement constraints and show that the resulting linear programming relaxation is non-integral. We then prove that minimizing SoC in goal-staying AMAPF is NP-hard via a reduction from 3-SAT. Together with the polynomial-time result for the disappearing variant, this establishes a sharp complexity boundary determined by whether completed agents remain at their goals.

</details>


### [394] [ASTRA - Agentic System for Ticket Resolution and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28790)
*Shashidhar Reddy Javaji,Mohamed Trabelsi,Jin Cao,Huseyin Uzunalioglu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出ASTRA系统，通过协调三个专业信息收集代理和评判优化循环，生成有证据支持的技术故障排查报告，在987个真实电信故障工单上达到4.13/5.0的质量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术运维团队需要从工单文本、历史案例、系统日志和技术文档等分散来源中合成证据来处理大量事件。现有自动化方法依赖单体生成，缺乏显式证据建模和溯源机制，当关键信号在多个来源中稀疏分布时，输出难以验证。

Method: ASTRA采用中心协调器协调三个专业代理：TicketSimilarityAgent通过稠密检索和LLM重排序检索相关历史案例；LogAgent通过确定性过滤和约束LLM分析将日志提炼为结构化、带引用的发现；DomainKnowledgeAgent通过MCP检索技术知识。输出转换为声明-证据表示，每个声明链接到源段落。JudgeAgent按五个标准评分，OrchestratorAgent将低分转化为针对性后续查询进行有限迭代优化。

Result: 在987个电信故障工单上，ASTRA平均质量评分为4.13/5.0，59.9%的报告在组件族级别或更好级别识别故障区域。相关性和清晰度评分分别为4.88和4.94，编造技术细节低于3%的错误案例。分层分析显示硬件故障比软件或配置故障显著更难（Cohen's d=0.80）。

Conclusion: ASTRA通过显式证据建模和迭代优化成功提升了工单解决质量，但基于文本的证据渠道对硬件故障诊断存在根本性局限，这指明了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Technical operations teams resolve large volumes of incidents by synthesizing fragmented evidence from ticket text, historical cases, system logs, and technical documentation. Existing automation often relies on monolithic generation without explicit evidence modeling or provenance, making outputs difficult to verify when critical signals are sparse across sources. We propose ASTRA, an agentic system for ticket resolution in which a central orchestrator coordinates three specialist information-gathering agents and drives a judge-orchestrator refinement loop to produce evidence-backed troubleshooting reports. TicketSimilarityAgent retrieves relevant historical precedents through dense retrieval and LLM reranking; LogAgent distills hundreds of thousands of log lines into structured, quote-grounded findings using deterministic filtering and constrained LLM analysis; and DomainKnowledgeAgent retrieves relevant technical knowledge via the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Their outputs are transformed into a claim-evidence representation linking each claim to a verbatim source passage, assigning a support level, and preventing cross-attribution. A JudgeAgent scores the report on five criteria, while the OrchestratorAgent converts low scores into targeted follow-up queries for bounded iterative refinement. Evaluated on 987 real-world telecom fault tickets across seven product lines, ASTRA achieves a mean quality score of 4.13/5.0, with 59.9% of reports identifying the fault area at the component-family level or better. Relevance and Clarity scores are 4.88 and 4.94, respectively, while fabricated technical details remain below 3% of error cases. Stratification by fault type reveals that hardware faults remain substantially harder than software or configuration faults (Cohen's d=0.80), pointing to a fundamental limitation of text-based evidence channels for hardware fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [395] [Generalizable Multi-Agent Planning from Signal Temporal Logic Specifications via Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29490)
*Joe Eappen,Zikang Xiong,Shreyash S. Iyengar,Suresh Jagannathan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的多智能体信号时序逻辑（STL）规划方法，解决了现有方法在可扩展性和泛化性之间的权衡问题，支持异构任务规范并显著提高了规划多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多智能体系统（如无人机群、自动驾驶汽车、仓库机器人）需要满足复杂的时间任务并避免碰撞。现有的基于优化的STL规划方法虽然能处理任意STL规范，但随智能体数量增加面临严重的可扩展性问题；而基于学习的方法虽然能高效处理大量智能体，但在部署时任务目标与训练时不同时表现不佳，且不支持异构规范或多智能体协调任务。这种可扩展性与泛化性之间的根本权衡是多智能体STL规划的实际应用挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的多智能体STL规划方法。使用STL的可微分近似，将STL梯度集成到去噪过程中，使方法能够泛化到训练覆盖的目标区域内任意位置的谓词所构成的新公式。该方法支持异构规范，并利用扩散模型的特性自然增强规划多样性。

Result: 该方法达到了与现有基于学习方法相同的可扩展性，同时能够泛化到训练时未见过的STL公式。支持异构任务规范，并通过增强规划多样性显著减少了智能体之间的安全相关违规（如碰撞）。详细评估研究验证了基于STL引导的扩散多智能体规划器在构建泛化性强、可扩展且多样化规划方面的实用性。

Conclusion: 扩散模型为多智能体STL规划提供了一种有效的解决方案，成功弥合了可扩展性与泛化性之间的差距。该方法不仅支持异构规范和团队协调任务，还通过自然增强规划多样性提高了安全性，为现实世界的多智能体系统规划提供了实用的算法框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems in the real-world (e.g., drone swarms, autonomous cars, warehouse robots) must satisfy rich, temporal tasks while avoiding collisions. Signal Temporal Logic (STL) elegantly encodes such objectives, but current STL planning methods face critical limitations. State-of-the-art optimization-based approaches can handle arbitrary STL specifications but struggle with scalability, becoming computationally impractical as the number of agents grows. Learning-based methods efficiently handle a large number of agents with rapid planning times but fare poorly when deployment-time objectives differ from those used during training, and do not support planning tasks that require different specifications to be ascribed to different agents (i.e., heterogeneity) or team-level specifications requiring coordination of multiple agents. This fundamental trade-off between generalizability and scalability presents a challenge for realizing multi-agent STL planning algorithms in practice. To overcome this challenge, we introduce a new diffusion method for multi-agent planning with STL specifications. Using a differentiable approximation of STL, we integrate the STL gradient in the denoising process, making our approach generalizable to novel formulas whose predicates are placed anywhere within the goal region covered during training, while achieving the same scalability as existing learning-based methods. Our method supports heterogeneous specifications, and by using diffusion models, naturally enhances plan diversity, thereby significantly reducing safety-related violations (e.g., collisions) among agents. A detailed evaluation study justifies the utility of STL-guided diffusion-based multi-agent planners for constructing generalizable, scalable, and diverse plans. Videos and code are available at https://www.jeappen.com/diff-ma-stl/ and https://github.com/jeappen/diff-ma-stl .

</details>


### [396] [Forward-Deployed Full-Stack Engineering for Autonomous Cloud MLOps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29615)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个证据门控的多智能体框架，用于将自然语言描述的MLOps云工程任务转换为经过验证的代码仓库和可操作的云部署，通过证据门控机制确保生命周期转换的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统在各行业的应用日益广泛，但实际部署运营需要协调应用开发、模型流水线、云基础设施、安全、部署、监控、再训练、恢复和回滚等多个复杂环节，现有方法缺乏对这些复杂协调过程的系统性保障。

Method: 框架结合图工程、循环工程和智能体线束工程。状态化图协调器协调专业智能体完成仓库生成、审查、执行、验证、发布和监控，同时管理工作流依赖关系、证据门控、重试边界、恢复路径和终止条件。关键的生命周期转换仅在可验证执行或运行时证据支持其所需谓词时才能进行。验证失败会触发有界的反思、修复和重新验证，运行时故障、漂移、退化或策略违规则会触发有界的适应、恢复或回滚。智能体线束工程通过受控能力和隔离执行环境来约束仓库生成、审查和修复、制品执行以及云操作。

Result: 在Google Cloud平台上实现了该框架，并评估了仓库完整性、受控执行、证据门控转换、云端升级和有界恢复。实验结果表明，该框架能够防止不受支持的生命周期转换，并驱动每次运行达到经过验证的可操作部署或可审计的终端失败状态。

Conclusion: 该框架通过证据门控机制有效保证了MLOps云工程任务的安全性和可靠性，实现了从自然语言描述到验证部署的自动化流程，为复杂机器学习系统的运营提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Across industries, machine-learning systems support applications ranging from prediction and anomaly detection to forecasting, optimization, and scheduling, yet operationalizing these systems requires coordinating application development, model pipelines, cloud infrastructure, security, deployment, monitoring, retraining, recovery, and rollback. We present an evidence-gated multi-agent framework for transforming a natural-language MLOps cloud engineering task into a verified repository and operational cloud deployment. The framework combines graph engineering, loop engineering, and agent harness engineering. A stateful Graph Orchestrator coordinates specialized agents for repository generation, review, execution, verification, release, and monitoring while governing workflow dependencies, evidence gates, retry bounds, recovery paths, and termination. Consequential lifecycle transitions proceed only when their required predicates are supported by verifiable execution or runtime evidence. Verification failures activate bounded reflection, repair, and re-verification, while runtime evidence of failure, drift, degradation, or policy violation can trigger bounded adaptation, recovery, or rollback. Agent harness engineering constrains repository generation, review, and repair, artifact execution, and cloud operations through controlled capabilities and isolated execution environments. We realize the framework on Google Cloud Platform and evaluate repository completeness, controlled execution, evidence-gated transitions, cloud promotion, and bounded recovery. Our experimental results show that the framework prevents unsupported lifecycle transitions and drives each run toward either a verified operational deployment or an auditable terminal failure.

</details>


### [397] [AgenticRag-R1: Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Stack Memory for Multi-Step Reasoning, Retrieval and Memorizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29622)
*Xinke Jiang,Yue Fang,Zhibang Yang,Jiaran Gao,Zhixin Zhang,Tao Feng,Rihong Qiu,Wentao Zhang,Hongxin Ding,Ruizhe Zhang,Yongxin Xu,Yuheng Huang,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了AgenticRag-R1框架，通过细粒度动作空间和层级奖励机制增强RAG系统的长程推理能力，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在需要自适应检索和持续修正的复杂多步推理任务上表现不佳；基于强化学习的Agentic RAG方法存在动作空间粗糙、轨迹级奖励分配弱、以及偏向短视推理模板的问题。

Method: 提出AgenticRag-R1强化学习框架，核心包含：1）深度融合推理、检索和记忆的记忆栈机制；2）细粒度动作空间设计；3）层级化动作感知奖励；4）信息感知的轨迹拒绝策略，以支持有效的长程学习。

Result: 在多跳推理、开放域和智能体推理等多个基准测试上，跨越不同规模的骨干模型，AgenticRag-R1均持续优于强基线方法；学习到了更鲁棒、可解释且具备记忆感知能力的推理行为。

Conclusion: 细粒度动作建模和信息感知优化对长程推理任务至关重要，所提框架成功解决了现有RAG系统在复杂推理场景下的局限性，为构建更智能的检索增强生成系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models (LLMs), yet existing RAG systems often struggle with complex, multi-step reasoning that requires adaptive retrieval and continuous revision of intermediate contexts. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based agentic RAG methods partially alleviate this issue, but typically rely on coarse-grained action spaces and trajectory-level rewards, resulting in weak reward assignment and a bias toward short-horizon, stereotyped reasoning template. To address, we propose AgenticRag-R1, a RL framework that deeply integrates reasoning, retrieval, and memory via a memory stack and fine-grained action space, supported by hierarchical action-aware rewards and an information-aware trajectory rejection strategy to enable effective long-horizon learning. Experiments across a diverse set of multi-hop, open-domain, and agentic reasoning benchmarks, spanning multiple backbone model sizes, demonstrate that AgenticRag-R1 consistently outperforms strong baselines. Moreover, AgenticRag-R1 learns more robust, interpretable, and memory-aware reasoning behaviors, highlighting the effect of fine-grained action modeling and information-aware optimization for long-horizon reasoning. Our code is anonymous available at https://github.com/jiangxinke/Harness-RL/tree/AgenticRAG-R1-Whitebox.

</details>


### [398] [Harness-RL: Black-Box Reinforcement Learning with Action-Args Decoupling for Central-Agent Multi-Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29641)
*Xinke Jiang,Zhixin Zhang,Zhibang Yang,Jiaran Gao,Rihong Qiu,Shijin Chen,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness-RL框架，通过CAPO算法解决多智能体协作中动作与参数优化冲突问题，并采用接口级黑盒轨迹构建处理动态调度，在多跳QA和检索任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体通过中心智能体协调多个子智能体、工具和环境来解决长时域任务。训练中心策略面临两个核心挑战：(1) 动作标签是低基数决策，而其参数是高维条件序列，共享序列级信号优化两者会产生冲突梯度；(2) 动态调度创建相互依赖的会话，包含分支、并行调用和重写上下文，无法忠实地简化为单一扁平token序列。

Method: 提出Harness-RL结构化强化学习框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 冲突感知策略优化(CAPO)：利用前向激活识别与动作和参数token相关的参数分区，将其策略梯度路由到对应子空间，避免优化冲突；(2) 接口级黑盒轨迹构建：捕获接口调用记录，构建每会话前缀树，通过可训练token对齐结果奖励和过程奖励。框架支持仅训练中心智能体和联合多智能体训练两种模式。

Result: 在七个多跳问答和智能体检索基准测试中，Qwen2.5-1.5B和Qwen2.5-3B分别达到平均F1分数42.93和47.79。消融实验验证了CAPO组件的贡献，并证明在评估设置下仅优化中心智能体更为有效。

Conclusion: Harness-RL有效解决了多智能体协作系统中中心策略训练的关键挑战，通过分离动作和参数的优化过程以及保留轨迹结构信息，显著提升了模型在复杂长时域任务上的性能，为多智能体系统训练提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly solve long-horizon tasks through multi-agent harnesses in which a central agent coordinates specialized sub-agents, tools, and environments. Training the central policy in such a harness raises two challenges. First, an action label is a low-cardinality decision, whereas its args form a high-dimensional conditional sequence; optimizing both with a shared sequence-level signal can produce conflicting gradients. Second, dynamic scheduling creates interdependent sessions with branches, parallel calls, and rewritten contexts, which cannot be faithfully reduced to one flat token sequence. We introduce Harness-RL, a structured reinforcement learning framework that combines Conflict-Aware Policy Optimization (CAPO) with interface-level black-box trajectory construction. The black-box component captures Interface Call Records, builds per-session prefix trees, and aligns outcome and process rewards with trainable tokens. CAPO uses forward activations to identify parameter partitions associated with action and args tokens, then routes their policy gradients to the corresponding subspaces. Harness-RL supports both central-only and joint multi-agent training. Across seven multi-hop question answering and agentic retrieval benchmarks, it reaches average F1 scores of 42.93 and 47.79 with Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-3B, respectively, while ablations validate the contribution of CAPO and favor central-only optimization in the evaluated setting. Our code is available at https://github.com/jiangxinke/Harness-RL.

</details>


### [399] [Evidence, Logic, and Compliance: Multi-Agent Structured Graph Reasoning with Expert Arbitration for Medical Referral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30938)
*Qi Peng,Yi Cai,Jialin Cui,Tong Zhu,Yujuan Ding,Qingbao Huang,Tao Wang,Jiayuan Xie,Changmeng Zheng,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出MASGR框架，通过结构化图推理解决医疗转诊中的信息过载和协作无序问题，显著优于现有LLM和多智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在医疗转诊任务中存在两个关键局限：(1)信息过载——模型过度关注高频疾病术语而忽视微妙但关键的紧急指标；(2)协作无序——现有多智能体框架依赖松散对话，导致语义漂移和确认偏差。

Method: 提出MASGR（多智能体结构化图推理）框架，将转诊重新定义为结构化图构建问题。部署专门智能体从不同模态提取证据，通过临床推理图协调它们，强制建立冲突证据间的显式逻辑连接。集成知识引导仲裁机制，优先考虑患者安全规则而非标准诊断分类。

Result: 在真实医疗记录上的大量实验表明，MASGR显著优于最先进的LLMs和现有多智能体系统，特别是在需要平衡慢性病管理和紧急干预的复杂病例中表现突出。

Conclusion: MASGR通过将非结构化多智能体对话转化为可验证的逻辑图构建，成功应用于复杂医疗决策系统，优化了复杂医疗转诊的精准度，为医疗AI提供了新的技术范式。

Abstract: Medical referral (directing patients to the appropriate hospital department) is a complex decision-making process requiring the synthesis of multimodal data, including patient narratives, laboratory indicators, and radiology imaging. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced medical dialogue systems, they struggle with real-world referral tasks due to two primary limitations: (1) Information Overload, where models fixate on high-frequency disease terms while overlooking subtle but critical urgency indicators; and (2) Unstructured Collaboration, where existing multi-agent frameworks rely on loose dialogue that leads to semantic drift and confirmation bias. To address these challenges, we introduce MASGR (Multi-Agent Structured Graph Reasoning), a framework that treats referral not as a classification task but as a structured graph construction problem. MASGR deploys specialized agents to extract evidence from distinct modalities and coordinates them through a clinical reasoning graph. This graph forces agents to establish explicit logical connections between conflicting evidence. Furthermore, we integrate a knowledge-guided arbitration mechanism that prioritizes patient safety rules over standard diagnostic classification. Extensive experiments on real-world medical records demonstrate that MASGR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art LLMs and existing multi-agent systems, particularly in complex cases requiring the balancing of chronic disease management and emergency intervention. The AI contribution lies in the Multi-Agent Structured Graph Reasoning framework that transforms unstructured multi-agent dialogue into a verifiable logical graph construction. The engineering application is demonstrated through its deployment in a complex healthcare decision-making system to optimize the precision of complex medical referrals.

</details>
